<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体α-MnTe在室温以下展现出巨大的反常霍尔电导率，伴随由自旋和轨道极化产生的弱铁磁性，但其大AHC值的微观起源尚不明确，需要系统阐明其能带拓扑结构与对称性根源。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算识别价带中两套由对称性强制（symmetry-enforced）的节点线，分别位于k_z=0（受镜像对称M_z保护）和k_z=π/c（受滑移对称G_z保护）；结合能带色散分析（墨西哥帽型与倒墨西哥帽型色散），并利用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）的线性二色性（linear dichroism）进行实验验证。

Result: 两套节点线具备近似C₆对称性，因Néel矢量的存在降为精确的C₂对称；节点线位于最高与次高价带的交叉处，是巨大AHC的根源；AHC与弱铁磁性存在强相互耦合，轻微自旋倾斜即可显著改变节点线与AHC，实现磁调控；在较小倾斜角下交替磁贡献主导AHC，大倾斜角时铁磁贡献显著；ARPES线性二色性信号在布里渊区边界证实了节点线的存在。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将α-MnTe中巨大的AHC归因于两套对称保护的能带节点线，揭示了交替磁性与弱铁磁性对电导率的协同与竞争机制，为磁性材料的拓扑输运调控提供了新的物理图像。

Abstract: Altermagnetic $α$-MnTe exhibits huge anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) up to room-temperature together with weak ferromagnetism arising from spin and orbital polarizations. We clarify the origin of the large value of the AHC by identifying two sets of distinct symmetry-enforced nodal lines in the valence bands with Mn character, located at $k_z=0$ and $k_z=\fracπ{c}$, protected by mirror symmetry $M_z$ and glide symmetry $G_z = \{M_z\,|\,0,0,\tfrac{c}{2}\}$, respectively. Both nodal lines are energy-dependent with an approximate C$_6$ symmetry, which is reduced to an exact C$_2$ symmetry due to the presence of the Néel vector. The highest valence band exhibits a Mexican-hat dispersion, whereas the second-highest valence band exhibits an inverted Mexican-hat dispersion, with nodal lines at the crossing between the two bands. Within first-principles accuracy, we demonstrate that these nodal lines give rise to the large AHC observed experimentally and exhibit a strong interplay with the weak ferromagnetism. We further show that even a small spin canting strongly modifies the nodal lines and the AHC, making them both magnetically tunable. By disentangling the altermagnetic and ferromagnetic contributions to the AHC, the altermagnetic contribution dominates at small canting angles, while the ferromagnetic contribution becomes sizeable for larger values. Using linear dichroism in angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, we show a signature of the nodal line at the border of the Brillouin zone.

</details>


### [79] [Real-time dynamics of the two-step charge-density-wave transition in bulk 1T-TaS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02456)
*Daeheon Kim,Jinseok Oh,Kahyeon Koh,Yejun Cho,Angel Rubio,Noejung Park,Dongbin Shin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用基于第一性原理数据训练的机器学习力场（machine-learning force field），对块体1T-TaS2中的电荷密度波（charge-density wave, CDW）相变进行了长达5纳秒、包含1404个原子的超胞实时模拟，揭示了CDW熔化的两步机制：在200 K以上，Star-of-David（SoD）团簇通过变形-再形成过程协同平移，保持局部SoD幅度不变但同时随机化层间堆叠顺序并成核畴壁；仅在接近350 K时SoD畸变本身才塌缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块体1T-TaS2的CDW相变通过两步序列熔化，并伴随电阻率数量级的变化，但这两步究竟是通过SoD幅度塌缩还是SoD晶格重排实现，一直悬而未决。相关动力学发生在远超从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics）可及的长度和时间尺度上，因而亟待新的模拟手段。

Method: 作者训练了一个基于第一性原理数据的机器学习力场，以访问1404个原子超胞内长达5纳秒的动力学过程，从而在真实时间尺度上追踪CDW相变。

Result: 模拟揭示了CDW熔化的两个步骤在机制上截然不同：在200 K以上，SoD团簇通过瞬态溶解并在移位中心重新形成的方式协同平移，该变形-再形成过程保持局部SoD幅度不变，但随机化层间堆叠顺序并成核畴壁；只有在接近350 K时SoD畸变本身才发生塌缩。

Conclusion: 这些结果为近期提出的CDW相变两步模型提供了微观层面的支持，并为解释1T-TaS2中光诱导隐藏相（light-induced hidden phases）和腔修正转变温度（cavity-modified transition temperatures）提供了理论框架。

Abstract: The charge-density wave (CDW) of bulk 1T-TaS2 is built from Star-of-David (SoD) clusters tiling a sqrt{13} x sqrt{13} superlattice, and it melts through a two-step sequence accompanied by order-of-magnitude changes in resistivity. Whether these steps proceed by collapse of the SoD amplitude or by rearrangement of the SoD lattice has remained unresolved, because the relevant dynamics occur on length and time scales beyond the reach of ab initio molecular dynamics. Here we follow the CDW transitions in real time using a machine-learning force field trained on first-principles data, giving access to 1404-atom supercells over 5 ns. The two steps are mechanistically distinct. Above 200 K, SoD clusters translate coherently by transiently dissolving and re-forming about shifted centers, a deformation-formation process that preserves the local SoD amplitude while randomizing the interlayer stacking order and nucleating domain walls. Only near 350 K does the SoD distortion itself collapse. These results provide microscopic support for the recently proposed two-step model of the CDW transition and offer a framework for interpreting light-induced hidden phases and cavity-modified transition temperatures in 1T-TaS2.

</details>


### [80] [Gradient expansion approximation of the inhomogeneous electron-gas revisited: Higher-order corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02524)
*Mario Benites,Angel Rosado,Efstratios Manousakis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在作者先前工作的基础上，将电子气梯度展开近似（GEA）的计算推广到次领头阶，获得了与Wigner-Seitz半径rs呈rs ln(rs)关系的梯度系数修正项，并证明该系数不受高阶图解表达式的修正，为广义梯度近似（GGA）泛函的发展提供了精确的约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者先前的工作解决了电子气梯度展开近似中领头阶系数B_xc[n]的历史争议，并指出流行GGA泛函曾被施加了错误的约束。本文旨在进一步计算次领头阶（scales as rs ln(rs)）的系数，以获得更完整的精确约束。

Method: 建立了系统化框架来评估长波极限（q→0）下密度-密度响应函数领头项（~q²）系数b_xc的积分表达式，这是计算B_xc[n]的前提；通过将计算扩展到次领头阶来获得与rs ln(rs)成正比的系数。

Result: 证明了rs ln(rs)项系数不受高阶图解表达式的修正，因此推导出的值构成一个精确、确定的约束条件，适用于未来GGA泛函的开发。

Conclusion: 在高密度慢变化极限下，任何有效的GGA泛函都必须同时复现作者先前工作与本文所确立的精确约束条件，这将指导泛函的改进与发展。

Abstract: In our recently published work (our Ref. 1) we revisited the gradient expansion approximation (GEA) of the interacting electron gas, and recalculated the leading-order contribution$-$with respect to the Wigner-Seitz radius $r_s$$-$to the coefficient $B_{xc}[n]$ of the square of the gradient of the electron density in the high-density and slowly varying limits. That work resolved historical controversies regarding these coefficients and demonstrated that serious misconceptions have led to incorrect constraints being imposed on popular functionals within the generalized gradient approximation (GGA). In the present paper, we extend this calculation to obtain the coefficient of the next-to-leading term, which scales as $r_s \ln(r_s)$ relative to the leading order. First, we establish a systematic framework to evaluate the integral expressions for the $b_{xc}$ coefficient of the leading term ($\sim q^2$) of the density-density response function in the long-wavelength limit ($q \to 0$)$-$a prerequisite for computing $B_{xc}[n]$. The significance of the calculation stems from the proof that the coefficient of this $r_s \ln(r_s)$ term receives no corrections from higher-order diagrammatic expressions. Consequently, our derived value serves as an exact, definitive constraint for future GGA functional development; in the high-density slowly-varying limit, any valid functional must reproduce the exact constraints established in both our previous work and the present paper.

</details>


### [81] [Revealing Intrinsic Anisotropy of Collective Magnetic Excitations in Twinned Crystals of a Kitaev-Heisenberg Quantum Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02531)
*Naipeng Zhang,Nikolai Simonov,Mykhaylo Ozerov,Sumedh Rathi,Nolan Heffner,Sara Huszar,Long Chen,Haidong Zhou,Guangxin Ni,Chaebin Kim,Martin Mourigal,Stephen M. Winter,Zhigang Jiang,Dmitry Smirnov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合磁光红外光谱（magneto-infrared spectroscopy）与域分辨微拉曼光谱（domain-resolved micro-Raman spectroscopy）的多模态光学方法，用于重建Kitaev-Heisenberg量子磁体Na₃Co₂SbO₆孪晶中本征磁激发谱。通过关联两种光谱技术，作者分离出单畴磁振子响应，发现远超g因子各向异性预期的面内二重磁振子各向异性，并将其归因于各向异性键依赖交换相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有竞争相互作用的量子磁体通常源于微观参数的微妙平衡，因此区分本征自旋动力学与外在无序效应至关重要，但孪晶结构会引入複制特征（replica features）掩盖本征响应。

Method: 采用高磁场下磁光红外光谱与域分辨微拉曼光谱相结合的多模态光学方法，对Kitaev-Heisenberg量子磁体Na₃Co₂SbO₆孪晶进行系统性测量，并通过关联两类光谱信号来分离单畴磁振子（magnon）响应。

Result: 远红外光谱揭示了多个可用磁场调谐的磁激发，同时孪晶域产生的複制特征被成功消除，从而分离出单畴磁振子响应，并发现了显著的面内二重磁振子各向异性。该各向异性远超由面内g因子各向异性所预期的数值，其主要来源于各向异性键依赖交换相互作用。

Conclusion: 通过统一高场、高分辨率与空间选择性光学探测手段，该工作建立了一种广泛适用的框架，用于揭示多畴量子磁体中的本征自旋动力学，并约束其自旋哈密顿量（spin Hamiltonian）参数。

Abstract: Quantum magnets with competing interactions often emerge from delicate balances among microscopic parameters, making it essential to disentangle intrinsic spin dynamics from extrinsic disorder effects. Here, we introduce a multimodal optical approach combining magneto-infrared spectroscopy with domain-resolved micro-Raman spectroscopy at high magnetic fields to reconstruct the intrinsic magnetic excitation spectrum of twinned crystals of the Kitaev-Heisenberg quantum magnet Na$_3$Co$_2$SbO$_6$. Far-infrared spectroscopy reveals multiple field-tunable magnetic excitations, but the intrinsic response is obscured by replica features arising from twin domains. By correlating magneto-infrared and domain-resolved Raman spectra, we isolate the single-domain magnon response and uncover a pronounced twofold in-plane magnon anisotropy. This anisotropy far exceeds that expected from the measured in-plane g-factor anisotropy and is instead dominated by anisotropic bond-dependent exchange interactions. By unifying high-field, high-resolution and spatially selective optical probes, our work establishes a broadly applicable framework for revealing intrinsic spin dynamics and constraining the spin Hamiltonian in multidomain quantum magnets.

</details>

### [82] [Solving the Dissipation Inequality not as a constitutive restriction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02215)
*Maximiliano Larrain Silva,Amit Acharya*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将连续介质力学（continuum mechanics）中的非线性耗散不等式（Dissipation Inequality）作为约束方程进行求解的程序，允许对材料本构行为掌握不完全，并通过对率相关弹塑性杆问题的演示验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续介质力学中，耗散不等式是热力学第二定律的体现，但将其作为完整约束处理往往因本构关系未知而困难。本文旨在建立一种通用求解程序，使耗散不等式在本构知识不完整的情况下仍能作为有效的约束方程被纳入求解过程。

Method: 将耗散不等式作为约束方程纳入连续介质力学的求解体系，形成非线性约束优化（constrained optimization）问题；通过构建一系列凸优化（convex optimization）问题进行数值求解，同时给出闭式解（closed form solution）与计算解。

Result: 以率相关弹塑性（rate-dependent elastoplastic）杆件响应为例，计算解被证明具有较高精度；该方法能够自动修正刻意设置的有缺陷本构规范，使最终解符合连续介质力学的基本公设。

Conclusion: 所提程序为本构行为知识不完整情形下强制执行耗散不等式约束提供了可行途径，并能自动纠正错误的本构设定，从而保证解满足热力学一致性与连续介质力学基本公设。

Abstract: A solution procedure is formulated and solved for treating the nonlinear Dissipation Inequality as a constraint equation within continuum mechanics, and allowing for incomplete knowledge of constitutive behavior. The scheme is demonstrated in the context of the rate-dependent, elastoplastic response of a bar, resulting in a nonlinear problem of constrained optimization. Both closed form and computational results are developed. The computational solutions utilize a sequence of convex optimization problems, and are shown to be accurate. In the example considered, the approach is shown to automatically correct an (intentionally) faulty constitutive specification, resulting in the solution to be in accord with the fundamental postulates of continuum mechanics.

</details>

### [83] [Self-Forces as Nonlocal Probes of Gravastar Interiors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00121)
*Bokai Zhang,Yungui Gong,Anzhong Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在薄壳引力星（gravastar）背景下计算标量电荷与电电荷的静态保守自引力（self-force），通过弱场展开并在闭式（closed form）中求和，发现自引力不仅依赖局部几何，还依赖时空全域结构。标量电荷在引力星外部的自引力非零（不同于同质量Schwarzschild黑洞的严格为零），并在远处呈现 2/5 q²MR²/r₀⁵ 的行为；电电荷则是对Smith–Will力的结构依赖修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有相同外部度规的致密天体对测试粒子而言在局部不可区分，但基于推迟场（retarded fields）的可观测量可以保留粒子邻域之外时空结构的信息。自引力作为弯曲时空中粒子的独特探针，能够同时揭示背景时空的局部几何与全局结构，因此值得深入计算研究。

Abstract: The entropy of the observable Universe is dominated, by fifteen orders of magnitude, by the horizons of supermassive black holes, and gravitational collapse into black holes has often been proposed as the Universe's preferred channel of entropy production. First, an entropy production history: the rate $dS/dt(z)$ resolved by channel, from the star formation history and a Soltan-normalized accretion history. Black hole horizon growth exceeds all radiative channels by a factor $\sim 10^{16-17}$ after the first seeds; the total rate peaked near $z\approx 1$--$2$ and has since declined; the late peak is structural, because horizon entropy production weights accretion by the accretor's mass and the most massive accretors are assembled last. Second, a causal-hydrodynamic envelope: the fastest entropy-producing trajectory permitted by causality and gas dynamics, $\dot M \sim c_s^3/G$ in atomic-cooling halos, which reaches $10^{7\pm1}\,M_\odot$ by $z\approx 8$--$10$ without radiative throttling, and whose phenomenology (obscured, radiatively inefficient, X-ray weak, red) closely resembles the JWST little red dots. The realized-to-envelope ratio defines an efficiency $F(z)$, which remains between $10^{-7}$ and $10^{-4}$ throughout $4\le z\le 12$, even under the most generous JWST-era bracket. Third, order-of-magnitude no-go results: Boltzmann weighting of collapse channels by entropy gain, dissipative adaptation applied to self-gravitating systems, and any strong maximum-entropy-production principle in cosmology all fail at leading order, by factors of up to $10^{16}$. The Universe produces entropy overwhelmingly through black holes, yet remains far below its own envelope at all times. Whatever selects cosmic structure, it does not maximize entropy production.

The shadow contour follows from the Carter-Hamilton-Jacobi separation. Method: 采用数值方法求解修正的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程，并应用非复数化（non-complexifying）算法构造数学正则的旋转度规拟设（rotating metric ansatz），构建描述修正引力引力真空星与赤道吸积流（equatorial accretion flows）之间动力相互作用的唯象命题模型。

Result: 研究表明修正引力耦合参数系统性改变有效势（effective potential），从而移动最小稳定圆轨道（Innermost Stable Circular Orbit, ISCO）的位置；等离激元（plasma）与引力真空星表面碰撞的理想化热力学分析表明，可能产生独特的热辐射（thermal emission），理论上可在干涉观测中形成"填充式"（filled-in）中央阴影。

Conclusion: 尽管存在观测简并性挑战且刻意忽略了复杂的辐射压反馈，本文提出的几何与热学特征仍可作为一种透明的概念基准，为未来广义相对论磁流体动力学（general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic, GRMHD）数值模拟研究提供激励与方向。

Abstract: While General Relativity remains our most rigorously tested framework for gravitation, the theoretical persistence of singularities within standard black hole solutions continues to motivate the exploration of mathematically regular alternatives. Gravitational vacuum stars (gravastars) offer a non-singular model, substituting the event horizon with a physical, ultra-stiff thin shell. Recent studies have demonstrated that extended theories, such as $f(R, L_m, T)$ gravity, can structurally support these objects by utilizing the non-minimal coupling between geometry and matter. Building upon these static foundations, this paper presents a phenomenological propositional model to explore the dynamic interactions between modified-gravity gravastars and equatorial accretion flows. By numerically solving the modified Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations and applying a non-complexifying algorithm, we construct a mathematically regular rotating metric ansatz. We demonstrate that the modified gravity coupling parameter systematically alters the effective potential, shifting the location of the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO). Furthermore, we explore the idealized thermodynamics of plasma colliding with the gravastar surface, suggesting a distinct thermal emission that could theoretically produce a ``filled-in'' central shadow in interferometric observations. While acknowledging the challenges of observational degeneracy and the deliberate omission of complex radiation pressure feedback, we offer these geometric and thermal signatures as a transparent conceptual baseline to motivate future general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic (GRMHD) campaigns.

</details>

*Jingxu Wu,Jie Shi,Liangyu Luo*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过严格不等式分析建立Δ(r)严格凸性及根计数的一极小值判据；采用模糊暗物质启发的基准轮廓满足该界；施加一般形变m/M_ADM=1+εh，利用一阶微扰理论推导外视界半径、极端分支、光子球半径及阴影泛函的响应。

Result: 证明了静态视界和光子响应由质量轮廓控制；自旋奇数（spin-odd）阴影位移依赖于具体补全（completion）方式；在尺度一致的弱场极限下，局域轮廓梯度效应对观测量的贡献远小于10⁻²⁰量级。

Conclusion: 强场区域可作为形式上的径向轮廓基准（formal radial-profile benchmark），但它并非一个自洽的旋转标量场（rotating scalar-field）解，因此不构成对真实暗物质旋转解的替代。

Abstract: We establish a sufficient one-minimum criterion for the off-shell Kerr family $Δ(r) = r^2 - 2rm(r) + a^2$ with a positive, nondecreasing mass profile $m(r)$, showing that $1 - 2m'(r) - rm''(r) > 0$ ensures strict convexity and determines root counts for $Δ$. Using a fuzzy-dark-matter-inspired benchmark satisfying this bound, we derive first-order responses for the outer horizon, extremal branch, photon sphere, and shadow functional under general deformations $m/M_{\text{ADM}} = 1 + \varepsilon h$. We demonstrate that static horizon and photon responses are profile-controlled, spin-odd shadow displacements are completion-dependent, and scale-consistent weak-field limits render local profile-gradient effects negligible ($\ll 10^{-20}$), confirming the strong-field box as a formal radial-profile benchmark rather than a self-consistent rotating scalar-field solution.

</details>

Method: 在非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）的P对偶表述框架中，作者分析了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦源项的符号结构，推导了有效标量源在内外视界之间发生符号翻转的充分条件，使积分约束（integral constraint）得以满足而不破坏柯西视界。

Result: 推导出了标量源在视界区间变号的一般性条件，表明柯西视界的存续取决于电磁耦合的具体形式，并筛选出了一类候选理论，其标量化视界可以持续存在。

Conclusion: 该框架开辟了研究视界间区域标量化及其与质量膨胀（mass inflation）相互作用的新途径，明确了柯西视界的命运与电磁耦合密切相关。

Abstract: Spontaneous scalarization provides a dynamical mechanism to evade the no-hair paradigm, but it has been argued to generically eliminate the Cauchy horizon in charged black holes. Within the $P$-dual formulation of nonlinear electrodynamics, we derive a general condition under which the effective scalar source changes sign between the horizons, allowing the integral constraint to be satisfied without destroying the Cauchy horizon. This establishes that the fate of the Cauchy horizon depends on the electromagnetic coupling and identifies candidate theories where scalarized horizons may persist. The resulting framework opens the possibility of studying scalarization in the inter-horizon region and its interplay with mass inflation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 模型给出q₀=-0.60和z_t=1.0，与观测相容；GHRDE状态方程参数ω_{G,0}=-0.81位于quintessence区域，有效状态方程单调趋向-1且无phantom跨越；statefinder将模型定位在r–s平面的quintessence区，远离ΛCDM不动点；相空间显示特征值为严格负的稳定node de Sitter吸引子；零、弱和主导能量条件在整个红移范围成立，强能量条件在低红移被违反。

Conclusion: 该模型为晚期宇宙加速提供稳健的理论描述，可作为ΛCDM的可行替代方案。后续工作将结合宇宙计时器数据、Pantheon+超新星和重子声学振荡测量数据进行观测约束验证。

Abstract: The cosmological dynamics of generalized holographic Ricci dark energy (GHRDE) of Xu type, embedded within power-law $f(\mathcal{T})=α\mathcal{T}+β\mathcal{T}^{m}$ teleparallel gravity and driven by the Hubble-parameter-dependent deceleration law $q=b-ν/H$, are investigated. The deceleration law yields an exact closed-form Hubble function $H(z)$ that interpolates between an early decelerating phase and an asymptotic de~Sitter regime, enabling all cosmological diagnostics to be expressed as explicit analytical functions of redshift. The present-day deceleration parameter $q_{0}=-0.60$ and transition redshift $z_{t}=1.0$ are consistent with current observational estimates. The GHRDE equation-of-state parameter $ω_{G,0}=-0.81$ lies within the quintessence band, and the effective equation of state evolves monotonically toward $-1$ without phantom crossing. The geometrical statefinder pair locates the model in the quintessence region of the $r$--$s$ plane, clearly displaced from the $Λ$CDM fixed point. A phase-space analysis reveals a stable-node de~Sitter attractor with strictly negative eigenvalues, guaranteeing robust late-time acceleration for generic initial conditions. The null, weak, and dominant energy conditions are satisfied throughout the redshift range considered, while the strong energy condition is violated at low redshift, as expected for an accelerating Universe. An observational constraint methodology combining cosmic chronometer data, Pantheon$+$ supernovae, and baryon acoustic oscillation measurements is outlined as the natural continuation of this work.

</details>


### [93] [A Simple Pendulum in Schwarzschild Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00866)
*Andrzej Czarnecki,Andrew Czezowski*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究确定了Schwarzschild时空中简单位置单摆的小振荡周期。瞬态衰减后，被位移的绳子与诱导空间度规中的一条测地线（geodesic）重合，从而确定摆锤在角振幅二次阶处的径向提升，并得到由摆锤平衡位置处观测者测量的周期公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论背景中研究经典单摆问题，探究弯曲时空下摆的周期如何修正，并与牛顿极限下的经典结果建立联系。该问题涉及测地线偏离（geodesic deviation）与度规诱导几何等基本物理概念。

Method: 作者在Schwarzschild时空背景下进行解析推导：首先假定瞬态衰减后摆绳与诱导空间度规（induced spatial metric）的测地线重合；将摆锤的径向抬升展开至角振幅的二次阶；随后在摆锤平衡位置处计算由当地观测者测量的振荡周期。

Result: 得到周期表达式 $T_{(2)}=4π\frac{r_2^2}{c r_s}\sqrt{N_2(N_1-N_2)}$，其中 $N(r)=\sqrt{1-r_s/r}$ 为Schwarzschild lapse函数。在牛顿极限下精确复现经典结果 $T=2π\sqrt{L_0/g}$。近地平线区域的周期可用等效摆长（effective pendulum length）加以解释。

Conclusion: 周期公式仅由Schwarzschild lapse函数（即决定时钟速率和引力红移的同一函数）表达，体现了广义相对论中几何与时钟速率的内在统一性。作者还论证了其处理方法与早前研究采用的坐标长度约束（coordinate-length constraint）在远离经典弱场极限时并不等价，两个问题是不可互换的。

Abstract: We determine the small-oscillation period of a simple pendulum in Schwarzschild spacetime. After transients decay, the displaced rope coincides with a geodesic of the induced spatial metric. This determines the radial lift of the bob to quadratic order in the angular amplitude and leads to the period measured by an observer at the bob's equilibrium position, $r=r_2$, with the fulcrum at $r_1$, \[ T_{(2)}=4π\frac{r_2^2}{c r_s}\sqrt{N_2(N_1-N_2)}, \] where $N_i=N(r_i)$ and $N(r)=\sqrt{1-r_s/r}$ is the Schwarzschild lapse. The result is therefore expressed in terms of the same function that determines clock rates and gravitational redshifts. In the Newtonian limit, we reproduce the classical result $T=2π\sqrt{L_0/g}$. We interpret the period close to the horizon in terms of an effective pendulum length. We compare our treatment with the coordinate-length constraint adopted in an earlier study and argue that the two problems are inequivalent away from the classical, weak-field limit.

</details>


### [94] [Unified remnant models for aligned-spin, precessing, and eccentric binary black hole mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00934)
*Tousif Islam,Digvijay Wadekar,Gaurav Khanna*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于约5000个数值相对论（numerical relativity, NR）模拟（质量比至q=128）和1200个黑洞微扰理论（black-hole-perturbation theory, BHPT）模拟（质量比至q=1000），构建了准圆双黑洞并合残余性质的完全解析模型gwModelRemS与gwModelRemP，分别描述非进动与进动双星系统的最终质量、最终自旋、峰值光度及反冲速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双黑洞并合的残余性质（如最终质量、自旋、峰值光度和反冲速度）对引力波天文学和天体物理群体研究至关重要，而现有解析模型在精度和适用范围上存在局限，亟需覆盖从等质量到极端质量比（extreme-mass-ratio）全域的高精度解析模型。

Method: 该方法融合了后牛顿（post-Newtonian）理论和点粒子极限的解析洞察，结合数据驱动拟合框架，并引入验证引导的AI智能体辅助优化。此外，对进动双星的反冲速度构建了归一化流（normalizing flow）模型gwModelRemP_flow，基于波形数据增广（data-augmentation）程序扩大了低保真训练集，并通过离心率与轨道近点角的领头阶依赖引入简单的偏心扩展。

Result: 所构建的拟合模型优于现有解析模型，在其有效域内实现了与数据驱动模型相当的精度。模型覆盖从等质量到极端质量比的全部质量比区间，已通过gwModels软件包公开发布，可应用于引力波天文学、天体物理群体研究和宇宙学研究。

Conclusion: 本文提供了覆盖全质量比范围的、精度可与数据驱动模型相当的完全解析残余性质模型，并通过流模型与数据增广技术有效处理了进动反冲速度的建模挑战，为引力波天文学及相关领域提供了实用且公开的工具。

Abstract: Using approximately $5000$ numerical-relativity (NR) simulations spanning mass ratios up to $q=128$ and $1200$ black-hole-perturbation-theory (BHPT) simulations extending to $q=1000$, we present fully analytic models for the remnant properties of quasi-circular binary black hole mergers. The models gwModelRemS (for final mass, final spin, peak luminosity, and recoil kick) and gwModelRemP (for final mass, final spin, and peak luminosity) describe nonprecessing and precessing binaries, respectively. Our approach combines analytic insights from post-Newtonian theory and point-particle limits with a data-driven fitting framework which also includes validation-guided AI-agent-assisted optimization. The resulting fits outperform existing analytic models and achieve accuracies comparable to data-driven models within their domains of validity. We also construct gwModelRemP\_flow, a normalizing-flow model for recoil kicks from precessing binaries, marginalized over the in-plane spin orientations. This model additionally utilizes an enlarged low-fidelity training set, beyond the available NR and BHPT simulations, constructed through a waveform-based data-augmentation procedure. We also include simple eccentric extensions through leading-order dependence on eccentricity and orbital anomaly. The models span the equal-mass to extreme-mass-ratio regimes and are publicly available through the gwModels package for applications in gravitational-wave astronomy, astrophysical population studies, and cosmology.

</details>


### [95] [Optimal frequency scales for probing black-hole geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01061)
*Saulo Albuquerque,Sebastian H. Völkel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过受控散射思想实验（Gedankenexperiment）研究引力波探测黑洞近视界几何的最优频率尺度。研究发现，虽然短波长通常能分辨更小尺度，但黑洞散射存在信息论意义上的最优脉冲宽度——约等于势垒峰值倒数的平方根，平衡了空间分辨率和反射信息量之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是引力波能否探测黑洞的近视界几何，以及哪个频率尺度是最优的。虽然直觉上更短波长能分辨更小尺度，但作者推测黑洞散射过程可能引入一个信息论层面的最优值，需要系统验证。

Method: 构建受控散射思想实验：将高斯标量测试场脉冲射向黑洞势垒，利用反射波形推断几何信息。用Rezzolla-Zhidenko度规参数化近视界偏差，通过高信噪比极限下的Fisher矩阵量化信息恢复量。窄高频脉冲虽能分辨短尺度但大多透射过势垒，宽脉冲虽有效反射但分辨率差，两者竞争产生最优脉冲宽度。利用Pöschl-Teller解析解模拟准正规模（quasinormal modes）的正确激发，并分离基模与全波形（含瞬态响应）中的信息。

Result: 数值结果表明最优脉冲宽度由势垒峰值的逆平方根决定。因此最优探测器并非最高频脉冲，而是平衡空间分辨率与反射信息量的波形。这一结果将黑洞光谱学（black-hole spectroscopy）、半经典势垒散射和信息论联系在一起。

Conclusion: 引力波确实可以探测黑洞近视界几何，但存在信息论意义上的最优频率尺度。最优探针不是最高频脉冲，而是平衡空间分辨力与反射信息的波形。

Abstract: Can gravitational waves probe the near-horizon geometry of black holes, and if yes, which frequency scale is optimal? Although shorter wavelengths usually resolve smaller scales, we show that black-hole scattering may impose an information-theoretic optimum. We study a controlled scattering Gedankenexperiment in which Gaussian pulses of scalar test fields are sent toward a black- hole potential and the reflected waveform is used to infer the geometry. Near-horizon deviations are parametrized with the Rezzolla-Zhidenko metric, and information recovery is quantified by the Fisher matrix in the high-signal-to-noise limit. Narrow, high-frequency pulses resolve short scales but are mostly transmitted through the barrier, while wide pulses are efficiently reflected but poorly resolve the potential. Their competition selects an optimal pulse width, numerically found to be set by about the inverse square root of the potential peak. Using the Pöschl-Teller analytical solutions, we further model the correct excitation of quasinormal modes and separate the information in the fundamental mode from that in the full waveform, including the prompt response. The optimal probe is therefore not the highest-frequency pulse, but the waveform that balances spatial resolution against reflected information, linking black-hole spectroscopy, semiclassical barrier scattering, and information theory.

</details>


### [96] [New constraints on modified gravity with dimension-six operators from gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01118)
*Justo López-Sarrión,Carlos M. Reyes,César Riquelme,Marco Schreck*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由能打破时空对称性的高导数项引起的引力波传播修正，基于引力标准模型扩充（Standard-Model Extension）中两个维数为6的贡献，推导了协变形式的修正色散关系，并利用GW170817、GRB170817A和GW150914三个引力波事件对相关系数进行了约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准线性化引力理论可能在高能尺度或存在洛伦兹破缺的情况下不再完全成立，因此需要检验更高导数项对引力波传播的影响。通过实际观测数据约束这些修正项的系数，可以量化标准线性化引力的适用范围。

Method: 论文以引力标准模型扩充（gravitational Standard-Model Extension）中两个维数为6的贡献为出发点，对作用量进行线性化处理，得到包含时空常数背景场的波动方程。由此推导引力波的修正色散关系（modified dispersion relations），并将系数按类似于电磁场的指标结构分类为非双折射（nonbirefringent）与双折射（birefringent）扇区。利用观测事件对两类系数分别进行约束。

Result: 基于GW170817事件引力波与GRB170817A光子到达时间差，对非双折射系数给出约束；基于GW150914事件中缺乏可观测的两种模式分离，对双折射系数给出边界。

Conclusion: 这些约束量化了在所考虑修正框架下标准线性化引力的有效程度，表明高导数修正对引力波传播的影响在现有观测精度下受到严格限制。

Abstract: The present article deals with gravitational-wave propagation affected by higher-derivative terms that break spacetime symmetries. Two dimension-6 contributions of the gravitational Standard-Model Extension pose our starting point. A linearization of the action implies a wave equation that contains additional terms with spacetime-constant background fields. The modified dispersion relations in covariant form for gravitational waves are derived, where we distinguish between nonbirefringent and birefringent sectors. A classification of the coefficients in terms of sets with index structures resembling those of the electromagnetic fields has proven to be valuable. We constrain the nonbirefringent coefficients based on the measured arrival time difference between the gravitational wave and photons from the events GW170817 and GRB 170817A, respectively. Bounds on the birefringent coefficients result from the absence of a perceivable separation of the two modes in the event GW150914. These findings quantify the extent to which standard linearized gravity is valid based on the modifications considered.

</details>


### [97] [Kerr-NUT Thermodynamics: Misner Strings and Nut Charges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01152)
*Mohamed Tharwat,Adel Awad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于e-Print 2206.09124提出的热力学方法，研究Kerr-NUT解时发现了一个与角动量对偶的新守恒量，其地位类似nut电荷与质量的对偶关系。该量无论转动参数a是否存在都存在，但仅在a≠0时影响热力学。结合N_c=cn与n两个电荷计算的热力学量满足第一定律、Smarr关系和Gibbs-Duhem关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论与黑洞热力学中，寻找新的守恒量及其对热力学关系的修正一直是核心问题。本文旨在将前期提出的热力学方法推广到Kerr-NUT解中，探索与角动量对偶的新守恒量是否存在，并验证其热力学自洽性。

Method: 采用e-Print 2206.09124 [hep-th]中引入的热力学方法（thermodynamic approach）应用于Kerr-NUT解，引入新守恒量并计算相关热力学量；定义了电荷N_c=cn，将其与nut电荷n共同纳入热力学框架，检验第一定律、Smarr关系与Gibbs-Duhem关系的成立性。

Result: 发现了一个与角动量对偶的新守恒量，其与角动量的对偶关系类似于nut电荷n与质量m的对偶关系。该量不依赖于Kerr转动参数a的存在，但仅在a≠0时才对热力学性质产生影响。将N_c=cn与n两个电荷纳入后计算的热力学量使得第一定律、Smarr关系和Gibbs-Duhem关系均成立。

Conclusion: 在Kerr-NUT解中确实存在一个与角动量对偶的新守恒量，且该量的引入能够使热力学关系保持完全一致，表明该守恒量是Kerr-NUT黑洞热力学框架中不可或缺的组成部分。

Abstract: Using the thermodynamic approach introduced in e-Print: 2206.09124 [hep-th] to the case of Kerr-NUT solution, we demonstrate the existence of a new conserved quantity that is dual to the angular momentum, in the same way that the nut charge "n" is dual to the mass "m". This quantity exists regardless of the presence of the Kerr rotation parameter $a$, but only affects its thermodynamics when $a\neq 0$. The thermodynamic quantities calculated together with the two charges $N_{c}=cn$ and $n$ lead to consistent thermodynamic relations. That is, the first law, Smarr's and Gibbs-Duhem relations are all satisfied.

</details>


### [98] [From Quantum Correlations to Inflationary Tracking Scalar Field Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01165)
*V. K. Oikonomou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为单标量场暴胀理论中的跟踪条件（tracking condition）$\dot{\phi}^2 = \gamma H^{-m}$ 寻找理论基础，通过将暴胀前的宇宙视为量子统计系统，展示了标量场可作为量子自由度集体凝聚态（condensate）出现，并利用信息论动机和威尔逊量子场论（Wilsonian quantum field theory）论证推导出该跟踪条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知分析性暴胀解（analytic inflationary solutions）与当前宇宙微波背景辐射（cosmic microwave background radiation）实验吻合，但此前缺乏对跟踪条件 $\dot{\phi}^2 = \gamma H^{-m}$ 的理论推导基础，作者旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将暴胀前的宇宙视为量子统计系统（quantum statistical system），论证标量场可作为量子自由度的集体凝聚态涌现；利用量子到经典转变（quantum-to-classical transition）中的两点关联函数推导出理论的磁化率（susceptibility）及内禀关联长度（correlation length）；结合信息论（information theory）动机与威尔逊量子场论论证，推导出跟踪条件。

Result: 作者证明了跟踪条件 $\dot{\phi}^2 = \gamma H^{-m}$ 可以从上述量子统计宇宙框架中自然涌现，为该条件提供了理论上的微观基础。

Conclusion: 该研究为与实验数据吻合的分析性暴胀解提供了量子统计的理论根基，表明跟踪条件并非经验性假设，而是可以从暴胀前宇宙的量子统计性质与威尔逊重整化思想中推导得出。

Abstract: The tracking condition $\dotφ^2=γH^{-m}$ for single scalar field theory leads to analytic inflationary solutions, which are compatible with the current cosmic microwave background radiation experiments. To our knowledge this is the only analytic solution of inflation which is compatible with the data, to date. In this work we seek for some theoretical basis that can lead to the tracking condition $\dotφ^2=γH^{-m}$. As we show, if the Universe is seen pre-inflationary as a quantum statistical system, the scalar field may emerge as a collective condensate of the quantum degrees of freedom. In the quantum-to-classical transition of the Universe, the scalar field two point function yields the susceptibility of the theory with an inherent correlation length. Using information theoretic motivation and Wilsonian quantum field theoretic arguments, we demonstrate that the tracking conditions $\dotφ^2=γH^{-m}$ emerge from this framework.

</details>


### [99] [Quasinormal modes response to thermodynamic phase transitions in the charged AdS black hole surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01200)
*Long-Xiang Li,Zhong-Wen Feng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究带电反德西特（AdS）黑洞在完美流体暗物质（PFDM）环境下的准正规模（QNM）如何反映热力学相变。研究发现小/大黑洞分支的QNM轨迹清晰分离，在一阶相变附近呈现显著不同的斜率，可作为分支转变的动力学特征，但二阶相变在QNM中无尖锐信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示热力学相变在黑洞准正规模谱中的动力学表现，特别是引入完美流体暗物质后对相结构和QNM响应的影响。

Method: 在扩展相空间中，利用切比雪夫伪谱方法（Chebyshev pseudospectral method）计算无质量标量扰动的基态QNM，并沿等压和等温过程分析其在临界点以下的演化。

Result: 增大正PFDM参数会提高临界温度和压力并减小临界视界半径；小/大黑洞分支的QNM轨迹清晰分离，一阶相变附近斜率差异显著；等温过程中QNM演化受视界半径与压力（即AdS半径）竞争影响而非仅视界半径；临界点处QNM频率随视界半径平滑变化，无二阶相变尖锐特征；共存曲线上的QNM分离随Gibbs自由能燕尾图收缩而减小并在临界点消失。

Conclusion: PFDM同时移动了热力学相结构和相应的QNM响应，基态标量QNM谱对一阶小/大黑洞相变保持敏感，但不呈现二阶相变的尖锐特征。

Abstract: We investigate how thermodynamic phase transitions are reflected in the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of charged anti-de Sitter (AdS) black holes surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM). In the extended phase space, increasing the positive PFDM parameter raises the critical temperature and pressure while reducing the critical horizon radius. We compute the fundamental QNMs of a massless scalar perturbation using a Chebyshev pseudospectral method and analyze their evolution along isobaric and isothermal processes below the critical point. The small and large black hole branches trace clearly separated QNM trajectories and display sharply different slopes near the first-order transition, providing a dynamical signature of the branch change. Along isotherms, this evolution results from the competing effects of the horizon radius and pressure, or equivalently the AdS radius, rather than from the horizon radius alone. At the critical point, however, the QNM frequencies vary smoothly with the horizon radius and show no sharp signature of the second-order transition. Along the coexistence curve, the separation between the small and large black hole QNMs decreases as the Gibbs free energy swallowtail shrinks and vanishes at criticality. These results show that PFDM shifts both the thermodynamic phase structure and the associated QNM response, while the fundamental scalar QNM spectrum remains sensitive to the first-order small/large black hole transition.

</details>


### [100] [A geometrical-optics analogy for gravitational lensing using axicon-type lenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01384)
*Santiago Hernández-Díaz,Ángel S. Sanz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于几何光学（geometrical optics）的引力透镜（gravitational lensing）类比模型，利用恒定折射率的轴锥透镜（axicon lens）进行光线追踪，通过折射曲面几何而非空间变化折射率来产生类透镜图像形态，可作为教学和计算工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力透镜常通过质量使光偏折来引入，但其几何本质在没有广义相对论（general relativity）完整框架时难以直观可视化，作者希望提供一种更易于教学和计算的类比途径。

Method: 推导了一般双曲面轴锥透镜的光线追踪方程，并将其应用于锥形和指数型剖面。通过改变轴向对称性条件，模拟了环形像、偏心入射产生的部分弧以及四像（类爱因斯坦十字）等透镜形态。

Result: 轴向对称配置产生环形像，未对准入射产生部分弧，破坏轴向对称则产生类爱因斯坦十字（Einstein cross）的四像图。模型使用恒定折射率光学元件，光线重分布完全由折射表面几何决定。

Conclusion: 该模型并非相对论透镜方程的物理替代，而是作为计算和教学工具，用于探索表面几何与对称性如何控制光线偏折、重分布和像的多重性。

Abstract: Gravitational lensing is often introduced through the bending of light by mass, but its geometrical nature can be difficult to visualize without invoking the full machinery of general relativity. We present a geometrical-optics analogy in which selected lensing-like image morphologies are generated by ray tracing through axicon-type lenses. In contrast with analogies based on a spatially varying refractive index, the present model uses optical elements with a constant refractive index; the redistribution of rays is produced by the geometry of the refracting surfaces. After deriving the ray-tracing equations for a general two-surface axicon, we apply the model to conical and exponential profiles. Axially symmetric configurations generate ring-like images, misaligned incidence produces partial arcs, and broken axial symmetry leads to four-image patterns reminiscent of an Einstein cross. The model is not intended as a physical substitute for a relativistic lens equation, but as a computational and pedagogical tool for exploring how surface geometry and symmetry control ray deflection, redistribution, and image multiplicity.

</details>


### [101] [How does nonmetricity shape quantum emission from rotating bumblebee black holes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01398)
*A. A. Araújo Filho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文比较了旋转蜂鸟黑洞（rotating bumblebee black holes）在度规解（metric solution）与度规仿射解（metric-affine solution）中非度规性（nonmetricity）对粒子产生和蒸发过程的影响，发现度规仿射几何中不存在全局霍金温度，且其辐射更低、蒸发更慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非度规性如何影响旋转黑洞的热力学与量子辐射性质，并区分度规与度规仿射两种几何框架下的物理差异。

Method: 在空间无穷远处归一化静止类Killing矢量（Killing vectors），定义物理频率、角速度和温度；通过度规与度规仿射解的对比分析，推导隧穿因子（tunneling factors）、量子占据数、径向势与轴对向灰体因子（greybody factor）的下界，并在极慢转展开与稳态极限下比较辐射性质。

Result: 度规解中，标定参数ℓ=3X/4移动视界、稳态极限面与极值边界，提高归一化视界角速度并抑制霍金温度（Hawking temperature），但表面引力保持均匀且标量波动方程可分离。度规仿射几何中，ℓ不改变Kerr坐标下的视界位置，但改变物理视界面积、角速度与子午扇区，g_θ分量耦合角向通道，且局域表面引力在aX≠0时依赖于纬度，导致不存在单一全局霍金温度。稳态极限下两者亮度均更低、寿命更长，度规仿射黑洞辐射更少、蒸发更慢。

Conclusion: 非度规性显著改变旋转蜂鸟黑洞的量子辐射与蒸发行为，尤其是度规仿射几何打破了全局热力学描述；弱场约束将静态量子发射观测量的小数修正限制在1.3×10⁻¹¹以下，且无法在全局尺度上建立旋转蒸发层次。

Abstract: We investigate how nonmetricity affects particle creation and evaporation in rotating bumblebee black holes by comparing metric and metric-affine solutions. We normalize the stationary Killing vectors at spatial infinity before defining physical frequencies, angular velocities, and temperatures. This procedure changes the static comparison and gives the leading calibration $\ell=3X/4$. In the metric solution, $\ell$ shifts the horizons, stationary limit surfaces, and extremal boundary, increases the normalized horizon angular velocity, and suppresses the Hawking temperature. Nevertheless, the surface gravity remains uniform and the scalar wave equation separable. We derive the tunneling factors, quantum occupation numbers, radial potential, and an analytical lower bound for the axisymmetric greybody factor. In the metric-affine geometry, nonmetricity preserves the Kerr coordinate locations of the horizons and stationary limit surfaces, but changes the physical horizon area, normalized angular velocity, and meridional sector. The component $g_{rθ}$ couples angular channels, whereas the local surface gravity depends on latitude when $aX\neq0$. Consequently, the generic rotating configuration admits neither a single global Hawking temperature nor an independent channel-by-channel emission spectrum. In the static limit, under the same Stefan-Boltzmann prescription, both Lorentz-violating geometries have lower luminosities and longer lifetimes, with the metric-affine black hole radiating less and evaporating more slowly. A local slow-rotation expansion preserves this tendency but does not establish a global rotating evaporation hierarchy. Existing weak-field constraints limit fractional corrections to static quantum-emission observables to below $1.3\times10^{-11}$.

</details>


### [102] [Fully global numerical evolutions of the linearised spin-2 equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01541)
*Merlyn Barrer,Jörg Frauendiener,Jörg Hennig,Oliver Markwell,Chris Stevens,Jed Thompson-Fawcett*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究共形紧化闵可夫斯基时空（conformally compactified Minkowski spacetime）上的线性化自旋-2场（linearised spin-2 field），给出在类光无穷远（null infinity）处避免对数奇点、保证光滑剥离（smooth peeling）行为的初始数据条件，并通过数值演化验证系统从过去无穷远稳定传播至未来无穷远。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力辐射的全局模拟需要处理时空无穷远的结构，而共形紧化框架下初始数据若不恰当会导致对数奇点（logarithmic singularities）出现，破坏光滑剥离行为。作者旨在明确在无穷远上自由指定的入射引力辐射ψ₀应满足何种条件，从而为完全全局的引力波数值模拟奠定基础。

Method: 在共形紧化闵可夫斯基时空上研究线性化自旋-2场，以过去类光无穷远mathscr{I}^-上的入射辐射ψ₀为自由数据。推导出保证规则初始数据的显式积分条件（对紧支撑数据给出充要条件）；对最简情形构造精确初始数据解，对一般情形给出计算剩余ψₖ的数值程序。采用两种规范进行数值演化，分别对应空间无穷远邻域紧化和整个闵可夫斯基时空紧化。

Result: 获得了避免了对数奇点、保持光滑剥离的入射引力辐射ψ₀的显式条件；对紧支撑数据得到了系统整体规则性与紧支撑性的充要积分条件。数值演化表明两种规范下系统均能从mathscr{I}^-稳定传播到mathscr{I}^+，验证了初始数据条件的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为完全全局的引力辐射模拟框架迈出了一步，阐明了mathscr{I}^-上初始数据在决定渐近结构（asymptotic structure）中的作用。

Abstract: We study the linearised spin-2 field on conformally compactified Minkowski spacetime. Initial data are prescribed on past null infinity $\mathscr{I}^-$, and the system is evolved through the cylinder at spatial infinity to future null infinity $\mathscr{I}^+$.
  To avoid the development of logarithmic singularities and thereby preserve smooth peeling behaviour, we derive explicit conditions on the freely specifiable ingoing gravitational radiation $ψ_0$ at $\mathscr{I}^-$ that ensure regular initial data. For compactly supported data, we obtain necessary and sufficient integral conditions guaranteeing regularity and compact support of the full system. In the simplest cases, we construct examples of exact initial data, while for the general case, we present a numerical procedure that computes the remaining $ψ_k$ to obtain a full initial data set.
  We perform numerical evolutions using two gauges corresponding to compactification of either a neighbourhood of spatial infinity or the entire Minkowski spacetime, and demonstrate stable propagation of the system from $\mathscr{I}^-$ to $\mathscr{I}^+$.
  These results provide a step towards a framework for fully global simulations of gravitational radiation and clarify the role of initial data on $\mathscr{I}^-$ in determining asymptotic structure.

</details>


### [103] [Effects of the inflaton mass on the pre-inflationary dynamics and primordial power spectra in loop quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01581)
*Kui Xiao,Abolhassan Mohammadi,Hongwei Tan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在圈量子宇宙学（Loop Quantum Cosmology, LQC）框架下，以标量场质量为自由现象学参数，研究了不同势能下的暴胀。结果表明较小质量更容易获得足够的e-folds数（二次势），而Starobinsky势则相反；慢滚暴胀概率在所有情形下均接近1。三种方法（dressed metric、hybrid、alternative mass function）计算的功率谱呈现相同的定性特征，但收敛速度不同；标量谱指数和张标比与当前观测数据吻合，且所有方法在高多极矩下与Planck 2018数据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在圈量子宇宙学中，暴胀动力学与势能形式和质量参数密切相关，需要系统研究质量作为自由参数时对不同势能暴胀的影响，并检验其理论预言与观测数据的一致性。

Method: 将质量视为自由现象学参数，考虑二次势和Starobinsky势驱动LQC中的正则标量场暴胀；通过估计慢滚暴胀概率分析足够e-folds数的获取条件；采用dressed metric、hybrid和alternative mass function三种方法计算原初功率谱；将标量功率谱输入CAMB代码生成角功率谱并与Planck 2018数据及最佳拟合ΛCDM模型比较。

Result: 对二次势而言较小质量更容易获得足够e-folds数，而Starobinsky势则相反；慢滚暴胀概率接近1。三种方法给出的功率谱定性特征相同：小k处压制、中间范围振幅放大且振荡、大k处近似标度不变，其中hybrid方法收敛最快。pivot尺度k⋆对暴胀子质量高度敏感，质量变化仅几个百分点即可使k⋆改变一个数量级以上。所计算的n_s和r与当前数据吻合；在高多极矩下三种方法均与Planck 2018数据一致，低多极矩下hybrid方法最接近，dressed metric方法偏差最大。

Conclusion: LQC框架下不同势能与质量参数组合均能产生与观测一致的暴胀信号，且三种功率谱计算方法在定性层面给出相同结论，但在收敛速度和低多极矩行为上存在差异，其中hybrid方法表现最优。

Abstract: Considering the mass parameters as free phenomenological parameters, we investigate inflation driven by a canonical scalar field with different potentials in LQC. We find that a smaller mass value makes it easier to obtain a sufficient number of e-folds for the quadratic potential, while for the Starobinsky potential, more e-folds are obtained for larger mass values. By estimating the probability of slow-roll inflation, we find that it remains very close to unity for all considered cases. We compute the primordial power spectrum using three approaches: the dressed metric, the hybrid, and the alternative mass function approaches for the case that the kinetic energy dominated at bounce. The resulting power spectrum for both potentials and different mass values shows the same qualitative pattern in all three approaches. It is suppressed at small $k$, amplified and oscillating over an intermediate range, and nearly scale-invariant at large $k$. The approaches mainly differ in how fast the power spectrum converges to this regime, with the hybrid approach converging the fastest, followed by the alternative mass function and dressed metric approaches. We find that the pivot scale $k_\star$ is highly sensitive to the inflaton mass, changing by more than an order of magnitude for a mass variation of only a few percent. Using the tensor power spectrum at $k_\star$, we calculate the scalar spectral index $n_s$ and tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$, finding good agreement with current data. Finally, the scalar power spectra are fed into the CAMB code to obtain the angular power spectrum and compare with the Planck 2018 data and the best-fit $Λ$CDM model. All three approaches are consistent with data at high multipoles, while at low multipoles the hybrid approach gives the closest agreement and the dressed metric approach shows the largest deviation.

</details>


### [104] [Cosmological dynamics based on Lovelock's gravity. Qualitative analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01702)
*Aleksei V. Nikolaev,Victor M. Zhuravlev,Sergei V. Chervon*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对平坦FRW宇宙中Lovelock引力的宇宙学动力学进行了完整的定性分析，将该动力学约化为Hubble参数H的一阶自洽方程，并给出了不动点、奇点的完整分类，及其所决定的渐近行为。文中识别出吸引/排斥不动点、"phantom区间"和有限时间奇点，并发现了$N>4$时在负耦合条件下的新场景——"大冲击"（Big Shock），取代了标准大爆炸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lovelock引力作为广义相对论的高维推广，其宇宙学动力学行为尚未得到完整理解。作者旨在从定性的角度对该理论在平坦FRW宇宙中的演化场景进行彻底分类，以揭示不同维度和耦合参数下的可能演化路径。

Method: 从以Riemann张量独立分量写出的广义N维Friedmann方程出发，将动力学约化为关于Hubble参数H的单一自洽一阶方程，其右端为$H^2$的多项式之比，多项式由Lovelock阶数n、维数N和耦合常数$\alpha_i$决定。通过分析这些多项式的实根来确定系统的不动点（fixed points）和奇点（singular points），并据此分类所有可能的演化场景。

Result: 完成了所有可能演化场景的完整分类，识别了吸引与排斥不动点，定位了有效物质密度为负的"phantom区间"，并证明了不动点在无限时间内达到而奇点在有限时间内达到。对$N>4$且合适负耦合的情形，发现了新的定性场景"Big Shock"：宇宙从有限密度和有限尺度因子但Hubble参数变化率无穷的状态开始，取代标准大爆炸。该分析分别以$n=1,N=4$（广义相对论）、$n=2,N=5$（Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力）和$n=3,N=7$（三次Lovelock引力）为例进行了具体说明。

Conclusion: Lovelock引力在平坦FRW宇宙中的动力学可由多项式根的代数结构完全刻画，其渐近行为被不动点与奇点所支配。高维且负耦合条件下出现的"Big Shock"场景代表了一种替代标准大爆炸的新宇宙起点，展示了Lovelock引力超越广义相对论的丰富定性结构。

Abstract: We perform a complete qualitative analysis of the cosmological dynamics of Lovelock gravity in a spatially flat Friedmann-Robertson-Walker (FRW) universe filled with a perfect fluid obeying a barotropic equation of state $p=ωρ$. Starting from the generalized $N$-dimensional Friedmann equations written in terms of the independent components of the Riemann tensor, we reduce the dynamics to a single autonomous first-order equation for the Hubble parameter $H$, whose right-hand side is a ratio of polynomials in $H^2$ fixed by the order $n$ of the Lovelock polynomial, the number of dimensions $N$, and the coupling constants $α_i$. The real roots of these polynomials determine the fixed points and the singular points of the system, which govern its asymptotic behaviour. We give a complete classification of the possible evolution scenarios, identify the attractive and repulsive fixed points, locate the "phantom intervals" in which the effective matter density becomes negative, and show that fixed points are reached in infinite time whereas singular points are reached in finite time. For $N>4$ and suitable negative couplings a qualitatively new scenario arises, which we call the "Big Shock": the universe starts from a state with finite density and finite scale factor but with an infinite rate of change of the Hubble parameter, replacing the standard Big Bang. The general analysis is illustrated for $n=1$, $N=4$ (general relativity), $n=2$, $N=5$ (Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity), and $n=3$, $N=7$ (cubic Lovelock gravity).

</details>


### [105] [High-Post-Newtonian-Order Dynamics Induced by Tail-of-Tail Interactions: The Non-Geodesic Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01774)
*G. Pratten*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了偏心率（eccentricity）非自旋致密双星系统在相对1PN阶、展开至O(e_t^12)的tail-of-tail效应对守恒动力学的贡献，通过Delaunay平均哈密顿量匹配有效单体（effective-one-body, EOB）描述，得到了非测地线Q势能的修正，并独立利用Fourier–Bessel分解验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋（spin）非自旋致密双星系统中的tail-of-tail效应是高阶自旋轨道耦合（self-force）计算中的重要贡献，此前对偏心轨道二阶自旋力的结果尚不完整，需要系统计算其守恒动力学贡献。

Method: 采用1PN准开普勒（quasi-Keplerian）动力学和调和坐标（harmonic coordinates），推导5.5PN与6.5PN阶的Delaunay平均哈密顿量，并将其匹配到有效单体描述，提取EOB Q势能至O(p_r^12)及对称质量比O(ν^2)；同时利用Fourier–Bessel分解独立重导平均哈密顿量以交叉验证。

Result: 质量比线性项再现了已有的一阶自旋力（first-order self-force）结果，二次项则提供了由tail-of-tail项产生的新的偏心二阶自旋力（second-order self-force）预测；通过第一定律（first law）在固定轨道频率下恢复了O(e^12)的一阶自旋力红移（redshift），并在5.5PN和6.5PN阶得到了完整的tail-of-tail对二阶逆红移的O(e^10)贡献。

Conclusion: 本文为偏心非自旋致密双星系统提供了tail-of-tail效应在守恒动力学中的系统高阶计算，其结果既验证了已有的一阶自旋力结论，又给出了全新的偏心二阶自旋力预测，对引力波模板（waveform templates）建模具有参考价值。

Abstract: We compute the tail-of-tail contribution to the conservative dynamics of eccentric, non-spinning compact binaries to relative 1PN order and to $\mathcal O(e_t^{12})$. Using the $1$PN quasi-Keplerian dynamics in harmonic coordinates, we derive the Delaunay-averaged Hamiltonian at $5.5$PN and $6.5$PN order and match it to the effective-one-body description, allowing us to determine the corresponding contributions to the non-geodesic EOB $Q$ potential through the $\mathcal{O}(p_r^{12})$, including the dependence on the symmetric mass ratio up to $\mathcal{O}(ν^2)$. The terms linear in the mass ratio reproduce the available first-order self-force results, while the quadratic terms provide qualitatively new eccentric second-order self-force predictions arising from the tail-of-tail terms. We independently rederive the averaged Hamiltonian using a Fourier--Bessel decomposition of the hereditary interaction. Applying the first law at fixed orbital frequencies, we recover the known first-order self-force redshift through $\mathcal O(e^{12})$ and obtain the complete tail-of-tail contribution to the second-order inverse redshift at $5.5$PN and $6.5$PN through $\mathcal O(e^{10})$.

</details>


### [106] [A Quantum Dominant Energy Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01814)
*Stefan Hollands,Roberto Longo,Gerardo Morsella*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在弯曲时空量子场论（quantum field theory in curved spacetimes）的框架下提出了量子主导能量条件（quantum dominant energy condition, QDEC），对Rindler楔（Rindler wedge）情形给出了严格证明，并在Minkowski时空中将其与态恢复界（state recovery bound）建立联系，还以自由标量场的相干态（coherent state）为例进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的主导能量条件（dominant energy condition, DEC）是经典物理学中对应力张量的约束，但在量子场论中，应力张量是量子算符，经典不等式不再直接适用。需要一个量子版本的DEC来取代其作用，从而在弯曲时空量子场论中刻画物理上可接受的量子态。

Method: 针对Rindler楔情形，作者对QDEC给出了严格的数学证明；对于更一般的几何结构（包括弯曲时空），提供了启发式的讨论。在Minkowski时空中，作者建立了QDEC与态恢复界之间的联系，并通过自由标量场的相干态对QDEC进行了阐释——此时QDEC与定义该相干态的经典解的应力张量所满足的普通DEC相关联。

Result: 作者为Rindler楔情形下的QDEC提供了严格的证明，并在Minkowski时空中证明了QDEC与态恢复界之间的关联。对于自由标量场的相干态，作者展示了QDEC会退化为对应经典解应力张量的普通DEC。

Conclusion: QDEC为弯曲时空中量子态定义了有意义的能量条件约束，在Rindler楔等特定几何下可被严格验证，且在相干态情形下与经典DEC保持一致，这为将其推广到更一般的时空几何提供了基础。

Abstract: We propose a quantum dominant energy condition (QDEC) for the stress tensor in the context of quantum field theory in curved spacetimes. A rigorous proof is given for the case of Rindler wedges, and a heuristic discussion about possible generalizations to more general geometric setups, including curved spacetime, is provided. In Minkowski spacetime, we establish a connection with state recovery bounds. We illustrate the QDEC for coherent states of the free scalar field, where it turns out to be related to the ordinary DEC for the stress tensor of the classical solution that defines the coherent state.

</details>


### [107] [The first law of black hole mechanics in conformal Einstein-Power-Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01815)
*Xiaokai He,Xiaoning Wu,Naqing Xie*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Iyer-Wald形式（Iyer-Wald formalism）框架下，在共形爱因斯坦-幂杨-米尔斯（conformal Einstein-power-Yang-Mills, CEPYM）理论中研究黑洞力学第一定律，并通过比较两个无穷小相邻的稳态黑洞解，获得了CEPYM理论中黑洞热力学第一定律的显式解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞热力学第一定律是黑洞物理学的核心议题之一，但该定律在不同引力理论中的具体形式尚需逐一推导。本文旨在将该定律推广至CEPYM理论这一含物质场的高阶引力框架下。

Method: 基于Iyer-Wald形式体系，对两个无穷小接近的稳态黑洞解进行比较，从而导出黑洞热力学第一定律的显式解析表达式。

Result: 获得了CEPYM理论中黑洞热力学第一定律的具体解析形式。

Conclusion: 该工作将Iyer-Wald形式应用于CEPYM理论，成功推导出该理论下黑洞热力学第一定律的显式表达式，拓展了黑洞热力学的研究范围。

Abstract: In the framework of the Iyer-Wald formalism, the first law of black hole mechanics is examined within the context of conformal Einstein-power-Yang-Mills (CEPYM) theory. By comparing two inffnitesimally neighbouring stationary black hole solutions, we obtain the explicit analytical expression for the first law of black hole thermodynamics in CEPYM theory.

</details>


### [108] [Analytically Approximate Black Hole Solution to Higher Curvature Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01869)
*Seyed Naseh Sajadi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用黑洞热力学（black hole thermodynamics）与连分数展开（continued fraction expansion）方法，在高阶曲率引力（higher-curvature gravity）中获得解析带电黑洞解，并通过热力学第一定律和Smarr公式研究静态黑洞的热力学性质，其结果与爱因斯坦引力中直接求解场方程的结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶曲率引力通常导致极其复杂的场方程，使得寻找解析解变得非常困难。本文旨在绕过直接求解场方程的繁琐过程，利用热力学方法构造解析的带电黑洞解。

Method: 作者采用黑洞热力学与连分数展开方法来获得高阶曲率引力中的解析带电黑洞解；随后利用热力学第一定律（first law of thermodynamics）和Smarr公式研究静态黑洞的热力学性质。

Result: 研究结果表明，通过热力学方法得到的解析解与在爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）中直接求解场方程所得结果一致。

Conclusion: 热力学方法结合连分数展开可以有效构造高阶曲率引力中的解析黑洞解，并验证了该方法的正确性。

Abstract: Higher-curvature gravity usually leads to very complicated field equations, which makes it hard to find analytical solutions. In this work, we obtain analytical charged black hole solutions in higher-curvature gravity by using black hole thermodynamics and the continued fraction expansion. We study the thermodynamics of static black holes using the first law of thermodynamics and the Smarr formula. We show that our results agree with those obtained by directly solving the field equations in Einstein gravity.

</details>


### [109] [A Time-Dependent Canonical Transformation between Bateman and Doubled Caldirola--Kanai Systems for a Homogeneous Massive Scalar Field on a Prescribed FLRW Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01894)
*Narakorn Kaewkhao,Chaiyaphat Phantusen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将经典力学中Bateman与Caldirola–Kanai（CK）两种耗散形式主义的对应关系推广到均匀标量场在平坦FLRW背景上的情形，构建了倍增作用量、Bateman拉格朗日量与加倍CK哈密顿量，并通过显式含时正则变换建立两者联系，且在幂律背景p=2/3时发现守恒的Bateman标量场哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散方程在Bateman和CK形式主义中具有不同的变分描述，作者希望将经典情形下两种形式主义之间的对应关系推广到宇宙学背景中的标量场，以统一描述膨胀导致的耗散效应。

Method: 采用倍增作用量（multiplier action）推导Klein–Gordon方程及含$3\dot{H}(t)\chi$的反阻尼方程；构造一阶Bateman拉格朗日量；对自由有质量场导出Bateman与加倍CK拉格朗日量及哈密顿量；通过由Bateman动量线性函数生成的显式含时正则变换（canonical transformation）建立两个体系的映射。

Result: 倍增作用量给出Klein–Gordon方程与含$-3\dot{H}(t)\chi$的反阻尼方程；因子$a^3(t)$和$a^{-3}(t)$分别生成阻尼与反阻尼CK扇区；含时正则变换将加倍CK系统映射到Bateman系统，其中$\dot{H}(t)$项对哈密顿量等价性不可或缺；在旋转变量下Bateman标量场哈密顿量取$H_{B,\mathrm{SF}} = E_u - E_v$形式，在常数H下守恒；对幂律背景$a(t)\propto t^p$，$p=2/3$的关联族即使在时变H下仍保持$H_{B,\mathrm{SF}}$守恒。

Conclusion: Bateman与CK形式主义的对应关系可成功推广到均匀标量场在FLRW背景上的经典描述中，且存在特定幂律背景（p=2/3）下哈密顿量守恒的非平凡结果；研究范围限于经典均匀场，不涉及引力相空间的量子化。

Abstract: Dissipative equations admit distinct variational descriptions in the Bateman and Caldirola--Kanai (CK) formalisms. The classical correspondence between them is extended to a homogeneous canonical scalar field on a prescribed spatially flat Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker (FLRW) background, where the expansion produces the time-dependent damping coefficient $3H(t)$. A multiplier action yields the Klein--Gordon equation and a complementary anti-damped equation containing the term $-3\dot{H}(t)χ$. A first-order Bateman Lagrangian derived from the same multiplier action reproduces this physical--auxiliary pair for a general potential. Specializing to a free massive field gives the Bateman and doubled CK Lagrangians and Hamiltonians used in the canonical comparison. The factors $a^{3}(t)$ and $a^{-3}(t)$ generate the damped and anti-damped CK sectors, respectively. An explicit time-dependent canonical transformation, generated by a function linear in the Bateman momenta, maps the complete doubled CK system to the Bateman system. For this point transformation, the terms proportional to $\dot{H}(t)$ are required for Hamiltonian equivalence. In rotated variables, the Bateman scalar-field Hamiltonian takes the difference form $H_{B,\mathrm{SF}} = E_{u} - E_{v}$. It is conserved for constant $H$ and generally varies with time otherwise. For the power-law background $a(t) \propto t^{p}$, however, a correlated family at $p = 2/3$ has conserved $H_{B,\mathrm{SF}}$ despite the time dependence of $H(t)$. These results concern classical homogeneous fields on a prescribed FLRW background and exclude the gravitational phase space.

</details>


### [110] [Superradiant Bose--Einstein condensates around Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02051)
*Sen Guo,Lin Wen,Xiao-Xiong Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在克尔（Kerr）时空中同时考虑四次自相互作用，通过将非线性项投影到单一模式并采用带边界的牛顿法在固定粒子数下积分Gross–Pitaevskii方程，系统研究了超轻玻色子暗物质在旋转黑洞周围形成的凝聚体的性质。研究发现自相互作用被旋转几何地调制（耦合系数含有Δ(r)因子并在视界处消失），整个解族仅依赖于单一无量纲参数N=λN；凝聚体呈环面（torus）而非球壳状，且仅在N≳10³时被显著修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究要么在静态背景下保留自相互作用，要么在克尔度规中忽略它，从未将两者同时处理。为完整理解旋转黑洞周围超辐射形成的超轻玻色子云的玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose–Einstein condensate）行为，有必要在克尔时空中同时纳入自相互作用。

Method: 从带四次势的Klein–Gordon方程出发，将线性问题分离为球谐（spheroidal）方程和径向（radial）方程并自洽求解；利用守恒的Noether流计算超辐射增长率；将非线性项投影到单一模式；采用带边界的牛顿方法（bordered Newton method）在固定粒子数下积分Gross–Pitaevskii方程。

Result: 恢复引力原子的类氢谱和增长率的α^(4ℓ+5)标度律；获得精确关系J_z=ħmN，且该关系不受自相互作用修正；发现凝聚体呈环面而非球壳状，密度在旋转轴上恒等于零；云仅在N≳10³时被显著改变。

Conclusion: 旋转通过几何方式调制自相互作用——有效耦合包含因子Δ(r)，在视界处自动关闭；场经重新标度后整个解族仅由单一无量纲参数N=λN决定。凝聚体形状为环面，自相互作用对系统的影响在参数N较大时才变得显著。

Abstract: Ultralight bosonic dark matter can accumulate around a rotating black hole, where superradiance amplifies the field until a macroscopic cloud forms. Whether such a cloud behaves as a Bose--Einstein condensate depends on the self-interaction, which earlier work has either retained on static backgrounds or dropped on the Kerr metric. Here we treat the two together. Starting from the Klein--Gordon equation with a quartic potential, we separate the linear problem into spheroidal and radial equations and solve them self-consistently, obtain the superradiant growth rate from the conserved Noether current, project the nonlinear term onto a single mode, and integrate the resulting Gross--Pitaevskii equation with a bordered Newton method at fixed particle number. Rotation modulates the self-interaction geometrically: the effective coupling carries a factor $Δ(r)$ and therefore switches off at the horizon. Once the field is rescaled, the whole solution family depends on the single dimensionless parameter $\mathcal{N}=λN$. We recover the hydrogenic spectrum of the gravitational atom and the $α^{4\ell+5}$ scaling of the growth rate, and we obtain the exact relation $J_{z}=\hbar mN$, which receives no correction from the self-interaction. We find that the condensate is a torus rather than a spherical shell, with its density vanishing identically on the rotation axis, and that the cloud is modified appreciably only for $\mathcal{N}\gtrsim10^{3}$.

</details>


### [111] [Charged Black Holes with a Lorentz--Violating Kalb--Ramond Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02196)
*Jia-Hui Yang,Xin-Yu Guo,Jia-Zhou Liu,Yu-Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在由背景Kalb–Ramond场诱导的自发洛伦兹对称性破缺引力理论中，电动力学精确静态球对称带电黑洞解及其热力学性质。作者首次同时考虑两个独立的非最小曲率耦合效应，获得了有无宇宙学常数的带电黑洞解，并利用Iyer–Wald协变相空间形式推导修正的热力学量，分析了Joule–Thomson膨胀、拓扑相变特征等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对含Kalb–Ramond背景场的引力理论中黑洞解的分析仅保留了单一非最小曲率耦合项，未能全面考察两个独立非最小耦合的联合效应，因此对带电黑洞及其热力学的理解存在缺失。

Method: 在含自发洛伦兹对称性破缺（spontaneous Lorentz-symmetry breaking）的引力理论中求解静态球对称带电黑洞解，同时保留两个独立的非最小曲率耦合；利用Iyer–Wald协变相空间形式（Iyer–Wald covariant phase-space formalism）推导修正的热力学量；并采用黑洞热力学的拓扑方法（topological approach）分析van der Waals-like相变。

Result: 获得了有无宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）两种情况下的精确带电黑洞解；导出修正后的热力学量并完成Joule–Thomson膨胀分析，包括反转曲线（inversion curve）与冷却/加热区域；通过拓扑缺陷曲线（topological defect curve）刻画了van der Waals型相变，并展示了临界温度与临界压力如何编码于其中。

Conclusion: 这些结果阐明了在含洛伦兹破坏（Lorentz-violating）Kalb–Ramond背景引力理论中，带电黑洞的热力学与拓扑特征，深化了对该理论与广义相对论框架下黑洞热力学差异的理解。

Abstract: We investigate exact static, spherically symmetric electrically charged black hole solutions in a gravitational theory with spontaneous Lorentz-symmetry breaking induced by a background Kalb--Ramond field. In contrast to previous analyses that retained only one nonminimal curvature coupling, we include the combined effects of the two independent nonminimal curvature couplings and obtain charged black hole solutions both with and without a cosmological constant. Using the Iyer--Wald covariant phase-space formalism, we derive the corrected thermodynamic quantities and analyze the Joule--Thomson expansion, including the inversion curve and the cooling/heating regions. We further apply the topological approach to black hole thermodynamics to characterize the van der Waals-like phase transition and show how the thermodynamic critical temperature and pressure are encoded in the corresponding topological defect curve. These results clarify the thermodynamic and topological signatures of electrically charged black holes in gravity with a Lorentz-violating Kalb--Ramond background.

</details>


### [112] [Hidden Symmetry of Kerr-deSitter from Manifest Symmetry of Painlevé VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02259)
*Stefan Hollands,Akihiro Ishibashi,Jochen Zahn*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过经典等单消变形（isomonodromic deformations）理论，将Kerr-de Sitter黑洞径向Teukolsky方程中发现的“质量对称性”（mass symmetries）与Painlevé VI方程的对称性联系起来，并证明由该对称性导出的对偶（dual）洛伦兹时空同样代表一个黑洞。利用这一几何见解以纯几何的方式重新获得了先前已知的部分模式稳定性（partial mode stability）结果，并指出了对偶时空的进一步特殊几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近在Kerr-de Sitter黑洞的径向Teukolsky方程中发现了“质量对称性”，但其深层来源尚未阐明。作者希望借助经典等单消变形理论与Painlevé VI方程之间的联系来解释这一对称性的起源，并探索对偶时空的物理与几何含义。

Method: 利用经典等单消变形理论，将径向Teukolsky方程的质量对称性与Painlevé VI方程的对称性建立对应关系。已知质量对称性的一个子集可将Teukolsky方程变换为对偶洛伦兹时空上的波动方程，作者据此分析该对偶时空的结构并推导其几何性质。

Result: 发现对偶时空同样代表一个黑洞；以此几何视角重新获得了先前已知的部分模式稳定性结果；并指出了对偶时空的若干特殊几何特征。

Conclusion: 质量对称性的本质源于Painlevé VI方程所隐含的等单消变形对称结构；对偶时空作为黑洞的几何解释为模式稳定性问题提供了新的视角，并揭示了更丰富的几何结构。

Abstract: We show that the recently discovered ``mass symmetries" of the radial Teukolsky equation for Kerr-deSitter black holes can be understood from a corresponding symmetry of the Painlevé VI equation via the classical theory of ``isomonodromic deformations". It is known that a subset of the mass symmetries transforms the Teukolsky equation to a wave equation on a ``dual" Lorentzian spacetime. We find that this dual spacetime again represents a black hole, and use this insight to re-obtain a previously known partial mode stability result in a geometric manner. Further special geometric features of the dual spacetime are pointed out.

</details>


### [113] [A parity selection rule for regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02481)
*Farid Thaalba,Julio Arrechea,Stefano Liberati*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了球对称引力理论中规则黑洞（regular black hole）动态一致性的必要局域条件：理论的二阶场方程定义函数在符号径向坐标反转下须具有相反宇称，并满足中心正则性和非退化条件。该准则在可积扇区等价于广义Misner–Sharp–Hernandez质量跨中心为奇函数且三次消失，对Hayward和Dymnikova几何成立，而Bardeen理论不满足，说明静态解的曲率正则性不足以保证动态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则黑洞度量通常仅被运动学地研究，但有限曲率的静态核心并不能保证底层理论能一致地演化通过规则中心的普通物质（generic matter）。作者旨在建立超越静态解的动态一致性必要条件，考察“曲率正则的静态解是否真的对应动态可行的引力理论”这一根本问题。

Method: 在最一般的基于作用量（action-based）、恒等守恒（identically conserved）、二阶（second-order）球对称引力场方程类中，推导规则性的必要局域一致性条件。通过要求两个定义理论函数的符号径向坐标反转宇称相反，并附加中心正则性与非退化条件，将准则等价转化为可积扇区中广义Misner–Sharp–Hernandez质量的奇性与三次零点要求，以及静态单参数真空族中的半径-质量同时反转协变性。

Result: Hayward和Dymnikova几何对应的理论满足该选择准则（selection rule），而Bardeen理论不满足——尽管其静态解曲率正则，仍与普通物质的动态演化不一致。文中还刻画了包含带de Sitter核心的Hayward型黑洞的无穷类可允许理论。

Conclusion: 该选择准则为描述规则坍缩的理论提供了必要条件，但本身不能确立适定性（well-posedness）或保证非奇异的最终结局。静态解的曲率正则性是不充分的，需结合动态一致性条件筛选候选引力理论。

Abstract: Regular black hole metrics are usually studied kinematically, but a finite-curvature static core does not guarantee that the underlying theory can consistently evolve generic matter through a regular center. We derive a necessary local consistency condition within the most general class of action-based, identically conserved, second-order gravitational field equations in spherical symmetry. Regularity requires that the two functions defining the theory have opposite parities under reversal of the signed radial coordinate, together with additional center-regularity and nondegeneracy conditions. In the integrable sector, this criterion is equivalent to requiring the generalized Misner--Sharp--Hernandez mass to be odd across the center, to vanish cubically there, and to contain no point-mass contribution. For theories reconstructed from static one-parameter vacuum families, the condition becomes covariance under simultaneous reversal of radius and mass. The theories associated with the Hayward and Dymnikova geometries satisfy this selection rule. In contrast, the Bardeen theory does not, demonstrating that curvature regularity of a static solution is insufficient for dynamical consistency with generic matter. We also characterize an infinite class of admissible theories containing Hayward-like black holes with de Sitter cores. The selection rule provides a necessary condition for theories intended to describe regular collapse, but does not by itself establish well-posedness or guarantee a nonsingular endpoint.

</details>

### [114] [Signatures of Lorentz violation in bright ring for Sgr A* images by radiation ineffective accretion flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01413)
*Cuiyu Zhao,Songbai Chen,Jiliang Jing*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了低能Hořava引力框架下旋转洛伦兹破缺（Lorentz violation, LV）黑洞周围辐射无效吸积流所产生的Sgr A*亮环特性，揭示LV参数对亮环直径、宽度、光度、方位不对称性和取向角的影响，并结合EHT观测数据约束了LV参数和黑洞自旋的允许范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低能Hořava引力框架中，洛伦兹破缺（Lorentz violation, LV）效应可能显著影响黑洞的时空结构及其周围辐射特征。作者希望检验LV参数对Sgr A*（人马座A*，银河系中心超大质量黑洞）亮环（bright ring）图像性质的具体影响，并利用事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope, EHT）观测数据对LV参数和黑洞自旋进行观测约束。

Method: 作者在旋转LV黑洞背景下，模拟了230 GHz热同步辐射（thermal synchrotron emission）来自辐射无效吸积流（radiation ineffective accretion flows）的图像，系统考察了LV参数、黑洞自旋参数、吸积盘厚度三个物理量对亮环直径、宽度、光度、方位不对称性及取向角的影响，并进一步分析了主像（primary image）与n=1光子环（photon ring）的峰值位置和宽度。

Result: 结果表明LV参数减小亮环直径但同时增加其宽度、光度、方位不对称性和取向角；更高的自旋参数会增强这一效应。吸积盘厚度增厚会减小环直径并增强LV参数对该直径的影响，而环宽度与盘厚度无系统依赖关系。基于EHT观测数据，在固定盘厚度下，LV参数的允许范围随自旋先增后减并向更小LV值偏移；同时LV参数会收窄自旋的允许范围，负LV值将自旋范围向更高值移动，正值则反之。主像和n=1光子环的峰值位置与宽度随LV参数减小（除极窄范围外），且纯开普勒（Keplerian）吸积流下不同LV参数的峰值位置差异更显著；纯径向自由落体（radially free-falling）流产生的主像和光子环比纯开普勒流产生的更宽。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了LV参数在Hořava引力框架下对Sgr A*亮环及光子环观测特征的影响，并通过EHT观测对LV参数与黑洞自旋给出了相互约束的关系。研究结果表明LV效应在黑洞成像观测中具有可探测的烙印，为利用EHT数据检验洛伦兹破缺理论提供了具体的观测判据。

Abstract: We have investigated effects of Lorentz violation (LV) on bright ring in Sgr A* images illuminated by the 230 GHz thermal synchrotron emission from radiation ineffective accretion flows around a rotating LV black hole within the low-energy Hořava gravity framework. Our results reveal that the LV parameter reduces the bright ring diameter yet increases its width, luminosity, azimuthal asymmetry and orientation angle. Higher spin parameter strengthens the LV-induced effects on bright ring properties.Increasing disk thickness reduces the ring diameter and enhances the LV parameter's effects on this diameter. The ring width shows no systematic dependence on the disk thickness. These quantities of bright ring display similar trends against black hole spin and the LV parameter for the rotating LV black hole. Using EHT observational data of Sgr A*, we find that, at fixed disk thickness, the allowed range of the LV parameter first broadens and then contracts with growing black hole spin, and shifts toward smaller LV parameter values. In addition, the LV parameter narrows the permitted range of black hole spin: negative LV parameter values shift this range to higher spin, while positive values shift it to lower spin. Finally, we probe effects of the LV parameter on the peak position value and the width of the primary image and the $n=1$ photon ring for the rotating LV black hole. The peak positions and their widths decrease with the LV parameter, except for a narrow range. The peak position differences for various LV parameter are more pronounced for pure Keplerian accretion flow. In additional, the primary image and the $n=1$ photon ring produced by pure radially free-falling flows are broader than their counterparts generated by pure Keplerian flows.

</details>

### [115] [Bessel-Like Multiple Orthogonal Polynomials of Mixed Type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00781)
*Manuel Mañas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文在单位圆上为 $q\times p$ 矩阵权重构造了一族Bessel型混合型多重正交多项式（mixed-type multiple orthogonal polynomials）。该矩阵权重在圆上除有限点集外具有一般秩 $\min\{q,p\}$，其倒数Gamma矩在 $q=1$ 时恢复多重Bessel系统，在 $p=1$ 时恢复Wolfs型Bessel系统。作者证明了平衡近对角指标的混合 $A$ 与 $B$ 多项式向量的正交性和弱正规性，并给出了分量级强正规性的刻画。对应的带状递推矩阵具有双对角Christoffel分解，$q=1$ 时每个Christoffel步骤保持于多重Bessel族内，Gamma-Vandermonde行列式给出完整分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重正交多项式（multiple orthogonal polynomials）在随机矩阵论、逼近论和正交多项式理论中具有重要地位。本文旨在构造单位圆上一类具有一般秩矩阵权重的Bessel型混合型多重正交多项式族，该族能将既有的一维Bessel系统和Wolfs Bessel系统统一纳入同一框架，并给出其正交性、正规性及递推结构等完整理论。

Method: 作者构造了单位圆上 $q\times p$ 矩阵权重的Bessel型混合型多重正交多项式。通过倒数Gamma矩（reciprocal-Gamma moments）联系多重Bessel系统，并利用缩放Markov-Stieltjes极限从秩一Jacobi型系统获得同一矩阵。对平衡近对角指标推导了混合 $A$ 和 $B$ 多项式向量的显式公式，利用终止广义超几何函数（terminating generalized hypergeometric functions）和Kampé de Fériet表示给出分量结构，并推导了带状递推矩阵的双对角Christoffel分解。

Result: 获得了近对角指标的混合 $A$ 和 $B$ 多项式向量显式公式，证明了正交性与弱正规性并刻画了分量级强正规性。$B$ 分量具有终止Kampé de Fériet表示和矩阵Rodrigues型公式。一列约化情形下双变量表示归结为受反射II型多重Hahn多项式支配的广义超几何多项式。有限Gamma-Pochhammer公式给出近对角和阶梯线递推系数，带状递推矩阵实现双对角Christoffel分解。$q=1$ 时每个Christoffel步骤保持于多重Bessel族内，Gamma-Vandermonde行列式给出完整分解。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一族统一的Bessel型混合型多重正交多项式体系，覆盖了多重Bessel系统（$q=1$）与Wolfs Bessel系统（$p=1$）作为特例，并完整刻画了其正交性、正规性、递推结构和Christoffel分解。该工作为矩阵权重多重正交多项式的理论与应用提供了新的统一框架。

Abstract: This article constructs a Bessel-like family of mixed-type multiple orthogonal polynomials for a $q\times p$ matrix weight on the unit circle. Unlike a rank-one product weight, this matrix has generic rank $\min\{q,p\}$ outside a finite subset of the circle. Its reciprocal-Gamma moments recover the multiple Bessel system when $q=1$ and the Bessel-like system of Wolfs when $p=1$. The same matrix is obtained as a scaled Markov-Stieltjes limit of a rank-one Jacobi-like system, although the interval measures themselves have no finite limit. For balanced near-diagonal indices, explicit formulas are obtained for the mixed $A$ and $B$ polynomial vectors. Their orthogonality and weak normality are proved, and componentwise strong normality is characterized. The components have terminating generalized hypergeometric representations; the $B$ components also admit finite Kampé de Fériet representations and a matrix Rodrigues-type formula. In the one-row reduction, the bivariate representation becomes a generalized hypergeometric polynomial governed by a reflected type-II multiple Hahn polynomial. Finite Gamma-Pochhammer formulas give the near-diagonal and step-line recurrence coefficients. The corresponding banded recurrence matrix has a bidiagonal Christoffel factorization: the lower factors are evaluated from transformed polynomial vectors, while the upper factors are expressed through finite tau determinants. When $q=1$, every Christoffel step remains within the multiple Bessel family, and Gamma-Vandermonde determinants yield the complete factorization.

</details>


### [116] [Positive Bidiagonal Factorizations for Banded Markov Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00788)
*Manuel Mañas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文利用有序正双对角分解（ordered positive bidiagonal factorization, PBF）建立了任意有限带宽马尔可夫转移矩阵的谱论与概率论框架。该分解产生两类混合型多重正交多项式（mixed-type multiple orthogonal polynomials）、一个逐项正的 q×p 测量矩阵，以及由死亡-停留和出生-停留基本转移构成的序列，据此无需可逆性或块对称化即可获得 Karlin–McGregor 公式、Green 核、预解式、势函数和首达变换。文中还刻画了有理随机 PBF 与有序有限罐试验的对应关系，并针对混合 Piñeiro 系统和类 Jacobi 系统给出了显式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将 Karlin–McGregor 谱理论与正交多项式方法推广到任意有限带宽的马尔可夫转移矩阵，摆脱传统方法对可逆性（reversibility）或块对称化（block symmetrizability）的依赖，从而覆盖更广泛的随机过程模型。

Method: 以有序正双对角分解（PBF）为核心工具，从分解因子中提取混合型多重正交多项式、测量矩阵和基本转移序列，进而构造 Karlin–McGregor 型公式；对有理随机 PBF 采用有序有限罐试验进行刻画；通过循环重排获得 Darboux 交织关系，利用因子分解的连分数表达给出首次返回律；对状态分组构造有限相位拟生灭过程（quasi-birth-and-death process）及矩阵连分数。

Result: 对于有界连续时间生成元，统一化（uniformization）保持谱数据不变；对无界速率情形，若移位前截断均可作标量 PBF，则生成元必为三对角形式，而 Q=V(T−I) 可保持任意固定带宽并将嵌入链与逗留速率分离。在 Piñeiro 系统中获得了精确的 PBF 区域和更大的结构正性区域；类 Jacobi beta 卷积权重可通过 Gamma 因子消去恢复 Piñeiro 情形。对 q∈{2,3,4}，严格序条件给出了唯一开放 PBF 区域及低维消去层。

Conclusion: 本文建立的 PBF 框架为有限带宽马尔可夫链提供了一套统一的谱—概率理论，在无可逆性假设下推广了经典 Karlin–McGregor 方法，并通过对 Piñeiro 与类 Jacobi 系统的显式分析展示了该理论的实际可操作性与丰富结构。

Abstract: An ordered positive bidiagonal factorization (PBF) is used to develop a spectral and probabilistic theory for Markov transition matrices of arbitrary finite bandwidth. The factors determine two families of mixed-type multiple orthogonal polynomials, an entrywise positive $q\times p$ matrix of measures, and a sequence of elementary death-or-stay and birth-or-stay transitions. This yields Karlin-McGregor formulas for transition probabilities, Green kernels, resolvents, potentials, and first-passage transforms without reversibility or block symmetrizability. Rational stochastic PBFs are characterized by ordered finite-urn experiments. Cyclic reorderings give Darboux intertwinings, while a factor-resolved continued fraction gives the first-return law. Grouping states produces a finite-phase quasi-birth-and-death process and a matrix continued fraction for return time and phase. For bounded continuous-time generators, uniformization preserves the spectral data. For unbounded rates, a conservative generator whose shifted leading truncations all admit scalar PBFs must be tridiagonal; nevertheless, $Q=V(T-I)$ preserves arbitrary fixed bandwidth and separates the embedded chain from the holding rates. The theory is explicit for mixed Piñeiro and Jacobi-like systems. For Piñeiro, the exact PBF region and larger structural-band positivity regions are obtained. Jacobi-like beta-convolution weights admit Gamma-factor cancellations, complete cancellation recovering Piñeiro. For $q\in\{2,3,4\}$, the strict ordering conditions give the only open PBF region, with further lower-dimensional cancellation strata. Rational $(3,2)$ examples provide all factors and the resulting Markov models.

</details>
