### [117] [The Lorentzian Geometry of Tunneling in Global de Sitter at Late Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00159)
*Thibaut Coudarchet,Ben Freivogel,Bjoern Hassfeld,Arthur Hebecker*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出Coleman–De Lucia（CDL）瞬子对de Sitter多重宇宙中隧穿跃迁的标准解释在引力主导区域（上隧穿和重畴壁跃迁）存在病态问题，并提出一种基于哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）描述的物理图像，说明小型非壳泡在大de Sitter空间球内生长并最终去壳（on-shell）的过程，通过WKB分析恢复了CDL隧穿速率，并阐明该过程与CDL瞬子对应同一去壳轨迹的两种不同解析延拓（analytic continuation）。该结果对Larfors–Johnson问题具有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CDL瞬子的最朴素解释是通过解析延拓到最小空间三维球面上的Lorentzian de Sitter时空，但实际上需要的物理场景是在旧母体de Sitter的巨大空间球内形成一个小型新真空泡。即使应用de Sitter等距变换，这一目标在一般情况下也无法实现——特别是在引力主导区域（上隧穿以及重畴壁跃迁）中失效，导致整个多重宇宙处于每个上隧穿事件的因果未来之中，构成病理性问题。

Method: 作者发展了一套哈密顿量描述，刻画一个小的非壳（off-shell）泡如何在晚期de Sitter的巨大空间球内生长并最终去壳（on-shell）的过程。研究提供了相应的WKB分析，并恢复了CDL隧穿速率。作者进一步论证，该隧穿过程与CDL瞬子过程在哈密顿量框架下对应于同一条去壳轨迹的两种不同解析延拓。

Result: 作者通过哈密顿量描述成功实现了小型泡在de Sitter大空间球内从非壳态生长到去壳态的物理图景，并通过WKB分析恢复了标准CDL隧穿速率。该结果表明，新的隧穿描述与CDL瞬子描述是同一去壳轨迹的两种解析延拓，为解决引力主导区域的标准解释失效问题提供了替代方案。

Conclusion: 该分析对Larfors–Johnson问题具有关键含义——该问题基于相关畴壁的不稳定性，质疑了标准机制对弦论通量景观（string-theoretic flux landscape）的填充。本文提出的修正隧穿描述为此提供了新的物理视角，可能有助于重新评估标准多重宇宙隧穿机制的适用性。

Abstract: It is widely believed that Coleman--De Luccia (CDL) instantons characterize tunneling transitions in a de Sitter multiverse. Their most naive interpretation uses analytic continuation to Lorentzian de Sitter at the minimal size of the spatial three-sphere. However, what one really wants is a geometry where a small bubble of new vacuum forms within the huge spatial sphere of an old parent de Sitter. Even by applying de Sitter isometries to the original CDL solutions, this cannot in general be achieved. In particular, it fails in the gravity-dominated regime, i.e. for up-tunneling and for transitions with heavy domain walls. These cases remain pathological in that the whole multiverse is in the causal future of every single up-tunneling event. To solve this problem, we develop a Hamiltonian description of how a small off-shell bubble grows and eventually goes on shell within the large spatial sphere of late-time de Sitter. We provide the corresponding WKB analysis, recovering the CDL rate. We explain that our tunneling process and that of CDL are described by two different analytic continuations of a unique on-shell trajectory in the Hamiltonian treatment. Our analysis has crucial implications for the Larfors--Johnson problem, which questions the standard mechanism for populating the string-theoretic flux landscape on the basis of an instability of the relevant domain walls.

</details>


### [118] [Dynamics of nucleation in thermal phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00169)
*Oliver Gould,Joonas Hirvonen,Andrey Shkerin,Sergey Sibiryakov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了场论中热一阶相变（thermal first-order phase transition）成核过程（nucleation）中由动力学预因子（dynamical prefactor）引起的修正效应，给出了热衰变率的普适公式、系统计算方案及非微扰修正的数值证据，并提出一种计算效率远高于直接模拟的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡理论给出的统计衰变率（statistical rate）忽略了成核过程中的动力学效应，可能导致实际热衰变率被高估；已有结果分散于随机力学与场论框架中，缺乏统一且系统化的处理方法。

Method: 建立了包含动力学预因子的热衰变率普适公式，并给出其系统化评估方案；通过实时数值模拟（real-time numerical simulation）简单场论模型，验证公式的正确性并探索非微扰贡献与振荡子（oscillon）之间的关系；所提出的数值方法在计算上比直接衰变模拟指数级省时。

Result: 公式能够复现随机力学与场论中已知的成核率结果，并统一扩展至更一般情形；在弱耦合场论中观察到了可主导动力学预因子的显著非微扰贡献，这些效应与振荡子具有关联；数值方法适用于任意强指数抑制的系统。

Conclusion: 热成核率在满足热化条件（thermality conditions）下可精确到指数小修正，但当系统小或热化不充分时，稳态衰变率不存在；非微扰动力学效应在弱耦合下不可忽视，需在理论预测中纳入。

Abstract: We study dynamical effects during nucleation in thermal first-order phase transitions in field theory. Focusing on the classical regime of the decay of a metastable state, we present the general formula for the thermal decay rate including the dynamical prefactor and give a recipe for its systematic evaluation. We describe the physical mechanism which reduces the actual thermal decay rate with respect to the statistical rate obtained in equilibrium theory. We also discuss the thermality conditions ensuring the existence of a steady-state thermal rate, in which case our formula is exact up to exponentially small corrections. We show that it reproduces the known results for the nucleation rate in stochastic mechanics and field theory, and allows us to unify and go beyond them. We illustrate this in real-time numerical simulations of simple field theory models. We observe significant non-perturbative contributions which can dominate the dynamical prefactor in weakly-coupled field theories at moderate exponential suppression of the decay rate. We explore the connection of these non-perturbative effects to oscillons. Notably, our numerical method requires exponentially less computing time than direct simulations of decays and is thus applicable to systems with arbitrarily strong exponential suppression. Finally, we discuss small or poorly thermalized systems when the thermality conditions are violated and the steady-state rate does not exist.

</details>


### [119] [Minimal Massive Gravity Coupled to Higher Spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00185)
*Franz Ciceri,Nihat Sadik Deger,Bastien Duboeuf,Henning Samtleben*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维大规模引力中的极简大质量引力（Minimal Massive Gravity, MMG），将其与有限塔的高自旋场（higher-spin fields）耦合，通过将一阶表述的场提升为sl(N)取值来构造耦合方案，并研究了群值Stückelberg标量实现的质量形变破坏的高自旋平移对称性，最终构建了携带高自旋毛的AdS₂×S¹解并分析了AdS₃周围高自旋模的质量谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三维大质量引力中，MMG具有一个独特的参数区域，使得AdS体理论与其对偶共形场论能够同时保持幺正性（unitarity），这使其成为值得深入研究的理论框架。作者希望将MMG扩展到高自旋领域，探索大质量高自旋引力理论的结构与特性。

Method: 将MMG一阶表述（first-order formulation）的场提升为sl(N)李代数取值（$\mathfrak{sl}(N)$-valued）来引入有限塔高自旋场；引入群值Stückelberg标量（group-valued Stückelberg scalar）构建扩展表述以恢复被质量形变破坏的高自旋平移对称性；在此基础上构造AdS₂×S¹解并分析AdS₃周围高自旋模的质量谱。

Result: 成功建立了MMG与高自旋场耦合的理论框架，构建了携带大质量理论特有高自旋毛（higher-spin hair）的AdS₂×S¹解，并获得了AdS₃周围高自旋激发模式的质量谱分析结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将MMG推广至高自旋场景，揭示了质量形变对高自旋平移对称性的影响机制，为三维大质量高自旋引力的进一步研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Among three-dimensional massive gravities, Minimal Massive Gravity (MMG) is distinguished by a parameter regime in which the AdS bulk theory and the dual conformal field theory can be simultaneously unitary. We couple MMG to a finite tower of higher-spins by promoting the fields of its first-order formulation to $\mathfrak{sl}(N)$-valued ones. We also present an extended formulation involving a group-valued Stückelberg scalar, which realizes the higher-spin translations broken by the massive deformation. We then construct AdS$_2\times$S$^1$ solutions carrying higher-spin hair specific to the massive theory. Finally, we analyse the mass spectrum of the higher-spin modes around AdS$_3$.

</details>


### [120] [Infrared Problem in Quantum Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00204)
*Igor Frolov,Albert Schwarz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的图解技术（diagram technique），在该技术下，量子电动力学（QED）中的包含截面（inclusive cross section）以及更一般的包含散射矩阵（inclusive scattering matrix）的费曼图不再包含红外发散（infrared divergences），从而避免了传统标准图解方法中依赖发散消除（cancellation of divergences）的处理方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子电动力学中，包含截面在红外区域是有限的，这一结果在传统标准图技术中需要通过红外发散的相互抵消来获得。这种抵消机制虽然是正确的，但在数学处理上较为繁琐且不直观，作者希望构建一种更加直接的图解方法，使得对应图在构造之初就不包含红外发散项。

Method: 作者构造了一种新的图解技术（diagram technique），用于计算包含截面以及更为一般的包含散射矩阵。与标准图技术不同，该技术通过在图的构造层面引入适当的定义或重整化处理，使得每个图本身即为红外有限的，无需依赖发散之间的抵消。

Result: 新构建的图解技术能够直接给出红外有限的包含截面和包含散射矩阵图，避免了传统方法中红外发散抵消的步骤。

Conclusion: 新图解技术为处理QED中红外发散问题提供了更直接的路径，证明包含过程的红外有限性可以在构造层面而非抵消层面实现。

Abstract: It is well known that the inclusive cross section in QED is infrared finite. In the standard diagram techniques this result follows from cancellation of infrared divergences. We construct a new diagram technique where the diagrams for inclusive cross sections (and, more generally, for inclusive scattering matrix) do not contain infrared divergences.

</details>


### [121] [Projecting Gravitational Fluctuations onto Near-Horizon Throats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00363)
*Alejandro Cabo-Bizet,Vasil Dimitrov,Dario Martelli,Lorenzo Ruggeri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文表明，对于线性化涨落方程可分离为径向与角向Heun方程（Heun equations）的静态时空几何，一对微分同胚（diffeomorphisms）可将完整问题投影为互补时空区域中两个耦合的等谱高斯超几何问题（isospectral Gauss hypergeometric problems），并在此基础上给出了Kerr-de Sitter与Kerr-anti-de Sitter黑洞近极端极限下引力涨落模态向Schwarzian/Jackiw-Teitelboim函数空间基连续约化的新识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heun方程与瞬时子配分函数（instanton partition functions）对应关系近期已获得若干成果，本文旨在为此类结果提供一种独立推导方法，并进一步探索其在黑洞近极端极限（near-extremal limit）引力涨落问题中的应用。

Method: 针对涨落方程可分离为径向和角向Heun方程的静态时空，引入一对微分同胚将完整问题投影为两个互补时空区域中耦合的等谱高斯超几何问题；在两个互补区域的交界面处施加Robin边界条件（Robin boundary conditions），并通过要求两个微分同胚在界面处匹配来唯一确定该边界条件。

Result: 该方法为Heun连接系数与Nekrasov-Shatashvili极限下瞬时子配分函数对应关系所得结论提供了独立推导；作为应用，识别出Kerr-de Sitter和Kerr-anti-de Sitter黑洞涨落模态的一个子集，这些模态在近极端极限下连续约化为Schwarzian/Jackiw-Teitelboim函数空间（gravitational fluctuations functional space）中的一组基。

Conclusion: 通过微分同胚投影与Robin边界条件构造，本文建立了Heun方程问题与高斯超几何问题之间的严格对应，并为近极端黑洞涨落的Schwarzian/Jackiw-Teitelboim描述提供了新的谱层面证据。

Abstract: We show that, for stationary geometries whose linearized fluctuation equations separate into radial and angular Heun equations, a pair of diffeomorphisms projects the full problem onto two coupled, isospectral Gauss hypergeometric problems in complementary spacetime regions. This projection requires Robin boundary conditions to be imposed at the intersection between the complementary regions, which are uniquely fixed by requiring the two diffeomorphisms to match there. The method provides an independent derivation of recent results obtained from the correspondence between Heun connection coefficients and instanton partition functions in the Nekrasov-Shatashvili limit. As an application, we identify a subset of modes in Kerr-de Sitter and Kerr-anti-de Sitter black holes that, in the near-extremal limit, continuously reduce to a basis of the Schwarzian/Jackiw-Teitelboim functional space of gravitational fluctuations.

</details>


### [122] [Fibre Inflation Beyond BHP: Constraints and a BHP-Free Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00376)
*Xin Gao,Zhiwei Li,Chuying Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本研究在IIB型大体积方案（Large Volume Scenario）的纤维暴胀（fibre inflation）模型中，突破传统Berg-Haack-Pajer（BHP）参数化的限制，系统分析依赖纤维模的α'回路修正，识别出BHP自由的新型暴胀机制（α'回路暴胀），该机制能在满足CMB、Kähler锥和大体积约束下稳定纤维模量并支持慢滚动暴胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纤维暴胀模型通常基于BHP参数化构建有效场论修正，但K3纤维Calabi-Yau定向折叠（orientifold）中可能存在超越BHP形式的α'回路修正；这些修正对暴胀动力学的影响此前尚未被系统研究，且暴胀对定向折叠和膜配置的敏感性仍不清楚。

Method: 在Type IIB大体积方案纤维暴胀框架下，研究受限的依赖纤维的α'回路扇区，将构型分为BHP完全驱动、部分BHP驱动和BHP自由三类进行分析；通过比较附加修正对标准纤维势的变形程度（以百分比水平衡量），并检查对数项的斜率与曲率效应，评估各构型的暴胀可行性。

Result: BHP驱动情形下，附加修正作为纤维势的有效变形，相对于BHP结构被限制在百分比水平，且对数项因影响拟合斜率和曲率而受到更强约束；BHP自由情形下发现α'回路暴胀机制，其中传统KK型和缠绕型BHP通道消失，而广义回路扇区稳定纤维模量并驱动慢滚动暴胀，该机制呈现由大体积抑制控制的回路结构层级，满足CMB、Kähler锥、大体积和重模量约束。

Conclusion: 纤维暴胀对定向折叠和膜配置敏感，超越BHP拟设的修正能够产生新的受控暴胀区域，拓展了弦论暴胀模型的有效场论空间。

Abstract: We study a restricted fibre-dependent $α'$-loop sector in Type IIB Large Volume Scenario fibre inflation beyond the conventional Berg-Haack-Pajer (BHP) parametrisation. Motivated by effective-field-theory corrections in K3-fibred Calabi--Yau orientifolds, we analyse BHP-complete, partially BHP-driven and BHP-free configurations. For BHP-driven cases, the additional corrections act as effective deformations of the standard fibre potential and are bounded at the percent level relative to the corresponding BHP structures, with logarithmic terms typically more restricted due to their effects on the slope and curvature. At the effective-field-theory level, we identify a BHP-free fibre-inflation regime, referred to as $α'$-loop inflation, in which the conventional KK- and winding-type BHP channels vanish while the generalized loop sector stabilises the fibre modulus and supports slow-roll inflation. This regime exhibits a characteristic hierarchy between fibre-dependent loop structures controlled by the large-volume suppression and satisfies CMB, Kähler-cone, large-volume and heavy-modulus constraints. Our results show that fibre inflation is sensitive to the orientifold and brane configurations, and that corrections beyond the BHP ansatz can generate new controlled inflationary regimes.

</details>


### [123] [IR Black Hole Instabilities Trigger Species-Scale Particle Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00590)
*Luis A. Anchordoqui,Alek Bedroya,Dieter Lüst,Houri-Christina Tarazi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子引力中的一个新型紫外-红外（UV-IR）机制：黑洞不稳定性充当通往理论最深紫外扇区的桥梁。当黑洞达到与轻态塔（light tower of states）相关的临界温度时，会经历一个相变，产生接近物种尺度（species scale）的粒子——该尺度是任何有效引力场论失效的阈值。基于现有数值结果，作者表明这类粒子通过霍金辐射（Hawking radiation）的产生始终是次主导的，并将研究扩展至一大类对偶黑洞（dyonic black holes）和膨胀子黑洞（dilatonic black holes），分析了结论如何依赖规范对称性起源、膨胀子耦合及其电荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力中紫外与红外尺度之间可能的物理联系，理解黑洞不稳定性如何在临界温度下触发通向紫外深部的物理过程。

Method: 提出一个概念性机制框架，并结合现有的数值计算结果来定量评估通过霍金辐射产生物种尺度附近粒子的效率；同时系统地将分析推广到对偶黑洞和膨胀子黑洞族，以考察不同规范对称性起源、膨胀子耦合和电荷对结论的影响。

Result: 表明在临界温度下通过霍金辐射产生的接近物种尺度的粒子始终是次主导的（subdominant）；该结论的一般性延伸至对偶黑洞和膨胀子黑洞，但其具体表现依赖于规范对称性的起源、膨胀子耦合强度以及黑洞所带的规范电荷。

Conclusion: 黑洞相变可以作为一种连接红外与紫外物理的桥梁机制，尽管霍金辐射在此过程中的粒子产生贡献有限，但该机制为量子引力中UV-IR联系提供了新的理论视角，且其影响会随黑洞类型和规范结构的不同而变化。

Abstract: We propose a novel UV-IR mechanism in quantum gravity in which black hole instabilities act as a bridge to the most ultraviolet sector of the theory. Specifically, we argue that when black holes reach a critical temperature associated with a light tower of states, they undergo a phase transition that sources particles near the species scale, a threshold beyond which any effective field theory of gravity fails. Using the existing numerical results, we show that production of such particles through Hawking radiation is always subdominant. We also extend our investigation to a large family of dyonic and dilatonic black holes and show how the conclusion depends on the origin of the gauge symmetry, its dilatonic coupling, and the charge under that gauge symmetry.

</details>


### [124] [Spectral Functions of $J/ψ$ Meson in Rotating Thermal Background from Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00665)
*Xin-Li Sheng,Jun-Xia Chen,Defu Hou,Hai-Cang Ren*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本论文在软墙全息模型（soft-wall holographic model）框架下，研究了旋转热背景中 J/ψ 介子的谱函数（spectral function）。研究发现，当动量平行于旋转轴时，峰值能量按 −ΩJ_z 位移、宽度几乎不受旋转影响；当动量垂直于旋转轴时，自旋为 ±1 态的谱函数偏离单峰行为，并出现三重态劈裂，为旋转体系中矢量介子的自旋依赖性质提供了全息视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究快速旋转重离子碰撞等旋转强子物质中矢量介子性质的变化，是理解强相互作用物质在极端条件下行为的重要课题。作者希望利用全息对偶（holographic duality）方法，在旋转背景下系统刻画 J/ψ 介子的谱结构如何受全局旋转和自旋耦合影响。

Method: 采用软墙全息模型，通过旋转 AdS 类度规（rotating AdS-like metric）实现全局旋转；引入局部惯性系（local inertial frame）以将矢量场分解为不同自旋态。在体（bulk）中求解矢量场运动方程，并在视界（horizon）附近施加入射波条件（incoming wave condition），进而计算延迟格林函数（retarded Green function），从中提取 J/ψ 的不变质量谱函数。

Result: 动量平行于旋转轴时，峰值能量按 −ΩJ_z 位移，符合旋转与角动量耦合的预期，且谱宽几乎与 Ω 无关；动量垂直于旋转轴时，自旋为 ±1 态的谱函数显著偏离单峰行为，能量位移也偏离 −ΩJ_z。谱函数出现三重态劈裂（triplet splitting）。

Conclusion: 全息计算揭示了旋转背景中矢量介子谱函数对动量方向和自旋状态的依赖性，三重态劈裂现象为理解旋转体系中自旋依赖的矢量介子性质提供了新的全息视角，可能对旋转夸克物质中的实验观测具有参考意义。

Abstract: We investigate the spectral functions of the $J/ψ$ meson in a rotating thermal background within the soft-wall holographic model. The global rotation is implemented through a rotating AdS-like metric, while a local inertial frame is introduced in which the vector field can be decomposed into different spin states. We solve the equations of motion of the vector field in the bulk with incoming wave condition near the horizon and compute the retarded Green function, from which we extract the invariant-mass spectral functions for $J/ψ$. When the momentum is parallel to the rotation axis, the peak energies shift by $-ΩJ_z$, as expected by a coupling between rotation and angular momentum, while the widths are nearly independent to $Ω$. When the momentum is in perpendicular direction, the spectral functions for spin-$\pm1$ states deviate significantly from the single-peak behavior and the energy shifts depart from $-ΩJ_z$. The resulting triplet splittings of the spectral functions provides a holographic perspective on spin-dependent vector meson properties in rotating systems.

</details>


### [125] [Aspects of the quantization of non-linear electrodynamics in an uniform magnetic background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00758)
*M. J. Neves*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文针对存在外部电磁场背景的非线性电动力学（non-linear electrodynamics）提出了正则量子化（canonical quantization）和路径积分量子化（path integral quantization）方案。通过在背景场附近对一般拉格朗日量做小涨落展开并保留至二次项，得到线性化的电磁场理论；进而计算了基态能量、讨论了微因果性（microcausality），并在库仑规范下构建了生成泛函和微扰论框架，最后以Modified Maxwell ED为例计算了一圈有效势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性电动力学在外部电磁场背景下的量子化问题尚未被系统解决，本文旨在建立一套在存在外部电磁场时可行的正则与路径积分量子化框架，并为其提供可计算的微扰论工具。

Method: 将一般非线性电动力学的拉格朗日量围绕外部电磁场背景对传播场的小涨落展开，保留至二次项从而获得线性化电动力学；通过正则量子化计算基态能量，利用Pauli–Jordan函数讨论微因果性；在库仑规范下定义生成泛函并导出格林函数，进而构建类似标准量子场论（QFT）的微扰论形式；将传播场与复标量场耦合，计算一圈有效势。

Result: 获得了由外部电磁场线性化后的电动力学模型；给出了基态能量与外部磁场的关系；通过Pauli–Jordan函数确认了模型的微因果性；在库仑规范下得到了格林函数并建立了微扰论框架；成功计算了线性化电磁场耦合复标量场时的一圈有效势，并将其应用于Modified Maxwell电动力学特例。

Conclusion: 所提出的量子化框架为非线性电动力学在外部电磁场背景下的量子化提供了统一且可行的路径，且该框架可与标准QFT的微扰论方法无缝衔接，适用于包括Modified Maxwell ED在内的多种非线性电动力学模型。

Abstract: The canonical and path integral quantization for non-linear electrodynamics in the presence of an external electromagnetic (EM) field are proposed in this work. The EM background field is introduced expanding a general lagrangian of a generic non-linear electrodynamics around the background for small fluctuations of the propagating fields, where we consider up to the quadratic terms in the propagating fields. As consequence, we obtain an electrodynamics linearized by the presence of the external EM field. Thereby, we study the canonical quantization calculating the energy for the ground state of the linearized EM field in terms of an external magnetic field. The microcausality of the model also is discussed through the Pauli-Jordan function. We also define a generating functional for the linearized EM field, in which, in the Coulomb gauge, the Green function of the model is obtained, and we can construct the perturbative formalism like in standard quantum field theory (QFT). As application of the perturbation theory, the effective potential at one loop is calculated for the linearized EM field coupled to a complex scalar field. We apply the results in the case of the Modified Maxwell ED.

</details>


### [126] [Classical Black Hole Scattering to Celestial Amplitudes in Effective Theories of Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00784)
*Saptaswa Ghosh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本论文运用世界线有效场论（worldline effective field theory）、世界线量子场论（worldline quantum field theory）与壳上散射振幅（on-shell scattering amplitudes）等量子场论方法，研究广义相对论（General Relativity）之外的经典引力理论，涵盖暗物质扇区模型、标量-张量引力、动力学Chern-Simons引力及爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-伸缩子理论，推导了一系列守恒与辐射可观测量，并将这些技术拓展至天体物理动力学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典引力在广义相对论框架之外仍有大量未充分探索的理论空间，而现代量子场论技术尚未被系统应用于这些超越爱因斯坦框架的引力动力学中，需要更细致地理解其可观测量行为。

Method: 采用世界线有效场论、世界线量子场论与壳上散射振幅三种互补的量子场论方法，研究工作覆盖暗物质扇区模型、标量-张量引力、动力学Chern-Simons引力以及爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-伸缩子理论，并纳入自旋（spin）、宇称破缺（parity violation）、红外结构（infrared structure）及多圈积分（multi-loop integrals）等效应。

Result: 推导出包括冲量（impulses）、波形（waveforms）、eikonal相（eikonal phases）、两体势能（two-body potentials）及散射角（scattering angles）在内的一系列守恒与辐射可观测量；同时构建了天体eikonal振幅（celestial eikonal amplitudes）并分析其共形数据（conformal data）。

Conclusion: 研究拓宽了现代量子场论技术在引力动力学中的应用范围，为超越爱因斯坦框架的引力理论提供了更详细和深入的理解。

Abstract: This thesis investigates quantum field theoretic approaches to classical gravity beyond General Relativity. Using worldline effective field theory, worldline quantum field theory, and on shell scattering amplitudes, it investigates compact-object dynamics in dark-sector models, scalar tensor gravity, dynamical Chern Simons gravity, and Einstein Maxwell dilaton theory. The thesis derives a range of conservative and radiative observables, including impulses, waveforms, eikonal phases, two-body potentials, and scattering angles, while incorporating effects associated with spin, parity violation, infrared structure, and multi-loop integrals. It also constructs celestial eikonal amplitudes and analyzes their conformal data in gravitational theories beyond General Relativity. Collectively, these results broaden the application of modern quantum field theoretic techniques to gravitational dynamics and contribute to a more detailed understanding of gravity beyond the Einsteinian framework. (Detailed abstract in the thesis.)

</details>


### [127] [Symmetric formulation for higher spin correlators, quantum effective action and anomaly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01125)
*Melik Karapetyan,Ruben Manvelyan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个构造性框架，用于分析三点高自旋共形关联函数（three-point higher spin conformal correlation function），并以此为基础构建相应高自旋共形规范理论（higher spin conformal gauge theory）的量子有效作用量（quantum effective action）。通过对称细化的构造，作者提取了三点函数的主奇异性（principal singularity），并提出了一种通用方法来分析有效作用量中的反常局域贡献（anomalous local contributions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已引入三点高自旋共形关联函数，本文旨在为其建立系统性的构造框架，并以此作为构建对应量子有效作用量的基础，从而深入理解高自旋规范理论中的奇异性结构与反常贡献。

Method: 采用先前构造的对称细化版本（symmetric refinement），分析三点高自旋共形关联函数的主奇异性，并据此推导关联的有效作用量；由此发展出一套通用方法，用于提取主导奇异性并研究作用于有效作用量的反常局域贡献。

Result: 成功建立了三点高自旋共形关联函数的构造性框架，并以其为基础构建了高自旋共形规范理论的量子有效作用量；获得了提取主奇异性的一般方法，并能够有效分析有效作用量中的反常局域贡献。

Conclusion: 该构造框架为高自旋共形关联函数及其有效作用量的系统分析提供了通用工具，为研究高自旋规范理论的量子性质与奇异行为奠定了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: We develop a constructive framework for the three-point higher spin conformal correlation function, originally introduced in our previous work, and apply it as the foundation for constructing the quantum effective action of the corresponding higher spin conformal gauge theory. Employing a symmetric refinement of the earlier construction, we analyze the principal singularity of the three-point function and the associated effective action. This leads to a general method for extracting the dominant singularity and investigating the anomalous local contributions to the effective action.

</details>


### [128] [Testing bare open-string-metric areas against flavour entanglement in finite-density D3-D7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01154)
*Fuminori Okabayashi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文检验了D7-膜开弦度量（open string metric, OSM）中的裸余维二面积是否能够复现零温无质量有限密度D3-D7系统中边界纠缠熵（boundary entanglement entropy）的前导探针膜贡献。结果表明裸OSM面积可以诊断开放扇区中的密度诱导交叉物理，但一般不能计算味纠缠熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有的Chang-Karch-Uhlemann基准在受控探针区域中包含了依赖形状的体积项，而本文希望检验一种更简单的几何构造——裸余维二面积——能否在零温质量有限密度D3-D7系统中复现前导探针膜对边界纠缠熵的贡献。

Method: 作者在D7-膜开弦度量框架下计算裸余维二面积，并将其与已确立的Chang-Karch-Uhlemann基准进行比较。具体分析了两种几何分支：连通的条形族（connected strip family）与较低面积的球面分支（lower-area spherical branch），同时引入低温调节器以考察零温极限的行为。

Result: 连通的条形族在最大宽度处终止，其选定条形的有限部分在断开参考值处饱和；较低面积的球面分支在较大半径下呈现面积类（area-like）而非体积类（volume-like）行为。OSM构造能够探测密度诱导的交叉标度，其几何标志出现在由极点定义的纵向响应长度（pole-defined longitudinal response length）的一阶倍数处。低温调节显示，没有任何密度控制的视界体积项能在零温极限中存留。

Conclusion: 裸OSM面积能够诊断开放扇区中的交叉物理，但一般而言不能用于计算味纠缠熵（flavour entanglement entropy）。

Abstract: We test whether bare codimension-two areas in the D7-brane open string metric (OSM) reproduce the leading probe-brane contribution to boundary entanglement entropy in the massless finite-density D3-D7 system at zero temperature. The established Chang-Karch-Uhlemann benchmark contains shape-dependent volume terms in a controlled probe regime. By contrast, the connected strip family terminates at a maximal width and the selected strip finite part saturates at the disconnected-reference value, while the lower-area spherical branch is area-like rather than volume-like at large radius. The OSM construction nevertheless detects the density-induced crossover scale, and its geometric landmarks occur at order-one multiples of a pole-defined longitudinal response length. A low-temperature regulator shows that no density-controlled horizon-volume term survives the zero-temperature limit. Bare OSM areas therefore diagnose open-sector crossover physics but do not, in general, compute flavour entanglement entropy.

</details>


### [129] [Exact Finite-$N$ eRS Surface-Defect Indices and Giant-Graviton Corrections in $\mathcal N=4$ SYM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01349)
*Ji-Seong Chae*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维 $\mathcal N=4$ $U(N)$ 杨-米尔斯理论（Yang–Mills theory）在有限 $N$ 超共形指标（superconformal index）中插入反对称椭圆 Ruijsenaars–Schneider（eRS）算子后形成的可观测系列。在特殊坐标点 $t=q$, $p=uv$ 处，eRS 算子共轭于自由平移，使得自胶合指标可化为双边自由费米子行列式，从而得到所有反对称秩的精确有限 $N$ 公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通超共形指标本身是无加权的谱和，仅靠它不足以完整刻画有限 $N$ 信息。插入 eRS 算子后，指标被 eRS 本征值加权，形成一族保留有限 $N$ 细节的可保护可观测量（protected observables），从而弥补普通指标的不足。

Method: 作者将 eRS 插入算子置于 codimension-one 流形 $t=q$, $p=uv$ 上，此时算子共轭于自由移位，自胶合指标化为双边自由费米子行列式。利用这一结构推导精确有限 $N$ 公式，并在 $v=0$ 边界上确定纯 $u^{kN}$ 缺陷修正的稳定塔；同时发展可解流形外二阶展开。

Result: 获得所有反对称秩的精确有限 $N$ 公式，使 $u\leftrightarrow v$ 对称结构显式化；在 $v=0$ 边界确定了完整的 $u^{kN}$ 缺陷修正稳定塔；双边双电荷公式捕获反射的 $v^{kN}$ 扇区和混合 $u$–$v$ 贡献（含首个混合巨-引力子（giant graviton）扇区）；给出可解流形外的二阶展开。

Conclusion: 从最大 D3 行列式复现了普通一巨贡献系数，并识别了完成缺陷修正体推导（bulk derivation）所需的额外微观输入。

Abstract: We study the finite-$N$ superconformal index of four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ $U(N)$ Yang--Mills theory with antisymmetric elliptic Ruijsenaars--Schneider (eRS) insertions. The eRS insertions turn the uninserted index, which is an unweighted spectral sum, into a family of protected observables weighted by eRS eigenvalues and therefore retain finite-$N$ information not determined by the ordinary index alone On the codimension-one locus $t=q$, $p=uv$, the eRS operators become conjugate to free shifts, allowing us to rewrite the self-glued index as a bilateral free-fermion determinant. This gives exact finite-$N$ formulas for all antisymmetric ranks and makes the $u\leftrightarrow v$ structure manifest. On the boundary $v=0$, we determine the complete stable tower of pure $u^{kN}$ defect-resolved corrections, while the bilateral two-charge formula captures the reflected $v^{kN}$ sectors and mixed $u$--$v$ contributions, including the first mixed giant-graviton sector. We also develop the expansion away from the solvable locus through second order. Finally, we reproduce the ordinary one-giant coefficient from a maximal-D3 determinant and identify the additional microscopic input required to complete the bulk derivation of the defect correction.

</details>


### [130] [Quantization of Galilean Electrodynamics: a non-trivially trivial theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01372)
*Rafael Hernandez,Juan Miguel Nieto Garcia,Ander Urtiaga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究伽利略电动力学（Galilean electrodynamics）的量子化问题，利用狄拉克括号（Dirac bracket）形式体系分析了该理论的约束结构与规范结构，结果表明该理论是完全约束的（fully constrained），从而从路径积分（path integral）中消除了所有自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动力学的非相对论极限——伽利略电动力学——在经典层面已被广泛研究，但其量子化问题尚未得到充分理解。本文旨在通过约束分析为该理论的量子化奠定基础。

Method: 应用狄拉克括号（Dirac bracket）形式体系来系统地处理该理论的约束（constraints）与动力学，并分析其规范结构（gauge structure）。

Result: 证明该理论是完全约束的，即所有自由度均被约束消除，路径积分中不保留任何物理自由度。

Conclusion: 伽利略电动力学在量子层面不存在传播的自由度（propagating degrees of freedom），其完全约束的本性决定了路径积分中无动力学内容。

Abstract: We consider the quantization of the non-relativistic limit of electrodynamics called Galilean electrodynamics. To that end, we apply the Dirac bracket formalism to understand the constraints and dynamics of the theory and analyze its gauge structure. We show that the theory is fully constrained, which eliminates all the degrees of freedom from the path integral.

</details>


### [131] [de Sitter holography from a Lorentzian torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01729)
*Kosei Fujiki,Michitaka Kohara,Javier Moreno,Kotaro Shinmyo,Tadashi Takayanagi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的 dS/CFT 对应（de Sitter/conformal field theory correspondence）实现方式：通过 Wick 旋转到一个具有两个类时方向（two timelike directions）的几何，将量子引力全息对偶到定义在洛伦兹环面（Lorentzian torus）上的共形场论，并证明了该对偶能够重现 dS 全息的关键特征，最后借助路径积分优化（path-integral optimization）解释了 dS 时空如何从环面上的 CFT 涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 dS/CFT 对应中，未来和过去边界上的共形场论之间的量子纠缠尚无统一且清晰的描述框架，需要一种新的实现方式来更自然地表征这一纠缠结构。

Method: 文中通过 Wick 旋转将原几何转换为具有两个类时方向的几何，从而自然地描述未来与过去边界 CFT 之间的量子纠缠；在此基础上提出该双时间几何上的量子引力全息对偶于定义在洛伦兹环面上的 CFT，并进一步将路径积分优化框架推广以推导 dS 时空的涌现。

Result: 所提出的对偶能够重现 dS 全息的关键特征，包括 dS 熵（de Sitter entropy）、关联函数（correlation functions）和伪熵（pseudoentropy）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种新的 dS/CFT 对应实现，不仅统一描述了边界 CFT 间的量子纠缠，还通过路径积分优化展示了 dS 时空可由洛伦兹环面上的 CFT 全息涌现。

Abstract: We show that the quantum entanglement between the conformal field theories (CFTs) living on the future and past boundaries in the de Sitter/conformal field theory (dS/CFT) correspondence can be described by Wick rotating to a geometry with two timelike directions. We propose a new realization of the dS/CFT correspondence in which quantum gravity on this two-time geometry is holographically dual to a CFT defined on a Lorentzian torus. We show that this duality reproduces key features of dS holography, including the dS entropy, correlation functions, and pseudoentropy. Finally, by extending the framework of path-integral optimization, we explain how dS spacetime emerges from the CFT on the Lorentzian torus.

</details>


### [132] [Primordial Correlators from a Kaluza-Klein Graviton Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01762)
*Shuntaro Aoki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究宇宙学对撞机（cosmological collider）信号在交换粒子形成连续质量谱时的修正，提出了连续场中介的暴胀关联函数谱表示，并在RS2型暴胀膜世界模型中实现，发现连续谱会消除单个大质量粒子产生的尖锐时钟振荡（clock oscillation），产生平滑的种子函数（seed function）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学对撞机信号通常针对孤立的大质量粒子讨论，其交换会在原始关联函数中产生特征性的对数振荡；然而当交换态形成连续质量谱时，该标准信号如何被修正尚未被系统研究，这是本文要解决的问题。

Method: 首先为连续场中介的暴胀关联函数建立谱表示：在软极限（soft limit）下，种子函数的非解析部分可表达为谱权重（spectral weight）关于对数动量变量的傅里叶-拉普拉斯变换，该表示显示连续谱叠加不同频率的时钟信号并可使尖锐振荡退相。随后在RS2型暴胀膜世界（braneworld）中实现该机制，其中暴胀子局域于五维AdS体中的de Sitter膜上，张量扇区包含局域无质量引力子和从m=3H/2开始的Kaluza-Klein引力子连续谱，并推导出膜上谱权重。

Result: 将膜上谱权重应用于暴胀子四点函数，发现得到的是平滑的种子函数而非持续的对数时钟振荡。该行为源于连续谱从零时钟频率起始，而KK谱权重在阈值附近消失。

Conclusion: 结果为偏离标准宇宙学对撞机信号的现象提供了一个具体的额外维度例子，表明这种偏差能够编码连续高能谱的结构信息。

Abstract: Cosmological collider signals are usually discussed for isolated massive particles, whose exchange produces characteristic logarithmic oscillations in primordial correlators. In this work, we study how this signal is modified when the exchanged states form a continuous mass spectrum. We first develop a spectral representation for inflationary correlators mediated by a continuum field. In the soft limit, the non-analytic part of the seed function is expressed as a Fourier--Laplace transform of the spectral weight with respect to logarithmic momentum variables. This representation shows that a continuum superposes clock signals with different frequencies and can dephase the sharp oscillation associated with a single massive particle. We then realize this mechanism in an RS2-like inflationary braneworld, where the inflaton is localized on a de Sitter brane in a five-dimensional AdS bulk. The tensor sector contains a localized massless graviton and a continuum of Kaluza-Klein gravitons starting at \(m=3H/2\). We derive the KK wavefunctions and identify the brane spectral weight fixed by the continuum wavefunction on the brane. Applying this weight to the inflaton four-point function, we find a smooth seed function rather than a persistent logarithmic clock oscillation. This behavior follows from the fact that the continuum starts at zero clock frequency, while the KK spectral weight vanishes near threshold. Our results provide a concrete higher-dimensional example in which deviations from the standard cosmological collider signal encode the structure of a continuum high-energy spectrum.

</details>


### [133] [Holographic Thermodynamics of Dyonic Dilaton AdS Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01818)
*Muhammad Fitrah Alfian Rangga Sakti,Robert Mann,Piyabut Burikham*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了允许宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）和牛顿引力常数（Newton's gravitational constant）均变化的dyonic dilaton AdS黑洞的扩展体热力学的全息对偶，在CFT热力学中引入了中心荷（central charge）C及共轭化学势μ等新变量。通过分析五对热力学变量组成的十六个系综，发现在固定体积和固定压力两类系综中均出现了从零阶到二阶的相变结构，其中固定压力系综表现出更丰富的相变行为，且μ的正负会强烈影响相空间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展体热力学通过在体理论中允许宇宙学常数和引力常数变化，为黑洞热力学提供了更丰富的相结构。本文动机在于将这些体热力学变化如何在全息对偶的CFT侧精确翻译为临界现象和更丰富的相变结构这一核心问题。

Method: 作者对dyonic dilaton AdS黑洞的扩展体热力学构建全息对偶CFT热力学，引入五对热力学变量$(\tilde{T},\tilde{S})$、$(\tilde{Q},\tilde{\Phi})$、$(\tilde{P},\tilde{\Psi})$、$(\tilde{\mathcal{P}},\tilde{V})$以及$(C,\mu)$，并基于$(\tilde{Q},\tilde{P})$对称性将十六个系综归纳为固定体积和固定压力两类各五个系综分别进行分析。

Result: 固定体积系综中，固定$(\tilde{Q},\tilde{P},\tilde{V},\mu)$和$(\tilde{\Phi},\tilde{P},\tilde{V},\mu)$表现出零阶相变；固定$(\tilde{\Phi},\tilde{\Psi},\tilde{V},C)$系综在临界势$\tilde{\Upsilon}_c$上下分别呈现零阶和一阶相变。固定压力系综中，固定$(\tilde{Q},\tilde{P},\tilde{\mathcal{P}},\mu)$和$(\tilde{\Phi},\tilde{P},\tilde{\mathcal{P}},\mu)$表现出零阶、一阶和二阶相变；固定$(\tilde{\Phi},\tilde{\Psi},\tilde{\mathcal{P}},\mu)$仅出现零阶相变，μ的正负对相空间结构有重要影响。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明，体引力参数（尤其是宇宙学常数和引力常数）的变化在CFT全息对偶中可精确映射为多样的临界现象，揭示出比传统体热力学分析更丰富的相变结构，为理解扩展热力学的全息对应关系提供了更深入的洞见。

Abstract: We investigate the holographic dual of the extended bulk thermodynamics of dyonic dilaton AdS black holes by allowing both the cosmological constant and Newton's gravitational constant to vary. In the dual CFT thermodynamics, the central charge $C$ and chemical potential $μ$ as its conjugate enter the thermodynamic relations, in addition to $(\tilde{T},\tilde{S})$, $(\tilde{Q},\tildeΦ)$, $(\tilde{P},\tildeΨ)$, $(\tilde{\mathcal{P}},\tilde{V})$. We consider sixteen different ensembles related to those five pairs of thermodynamic variables which we separate into fixed volume and pressure ensembles. Due to the symmetry on ($\tilde{Q},\tilde{P}$), we only need to analyze five ensembles each in fixed volume and pressure ensembles. In the fixed volume ensembles, it is found that the fixed ($\tilde{Q},\tilde{P},\tilde{V},μ$) and ($\tildeΦ,\tilde{P},\tilde{V},μ$) exhibit a zeroth-order phase transition. Interestingly, the fixed ($\tildeΦ,\tildeΨ,\tilde{V},C$) ensemble shows the zeroth- and first-order phase transitions at above and below critical potential $\tildeΥ_c$, respectively. In the fixed pressure ensembles, it is found that richer structures appear where the sign of $μ$ heavily influences phase space for several ensembles. For fixed ($\tilde{Q},\tilde{P},\tilde{\mathcal{P}},μ$) and ($\tildeΦ,\tilde{P},\tilde{\mathcal{P}},μ$), they show the zeroth-, first-, and second-order phase transitions. For the fixed ($\tildeΦ,\tildeΨ,\tilde{\mathcal{P}},μ$) ensemble, there is only a zeroth-order phase transition occurs. The other ensembles which we do not mention eventually do not show any phase transition. These findings provide deeper insight into how bulk gravitational variations, particularly regarding the cosmological and gravitational constants, translate to exact critical phenomena and richer phase structures within CFT.

</details>


### [134] [Coarse-Graining and the Classification of Long-Range Correlations in Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01989)
*Jinku Guo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由守恒律（conservation laws）指导的分类框架，用于量子场论中的粗粒化（coarse-graining）运算。该框架依据两个判据——注入项（injection term）是否非零以及单气泡（single-bubble）贡献的符号——对费曼图（Feynman diagrams）进行结构性分类，并在涌现（emergence）框架下对局域算符建立了一般性分类，经八个物理通道和三个已知可解系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论中的粗粒化操作会产生费曼图层面的选择，但不同算符受不同守恒律保护，其代数保证强度不同，这导致需要一种系统化的分类框架来区分算子可能的涌现路径。

Method: 通过守恒律指导费曼图分类：零动量传递的梯图（ladder diagrams）可通过几何级数求和累积产生谱极点（spectral pole）；单气泡拓扑仅贡献连续谱。分类以两个属性为判据：注入项是否非零（由 Ward 恒等式或 BRST 对称性及其对应的 Slavnov-Taylor 恒等式决定）和单气泡贡献的符号（由自旋统计关系决定）。正号导致梯图重求和中放大性反馈，负号导致抑制性反馈。

Result: 在涌现框架下建立了一般性局域算符分类，并在八个物理通道和三个已知可解系统上得到验证。该框架可指示每个算符哪些涌现路径是可能的，但临界条件（critical condition）是否达到需交由独立的非微扰计算判定。

Conclusion: 该分类的逻辑结构与经典统计物理中从分子动力学理论推导流体方程的策略平行。

Abstract: A conservation-law-guided classification framework is developed for the execution of coarse-graining operations in quantum field theory. Coarse-graining produces a selection at the level of Feynman diagrams. Diagrams with nonzero momentum transfer are suppressed by oscillatory factors, zero-momentum-transfer ladder diagrams can accumulate through geometric series resummation to produce a spectral pole, and single-bubble topologies contribute only to the continuum. Conservation laws govern this classification. For operators protected by a Ward identity, the matrix element at zero momentum is guaranteed to be nonvanishing. For operators protected by BRST symmetry, the Slavnov-Taylor identities provide no mandatory suppression. The two cases differ in the strength of the algebraic guarantee. For unprotected operators the injection term vanishes and the spectral function remains continuous. The sign of the single-bubble contribution is determined by spin statistics. A positive sign leads to amplificative feedback in the ladder resummation, a negative sign leads to suppressive feedback. These two attributes, the nonvanishing of the injection term and the sign of the single-bubble contribution, are the defining criteria of the classification. As a direct application, a general classification of local operators is established within the emergence framework and verified on eight physical channels and three known solvable systems. The framework indicates which emergence paths are possible for each operator; whether the critical condition is reached is left for independent nonperturbative computation. The logical structure of this classification is parallel to the strategy used in deriving fluid equations from molecular kinetic theory in classical statistical physics.

</details>


### [135] [Superloop Equations and Minimal Surfaces I: Confining minimal surface in $4D, N=1$ SYM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02262)
*Alexander Migdal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种路径有序算子微积分（path-ordered operator calculus, POOC）方法，将纯 4 维 N=1 超杨-米尔斯（super Yang–Mills, SYM）理论的圈方程写成有限几何形式，消除了共点导数引起的赝运动学奇点。应用 POOC 于 Itoyama-Takashino 超圈层级后，构造了洛伦兹超对称霍奇对偶（supersymmetric Hodge-dual, SHD）曲面泛函，其零模指数乘以有限 N 层级的任意解仍保持方程不变。对于平面长矩形威耳孙圈（Wilson loop），该泛函退化为几何面积，给出精确的非微扰禁闭面积律因子 W[C_{T,L}] ~ exp[-iσ_kLT]，E_k(L)=σ_kL>0。欧几里得延拓后，该 SHD 因子是确定性的零噪声 SYM 梯度流的精确不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常的等点圈导数在轮廓点重合时因规范场插入顺序丢失而产生奇异行为，需要一种能够在保持插入顺序的同时消除赝运动学奇点的形式体系，以便处理 N=1 SYM 的精确非微扰结构（尤其是禁闭面积律）。

Method: 本文发展路径有序算子微积分（POOC）：将规范场插入保留在分离的有序槽位中，先构造分级对易子与雅可比组合，再取重合极限，从而无需引入截断即可消除赝运动学奇点；随后将 POOC 应用于精确的 Itoyama–Takashino 超圈层级，构造洛伦兹超对称霍奇对偶（SHD）曲面泛函并研究其面积导数与加法性质。

Result: SHD 泛函的霍奇解析面积导数被局域手性与反手性圈算子湮灭；在曲面-测地线拼接下具有精确可加性，其指数（零模）乘上有限 N 层级的任意解不改变方程。对平面轮廓，SHD 泛函即几何面积，长矩形威耳孙圈给出 W[C_{T,L}] ~ exp[-iσ_kLT] 且 E_k(L)=σ_kL>0，从而得到 N=1 SYM 中精确的非微扰禁闭面积律因子。欧几里得延拓后，该因子是零噪声 SYM 梯度流的精确不动点。

Conclusion: 该构造给出了 N=1 SYM 精确的非微扰禁闭面积律因子，以及有限 N 层级下的精确零模与修饰（dressing）定理。其作为动力学选择平衡态的物理解释仍需在长流时间、大体积与零噪声极限下的稳定性验证；完整的平面威尔孙圈解（含未修饰的涨落因子与激发谱）将在后续论文中给出。

Abstract: We formulate the loop equations of pure $4d, N=1$ super Yang--Mills (SYM) theory in a finite geometric form. The usual equal-point loop derivatives are singular because the order of gauge-field insertions is lost when contour points coincide. Our path-ordered operator calculus (POOC) keeps the insertions in distinct ordered slots, forms the graded commutators and Jacobi combinations, and only then takes the coincidence limit. This removes the spurious kinematical singularities without introducing a cutoff; the genuine ultraviolet contact remains a separate physical distribution.
  We apply POOC to the exact Itoyama--Takashino superloop hierarchy and construct a Lorentzian supersymmetric Hodge-dual (SHD) surface functional. Its Hodge-resolved area derivative is annihilated by the local chiral and anti-chiral loop operators. Exact additivity under surface-geodesic sewing shows that the exponential of this zero mode multiplies any solution of the complete finite-$N$ hierarchy without changing the equations. For planar contours the SHD functional is the geometric area. In particular, for a long rectangular Wilson loop, $W[C_{T,L}]\sim\exp[-iσ_kLT]$ and $E_k(L)=σ_kL>0$. Thus the construction gives an exact nonperturbative confining area-law factor in $ N=1$ SYM.
  After Euclidean continuation, the same SHD factor is an exact fixed point of the deterministic zero-noise SYM gradient flow. Its interpretation as a dynamically selected equilibrium still requires stability in the long-flow-time, large-volume, and zero-noise limits, but this does not affect the exact zero-mode and finite-$N$ dressing theorems. The complete planar Wilson-loop solution, including the undressed fluctuation factor and excitation spectrum, will be developed in the next papers of this series.

</details>


### [136] [Subleading Asymptotic Charges in Massless Scalar QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02368)
*Éanna É. Flanagan,Zhihan Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究闵可夫斯基时空中与带电荷质量标量场耦合的U(1)规范场的次领头渐进电荷（subleading asymptotic charges），在标量场初始数据紧支撑、规范场初始数据具有逆幂尾部（inverse-power tails）的条件下，识别出三个在散射下守恒的有限电荷，并猜想第三个电荷与一类在推迟时间上线性发散的新型大规范变换（large gauge transformations）相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在闵可夫斯基时空的渐近平直几何中，渐进对称性与电荷守恒是研究散射过程的重要工具。先前研究多关注领头阶（leading order）软电荷，而对次领头阶（subleading order）电荷在存在初始数据尾部（initial-data tails）时的结构尚不清楚，本文旨在系统刻画这一情形下的守恒电荷。

Method: 论文考虑一个由U(1)规范场与带电荷质量标量场耦合构成的相空间：标量场在类光无穷远（null infinity）上的初始数据具有紧支撑，而规范场的初始数据在超前（推迟）时间上允许带有逆幂尾部的渐近展开。作者在这些条件下来识别和构造在散射下守恒的三个有限电荷。

Result: 研究识别出三个有限且散射守恒的电荷：其一是标准次领头软电荷（subleading soft charge）的一个重整化（renormalized）版本；其二是对数型电荷（logarithmic charge），它等于类光无穷远未来端与过去端领头阶尾部系数之差；其三是探测尾部系数平均值的新电荷。

Conclusion: 作者推测第三个新电荷与一类新的、在推迟时间上线性发散的大规范变换（large gauge transformations）相关联，这暗示渐进对称性代数可能比此前认知的结构更为丰富。

Abstract: We study subleading asymptotic charges of a U(1) gauge field coupled to a charged massless scalar field in Minkowski spacetime. We consider a phase space for which the scalar-field initial data on null infinity have compact support, while the gauge-field initial data admit an asymptotic expansion with inverse-power tails in retarded (advanced) time, which we refer to as initial-data tails. We identify three finite charges at the null infinities which are conserved under scattering. One is a renormalized version of the standard subleading soft charge. The second is a logarithmic charge which is the difference of the coefficients of the leading order tails at the future and past ends of null infinity. The third is a new charge that probes the average of the tail coefficients. We conjecture that the third charge is associated with a new class of large gauge transformations which diverge linearly in retarded time.

</details>


### [137] [Rank matching in renormalization-group irreversibility: Exact defect and entropic tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02447)
*Francesco Scardino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出"秩匹配"（rank matching）框架来解释局域减法（local subtractions）为何在部分设定中产生重整化群（renormalization group）单调量而在其他设定中失效。核心观点是：一阶（degree one）是最后一种其尺度导数（scale derivative）落在正定性输入直接可及范围内的减法阶数；更高阶需要额外动力学支持。作者通过三个可精确求解的测试模型验证了这一理论，展示了端点不等式与运行单调性可以分别成立，并表明秩匹配可将端点排序、运行单调性和损失随尺度分布三者的行为区分开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域减法在某些情况下能产生重整化群单调量（renormalization-group monotones）而在其他情况下失败，此前缺乏统一原理来解释这一差异。作者希望通过提出秩匹配机制，厘清减法阶数与正定性输入（bilinear forms 或 positive second variations）可控制的数据维度之间的关系，从而解释单调性成立的边界条件。

Method: 作者提出秩匹配框架：局域反项（local counterterms）固定了标度不变性（scheme independence）所需的尺度导数数量；每次导数添加一个连通插入（connected insertion），而可用的正定性输入为双线性形式或正二阶变分，仅能控制二次数据。因此一阶减法是最后一个尺度导数仍在正定性直接可及范围内的减法。作者随后通过三个可精确求解的模型进行验证，包括奇维 p-球面上的大质量标量、广义自由表面缺陷 b 函数，以及四维单值性缺陷（monodromy defect）。

Result: 三个可精确求解的测试呈现两种结局。大质量标量在 p≥3 的奇数维 p-球面上产生非单调的滤波自由能（filtered free energies）。广义自由表面缺陷 b 函数在缺陷主算符维度 1/2≤Δ̂<1 时严格单调，而在 0<Δ̂<1/2 时必然非单调；在阈值处其流动系数在正则谱坐标（canonical spectral coordinate）上完全单调，各阶导数交替变号。局域四维单值性缺陷实现了完整的转变。在关于大分量熵极限的假设下，盘熵（disk entropy）与球面自由能共享端点和总 F 损失，但损失的尺度分布不同。

Conclusion: 秩匹配框架将端点排序、运行单调性以及损失随尺度的分布区分开来，为理解局域减法在重整化群单调性设定中的适用性提供了统一原理。一阶减法只有在 Ward 恒等式或熵恒等式（entropic identity）提供带符号二次型时才能闭合；更高阶减法需要额外的动力学输入。

Abstract: Why do local subtractions produce renormalization-group monotones in some settings but fail in others? We propose rank matching. Local counterterms fix how many scale derivatives scheme independence requires. Every derivative adds one connected insertion, while the available positivity inputs are bilinear forms or positive second variations and therefore control only quadratic data. Degree one is the last subtraction whose scale derivative stays within their direct reach. First-order subtractions can close when a Ward or entropic identity supplies a signed quadratic form. Higher orders need extra dynamics. Three exactly solvable tests exhibit both outcomes and show that the endpoint inequality can hold while running monotonicity fails. Massive scalars yield nonmonotone filtered free energies on every odd dimensional $p$-sphere with $p\geq3$. A generalized-free surface-defect $b$-function is strictly monotone when the defect-primary dimension $\widehatΔ$ satisfies $1/2\leq\widehatΔ<1$, and necessarily nonmonotone for $0<\widehatΔ<1/2$. At the threshold, its flow coefficient is completely monotone in the canonical spectral coordinate, with derivatives of every order alternating in sign. A local four-dimensional monodromy defect realizes the full transition. Under a stated assumption on the large-component entropy limit, disk entropy and sphere free energy share endpoints and total $F$ loss but distribute it differently over scale. Rank matching separates endpoint ordering, running monotonicity, and the distribution of loss over scale.

</details>


### [138] [Confinement as Decoding: Higher Form Codes and Lattice Yang-Mills Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02452)
*Ning Bao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子纠错（quantum error correction）、禁闭（confinement）与格点杨-米尔斯理论（lattice Yang-Mills theory）之间的联系。作者将有限阿贝尔同调码的译码问题表述为高阶规范场，证明其逻辑类对应于西森森系综（Nishimori ensemble）的拓扑扇区，并建立了逻辑概率、无序算符与信道环境信息之间的傅里叶关系。进一步，文章研究了四维Z_N记忆体与禁闭PSU(N)真空的潜在联系，并引入了一个与SU(N)链接变量耦合的有限曲率中心片模型，其条件逻辑概率即为中心扭曲杨-米尔斯配分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错理论中的拓扑有序（topological order）与高能物理中的禁闭现象之间存在深刻联系，但二者之间的数学结构尚未被系统阐明。本文旨在将量子纠错的译码问题与格点规范理论中的拓扑扇区、禁闭真空等概念统一起来，从而为理解量子记忆的稳定性与质量隙（mass gap）等问题提供新的理论框架。

Method: 本文将有限阿贝尔同调码的译码问题用高阶规范场（higher form gauge fields）表述，利用西森森系综和傅里叶变换建立逻辑概率、无序算符（disorder operators）与信道环境信息之间的数学关系；给出阈值（threshold）的轮廓与分数矩判据。随后构造了一个四维Z_N记忆体模型，并将其与禁闭的PSU(N)真空联系起来。最后定义了一个与Wilson SU(N)链接变量耦合的有限曲率中心片模型，通过强耦合展开（strong coupling expansion）推导出症状（syndrome）的领先有效相互作用，并将成对症状世界线（worldlines）的似然函数与中心磁单极子关联函数（center monopole correlator）联系起来。

Result: 研究发现，在正局部噪声下，逻辑类对应西森森系综的拓扑扇区，最优译码误差由非平凡扇区的相对权重决定。在中心片模型中，即使全局片扇区混合，局部症状关联仍可衰减。成对症状世界线的似然函数由中心磁单极子关联函数给出，其衰减决定转移矩阵质量。该结果区分了全局通量扇区的抑制与讨论质量隙所需的局部谱信息。

Conclusion: 本文建立了量子纠错译码与格点规范理论中禁闭现象之间的严格对应关系，证明拓扑扇区的相对权重直接决定译码误差，而中心磁单极子关联函数的衰减则提供了区分全局拓扑抑制与局部谱质量的信息判据。这一框架为从规范理论视角理解量子纠错码的稳定性提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study the relationship between quantum error correction, confinement, and lattice Yang-Mills theory. We first formulate decoding for finite Abelian homological codes in terms of higher form gauge fields. For positive local noise, the logical classes are topological sectors of a Nishimori ensemble, and the optimal decoding error is determined by the relative weights of the nontrivial sectors. We derive Fourier relations between logical probabilities, disorder operators, and information in the channel environment, and we give contour and fractional moment criteria for a threshold. We then study a four dimensional $\Z_N$ memory and its possible relation to confining $\PSU(N)$ vacua. Finally, we define a finite curvature center sheet model coupled to Wilson $\SU(N)$ link variables. In this model the conditional logical probabilities are center twisted Yang-Mills partition functions. A strong coupling expansion gives the leading effective interaction for the syndrome and shows that local syndrome correlations can decay even when the global sheet sectors are mixed. We also show that the likelihood for a separated pair of syndrome worldlines is the center monopole correlator. Its decay determines a transfer matrix mass. This distinguishes the suppression of global flux sectors from the local spectral information needed to discuss a mass gap.

</details>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Channel selection at identically vanishing dissipation difference: isolating the frenetic sector of the overdamped path measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00041)
*Shlomo Segal*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造一个精确可控的反例证明，过渡态理论和Kramers-Langer理论所依赖的单一鞍点信息不足以决定反应通道选择。作者构造了一个双通道梯度景观，其中熵扇区被完全关闭、鞍点Hessian矩阵相同、势垒高度恒等，但朗之万模拟给出分支比偏离50:50，从而表明选择必须由费伦尼斯（frenesy）扇区中的协变几何贡献决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡态理论（transition-state theory）和Kramers-Langer理论仅利用鞍点处的局部数据来预测反应通道选择。作者质疑这一做法在精确可控意义下是否成立，并试图构造一个具体反例来证明局部鞍点信息是不充分的。

Method: 利用Onsager-Machlup路径权重标准分解为时间反对称部分（由熵通量固定）和时间对称部分（即费伦尼斯，frenesy）。首先推导两个历史之间的熵差等于非保守力围绕环路环流的β倍，且对任何梯度流恒为零。然后构造一个双通道梯度景观，使两个通道共享端点、势垒高度恒等、鞍点Hessian相同，通过朗之万模拟测量分支比，并识别出费伦尼斯扇区中协变的几何贡献(1/2)ln det(D_perp H_perp)。

Result: 构造的梯度景观即使所有局部鞍点理论都预测50:50的选择概率，朗之万模拟却给出P(+)=0.4568±0.0012。协变几何贡献(1/2)ln det(D_perp H_perp)在无拟合参数条件下与测量到的分支比在0.5σ内吻合；发散-漂移可观测量不具有坐标协变性而该量具备；刚性旁观模因行列式之比而精确抵消。此外给出无参数预测：螺线管力强度ε对ln[P(+)/P(-)]的贡献恰好为2βε/π。

Conclusion: 这些结果构成了一个精确可控的反例，证明单一鞍点处的局部数据不足以决定反应通道选择；通道选择必须考虑费伦尼斯扇区中的全局几何信息。

Abstract: Transition-state and Kramers-Langer theory determine reaction channel selection from data at a single point: the saddle. We show this is insufficient in a precise, constructive sense. Using the standard split of the Onsager-Machlup path weight into a time-antisymmetric part fixed by the entropy flux and a time-symmetric part, the frenesy, we first settle when a dissipation-based criterion can distinguish two forward histories at all: the entropy difference between two histories sharing endpoints equals beta times the circulation of the non-conservative force around the loop they enclose, and vanishes identically for every gradient flow. We then construct a two-channel gradient landscape whose channels share both endpoints, whose barrier heights are equal identically, and whose saddle Hessians are the same matrix. The entropy sector is switched off by construction, all local saddle-point theories predict 50:50 exactly, and the branching ratio isolates the frenetic sector. Langevin simulation gives P(+) = 0.4568 +/- 0.0012. We identify a covariant geometric contribution to pathway selection within the frenetic sector, (1/2) ln det(D_perp H_perp), show that the divergence-of-drift observable proposed for this role is not coordinate-invariant whereas this one is, and find that it reproduces the measured branching to within 0.5 sigma with no fitted parameters. Stiff spectator modes cancel identically, since selection depends only on a ratio of determinants. We also give a parameter-free prediction: a solenoidal force of strength epsilon must contribute exactly 2*beta*epsilon/pi to ln[P(+)/P(-)]. These results are an exactly controlled counterexample to the sufficiency of local saddle-point data for channel selection.

</details>

### [140] [Physics-Constrained Generative Inference of Sub-Crystal Electromagnetic Shower Structure in a Segmented Calorimeter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00348)
*Yu-Sheng Liu,Yu-Chen Tung*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了分割型电磁量能器（electromagnetic calorimeter）有限横向颗粒度对簇射信息重建的限制，提出将重建视为反问题（inverse problem）并用受低阶空间矩约束的生成模型求解。在KOTO实验的CsI量能器上，该方法将1 GeV能段中每个事件的低阶矩残差相对真值减小约40-62%，并能泛化到训练中未涉及的物理量（如光子入射角和π⁰衰变顶点）重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割型电磁量能器的有限横向颗粒度从根本上限制了簇射可观测量的重建精度（包括位置、横向轮廓和入射粒子方向）。论文旨在探究分割抑制的信息能否在物理约束下被恢复并传播到下游物理量，从而重新定义分割性对量能器信息获取能力的限制本质。

Method: 将簇射重建形式化为一个逆问题（inverse problem），用受簇射低阶空间矩约束的生成模型（generative model）求解。该约束将解空间导向物理上一致的能量分布，而非仅依赖图像相似度。以KOTO实验中未掺杂CsI量能器作为参考系统进行验证。

Result: 在代表性1 GeV能段中，该方法将每个事件的低阶空间矩残差相对真值降低约40-62%。在$K_L→π^0ν\barν$蒙特卡洛样本上，重建形态可泛化到训练目标之外：改善了入射光子角度的重建（该量在训练中从未使用），也改善了π⁰衰变顶点的重建。

Conclusion: 有限分割不应被视为量能器保留物理信息的绝对极限，而应视为对可直接测量内容的限制。可恢复的信息比例取决于观测量的类型，并随簇射能量增加而增长。

Abstract: The finite transverse granularity of a segmented electromagnetic calorimeter fundamentally limits the precision with which the observables of a shower can be reconstructed, among them its position, its lateral profile, and the direction of the incident particle. We show that a substantial fraction of the information suppressed by the segmentation can be inferred under physical constraints, and that it propagates to downstream physics quantities. The reconstruction is cast as an inverse problem and solved with a generative model constrained by the low-order spatial moments of the shower, driving the solution toward physically consistent energy distributions rather than image similarity alone. Using the undoped CsI calorimeter of the KOTO experiment as a reference system, the reconstruction reduces the per-event residual of these moments with respect to the truth-level reference by roughly 40-62% in a representative 1 GeV bin. The inferred morphology also generalizes beyond the training objective: on a $K_{L}\toπ^{0}ν\barν$ Monte Carlo sample it improves the reconstruction of the photon incident angle, a quantity never used during training, and of the $π^{0}$ decay vertex. These results indicate that finite segmentation is better viewed as a limit on what a calorimeter measures directly than as an absolute limit on the physics information it retains, with the recoverable fraction depending on the observable and growing with the shower energy.

</details>


### [141] [An eightfold equivalence-preserving speedup of the JUNO OMILREC vertex and energy reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00461)
*Guangbao Sun,Qishan Liu,Wenjie Wu,Jun Cao,Xuefeng Ding,Wenxing Fang,Wuming Luo,Liangjian Wen,Zeyuan Yu,Xiang Zhou*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 针对江门地下中微子实验（JUNO）事件重建核心算法OMILREC的CPU计算瓶颈，本文提出一系列保持等价性的分阶段优化策略，包括扁平数据布局、矢量化的几何、Minuit不变工作外提、预计算、循环拆分与索引以及降精度快速路径。优化后的单线程性能在Intel Xeon Platinum 8358P上提升8.06倍（从1524.8ms降至189.2ms/事件），在AMD EPYC 9654上提升5.22倍（从705.1ms降至134.9ms/事件），且似然值在七个版本发布中保持比特级一致，后续相对漂移低于1.3×10⁻¹⁴，远低于设定的10⁻¹³契约。典型事件的重建顶点与能量与基线一致（误差在4mm和7keV以内），约86.1万个⁶⁸Ge标定事件通过八指标物理验收测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JUNO使用OMILREC算法对每个事件进行最大似然拟合，每次Minuit函数求值需扫描全部17,612个大型光电倍增管（LPMT），每事件约执行470次扫描，该内层循环主导了重建的CPU开销。性能剖析显示，生产代码受延迟限制，由于虚函数分发（virtual-function dispatch）、ROOT直方图指针追踪（pointer chasing）及重复计算，仅达到标量浮点峰值性能的9.9%，亟需优化以提升计算吞吐。

Method: 采用分阶段保持等价性（equivalence-preserving）的优化策略，具体包括：扁平化数据布局（flattened data layouts）、可矢量化几何（vectorizable geometry）、Minuit不变工作外提（hoisting）、每事件预计算（per-event precomputation）、拟合阶段循环拆分与索引（loop splitting and indexing）以及降精度快速路径（reduced-precision fast paths）。每一步优化均以未修改代码的冻结参考（frozen reference）为基准进行一致性校验，并在AI编码助手的协助下在验证门控下开发。

Result: 优化实现实现单线程加速比：Intel Xeon Platinum 8358P上8.06倍（1524.8→189.2 ms/事件），AMD EPYC 9654上5.22倍（705.1→134.9 ms/事件），进一步优化后可达8.6倍（177.7 ms/事件）。似然值在前七个发布版本中保持比特级一致，后续相对漂移为1.3×10⁻¹⁴，低于10⁻¹³的契约。典型事件重建顶点与能量与基线一致（分别不超过4mm与7keV），约86.1万个⁶⁸Ge标定事件通过八指标物理验收测试。

Conclusion: 该工作流为大型中微子与对撞机探测器中基于似然的重建加速提供了可迁移模板，在不改变物理输出的前提下实现了显著的性能提升。通过AI编码代理在严格验证门控下辅助开发，展示了此类优化流程的可用性与通用性。

Abstract: The Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO) reconstructs each event's vertex and energy with OMILREC, a maximum-likelihood fit that scans all $17{,}612$ large photomultiplier tubes (LPMTs) in every Minuit function evaluation, about $470$ times per event. This inner loop dominates the reconstruction CPU cost. Profiling shows that the production algorithm is latency-bound, sustaining only $9.9%$ of scalar floating-point peak because of virtual-function dispatch, ROOT-histogram pointer chasing, and repeated computation. We apply staged \emph{equivalence-preserving} optimizations: flattened data layouts, vectorizable geometry, hoisting of Minuit-invariant work, per-event precomputation, fit-phase loop splitting and indexing, and reduced-precision fast paths. Each stage is checked against a frozen reference from the unmodified code. The optimized implementation achieves single-thread speedups of $8.06\times$ ($1524.8 \rightarrow 189.2$~ms/event) on an Intel Xeon Platinum~8358P and $5.22\times$ ($705.1 \rightarrow 134.9$~ms/event) on an AMD~EPYC~9654, increasing to $8.6\times$ ($177.7$~ms/event) after further optimization. The likelihood remains bit-identical through the first seven releases and later agrees within a relative drift of $1.3\times10^{-14}$, below the $10^{-13}$ contract. For typical events, reconstructed vertex and energy agree with the baseline within $4$~mm and $7$~keV; a few boundary cases reach different valid minima owing to an improved minimizer seed. An eight-metric physics-acceptance test also passes on about $861{,}000$ $^{68}$Ge calibration events. Developed with assistance from an AI coding agent operating under these verification gates, this workflow offers a transferable template for accelerating likelihood-based reconstruction in large neutrino and collider detectors without changing physics output.

</details>


### [142] [Spectroscopic study of argon electroluminescence light with a wavelength-sensitive particle detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01842)
*R. Santorelli,V. Pesudo Fortes,G. Vera Díaz,C. K. Guérard,R. López Manzano,L. Luzzi,J. Martínez Morales,L. Romero*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文通过配备不同光谱灵敏度光电倍增管（photomultiplier tubes）的紧凑探测器，对气态时间投影室（time projection chamber, TPC）中氩电极发光（electroluminescence, EL）进行了光谱研究，测量了[110,160] nm和[160,650] nm两个标称波段在不同气压下的光发射。研究发现[160,650] nm波段存在显著的快速纳秒级响应发射，而[110,160] nm波段的发射则表现出明显较慢的时间演化，由氩第二连续谱的长寿命三重态组分主导，并证明完整的真空紫外（VUV）脉冲形状可通过将快速UV3响应与准分子形成和衰减函数卷积来重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解氩电极发光的谱和时间特性对于设计和优化基于气态TPC的下一代稀有事件探测器（rare-event detectors）至关重要，本研究旨在填补对氩EL发射在更宽波长范围内的系统性表征空白。

Method: 采用配备具有不同光谱敏感度的光电倍增管的紧凑探测器，在一个气态时间投影室中测量氩EL在两个标称波长区域（约[110,160] nm和[160,650] nm）在不同气体压力下的光发射，并分析各波段的发射时间结构以揭示其物理机制。

Result: 除已知的128 nm第二连续谱发射外，在[160,650] nm波段观察到显著的快速、纳秒级响应发射，表明光子产生密切跟随漂移电子穿越高场EL区域的过程；而[110,160] nm发射时间演化明显较慢，主要受长寿命三重态组分的准分子形成和退激发动力学支配。

Conclusion: 完整的VUV脉冲形状可通过将快速UV3响应与准分子形成和衰减函数卷积来重现，为观测信号提供了连贯的唯象学解释；这些结果对稀有事件探测器的设计与优化具有直接影响，后续研究将更精确表征该发射并探索其用于粒子鉴别的潜力。

Abstract: We present a spectroscopic study of argon electroluminescence (EL) light in a gaseous time projection chamber (TPC). Using a compact detector equipped with photomultiplier tubes with different spectral sensitivities, we measure the light emission in two nominal wavelength regions, approximately [110, 160] nm and [160, 650] nm, at different gas pressures. In addition to the well-known 128 nm emission of the second continuum, significant emission is observed in the [160, 650] nm band, with a prompt, nanosecond-scale response indicating that photon production closely follows the transit of drifting electrons across the high-field EL region. In contrast, the [110, 160] nm emission displays a markedly slower time evolution, dominated by excimer formation and de-excitation dynamics, with the light output governed primarily by the long-lived triplet component of the argon second continuum emission. We demonstrate that the full VUV pulse shape can be reproduced by convolving the fast UV3 response with excimer formation and decay functions, providing a coherent phenomenological interpretation of the observed signals. Our results provide new insights into the spectral and temporal properties of argon electroluminescence and have direct implications for the design and optimization of next-generation rare-event detectors based on gaseous TPCs. Further studies are underway to characterize this emission more precisely and to investigate its potential for particle discrimination.

</details>
