### [143] [Symmetry-initialized quantum Gibbs sampling: a non-Abelian asymmetry cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00055)
*Soo-Jong Rey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子 Gibbs 采样器中由弱破缺对称性导致混合瓶颈的问题，证明正确的初始化方案由表示论（representation theory）决定。作者建立了"速度提升与前置因子"的二分定理，证明通过精确消除慢模重叠可将名义上的前置因子缩减转化为渐近混合时间加速；当瓶颈源于弱破缺对称性时，瓶颈本征向量即为对称电荷，且非阿贝尔群的正确初始化目标是对称群平均不对称性。论文在 SU(2) Davies 采样器中解析和数值验证了这些结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子 Gibbs 采样器常因弱破缺对称性（weakly broken symmetry）引发混合瓶颈，导致采样效率显著下降。现有方法通常依赖匹配低阶矩或经验试探来初始化，缺乏严格的群论依据。本文试图从表示论角度系统刻画瓶颈结构，并据此推导正确的初始化目标，从而获得渐近级别的混合加速。

Method: 作者首先证明一个关于混合加速与前置因子缩减的二分定理：通过精确消除慢模（slow mode）重叠，可将名义上的前置因子缩减转化为渐近混合时间加速。随后证明当瓶颈来自弱破缺对称性时，指数昂贵的瓶颈本征向量正是对称电荷（symmetry charge）。对非阿贝尔群 G，证明正确初始化目标为群平均不对称性（group-averaging asymmetry），消除该不对称性需要 G 不变输入；部分子群不变输入会在子群格（subgroup lattice）上产生级联加速。作者在 SU(2) Davies 采样器中进行解析与数值验证。

Result: 在 SU(2) Davies 采样器中，总自旋多重态构成慢模，预测的加速级联得以显现，弛豫速率随对称性破缺参数线性缩放。匹配一阶矩（first moments）被证明不足以实现加速；不对称性是可直接测量的初始化目标。模型还刻画了不对称性目标的边界，在边界处需要单独匹配守恒逻辑数据。

Conclusion: 论文确立了弱破缺对称性瓶颈下初始化目标与表示论之间的严格联系，为非阿贝尔对称群情形提供了可测量、可操作的初始化策略，并揭示了其加速机制与适用边界。

Abstract: For a quantum Gibbs sampler whose mixing bottleneck is a weakly broken symmetry, I show that the correct initialization is determined by representation theory. I first prove a general speedup-versus-prefactor dichotomy: by exactly eliminating slow-mode overlap, I convert a nominal prefactor reduction into a fundamental, asymptotic acceleration of the system's mixing time. I then show that when the bottleneck is a weakly broken symmetry, the otherwise exponentially expensive bottleneck eigenvector is the symmetry charge.For a non-Abelian group $G$, I prove that the correct initialization target is the group-averaging asymmetry. Projecting this asymmetry out requires a $G$-invariant input. Partial, subgroup-invariant inputs produce a cascade of mixing-time speedups indexed by the subgroup lattice. Conversely, matching first moments alone is provably insufficient.The asymmetry is a directly measurable initialization target. I verify these results analytically and numerically in an $SU(2)$ Davies sampler where total-spin multiplets constitute the slow modes. The predicted speedup cascade emerges with relaxation rates scaling linearly with the symmetry-breaking parameter. Finally, the model maps the boundaries of the asymmetry target, illustrating where the separate matching of conserved logical data becomes necessary.

</details>


### [144] [Geometry-Informed Polynomial Time Quantum Approximation Schemes for Constrained Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01121)
*Chinonso Onah,Kristel Michielsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对含噪量子采样器（noisy quantum sampler）何时能在多项式时间内给出带性能保证的端到端优化算法这一问题给出回答。作者基于约束增强型QAOA（Constraint-Enhanced QAOA）的有限深度与有限采样保证，证明理想最优集合上的逆多项式概率加上独立采样、多项式时间可行性修复与评分，可产生精确命中完全多项式随机近似方案（FPRASq）。该保证在实例相关的噪声窗口内仍然成立，且所提出的NP-HQ算法契合Chen-Cotler-Huang-Li预言机模型；在此结构下，若经典多项式时间采样器能复现其逆多项式最优重叠，则意味着NP包含于BPP。文中还引入Heavy-Hitter QAOA以降低候选集规模与经典后处理成本，并在IBM Eagle r3处理器上通过多达一百个逻辑变量的硬件实验验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是回答"有噪量子采样器在何种条件下能够催生具有性能保证的多项式时间端到端优化算法"这一问题，并建立量子采样噪声容忍性与经典可模拟性之间分离的理论边界。

Method: 方法包括：在约束增强型QAOA的有限深度与有限采样保证基础上，将逆多项式理想最优概率与独立采样、多项式时间可行性修复及评分相结合，构造精确命中完全多项式随机近似方案FPRASq；同时引入Heavy-Hitter QAOA，通过减少保留候选集规模来降低经典后处理成本一个问题规模的幂次。

Result: 结果表明，在实例相关的噪声窗口内该保证依然成立：当有效电路深度与层数×问题规模成线性关系且保留逆深度比例的理想最优质量时，所需采样复杂度增加一个问题规模的幂次；超出窗口时，确定性修复仍保证可行性与实例相关的近似保证。硬件实验在IBM Eagle r3上覆盖至一百个逻辑变量的实例，匹配或优于Qoptlib所有测试参考路径。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：在一个NP-hard内核可容许承诺族上，若经典多项式时间采样器能复现逆多项式最优重叠，则NP包含于BPP，因此量子-经典分离的关键在于采样分布的生成本身。所有理论与实验结论均严格基于文中所述假设与约束条件。

Abstract: When does a noisy quantum sampler yield an end-to-end polynomial-time optimization algorithm with performance guarantees? Building on finite-depth and finite-shot guarantees for Constraint-Enhanced QAOA, we show that inverse-polynomial ideal probability on the optimal set, together with independent sampling, polynomial-time feasibility repair, and scoring, produces an exact-hit fully polynomial randomized approximation scheme, which we call an FPRASq.
  This guarantee survives device noise within an instance-dependent window. For effective circuit depth linear in the product of layer count and problem size, preserving an inverse-depth fraction of the ideal optimal mass increases the required shot complexity by one power of the problem size. Beyond this window, deterministic repair guarantees feasibility and provides an instance-dependent approximation guarantee whenever the induced objective inflation is controlled.
  The resulting NP-HQ algorithm fits the Chen-Cotler-Huang-Li oracle model. On any NP-hard kernel-admissible promise family, reproducing its inverse-polynomial optimal overlap with a polynomial-time classical sampler would imply that NP is contained in BPP, even with identical repair and perfect access to the constraint structure. Thus, the separation lies in generating the sampling distribution.
  We further introduce Heavy-Hitter QAOA, which preserves these conditional guarantees while reducing the retained candidate set and classical post-processing cost by one power of the problem size. Hardware experiments on IBM Eagle r3 processors cover instances with up to one hundred logical variables and match or improve every tested QOptlib reference tour.

</details>


### [145] [Canonical quantization of the Pais-Uhlenbeck oscillator with a higher-derivative perturbation: a covariant phase space approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00108)
*Jie-qiang Wu,Jinan Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将协变相空间形式（covariant phase space formalism）应用于Pais-Uhlenbeck振子的微扰正则量子化，将加速度平方项视为微扰。通过在低能解空间上构造辛形式进行量子化，微扰计算能谱和不等时对易子，结果与精确低能理论的展开一致，且绕过了标准的Ostrogradsky构造并自然解耦了Ostrogradsky鬼态（Ostrogradsky ghost）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是将此前提出的微扰量子化方案扩展到真正的高阶导数理论（higher-derivative theories）中，以规避高阶导数系统固有的Ostrogradsky不稳定性问题。

Method: 采用协变相空间形式（covariant phase space formalism），将加速度平方项（acceleration-squared term）作为微扰处理，在低能解空间（low-energy solution space）上构造辛形式（symplectic form）并实施微扰正则量子化。

Result: 微扰计算得到的能谱（energy spectrum）和不等时对易子（unequal-time commutator）与精确低能理论的展开结果一致；该方法绕过标准Ostrogradsky构造，并自然地解耦了Ostrogradsky鬼态。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了此前的微扰量子化方案可推广至真正的高阶导数理论，为处理此类系统的量子化提供了一种有效途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we apply the covariant phase space formalism to the perturbative canonical quantization of the Pais-Uhlenbeck oscillator, with the acceleration-squared term treated as a perturbation. We quantize this model by constructing the symplectic form on the low-energy solution space. We then compute the energy spectrum and the unequal-time commutator in a perturbative way, and obtain the results that agree with the expansion of the exact low-energy theory. The perturbation method bypasses the standard Ostrogradsky construction and naturally decouples the Ostrogradsky ghost. This work extends our previous perturbative quantization scheme to genuine higher-derivative theories.

</details>


### [146] [Coordinate space representation for quantum simulation of scalar field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00670)
*Gaetan Bardy,Matthieu Saubanere,Adrian Tanasa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于坐标空间谐振子基组（harmonic-oscillator basis）的格点φ⁴模型哈密顿量表述，证明了其单体力矩阵和相互作用张量具有有效的带状对角结构（band-diagonal structure），可进行受控截断而不损失低能谱。数值验证表明该表述与标准动量空间表述一致，且通过利用有效局域性（effective locality），坐标空间表述在广泛参数范围内降低了量子模拟资源需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算为量子场论（quantum field theory）模拟提供了有前景的框架，但计算成本同时取决于所采用的量子算法和哈密顿量的表示方式。因此需要探索更高效、资源需求更低的哈密顿量表述以提升量子模拟的可行性。

Method: 作者在坐标空间中基于谐振子基组推导了格点φ⁴模型哈密顿量，分析了单体力矩阵和相互作用张量的结构，并利用其带状对角特性进行受控截断。通过数值对角化比较低能可观测量，验证了该表述与标准谐振子动量空间表示的一致性；随后使用二进制和一元（binary and unary）玻色子-量子比特映射估算量子编码资源。

Result: 坐标空间的谐振子基组表述使单体力矩阵和相互作用张量均呈现有效的带状对角结构，允许在不破坏低能谱的前提下截断哈密顿量。数值结果验证了该表述的可靠性，且坐标空间表述利用有效局域性在广泛参数范围内减少了量子模拟所需的资源。

Conclusion: 坐标空间中的谐振子基组表述为φ⁴模型的量子模拟提供了更高效的哈密顿量编码方案，通过利用有效局域性显著降低了资源需求，为量子场论的实用化量子模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum computing provides a promising framework for the simulation of quantum field theories, where the computational cost depends both on the quantum algorithm employed and on the representation of the Hamiltonian. We investigate a formulation of the $φ^4$ model based on the harmonic-oscillator basis in coordinate space. We derive the lattice $φ^4$ Hamiltonian in this representation and analyze the structure of the resulting one-body matrix and interaction tensor. We show that both exhibit an effective band-diagonal structure, allowing controlled truncations of the Hamiltonian while preserving the low-energy spectrum. We validate this formulation by comparing low-energy observables obtained from numerical diagonalization with those computed in the standard harmonic-oscillator momentum-space representation. Finally, we estimate the resources required to encode the Hamiltonian on a quantum computer using both binary and unary boson-to-qubit mappings. By exploiting effective locality, the coordinate-space representation reduces the resources required for quantum simulation over a broad range of parameters.

</details>


### [147] [Optimality of Gaussian Entanglement of Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01909)
*Gerardo Adesso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明任意双模高斯态（two-mode Gaussian state）的纠缠形成（entanglement of formation）与其高斯受限版本一致，解决了连续变量量子信息理论中长期未解的问题。这一证明依赖于一个适用于任意纯双模态（包括非高斯态）的、纠缠与广义爱因斯坦-波多尔斯基-罗森（Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen）可观测量之间的尖锐仿射关系，并进一步推广到双对称多模高斯态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子信息理论中长期存在一个开放问题：双模高斯态的纠缠形成是否等于其高斯受限形式。该问题的解决对于理解高斯态纠缠的理论表征及量子信息处理的实际应用具有基础性意义。

Method: 作者证明了一个关键引理：对任意纯双模态（包括非高斯态），纠缠形成与某个广义EPR可观测量之间满足尖锐的仿射（affine）关系，以此建立了纠缠形成的下界，并通过构造约束使得该下界在高斯态情形下达到饱和。

Result: 证明了任意双模高斯态的纠缠形成与其高斯受限纠缠一致，解决了该开放问题；该结果可推广至双对称（bisymmetric）多模高斯态，并为任意非高斯态提供了纠缠形成的可测量下界。

Conclusion: 该工作为连续变量系统中的纠缠刻画提供了精确的理论结果，不仅闭合了双模高斯态纠缠形成问题的争议，还为非高斯态的纠缠下界提供了直接的实验可测方案。

Abstract: We prove that the entanglement of formation of every two-mode Gaussian state coincides with its Gaussian restriction, solving a longstanding open problem in continuous variable quantum information theory. The key result is a sharp affine relation between entanglement and a generalized Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen observable, valid for arbitrary pure two-mode states, including non-Gaussian ones. Our result extends to bisymmetric multimode Gaussian states, and also provides a measurable lower bound on the entanglement of formation of arbitrary non-Gaussian states.

</details>


### [148] [Estimating spacetime fluctuation strength in SU(1,1) and SU(2) interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00907)
*B. Sharmila*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了在探测时空涨落（spacetime fluctuations）信号时，SU(1,1)干涉仪相比传统SU(2)干涉仪是否具有优势。作者分别计算了两类干涉仪中用于估计时空涨落强度和关联长度的量子Fisher信息（quantum Fisher information）及对应实验测量方案下的经典Fisher信息（classical Fisher information），并在不同参数区间下进行比较，以判断SU(1,1)干涉仪在哪些条件下更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度激光干涉仪常被用于搜索随机时空涨落的特征信号，以理解引力的基本性质。传统实验普遍采用SU(2)干涉仪，本文受Zheng等人（2020）工作的启发，旨在探究改用SU(1,1)干涉仪能否提供超越SU(2)方案的估计优势。

Method: 作者分别计算在SU(1,1)和SU(2)两类干涉仪中，估计时空涨落强度和关联长度的量子Fisher信息，并进一步针对不同实验相关的测量方案计算对应的经典Fisher信息。随后，作者比较了与当前及未来可能实验设置相关的不同参数区间下的这些信息度量。

Result: 通过比较两类干涉仪在不同参数区间下的Fisher信息度量，本文评估了SU(1,1)干涉仪在估计时空涨落强度或关联长度方面是否以及何时优于SU(2)干涉仪。

Conclusion: 本文给出了SU(1,1)与SU(2)干涉仪在时空涨落参数估计中的优势条件判断，为未来高精度实验方案选型提供了理论参考。

Abstract: High-precision laser interferometers are commonly used to search for signatures of random spacetime fluctuations, in an attempt to understand the fundamental nature of gravity. Conventionally, $SU(2)$ interferometers have been used in such experimental investigations. Motivated by [K. Zheng \textit{et al.}, Photon. Res. \textbf{8}, 1653 (2020)], I assess if there is any advantage to be gained by using an $SU(1,1)$ interferometer instead of the $SU(2)$ interferometer. To this end, I compute the quantum Fisher information for estimating the strength and the correlation length of the spacetime fluctuations, and the corresponding classical Fisher information considering different experimentally relevant measurement schemes, in both types of interferometers. I compare the information metrics corresponding to different parameter regimes that are relevant to both current and possible future experimental setups. This helps me assess if and when the $SU(1,1)$ interferometer offers any advantage over the $SU(2)$ interferometer for estimating either the fluctuation strength or correlation length of the spacetime fluctuations.

</details>


### [149] [Finite-Syndrome Compression of Quantum Fisher Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02333)
*Jianqi Sheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子传感中有限综合征（finite syndrome）压缩对计量信息损失的影响，证明了丢失的SLD量子Fisher信息可精确表示为细粒与粗粒SLD得分之间状态加权平方距离之和，从而将最优有限综合征设计转化为算子值聚类问题，并给出了精确恢复量子码所需最小可读综合征数的图论刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可读错误记录能保护量子传感，因为其防止物理上不同的噪声轨迹被不可逆地混合；然而有限探测器或辅助比特只能保留有限多个综合征值，目前缺乏判断哪些记录可以合并而不丢失计量信息的一般准则。

Method: 在固定的细粒度经典-量子记录和参数无关压缩至最多M个标志（flag）的框架下，证明了丢失的SLD量子Fisher信息（SLD QFI）精确等于细粒与粗粒SLD得分间状态加权平方距离之和；该恒等式被推广至完整的多参数SLD QFI矩阵；对量子码精确恢复，利用Knill-Laflamme不相容图将最小可读综合征数刻画为图的色数；对量子比特随机酉噪声给出了有限划分公式，并对平面随机Pauli模型推导出条件方差表示及精确最优值。

Result: 零损失由支撑分解的公共SLD条件刻画；对量子比特随机酉噪声获得有限划分公式；均匀误差轴下平面随机Pauli模型的精确最优值为F_M^*=[M sin(π/M)/π]^2，亏损为π^2/(3M^2)+O(M^{-4})。

Conclusion: 这些结果确定了有限综合征分辨率的信息论代价，并明确了对任何被动噪声到擦除（noise-to-erasure）解释所必需的侧信息假设。

Abstract: Readable error records can protect quantum sensing because they prevent physically distinct noise trajectories from being irreversibly mixed. A finite detector or ancilla, however, can retain only finitely many syndrome values, and a general criterion for deciding which records may be merged without losing metrological information is absent. We formulate the problem for a fixed fine-grained classical-quantum record and parameter-independent compression into at most $M$ flags. We prove an exact identity expressing the lost symmetric-logarithmic-derivative quantum Fisher information (SLD QFI) as a sum of state-weighted squared distances between fine and coarse SLD scores. Consequently, optimal finite-syndrome design is exactly an operator-valued clustering problem, and zero loss is characterized by a support-resolved common-SLD condition. We extend the identity to the full multiparameter SLD QFI matrix and distinguish local QFI preservation from recovery of an entire statistical model. For exact recovery of a quantum code, we separately show that, when each fine error is individually correctable, the minimum number of readable syndromes is the chromatic number of a Knill-Laflamme incompatibility graph. For qubit random-unitary noise we obtain a finite partition formula. A planar random-Pauli model admits a conditional-variance representation and, for a uniform error axis, the exact optimum $F_M^{\star}=[M\sin(π/M)/π]^2$, with deficit $π^2/(3M^2)+O(M^{-4})$. These results identify the information-theoretic cost of finite syndrome resolution while making explicit the side-information assumptions required for any passive noise-to-erasure interpretation.

</details>


### [150] [Caustics and Superenergy in the Quantum Bouncer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02427)
*Marko. M. Ćosić,Andrew N. Jordan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子弹跳球（quantum bouncing ball）问题中的经典焦散（classical caustics）所对应的量子干涉与能量现象，即“量子焦散”（quantum caustics）。通过初始高斯波包，作者展示了与经典轨迹族相关的焦散结构，并将其邻近的相位奇点链与基于尖点灾变线（cusp catastrophe lines）构造的半经典Pearey函数（semiclassical Pearcey function）联系起来，同时量化了远超能量（superenergy）的大小，并利用Madelung/Bohm轨迹提供了补充描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典焦散结构在量子系统中如何体现以及其引发的能量异常现象，是需要被系统化的关键问题。

Method: 采用初始高斯波包（Gaussian wavepacket）刻画量子弹跳球系统，将焦散邻近的相位奇点链（phase singularity chains）与半经典Pearcey函数建立联系，并利用Madelung/Bohm轨迹补充描述。

Result: 焦散与经典轨迹族在量子波包中得以呈现；利用Pearey函数可描述焦散附近的相位奇点链；量化了远超能量的大小；Madelung/Bohm轨迹为能量与奇点链穿越行为提供了额外洞见。

Conclusion: 量子焦散可通过半经典Pearey函数与Madelung/Bohm轨迹获得一致且互补的完整描述，涵盖干涉、相位奇点和超能能量现象。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum interference and energetic phenomena associated with classical caustics in the quantum bouncing ball problem, we refer to as quantum caustics. By considering an initial Gaussian wavepacket, we show that caustics associated with the underlying classical trajectory families are exhibited. We connect the associated phase singularity chains in the vicinity of the caustic with the semiclassical Pearcey function built on the cusp catastrophe lines. We also quantify the amount of superenergy exhibited in these solutions - regions of space where the local energy exceeds the largest constituent energy eigenvalue. We give a complimentary description of the caustic and superenergy behavior using the Madelung/Bohm trajectories, which gives additional insight about the energy of the trajectories and how they traverse the phase singularity chains.

</details>


### [151] [Optimal Decoding for Measurement-Based GHZ State Preparation: The Maximum-Utility Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00160)
*Misha Yutushui,Theo Haas,Simon Trebst*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出最大效用解码器（maximum-utility decoder, MUD），将测量基GHZ态制备的译码问题框架化为最小贝叶斯风险推断，以最大化解码态的连续长程有序性。通过将MWPM与卷积神经网络结合的两阶段可扩展解码方案，作者实现了接近最优的译码性能，并证明仅使匹配知晓规范选择的MWPM第一阶段就能在N=256×256的规模下闭合裸MWPM与最优译码阈值间87%的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观GHZ态是量子计量学、密码学和容错码等量子技术的基础资源。然而，现有测量基协议使用的传统译码器（如MWPM或MLD）优化的是二元逻辑恢复，未能最大化二维几何下GHZ态所特有的连续长程有序特性，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 作者将译码问题重新框架为最小贝叶斯风险推断，引入最大化解码态期望效用的一般性范式。在此基础上构建最大效用译码器，并设计两阶段可扩展解码方案：先通过MWPM将症候编码为边权，再用为最大化期望效用训练的卷积神经网络（CNN）进行细化。

Result: 该两阶段解码器以远低于最优译码器的计算成本实现接近最优的性能。仅第一阶段（使匹配知晓规范选择，成本与裸MWPM相当）在多达N=256×256的规模下即可闭合裸MWPM与最优译码阈值之间87%的差距。

Conclusion: MUD作为MWPM和MLD的一般化推广，建立了一个通用框架，可通过重新定义效用函数来针对具体实验的操作需求进行量身定制，为测量基GHZ态制备提供了最优译码策略。

Abstract: The meticulous preparation of macroscopic Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) states provides a foundational resource for quantum technologies such as metrology, cryptography, and fault-tolerant codes. While state-of-the-art measurement-based protocols offer efficient low-depth execution, their performance can be bottlenecked by conventional decoders, such as minimum weight perfect matching (MWPM) or even maximum-likelihood decoding (MLD), which optimize for $binary$ logical recovery and fail to maximize the $continuous$ long-range order characteristic of a GHZ state for two-dimensional geometries. Here we overcome this limitation by framing the decoding problem as minimum Bayesian risk inference, introducing a general paradigm that maximizes the expected ${utility}$ of the decoded state. Implementing this maximum-utility approach, we construct an algorithm that achieves the highest possible per-shot decoded quantum order and thereby establish an optimal decoding strategy for measurement-based GHZ state preparation. To improve its computational efficiency, we design a scalable two-stage decoder, which first encodes the syndromes into the edge weights of MWPM and then refines the result with a convolutional neural network trained to maximize the expected utility, at a fraction of the cost of the optimal decoder. Remarkably, we find that the first stage alone$\unicode{x2014}$which makes the matching aware of the gauge choice at no cost beyond bare MWPM$\unicode{x2014}$already performs near-optimally up to the largest sizes we study, $N=256\times256$, closing up to $87\%$ of the gap between the bare-MWPM and optimal decoding thresholds. Generalizing MWPM and MLD, the maximum-utility decoder (MUD) establishes a versatile framework that can be explicitly tailored to the operational demands of specific experiments by redefining the utility function.

</details>


### [152] [Realization of a Quantum Topological Photon Pump](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00162)
*Qianao Yue,Bernardo Barrera,Martin Ritter,David M. Long,David A. Lane,Anushya Chandran,Alicia J. Kollár*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在实验上观测到拓扑光子泵（topological photon pump），利用与微波腔耦合的transmon量子比特，在量子区域中从真空态泵浦至约7个光子，并产生可验证的非经典腔态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑泵（topological pump）以量化速率传输电荷或能量，其速率仅由能带拓扑决定，与控制场细节无关。基于该原理的光子泵有望在存在控制缺陷的情况下稳健地制备量子腔中的非经典态，但此前尚未被直接观测到，因此实验验证成为关键目标。

Method: 实验采用一个transmon量子比特与微波腔耦合的系统，通过拓扑泵浦机制在量子区域中驱动腔场，从真空态逐步泵浦至约7个光子，并对泵浦产生的腔态进行非经典性验证。

Result: 实验在量子区域中成功实现了拓扑光子泵浦，腔内光子数从真空态增加至约7个，且在前几个周期中产生了可明确验证的非经典腔态（non-classical cavity states）。

Conclusion: 该实验首次直接观测了拓扑光子泵，证明其在存在控制不完美的情况下仍能稳健地制备非经典电磁态，为利用拓扑原理实现量子态制备提供了实验基础。

Abstract: Topological pumps transfer charge or energy at quantized rates determined solely by band topology, independent of the details of the control fields. Photon pumps operating on this principle could enable the robust preparation of non-classical states in a quantum cavity, even in the presence of control imperfections, but have not been directly observed. We present the first experimental observation of a topological photon pump, using a transmon qubit coupled to a microwave cavity. The pump operates in the quantum regime, pumping up from the vacuum state up to $\approx 7$ photons, and produces demonstrably non-classical cavity states for the first few cycles.

</details>


### [153] [Phase transitions in quantum-circuit compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00189)
*Andrea De Girolamo,Davide Rattacaso,Simone Notarnicola,Ilaria Siloi,Simone Montangero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子电路编译（quantum-circuit compilation）与多体自旋系统热力学之间的类比：电路保真度对应能量函数，低温态对应编译后的电路。在存在并行门串扰（crosstalk）的情况下，系统从无序相过渡到反铁磁砖墙相（antiferromagnetic brick-wall phase），符合Ising普适类（universality class）；更强的串扰下出现Z₃有序相，暗示更串行化的编译电路与Z_n有序相相关。去除酉等价性（unitary-equivalence）约束后这些相消失，表明等价性约束是涌现临界性（emergent criticality）的根源，而电路的Kolmogorov复杂度（Kolmogorov complexity）促进有序相的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路编译旨在约束条件下寻找优化电路，但该优化问题的计算复杂度来源尚不清晰。作者希望揭示酉等价性约束如何导致编译过程的固有复杂性，并通过热力学类比获得新的物理洞察。

Method: 将电路编译过程映射为多体自旋系统的热力学问题：以电路不保真度（infidelity）作为能量函数，低温态对应编译后的电路。在存在门间串扰的条件下，通过统计力学分析识别相变和有序相。

Result: 发现从无序相到反铁磁砖墙相的相变，属于Ising普适类；更强串扰下出现Z₃有序相，推论出Z_n有序相的涌现。去除酉等价性约束后有序相消失，且Kolmogorov复杂度越高越有利于有序相的形成。

Conclusion: 酉等价性约束是量子电路编译中涌现临界性和计算复杂性的根本来源，这一发现在更广泛的等价性约束优化（equivalence-constrained optimization）问题中具有普适意义。

Abstract: Quantum-circuit compilation aims at finding an optimized realization of a target circuit under given constraints, e.g., the minimization of hardware-induced errors and the unitary-equivalence of the circuit. We connect the compilation process with the thermodynamics of a many-body spin system: circuit infidelity plays the role of the energy function and low-temperature states correspond to compiled circuits. In the paradigmatic case where crosstalk between parallel gates is present, we find a phase transition between a disordered phase and an antiferromagnetic brick-wall phase, compatible with the Ising universality class. At larger crosstalk, we observe a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-ordered regime, suggesting that increasingly serial compiled circuits are associated with emergent $\mathbb{Z}_n$-ordered phases. When the unitary-equivalence constraint is removed, these phases disappear, showing that the equivalence between circuits underlies the emergent criticality and constitutes a source of complexity in quantum-circuit compilation and, more generally, in equivalence-constrained optimization. Finally, we observe that the Kolmogorov complexity of the circuit enhances the emergence of ordered phases.

</details>


### [154] [Marked vertex search on disordered graphs with Rosenzweig-Porter phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00224)
*Sabyasachi Chakraborty,Tilen Cadez,Sonjoy Majumder,Rohit Kishan Ray*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了无序随机图上的量子标记顶点搜索问题，通过在 Erdős-Rényi 图上实现 Rosenzweig-Porter 随机矩阵模型构建双重随机系统，发现搜索性能直接跟随底层量子相，且局域化相反而比遍历相具有更高的成功概率，表明无序可作为量子搜索协议中可调参数加以利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子标记顶点搜索算法已被证明优于经典算法，但其在无序存在条件下的行为尚未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 在 Erdős-Rényi (ER) 随机图上实现 Rosenzweig-Porter (RP) 模型，该随机矩阵系综具有可调的遍历相、非遍历扩展相和局域化相，从而构建双重随机系统；图中 ER 连通性决定哪些相互作用存在，而 RP 无序控制其强度和"在位势"（on-site potential），形成研究无序网络上量子动力学的双参数框架。基于此框架研究连续时间量子行走（continuous-time quantum walk）的标记顶点搜索问题，并推导了有限尺寸局域化边界的解析估计。

Result: RP 系综的 Wigner-Dyson 到 Poisson 谱交叉特征在从稀疏到稠密的图约束下仍然存在；有限尺寸局域化边界随图边概率 p 系统性地移动，与共振-杂化（resonant-hybridization）论证一致。在搜索问题中，搜索性能直接追踪底层量子相；反直觉地，遍历相尽管支持快速传输却产生较低的成功概率，而局域化相虽需显著更长的搜索时间却能获得较高的成功概率。

Conclusion: 研究建立了图上随机矩阵无序与连续时间量子行走搜索性能之间的直接定量联系，并表明无序而非仅仅是障碍，可以被利用为量子搜索协议中的可调参数。

Abstract: Quantum marked vertex search algorithms are known to outperform their classical counterparts, yet their behavior in the presence of disorder remains largely unexplored. Here, we address this gap by studying marked vertex search on disordered random graphs. To introduce disorder, we implement the Rosenzweig-Porter (RP) model, a random matrix ensemble with tunable ergodic, non-ergodic extended, and localized phases, on Erdős-Rényi (ER) graphs. This produces a doubly random system where ER graph connectivity randomizes which interactions exist, while RP disorder controls their strength and `on-site' potentials, providing a two-parameter framework to study quantum dynamics on disordered networks. First, we show that the characteristic Wigner-Dyson-to-Poisson spectral crossover of the RP ensemble survives under graph constraints across the sparse-to-dense range, and we derive an analytical estimate for the finite-size localization boundary that shifts systematically with the graph edge probability $p$, consistent with a resonant-hybridization argument. Thereafter, using this disordered graph ensemble, we study the marked vertex search problem and find that search performance tracks the underlying quantum phase directly. Counterintuitively, the ergodic phase, despite supporting fast transport, yields lower success probability than the localized phase, which achieves high success probability at the cost of significantly longer search times. These results establish a direct and quantitative link between random matrix disorder on graphs and the performance of continuous-time quantum walk search, and suggest that disorder, rather than being merely an obstacle, can be exploited as a tunable parameter in quantum search protocols.

</details>


### [155] [Dissipation in Periodically Driven Quantum Systems: Partial Secularization and Thermodynamic Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00225)
*Luísa T. Tude,Carlos Ortega-Taberner,Roberta Zambrini,Gonzalo Manzano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出周期性驱动的开放量子系统（periodically driven open quantum systems）在采用完全久期近似（full secular approximation）的Floquet-Born-Markov主方程（master equation）下，可能对稳态能量流给出非物理的预测。作者提出一种粗粒化（coarse-grained）主方程表述，在保持动力学完全正定（completely positive）的同时，产生一致的能量流，并在驱动二能级系统和三能级maser等范例中通过与精确非马尔可夫模拟的比较验证了该方法的有效性与物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动的开放量子系统在量子热力学和量子控制中至关重要，但其常用的Floquet-Born-Markov主方程依赖完全久期近似，该近似的热力学后果往往被忽视，可能导致稳态能量流的非物理预测。作者希望找到一种既能避免这些伪影，又保持主方程结构（GKSL形式）一致的实用修正方法。

Method: 提出一种基于粗粒化时间尺度的主方程表述，在时间粗粒化窗口内对Floquet-Born-Markov方程进行重整化，从而消除完全久期近似带来的能量流非物理发散。该粗粒化时间具有明确的物理含义，它定义了马尔可夫主方程能够描述周期性驱动系统演化的最小时间分辨率。通过与精确非马尔可夫模拟的比较，在驱动二能级系统和三能级maser两个范例中验证了方法的正确性。

Result: 完全久期近似可导致周期性驱动耗散系统的稳态能量流出现非物理行为，而粗粒化表述能够修正这一问题，同时保持动力学的完全正定性并给出一致的能量流。在范例系统中，粗粒化方法的预测与精确非马尔可夫模拟结果高度吻合，验证了其有效性与适用范围。

Conclusion: 该工作为在周期性驱动-耗散系统中正确应用久期近似提供了一个实用框架，并为评估GKSL形式主方程的准确性提供了判据。粗粒化时间赋予了马尔可夫描述明确的物理边界，有助于在实际应用中选择合适的主方程近似层级。

Abstract: Periodically driven open quantum systems are central to quantum thermodynamics and quantum control. These systems are typically described using Floquet-Born-Markov master equations, derived with the use of a full secular approximation, and whose thermodynamic implications are often overlooked. In this context, we show that such a strong secular approximation may lead to unphysical predictions for steady state energy currents. We then demonstrate that a coarse-grained formulation of the master equation can regularize these issues while yielding completely positive dynamics and consistent energy currents. The coarse-graining time has a clear physical interpretation, as it defines the temporal resolution at which a Markovian master equation can describe the evolution of the periodically driven system. We show the consistency and validity of our approach by comparing to an exact non-Markovian simulation in paradigmatic examples: a driven two-level system and a three-level maser coupled to hot and cold thermal reservoirs. Our work provides a practical framework for correctly applying the secular approximation in periodically driven-dissipative systems and for assessing the accuracy of master equations of the GKSL form.

</details>


### [156] [Work Statistics of Autonomous Quantum Energy Pumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00638)
*Yang Peng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种针对自主量子能量泵（autonomous quantum energy pump）的终端分辨（terminal-resolved）功统计理论。通过将供能与受能系统建模为显式量子终端，能量交换直接由哈密顿量变化定义；理想钟模型下可实现泵希尔伯特空间上的精确表示，恢复周期性及准周期性驱动系统的相位导数电流。该框架还处理了初始泵–终端关联、周期性泵的Floquet功统计、噪声匹配现象，并引入了正功方差间隙以量化泵–终端耦合的涨落效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主量子能量泵在无外部驱动的情况下实现能量的定向输运，但传统理论仅关注泵本身的功统计，缺乏对能量供给与接收终端（terminals）的显式描述，无法准确刻画能量交换机制及其涨落特性。

Method: 本文将供能终端和受能终端建模为显式量子系统，从各自的哈密顿量变化（Hamiltonian change）直接定义交换能量的可观测量。对于理想钟（ideal clock）模型，全空间可观测量可在泵希尔伯特空间上精确表示，并与传统相位导数（phase-derivative）电流形式一致。理论还推广至任意初始泵–终端关联、周期性泵的Floquet本征态功统计，以及超越理想钟极限的物理终端情形。

Result: 研究的核心结果包括：(1) 实现输运功与泵内部积累能量的区分；(2) 在不确定驱动相位下识别关联增强定向能量输运的条件；(3) 推导周期性泵在Floquet本征态中的有限周期功统计，并将其涨落与Floquet量子几何（quantum geometry）关联；(4) 在可解析求解的双终端量子比特（qubit）系统中发现噪声匹配（noise matching）现象；(5) 对物理终端引入正功方差间隙（positive work-variance gap），并以相干腔基准验证了平均电流的一致性并不保证功统计的准确性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的终端分辨理论为自主量子能量泵的功统计提供了统一框架，揭示了终端涨落对功统计的关键影响，并指出仅依赖平均电流的验证方式在量子输运统计层面是不够充分的。

Abstract: We develop a terminal-resolved theory of work statistics for autonomous quantum energy pumps. By modeling the systems that supply and receive energy as explicit quantum terminals, the energy exchanged with each terminal is defined directly from its Hamiltonian change. When the terminals are modeled by ideal clocks, these full-space observables admit exact representations on the pump Hilbert space and recover the conventional phase-derivative currents of periodically and quasiperiodically driven systems. The framework resolves transported work from energy accumulated in the pump. It also incorporates arbitrary initial pump--terminal correlations and identifies when correlations can enhance directional energy transfer under uncertain driving phases. For periodic pumps, we derive finite-cycle work statistics in Floquet eigenstates, relate their fluctuations to Floquet quantum geometry, and show that the long-time terminal currents become mutually compatible. An exactly solvable two-terminal qubit exhibits noise matching, in which transport becomes sharp through cancellation of common-mode terminal fluctuations even though the individual terminal energies remain noisy. Finally, for physical terminals beyond the ideal-clock limit, we introduce a positive work-variance gap that quantifies the fluctuations missed by a pump-only description. A coherent-cavity benchmark shows systematic convergence toward the ideal-clock regime with increasing occupation while demonstrating that agreement of the mean current alone does not guarantee accurate work statistics.

</details>


### [157] [On Identification of Heat and Work in Quantum Many-Body Systems with Local Operations and Classical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00993)
*Hiroaki Matsueda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过量子能量传输（quantum energy teleportation, QET）协议，基于局部操作与经典通信（LOCC）的优化条件，为量子热力学中热（heat）与功（work）的判别提供了一种无歧义的方案：优化的QET可提取能量被归为功（daemonic ergotropy），而未被挤出的不可控能量归为热。作者推导了两种广义Clausius不等式，并通过一维Kitaev-like模型验证其观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子热力学中，热与功通常分别归因于时间演化过程中密度矩阵与哈密顿量的变化，但近期的研究表明这种判别存在歧义；同时，涉及量子测量的量子热力学仍处于发展阶段。作者试图基于QET协议和LOCC优化条件为热与功提供清晰且无歧义的定义。

Method: 作者考虑一个量子多体系统，通过局部操作与经典通信（LOCC）按照量子能量传输（QET）协议提取能量。核心方法是利用LOCC的优化条件来判定能量特征：当LOCC被优化时，QET可提取能量成为daemonic ergotropy，归为功；当LOCC未优化时，未被幺正操作挤出的能量被视为不可控能量，经过对多体相互作用的细致处理后归为热。此外，作者推导了两种广义Clausius不等式，并通过一维Kitaev-like模型进行分析。

Result: 作者确立了基于LOCC优化条件的功与热判别框架，表明热项包含因远端参与者之间通信而产生的非局域关联（nonlocal correlation），这种关联无法被子系统参与者直接观测，与传统热作为不可控能量的视角一致。作者推导了两种广义Clausius不等式并讨论了不等式方向，同时在一维Kitaev-like模型中检验了该视角并讨论了有效温度（effective temperature）的物理意义。

Conclusion: 基于QET协议与LOCC优化条件的框架为量子热力学中热与功的判定提供了无歧义的方案，其热概念与非局域关联及不可控能量密切相关，符合传统热力学视角。广义Clausius不等式和一维Kitaev-like模型的检验支持该观点的合理性。

Abstract: How we identify heat and work is a fundamental question in modern quantum thermodynamics. Usually, heat and work are attributed to changes in the density matrix and the Hamiltonian, respectively, during time-evolution processes in quantum systems. Recently, it has been recognized that this identification is ambiguous. Furthermore, quantum thermodynamics involving quantum measurement is still under development. Motivated by these on-going works, we consider a quantum many-body system from which we extract energy by local operations and classical communication (LOCC) according to the quantum energy teleportation (QET) protocol. The central idea to define heat and work unambiguously is based on a sharp insight into the optimization condition of LOCC. When LOCC is optimized, the extractable energy by QET becomes a daemonic ergotropy; thus, it can be attributed as work. On the other hand, when LOCC is not optimized, we have not squeezed out all the energy with the unitary operation. It means there is uncontrollable energy left in the system. The uncontrollable energy can be attributed as heat after careful treatment of many-body interactions. The heat term consists of nonlocal correlation due to communication between remote participants, and the correlation cannot be directly observed for the participant in the subsystem. Thus, this feature is consistent with the traditional perspective of heat as an uncontrollable energy. To deeply understand the nature of heat, we derive two types of generalized Clausius inequality in our effective quantum thermodynamics, and discuss the direction of the inequality. To justify our perspective, we examine a one-dimensional Kitaev-like model and discuss the physical meaning of effective temperature in our thermodynamics.

</details>


### [158] [Surface adsorbates suppress low-frequency noise for shallow nitrogen-vacancy centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01478)
*Zhiyang Yuan,David A. Fehr,Kalliope Zervas,Sorawis Sangtawesin,Lila V. H. Rodgers,Patryk Gumann,Michael E. Flatte,Nathalie P. de Leon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过在高真空（ultrahigh vacuum, UHV）环境下表征浅层金刚石氮-空位（nitrogen-vacancy, NV）色心，发现去除表面吸附物反而使Hahn回波相干时间T2降低约4倍，揭示了表面吸附物对噪声环境复杂的、此前未被充分认识的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层NV色心是前景广阔的纳米级量子传感器，但其相干性受表面诱导噪声的强烈限制，而表面吸附物被普遍认为是退相干的主要来源之一。作者希望通过直接对比有无吸附物的环境来检验这一假设。

Method: 在UHV条件下（使金刚石表面保持无吸附物）表征浅层单NV色心，并与环境大气条件下的行为进行对比；结合单量子（single-quantum, SQ）和双量子（double-quantum, DQ）基下的Hahn回波测量来分离不同噪声源的贡献，同时进行T1测量以探测高频噪声。

Result: 观察到UHV条件下Hahn回波相干时间T2约降低4倍，SQ和DQ测量显示电噪声和磁噪声在UHV下均增强；而T1测量表明DQ T1在UHV下增大，指示~100 MHz频率范围内的电场噪声被抑制。

Conclusion: 表面吸附物的去除导致噪声谱出现频率依赖的改性，不同频率区域源自不同的微观机制：低频噪声与UHV下增加的表面电荷一致，而该表面电荷可在环境条件下被吸附物补偿。这一发现凸显了表面吸附物在塑造浅层NV色心噪声环境中的复杂作用，对纳米级量子传感具有重要意义。

Abstract: Shallow nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond are promising nanoscale quantum sensors, yet their coherence is strongly limited by surface-induced noise. Surface adsorbates are widely believed to be a major source of decoherence. Here, we test this assumption by characterizing shallow single NV centers under ultrahigh vacuum (UHV) conditions, where the diamond surface is kept free of adsorbates, and comparing their behavior to ambient conditions. Surprisingly, we observe a ~4x reduction in the Hahn echo coherence time T2 in UHV. By combining Hahn echo measurements in the single-quantum (SQ) and double-quantum (DQ) bases, we separate contributions from different noise sources and find that both electric and magnetic noise are enhanced in UHV. In contrast, T1 measurements reveal an increased DQ T1 in UHV, indicating suppressed electric field noise in the ~100 MHz frequency regime. These results point to a modification of the surface noise spectrum upon adsorbate removal, with different frequency regimes arising from distinct microscopic mechanisms. Specifically, we find that the low frequency noise is consistent with increased surface charge in UHV that can be compensated by surface adsorbates in ambient conditions. Our findings highlight a complex and previously underappreciated role of surface adsorbates in shaping the noise environment of shallow NV centers, with important implications for nanoscale quantum sensing.

</details>


### [159] [Validation and calibration of quantum hardware through the many-body quantum Mpemba effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01788)
*Francesco Campaioli,Marco Avesani,Oren Raz,Roderich Moessner,Gianluca Teza*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种利用工程化多体弛豫（engineered many-body relaxation）来验证和校准量子硬件的方案，并在两个独立开发的中性原子处理器上首次实现了开放系统中的多体量子美普巴效应（many-body quantum Mpemba effect），通过快慢两条弛豫路径分别访问不可达的混合态物理和放大制备与控制中的隐藏缺陷，从而实现对处理器动态可靠性的直接基准测试，并为硬件开发者提供可执行的校准校正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程量子处理器作为多体模拟器的可靠性缺乏系统性的验证和校准手段，传统的基准测试方法难以揭示器件在真实动态演化中的操作窗口与误差来源，因此需要一种能够直接利用目标动力学本身来评判硬件的可迁移验证工具。

Method: 文章利用初态工程（initial-state engineering）在开放系统中构造两条弛豫路径：快速路径在硬件噪声掩盖目标动力学之前开放对不可达混合态物理的访问，而慢速路径则放大制备与控制过程中的隐藏缺陷；结合计算基（computational basis）测量，从互补响应对中分离控制误差，并驱动一个自适应的、时间分辨的校正方案。

Result: 该方法在两个独立开发的中性原子处理器上首次实现了开放系统中的多体量子美普巴效应，直接且独立地基准测试了每个处理器的动态可靠性，揭示了其作为多体模拟器的实际操作窗口，并成功提供了对硬件开发者直接可操作的校准校正。

Conclusion: 多体弛豫被确立为一种可迁移的（transferable）量子处理器验证与校准工具，能够以处理器自身旨在复现的动力学为评判标准，提升对目标动力学的忠实复现能力。

Abstract: We introduce a validation process that harnesses engineered many-body relaxation to control and calibrate quantum hardware. On two independently developed neutral-atom processors, we realize the many-body quantum Mpemba effect in an open system for the first time. Initial-state engineering creates fast and slow relaxation pathways: the fast pathway opens access to unreachable mixed-state physics before hardware noise obscures the target dynamics, whereas the slow pathway amplifies hidden imperfections in preparation and control. Computational-basis measurements directly and independently benchmark dynamical reliability, revealing each processor's actual operating window as a many-body simulator. The processors are thus judged by the very dynamics they are built to reproduce. Complementary responses disentangle control errors and drive an adaptive, time-resolved scheme that supplies hardware developers directly actionable corrections, enhancing faithful reproduction of the target dynamics. These results establish many-body relaxation as a transferable validation and calibration tool for programmable quantum processors.

</details>


### [160] [Characterizing the nitrogen-vacancy center singlet transition and its phonon sideband for absorption-based room-temperature magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02060)
*Tobias Probst,Florian Schall,Sebastian Heuft,Janina J. Schindler,Lukas Lindner,Sven Mägdefessel,Rüdiger Quay,Philipp Reineck,Alexander M. Zaitsev,Takeshi Ohshima,Matthias Weidemüller,Jan Jeske*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于金刚石中氮空位（NV）色心的吸收型磁测量技术，通过腔增强光谱方法在680-1050 nm波段测量了NV⁻单重态跃迁的吸收和光探测磁共振（ODMR）对比度，并利用微波诱导信号变化的新方法测定了单重态跃迁在室温下的宽带吸收截面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于NV⁻三重态荧光的磁测量方法在对比度和灵敏度上存在局限；吸收型磁测量技术利用腔增强吸收长度可提升性能，但需要更精确地理解NV⁻单重态跃迁在宽谱范围内的吸收特性和ODMR对比度分布。

Method: 采用腔增强光谱学（cavity-enhanced spectroscopy）方法，在室温下对680-1050 nm光谱范围测量泵浦激光和微波诱导的腔信号变化；通过归一化消除腔增强效应，获得准单程吸收值和ODMR对比度；此外，开发了一种利用微波诱导信号变化测量宽带单重态吸收截面的新方法，该方法不受其他缺陷引起的泵浦激光诱导信号变化干扰。

Result: 最高ODMR对比度出现在1042 nm处，但在声子边带（phonon sideband）的多个峰位置也有高对比度；腔增强ODMR对比度可达50-80%；测得1042 nm处吸收截面为σ*= (0.89±0.14)×10⁻²¹ m² 或 σ▲= (2.9±0.5)×10⁻²¹ m²，取决于采用的532 nm NV⁻吸收截面值。

Conclusion: 吸收型磁测量方法在宽谱范围内具有多处高对比度工作点，其中1042 nm处对比度最高；新提出的微波诱导方法能够可靠地定量室温下NV⁻单重态跃迁及其声子边带的吸收强度，为优化基于吸收的NV色心磁测量技术提供了关键数据支持。

Abstract: Magnetometry with nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond has shown great promise in recent years. In particular, absorption-based magnetometry techniques, employing a cavity to enhance the absorption length, can improve the contrast and sensitivity compared to conventional techniques based on reading out the NV$^-$ triplet fluorescence. The absorption techniques rely on magnetic-field-dependent absorption at the NV$^-$ singlet zero phonon line at 1042$\,$nm and its phonon sideband. In a cavity-enhanced spectroscopy approach, we study pump-laser- and microwave-induced cavity signal changes at room temperature over a spectral range of 680-1050$\,$nm. Through normalization, we eliminate the cavity-enhancement effect and provide quasi-single-pass values for the absorption and optically detected magnetic resonance (ODMR) contrast. The highest contrast is found at 1042$\,$nm, but multiple points of high contrast are found at the peaks of the phonon sideband. Additionally, cavity-enhanced ODMR contrasts in the range of 50-80$\,\%$ are presented. We further measure the broadband singlet absorption cross section at room temperature with a novel method through microwave-induced signal changes. This method is insensitive to pump-laser-induced signal changes by other defects and quantifies the room-temperature absorption strength of the singlet transition and its entire phonon sideband. We determine the absorption cross section at 1042$\,$nm to be $σ^{\,\bigstar}_{1042}=(0.89\pm0.14)\cdot 10^{-21}\,\text{m}^2$ or $σ^{\,\blacktriangle}_{1042}=(2.9\pm0.5)\cdot 10^{-21}\,\text{m}^2$. depending on the employed 532$\,$nm NV$^-$ absorption cross section.

</details>


### [161] [Adaptive Spectroscopy of Fast Two-Level-System Dynamics in Superconducting Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02086)
*Fabrizio Berritta,David Pahl,Lukas Pahl,William P. Banner,Gabriel Cutter,Jan A. Krzywda,Spencer Weeden,Shravan Patel,Paul Buttles,Stanislav Eilhart,Michael Gingras,Bethany M. Niedzielski,Robert McDermott,Mollie E. Schwartz,Kyle Serniak,Max Hays,Jeffrey A. Grover,William D. Oliver*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过基于FPGA的自适应光谱技术，在亚秒时间分辨率下观测到超导量子处理器中寄生两级系统（two-level system, TLS）缺陷的快速电报切换与光谱扩散现象，其时间尺度比传统非自适应光谱观测快了约300倍，并揭示了此类TLS动力学与门级误差之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寄生两级系统缺陷是超导量子处理器中能量弛豫与时间不稳定性的主要来源，但传统非自适应光谱测量需要数小时，无法捕捉快速变化的TLS动力学。因此需要开发能够在亚秒时间尺度上解析TLS快速动力学的方法。

Method: 作者利用基于FPGA（field-programmable gate array）的控制器，在磁通可调超导量子比特上实现亚秒级自适应光谱测量，结合频谱与时间分辨的弛豫测量，并通过随机基准测试（randomized benchmarking）将TLS诱导的涨落与门级误差相关联。

Result: 观测到TLS的电报式切换（telegraphic switching）特征时间约为数秒，光谱扩散系数D ≈ 0.9 MHz²/s，其时间尺度比传统方法快约300倍。类似缺陷动力学在不同实验室独立制备的多个量子比特上均被观测到，表明该现象具有普遍性。

Conclusion: 该研究打开了频率分辨TLS动力学的此前不可及的新领域，重新定义了与TLS感知的诊断与校准相关的时间尺度，对超导量子处理器的稳定性表征与优化具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Parasitic two-level-system (TLS) defects are a major source of energy relaxation and temporal instability in superconducting quantum processors. Our sub-second adaptive spectroscopy reveals telegraphic switching of TLSs with a characteristic timescale of a few seconds and spectral diffusion with diffusivity $D \approx 0.9~\mathrm{MHz}^2/\mathrm{s}$. These timescales are about $3 \times 10^2$ times faster than what is observed in conventional nonadaptive spectroscopy, which typically requires hours of measurement time. We resolve such fast dynamics on a field-programmable gate array (FPGA)-based controller that enables measurement of frequency- and time-resolved relaxations with sub-second temporal resolution in flux-tunable superconducting qubits. We observe similar defect dynamics across multiple qubits in independently fabricated devices measured in different laboratories. We correlate TLS-induced fluctuations with gate-level errors using randomized benchmarking. Our results reveal a previously inaccessible regime of frequency-resolved TLS dynamics and redefine the timescales relevant to TLS-aware characterization and calibration of superconducting quantum processors.

</details>


### [162] [Quantum computer-based simulation of Stark many-body localization in a 1D Fermi-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02245)
*Abdul Kalam,Prasenjit Deb,Akitada Sakurai,Tapan Mishra,V. S. Prasannaa,B. P. Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在IBM超导量子比特计算机上，通过对一维Fermi-Hubbard模型进行哈密顿量模拟，利用多种编译优化技术（包括自旋分辨Jordan-Wigner变换、SWAP网络和张量网络电路优化），在12量子比特关联费米子系统中实验研究了Stark多体局域化（MBL）现象，实现了两比特门数约88%和电路深度约87%的缩减，并观察到从弱倾斜场下的热化动力学到大倾斜场下强局域化行为的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域化（many-body localization, MBL）是描述孤立量子多体系统非遍历性的动力学现象，与传统热化行为截然不同，对理解量子系统的基础物理具有重要意义。在嘈杂的当前量子硬件上实现可扩展的Stark MBL数值模拟需要克服量子电路的高复杂度问题，因此亟需有效的编译优化策略。

Method: 研究采用一维Fermi-Hubbard模型描述12量子比特关联费米子系统，并通过一系列编译步骤在IBM超导量子比特量子计算机上进行哈密顿量模拟。具体编译技术包括使用自旋分辨Jordan-Wigner变换（spin-resolved Jordan-Wigner transformation）、采用SWAP网络（SWAP networks），以及在标准电路优化流水线之上集成基于张量网络的量子电路优化例程（tensor-network-based quantum circuit optimization routine）。真实时间动力学通过Trotter化量子电路模拟实现。

Result: 编译优化实现了约88%的两量子比特门数缩减和约87%的电路深度缩减。通过模拟真实时间动力学，实验展示了系统在小倾斜场（弱倾斜）下呈现热化动力学，而在大倾斜场下表现出强局域化行为，且这一转变在短演化时间内即可观察到。研究结果还与精确模拟结果进行了基准对比。

Conclusion: 本工作证明了在含噪中等规模量子（NISQ）设备上，通过组合多种编译优化技术能够有效实现Stark MBL的量子模拟，并成功复现了从热化动力学到局域化行为的转变。该系统为在量子硬件上研究多体物理现象提供了可行的实验途径。

Abstract: Many-body localization (MBL) is a dynamical phenomenon that describes the non-ergodicity of isolated quantum many-body systems. In contrast to thermalization, this phenomenon leads to a long-lived memory of initial states of local systems and slow growth of entanglement. In this work, we study Stark MBL in a 12-qubit correlated fermionic system described by the one-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard model using Hamiltonian simulation on an IBM superconducting qubit quantum computer. To enable such a computation on current-day noisy hardware, we combine a series of compilation steps, including the use of the spin-resolved Jordan-Wigner transformation, employing SWAP networks, and integrating a tensor-network-based quantum circuit optimization routine on top of a standard circuit optimization pipeline. As a result, there is approximately an 88$\%$ and 87$\%$ reduction in two-qubit gate count and circuit depth, respectively. Through such simulations of the real-time dynamics using Trotterized quantum circuits, we exhibit a crossover from thermalizing dynamics of the system at a weak tilt of the field to a strongly localized behavior at large tilt with short evolution times. We also benchmark our obtained results with respect to those from exact simulations.

</details>


### [163] [Spread complexity as a probe in generalized and long-range Aubry-Andre-Harper models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02451)
*Triyas Sapui,Tanoy Kanti Konar,Subinay Dasgupta,Aditi Sen De*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义及长程奥布里-安德烈-哈珀（Aubry-Andre-Harper, AAH）模型中量子淬火（quantum quench）的扩散复杂度（spread complexity）。研究发现，当淬火后准周期势交叉初始本征态能量对应的迁移率边时，长时间平均扩散复杂度呈现非解析行为，可精确识别迁移率边跃迁；Lanczos系数在有无迁移率边的情况下表现出定性不同的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散复杂度作为量子混沌和多体物理中衡量态空间扩展的新工具，其在具有迁移率边（mobility edge）的准周期体系中的行为尚未被系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白并探索其作为迁移率边跃迁探测器的可行性。

Method: 研究涵盖含无迁移率边和含迁移率边的广义AAH模型以及长程（long-range, LR）跳跃模型，具体通过计算长时间平均扩散复杂度、局域态密度扩展（spreading of local density of states）以及解析推导矩（moments）和Lanczos系数来表征动力学行为。

Result: 广义AAH模型中，扩散复杂度在能量依赖迁移率边处呈现非解析行为，可精确识别迁移率边跃迁；Lanczos系数在存在迁移率边时呈现"初始平台-随Krylov基指标衰减"的特征形态，而传统无迁移率边的AAH模型则近似常数。在LR跳跃中，从局域相到扩展相的淬火使系数随Krylov基指标衰减，反方向淬火则与AAH结果一致。

Conclusion: 扩散复杂度与相应的Lanczos系数能够可靠地标识迁移率边跃迁，且其定性特征可区分有无迁移率边的准周期体系，为识别量子淬火中的局域化-退局域化相变提供了新的诊断工具。

Abstract: We investigate the spread complexity of quantum quenches in generalized and long-range Aubry-Andre-Harper (AAH) models, encompassing regimes with and without mobility edges. In particular, in the generalized AAH models supporting energy-dependent mobility edges, we demonstrate that the long-time averaged spread complexity exhibits nonanalytic behavior when the post-quench quasiperiodic potential crosses the mobility edge associated with the energy of the initial eigenstate, thereby accurately identifying the mobility-edge transition. Such a behavior is supported by the spreading of local density of states. We further derive analytical expressions for the moments and the corresponding Lanczos coefficients for quenches between the limits of vanishing and strong quasiperiodic potentials. The Lanczos coefficients display qualitatively distinct behavior depending on the presence of mobility edges - they exhibit an initial plateau followed by a decay with the Krylov basis index, in contrast to the nearly constant behavior of the conventional AAH model without mobility edges. For LR hopping, the coefficients decay with the Krylov basis index for quenches from the localized to the extended phase, while they coincide with the AAH results for quenches in the opposite direction.

</details>


### [164] [Thermalization of open quantum systems with pseudomodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02517)
*R. Kevin Kessing,Thibaut Lacroix,Susana F. Huelga,Martin B. Plenio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了赝模式（pseudomode）方法中体系是否满足细致平衡（detailed balance）进而热化到吉布斯态（Gibbs state）的条件，并探讨了通过参数选择迫使其仅在有限频率范围内满足“弱”细致平衡的方案，以及结合厄米与非厄米赝模式以获得平坦有效温度曲线的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赝模式方法能以精确无误的方式描述与热库强耦合的量子体系，但体系通常不会热化到吉布斯态。当体系-热库耦合为微扰极限时，缺乏热化与常见热力学直觉相冲突，因此需要厘清在何种条件下赝模式模型能够满足细致平衡并实现热化。

Method: 本文系统分析了赝模式模型中细致平衡成立的条件，考察了不同参数选择如何实现仅局限于有限频率范围的“弱”细致平衡，并研究了联合使用厄米（Hermitian）与非厄米（non-Hermitian）赝模式以产生平坦有效温度（flat effective-temperature profile）的构造方案。

Result: 论文确定了赝模式模型满足细致平衡并热化到体系吉布斯态的条件，揭示了特定参数选择可迫使模型仅在小频率范围内满足“弱”细致平衡，并展示了厄米与非厄米赝模式组合可实现平坦的有效温度分布。

Conclusion: 所建立的结果与判据对于在需要热力学一致性（thermodynamic consistency）的背景下构造赝模式模型具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Pseudomode approaches allow for an exact and unapproximated description of a quantum system interacting arbitrarily strongly with a bath. In general, a system coupled to pseudomodes will not thermalize to the system's Gibbs state: This is to be expected when the system-bath coupling is non-perturbative, but conflicts with common thermodynamic intuition when the system-bath coupling is asymptotically weak. We explore under which circumstances pseudomode models satisfy detailed balance (and subsequently thermalize to the system Gibbs state) and how specific choices of parameters can force "weak" detailed balance that is restricted to a limited frequency range. A combination of Hermitian and non-Hermitian pseudomodes that yields a flat effective-temperature profile is also considered. The results and criteria established here are relevant for the construction of pseudomode models in contexts where thermodynamic consistency is required.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Triple Hodge integrals and constant Poisson brackets for rank-one Dubrovin-Zhang hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00462)
*Xavier Blot,Guido Carlet,Dimitrios Makris,Sergey Shadrin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个由Dubrovin、Liu、Yang和Zhang提出的猜想：对于秩一（rank-one）上同调场论（cohomological field theory）的Dubrovin-Zhang层级，其泊松括号（Poisson bracket）为常数的充要条件是它由满足Calabi-Yau条件的参数所定义的三重Hodge类（triple Hodge class）给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是证明Dubrovin、Liu、Yang和Zhang所提出的一个猜想，该猜想建立了积分系统（integrable systems）——具体为Dubrovin-Zhang层级——与上同调场论之间的深刻联系，并刻画了泊松括号为常数（constant Poisson bracket）这一特殊结构在代数几何中的几何来源。

Method: 作者通过严格的数学证明（proof）来验证猜想，在秩一上同调场论的框架下，系统地分析了Dubrovin-Zhang层级的泊松括号结构，并建立了其常值性与三重Hodge类参数满足Calabi-Yau条件之间的等价关系。

Result: 成功证明了猜想成立：秩一上同调场论的Dubrovin-Zhang层级的泊松括号为常数，当且仅当该层级由满足Calabi-Yau条件参数的三重Hodge类所决定。

Conclusion: 该结果从理论上完全解决了Dubrovin-Liu-Yang-Zhang猜想，揭示了上同调场论中泊松结构的常值性与Calabi-Yau几何条件之间的内在联系，为理解可积系统与代数几何的结构张量（structure tensors）提供了更深入的刻画。

Abstract: We prove a conjecture of Dubrovin, Liu, Yang, and Zhang that states that the Poisson bracket of the Dubrovin-Zhang hierarchy of a rank-one cohomological field theory is constant if and only if it is given by the triple Hodge class with the parameters satisfying the Calabi-Yau condition.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.TR'></div>

# q-fin.TR [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Exactly solvable model for the diffusive price-dynamics paradox under long-range correlated market-order flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00988)
*Yuki Sato,Shunta Fujiwara,Kiyoshi Kanazawa*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文通过将平方根价格冲击法则（square-root price-impact law）引入Lillo-Mike-Farmer（LMF）模型，构建了一个可精确求解的非线性时间序列模型，证明了即使在可预测的市价单流（market-order flow）下，价格动态在长期仍然呈现扩散性（diffusive），从而解决了金融微观结构中的扩散价格动态悖论（diffusive price-dynamics paradox）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融微观结构领域存在一个核心悖论：长期来看价格动态近似由布朗运动（Brownian motion）描述，但市价单流因长程相关性（long-range correlations）而明显可预测（predictable），且市价单通常具有正向价格冲击（price impact），这与布朗式价格动态看似矛盾。作者旨在解决这一扩散价格动态悖论。

Method: 作者基于平方根价格冲击法则推广LMF模型，构建非线性时间序列模型；该模型可数学映射到Lévy游走（Lévy-walk）框架——统计物理中一类可精确求解的非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）随机过程。

Result: 作者证明了在平方根法则下，即使存在可预测的市价单流，价格动态在长期仍呈现扩散性；由此解决了扩散价格动态悖论。

Conclusion: 该工作凸显了平方根法则在理解有效市场假说（Efficient Market Hypothesis）微观结构基础方面的关键实践重要性。

Abstract: We develop an exactly solvable nonlinear time-series model by incorporating the square-root price-impact law into the Lillo--Mike--Farmer (LMF) model to resolve the diffusive price-dynamics paradox under predictable market-order flow. In financial market microstructure, it is well established that the price dynamics are approximately described by Brownian motion at long times. However, it is also well-known that market-order flow is clearly predictable due to long-range correlations, as mathematically formulated by the LMF model. Since market orders have a positive price impact in general, predictable market-order flow seems to contradict Brownian price dynamics. In this work, we resolve this diffusive price-dynamics paradox by developing nonlinear time-series models that generalize the LMF model based on the square-root price-impact law. Our time-series models can be mathematically mapped onto the Lévy-walk framework---an exactly solvable class of non-Markovian stochastic processes developed in statistical physics. We prove that the price dynamics are diffusive at long times under the square-root law even under predictable market-order flow. Our work highlights the crucial practical importance of the square-root law in understanding the microstructural foundation of the Efficient Market Hypothesis.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Counterexamples to the Landis conjecture in dimensions three and higher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00802)
*Rupert L. Frank,Paata Ivanisvili*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在所有三维及更高维度中构建了一个非零函数，满足具有有界实值势能的定态薛定谔方程（stationary Schrödinger equation），且该函数以 $\exp(-c|x|^{4/3})$ 的速率衰减，从而为三维及以上维度的 Landis 猜想（Landis conjecture）提供了反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landis 猜想是关于薛定谔方程解衰减速率的一个长期未解决的问题，其断言在具有有界势能的方程中，非零解不能以超指数速率衰减。该猜想在二维及更高维度的有效性尚不明确，作者旨在构造显式反例以否定该猜想在高维情形下的成立。

Method: 作者在三维及所有更高维度中显式构造了一个非零函数，使其满足势能有界且实值的薛定谔方程，同时验证该函数具备 $\exp(-c|x|^{4/3})$ 的指数型衰减行为。

Result: 成功构造了满足条件的非零解函数，其衰减指数为 $4/3$，该衰减速率低于 Landis 猜想所预期的超指数衰减，从而在三维及以上维度推翻了 Landis 猜想的正确性。

Conclusion: 三维及以上维度的 Landis 猜想不成立，作者提供的显式构造为其给出了明确的反例。

Abstract: In every dimension three and higher we construct a nonzero function that satisfies the stationary Schrödinger equation with bounded, real-valued potential and decays like $\exp(-c|x|^{4/3})$. This gives a counterexample to the Landis conjecture in dimension three and higher.

</details>


### [168] [A computer-assisted counterexample to the planar Pompeiu and Schiffer conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01579)
*Matthew J. Colbrook,George Stepaniants*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个有界单连通非圆形域 Ω⊂ℝ²，其边界为实解析Jordan曲线（Jordan boundary），并存在非平凡函数 u 满足 (Δ+k²)u=0（Ω内），u=1 且 ∂_ν u=0（边界上），k≈31.967。该构造同时反驳了Schiffer猜想（Schiffer's conjecture）和平面上的Pompeiu猜想（Pompeiu conjecture）。该域通过十重对称共形映射（conformal map）φ=φ(𝔻)获得，φ 接近一个明确列出的301次多项式，分析问题转化为单位圆盘上的三次算子方程，并通过后验收缩论证（a posteriori contraction）证明精确零点的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面Pompeiu问题（Pompeiu problem）自1929年提出，与其相关的Schiffer猜想是连接刚体运动积分变换、Fourier零点集与超定Neumann特征值问题的长期开放刚性（rigidity）问题，长期以来缺乏反例或证明。

Method: 将问题约化为共形映射 φ（十重对称、接近一个度数为301的显式多项式）下的单位圆盘解析问题，得到三次算子方程 F(g,p)=g+|p|²(1+Kg)=0；其中 K 是在圆盘多项式基下Laplacian的显式逆算子。通过圆盘多项式线性化系数的正性、对 K 的尖锐估计以及对无穷尾部的单调控制，在加权系数代数中建立后验收缩论证，从而证明 F 存在精确零点。

Result: 构造出满足Schiffer猜想反例条件的域 Ω：有界单连通非圆形、实解析Jordan边界，以及非平凡Neumann特征函数 u 在边界上为常数，其中 k 位于区间 (31.967007261, 31.967007293) 内。同时由Green恒等式（Green's identity）得到 ħ_{1_Ω}(kω)=0，表明 Ω 不满足Pompeiu性质（Pompeiu property），构成有界单连通Lipschitz域情形下平面Pompeiu猜想的反例。

Conclusion: 该构造同时反驳了两项长期存在的猜想：Schiffer猜想和有界单连通Lipschitz域情形下的平面Pompeiu猜想，为解决这一古老的刚性问题的方向提供了实质性突破。

Abstract: The planar Pompeiu problem, originating in 1929, and the associated Schiffer conjecture are long-standing rigidity questions linking rigid-motion integral transforms and Fourier zero sets to overdetermined Neumann eigenvalue problems. We construct a bounded simply connected noncircular domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^2$ with real-analytic Jordan boundary and a nonconstant function $u$ such that $(Δ+k^2)u=0$ in $Ω$, $u=1,\partial_νu=0$ on $\partialΩ$ for some $k\in(31.967007261,31.967007293)$. Thus $u$ is a Neumann eigenfunction which is constant on the boundary, and $Ω$ is a counterexample to Schiffer's conjecture. Green's identity also gives $\widehat{\mathbf 1_Ω}(kω)=0$ $(ω\in\mathbb S^1)$, so $Ω$ fails the Pompeiu property and is also a counterexample to the planar Pompeiu conjecture for bounded simply connected Lipschitz domains. We obtain the domain as $Ω=φ(\mathbb{D})$, where $φ$ is a ten-fold symmetric conformal map close to an explicitly listed polynomial of degree $301$. On the unit disc, the analytic problem becomes a cubic operator equation on real coefficient spaces, $F(g,p)=g+|p|^2(1+Kg)=0$, where $K$, expressed in a disk-polynomial basis, is an explicit inverse of the Laplacian on the range compatible with zero Dirichlet and Neumann traces, and $p=kφ'$. Positivity of the disk-polynomial linearisation coefficients, sharp bounds for $K$, and monotone control of the infinite tails establish an a posteriori contraction near the listed polynomial in a weighted coefficient algebra, and hence an exact zero of $F$.

</details>


### [169] [The Riemann Problem for a 3x3 Generalized Chaplygin Gas System with Variable Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01580)
*Thomas Allen,Ella Kim,Jonathan Nunes,Rita Xing,Charis Tsikkou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个带有广义Chaplygin压力（generalized Chaplygin pressure）的三乘三守恒律系统的黎曼问题（Riemann problem）。当经典解（冲击波、稀疏波和接触间断）不存在时，奇异解以狄拉克δ函数（Dirac delta）形式出现；作者验证了这些解在分布意义下满足守恒律，与Nedeljkov的阴影波（shadow-wave）构造互为补充描述，并通过Dafermos最大熵耗散原理（maximum entropy dissipation principle）讨论解的适定性，最后通过构造过压缩δ-冲击波的粘性剖面（viscous profile），证明其可作为零粘性极限实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Chaplygin气体模型在宇宙学和流体力学中具有重要应用，但其相关的三乘三守恒律系统的黎曼解结构尚未被充分研究。当经典波结构无法存在时，需要发展奇异解的理论框架和适定性判别准则，并建立其与粘性极限之间的严格联系。

Method: 作者首先对系统进行波结构分析和黎曼解分类；对奇异解在经典Dirac delta框架下验证其分布意义下的守恒律，并与Nedeljkov的阴影波构造进行对比。随后利用Dafermos最大熵耗散原理研究解的适定性，并辅以Lax-Friedrichs数值模拟验证。为构造过压缩δ-冲击波的粘性剖面，假设α∈Q∩(0,1]，结合Dafermos正则化与球面爆破（spherical blow-up）方法，在三个方向坐标系中构造约化奇异拼接（singular concatenation），并证明对小正粘性该拼接可微扰为异宿轨道（heteroclinic orbit）。

Result: 作者分类了黎曼解的结构并证明奇异解在分布意义下满足守恒律；阴影波构造与其给出了同一奇异解的两种互补描述；Dafermos最大熵耗散原理能够选择物理相关的解。对于孤立的过压缩δ-冲击波，所构造的奇异拼接在小粘性下可微扰为异宿轨道，从而将其实现为自相似Dafermos粘性剖面的零粘性极限。

Conclusion: 本文建立了奇异δ-冲击波解与Dafermos粘性剖面之间的严格对应关系，为广义Chaplygin气体模型下守恒律系统的奇异解分析提供了系统的理论框架和数值验证。

Abstract: We consider the Riemann problem for a 3x3 system of conservation laws with generalized Chaplygin pressure $p(ρ,v)=-\frac{A(v)}{ρ^α}$, $0<α\leq 1$, where the pressure depends on an additional transported variable. We analyze the system's wave structure and classify the Riemann solutions. Whenever classical solutions consisting of shocks, rarefaction waves, and contact discontinuities fail to exist, singular solutions arise. We verify that these satisfy the conservation laws in the distributional sense within the classical Dirac delta framework, and compare them with Nedeljkov's shadow-wave construction, giving two complementary descriptions of the same singular solution. We further study admissibility via the Dafermos maximum entropy dissipation principle, with several examples showing how it selects the physically relevant solution. Lax-Friedrichs simulations illustrate the Riemann wave patterns and provide a comparison with the analytical results.
  To construct viscous profiles for the isolated overcompressive $δ$-shock, we assume $α\in\mathbb{Q}\cap(0,1]$ and apply the Dafermos regularization together with a spherical blow-up. Working in three directional charts, we construct the reduced singular concatenation consisting of the left outer orbit, the middle orbit on the blown-up boundary, and the right outer orbit. We then prove that, for sufficiently small positive viscosity, this singular concatenation perturbs to a heteroclinic orbit. Consequently, the isolated overcompressive $δ$-shock is realized as the zero-viscosity limit of a family of self-similar Dafermos viscous profiles.

</details>


### [170] [Talbot effect for the periodic Benjamin--Ono equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02567)
*Xi Chen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究具有粗糙初值的周期Benjamin–Ono方程的Talbot效应，利用Gérard–Kappeler–Topalov的光滑性定理将奇异性分析归约为显式二次规范剖面，对一般有界变差数据建立了有理时间规范Hardy表示，并在Talbot许可数据子类上给出有限个对数核与单跳核之和的表示，证明有限跳分段光滑有界变差数据（含方波）属于该子类，同时在无理时间下证明了连续性并揭示了Liouville型障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对周期Benjamin–Ono方程在粗糙初值下的Talbot效应（Talbot effect）进行严格的数学分析，此前相关结构结果仅存在于数值层面，缺乏严格的解析证明。

Method: 借助Gérard–Kappeler–Topalov关于Tao规范变换（gauge transform）的光滑性定理，将奇异性分析归约到显式的二次规范剖面；对一般有界变差（bounded variation）数据建立有理时间下的规范Hardy表示，并在Talbot许可数据的子类中将其展开为有限个对数核与单跳核之和加上连续余项。

Result: 证明有限跳、分段光滑的有界变差数据均为Talbot许可数据，方波（square wave）是典型实例；在有限Diophantine型条件下证明了无理时间的连续性，同时构造了Liouville型障碍表明对应单侧规范剖面并非在所有无理时间都连续。

Conclusion: 所得结果在严格性层面与Alama Bronsard–Laurens的数值剖面定性一致，为周期Benjamin–Ono方程的Talbot效应提供了一个严格的解析结构描述。

Abstract: We study the Talbot effect for the periodic Benjamin--Ono equation with rough initial data. Using the smoothing theorem of Gérard--Kappeler--Topalov for Tao's gauge transform, we reduce the singularity analysis to an explicit quadratic gauge profile. For general bounded-variation data we obtain a rational-time gauge-Hardy representation. For a natural subclass of Talbot-admissible data, whose initial gauge profile has only finitely many edge singularities, this representation becomes a finite sum of logarithmic kernels and one-jump kernels, up to a continuous remainder. We show that finite-jump, piecewise smooth BV data are Talbot-admissible; in particular, this applies to the square wave. At irrational times, we prove continuity under a finite Diophantine type condition, and we also exhibit a Liouville obstruction showing that the corresponding one-sided gauge profile need not be continuous at all irrational times. The results give a rigorous structural result that is qualitatively consistent with the numerical profiles of Alama Bronsard--Laurens.

</details>


### [171] [An autonomous Lipschitz fast dynamo on the three-torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02586)
*Lukas Niebel*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在三维环面上的动力学感应方程（kinematic induction equation）研究中，构造了一个实值、无散、与时间无关的速度场，该速度场在足够小的磁扩散率范围内是一个快速发电机（fast dynamo）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在光滑速度场框架下是否存在快速发电机机制，特别是验证无散速度场能否在扩散率趋于零时仍保持均匀正的增长速率。

Method: 构造一个属于 W^{1,∞} 空间的速度场 u，其处处可微且除一条圆以外处处光滑，但不属于 C^1。通过分析对应感应算子的特征值及其与扩散率无关的正下界来控制指数增长。

Result: 对每个充分小的正磁扩散率，感应算子存在实部被正常数下界的特征值；相应的非零无散解具有均匀正速率的精确指数增长规律。同时该速度场的粒子流及其逆的Lipschitz常数至多线性增长，每个时刻映射的拓扑熵为零，理想感应群在算子范数下的指数增长率为零。

Conclusion: 构造的速度场展示了快速发电机现象，同时其粒子流表现出温和（线性增长）的几何复杂性，说明快速发电机的存在并不依赖于混沌粒子动力学或指数增长的李雅普诺夫行为。

Abstract: We construct a single real-valued, divergence-free, time-independent velocity field $u\in\mathrm{W}^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{T}^3;\mathbb{R}^3) $ that is a fast dynamo for the kinematic induction equation on the flat three-torus. For every sufficiently small positive magnetic diffusivity, the corresponding induction operator has an eigenvalue whose real part is bounded below by a positive constant independent of the diffusivity. For each such diffusivity, there is a non-zero real-valued, divergence-free solution of the induction equation whose $\mathrm{L}^2 $-norm obeys an exact exponential growth law with a uniformly positive rate. For the same velocity field, the particle flow and its inverse have Lipschitz constants growing at most linearly in time, every time map has zero topological entropy, and the ideal induction group has zero exponential growth rate in operator norm. The velocity is differentiable everywhere and smooth away from a single circle, but is not $\mathrm{C}^1 $.

</details>

### [172] [Active Passivation Tunes Hotspot Locations in GaN Transistors with In Situ Thermal Mechanical Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01257)
*Yicheng Wei,Sihang Liu,Zimu Jiang,jinquan Zhang,Zifeng Huang,Han Yang,Yang He,Jin Wei,Zhe Cheng*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种主动钝化（active passivation）技术，通过调控GaN晶体管中主动钝化层长度，可主动移动热点（hotspot）位置从栅极边缘至漏极侧钝化层边缘，实现热源空间分布的主动调控，并通过微拉曼热成像（micro-Raman thermography）和多物理场电-热-机械耦合模拟（multi-physics electro-thermal-mechanical simulations）加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子器件热管理研究大多聚焦于工程化散热路径，忽略了热源本身的内在行为，缺乏对热点位置进行主动调控的策略。

Method: 通过调节GaN晶体管中主动钝化层长度来改变热点位置；采用微拉曼热成像进行原位热-机械可视化（in-situ thermal-mechanical visualization），并结合多物理场电-热-机械耦合模拟捕捉温度和热应力（thermal stress）的空间分布；通过电学分析确认热点迁移由峰值电场和局部焦耳热（Joule heating）的空间偏移驱动。

Result: 热点实现了从栅极边缘至漏极侧主动钝化层边缘的空间移动，建立了清晰的一对一空间对应关系；温度和热应力分布的空间重分配被直接捕获，电学分析证实热点迁移由峰值电场与局部焦耳热的空间偏移驱动。

Conclusion: 该主动热源调控策略为功率电子（power electronics）提供了关键的设计指导原则，拓展了器件热管理从被动散热到主动热源调控的新思路。

Abstract: Efficient thermal dissipation has become critical in emerging electronic devices. However, most existing studies have primarily focused on engineering heat dissipation pathways, largely overlooking the intrinsic behavior of the heat source itself. We demonstrate an active passivation technology that proactively tunes hotspot locations in GaN transistors. By adjusting the active passivation layer length, the hotspot is shifted from the gate edge to the drain-side AP edge, establishing a clear one-to-one spatial correlation. In-situ thermal-mechanical visualization via micro-Raman thermography, combined with multi-physics electro-thermal-mechanical simulations, directly captures the spatial redistribution of both temperature and thermal stress profiles. Electrical analysis confirms that this hotspot migration is driven by the spatial shift of the peak electric field and localized Joule heating. This proactive heat-source tuning strategy provides critical design guidelines for power electronics.

</details>


### [173] [Reduced Dark Current in Cd0.9Zn0.1Te Detector Arrays by Aluminium Oxide Passivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02516)
*Sudarshan Singh,Luc Montpetit,Eloïse Rahier,Mahmoud R. M. Atalla,Sebastian Koelling,Oussama Moutanabbir*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本研究推翻了以往认为CZT半导体在150°C以上加工会导致不可逆性能退化的观点，证明在250°C下通过原子层沉积（atomic layer deposition）制备的Al2O3钝化层不仅不会损害碲锌镉（CZT）探测器性能，反而能显著改善其电学特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点普遍认为CZT半导体在150°C以上的加工温度会导致不可逆的设备退化，这限制了CZT探测器加工工艺的温度窗口。作者旨在验证高温Al2O3钝化是否真的有害，并探索其在高温下提升探测器性能的可能性。

Method: 采用原子层沉积（atomic layer deposition, ALD）技术在250°C下对像素化金属-半导体（metal-semiconductor, MS）和金属-绝缘体-半导体（metal-insulator-semiconductor, MIS）CZT探测器沉积薄层Al2O3钝化膜，并进行系统性电学表征，包括暗电流、像素间漏电流、电流-电压（I-V）特性及像素均匀性测试。

Result: 钝化后的器件在1000 V高压偏置下无可测退化；暗电流降低约5倍；像素间漏电流从约41 nA降至约2-3 nA（在-200 V时），降低近一个数量级；暗电流特性在相邻像素间高度均匀；电流-电压滞后现象完全消除，表明缺陷辅助电荷输运被抑制。此外，MS与MIS探测器钝化后暗电流无显著差异，表明Al2O3层主导了表面电学行为。

Conclusion: 250°C下优化的Al2O3钝化工艺与高性能CZT探测器加工完全兼容，为构建更低噪声、更高光谱分辨率的X射线成像系统提供了切实可行的技术路线。

Abstract: Contrary to the prevailing view that processing cadmium zinc telluride (CZT) semiconductors above 150 °C leads to irreversible device degradation, here we show that Al2O3 passivation layer deposited by atomic layer deposition at 250 °C not only preserves CZT detector performance but also substantially improves it. Pixelated metal-semiconductor (MS) and metal-insulator-semiconductor (MIS) CZT detectors were passivated with a thin atomic-layer-deposited Al2O3 film and characterized electrically. The passivated devices exhibited no measurable degradation, even under a high-bias operation of 1000 V. Instead, the dark current decreased by approximately a factor of five, while the interpixel leakage current was reduced by nearly one order of magnitude, from ~41 nA to ~2-3 nA at -200 V. Passivation also produced highly uniform dark-current characteristics across adjacent pixels and completely eliminated current-voltage hysteresis, indicating suppression of defect-assisted charge transport. Furthermore, no significant difference in dark current was observed between the MS and MIS detectors after passivation, suggesting that the Al2O3 layer dominates the surface electrical behavior. These results demonstrate that optimized Al2O3 passivation at 250 °C is fully compatible with high-performance CZT detector processing and provides a practical route toward lower-noise, higher-spectral-resolution X-ray imaging systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Analytical Charge Density Profile of Vortex Core in Weak-Coupling Superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00226)
*Chi-Ken Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文首次以初等（elementary）方式解释了超导涡旋（vortex）内电荷密度符号交替调制的来源：通过分析阶梯能隙中Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon束缚态的归一化性质，发现涡旋缠绕使密度在涡旋线上消失并携带2k_F的Friedel振荡；长波长下束缚态电荷被扩展态完全补偿，但在费米圆直径尺度补偿失效，残留下仅被4k_F²/(4k_F²+k_TF²)因子削弱的符号交替振荡，这是电荷中性（charge neutrality）和大动量转移下屏蔽低效的直接后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长久以来，自洽Bogoliubov-de Gennes计算表明求解涡旋内泊松方程可将单符号电荷耗尽转变为周期为π/k_F的符号交替调制，但这一结果缺乏简单直观的解释。作者旨在给出该结果的初等描述，使这一现象不依赖于复杂的数值计算即可被理解。

Method: 作者在阶梯状能隙中处理Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon束缚态，证明束缚态旋量（spinor）的归一化几乎与角动量无关，从而将模式求和化为闭式形式。在芯内，涡旋缠绕从完备性求和中移除一个Bessel通道，使密度在涡旋线上消失并产生波矢为2k_F的Friedel振荡；在芯外，求和给出以相干长度为衰减尺度的1/r包络及残余波纹。通过电荷中性条件分析束缚态电荷与扩展态的精确补偿关系。

Result: 推导得出束缚态电荷不积分为零，须由扩展态精确补偿。这种补偿在长波长下完全有效，但在费米圆直径尺度上失效，残存的是符号交替的2k_F调制，其幅度仅被因子4k_F²/(4k_F²+k_TF²)削弱——该因子对任何金属都介于二分之一和四分之三之间，且与屏蔽波矢k_TF有关。

Conclusion: 该振荡并非微妙的精细效应，而是电荷中性要求与大动量转移下屏蔽效率低下的必然结果：在屏蔽后的总密度中，光滑项彼此抵消，仅剩波纹项。这为理解涡旋核内的电荷结构提供了物理上简洁且普遍的机制解释。

Abstract: Self-consistent Bogoliubov-de Gennes calculations have long shown that solving the Poisson equation inside a superconducting vortex turns a one-signed charge depletion into a modulation that alternates in sign with period $π/k_F$. We give an elementary account of that result. Taking the Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon bound states in a step-like gap, we show that the normalization of the bound-state spinor is nearly independent of angular momentum, which collapses the mode sum into closed form. Inside the core the vortex winding removes one Bessel channel from a completeness sum, so the density vanishes on the vortex line and carries Friedel-like oscillations of wavevector $2k_F$; outside it the sum gives a $1/r$ envelope decaying over a coherence length, with a residual ripple. The bound-state charge does not integrate to zero, so neutrality obliges the extended states to compensate it exactly. That compensation is complete at long wavelength but fails at the diameter of the Fermi circle, and what survives is a sign-alternating $2k_F$ modulation reduced only by $4k_F^2/(4k_F^2+k_{TF}^2)$, a factor lying between one-half and three-quarters for any metal. The oscillation is therefore not a delicate effect but a consequence of neutrality and the inefficiency of screening at large momentum transfer: in the screened total the smooth terms cancel and only the ripple is left.

</details>


### [175] [Ferroelectric superconductivity in noncentrosymmetric metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00338)
*Jason G. Kattan,T. Pereg-Barnea*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究铁电金属（ferroelectric metals）中的超导电性，通过将离子极化场（ionic polarization fields）与巡游电子（itinerant electrons）在微观层面耦合的框架，证明极化涨落（polarization fluctuations）可诱导电子间的吸引相互作用，从而在特定晶格构型下实现库珀配对，使超导电性与铁电性可以共存甚至由铁电性诱导产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验已发现某些材料能同时表现出超导电性和铁电性，这颠覆了长期以来认为二者互斥或无关的猜想，因此有必要从理论上阐明这两种有序现象共存与相互作用的机制。

Method: 采用离子极化场与巡游电子耦合的形式化框架（formalism），两者均在微观层面处理；铁电序表现为均匀或空间调制的自发极化，极化涨落介导电子间相互作用，并基于简化BCS模型推导超导与铁电共存的充要条件。

Result: 极化涨落可产生吸引性相互作用，在特定晶格构型下能够导致库珀配对（Cooper pairing），其机制与非极性声子（nonpolar phonons）在传统BCS理论中的作用类似。

Conclusion: 结论表明超导电性可以与铁电性共存，甚至可由铁电性直接产生，从而为解释实验观测提供了理论框架。

Abstract: It has recently been shown in experiments that certain materials can display both superconductivity and ferroelectricity, contrary to a long-standing conjecture that these two phenomena are incompatible, or at least unrelated. In this work we study superconductivity in ferroelectric metals, using a formalism of ionic polarization fields coupled to itinerant electrons, both of which are treated at the microscopic level. The ferroelectric order manifests as a spontaneous polarization that may be uniform or spatially modulated, and fluctuations of the polarization mediate interactions between the electrons. The polarization fluctuations give rise to attractive interactions that can lead to Cooper pairing in certain lattice configurations, analogous to the nonpolar phonons in conventional BCS theory. Working with a simplified BCS model, we derive conditions under which superconductivity can coexist with and even emerge from ferroelectricity.

</details>


### [176] [{\it Ab initio} prediction of $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave superconductivity in infinite-layer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00512)
*Guang-Yu Guo,Ren-Guo Guo,Yun-Chen Liao,Yang-hao Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文基于包含电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling, EPC）、屏蔽库仑排斥和自旋涨落（spin fluctuation, SF）相互作用的密度泛函超导理论，对最佳掺杂镍酸盐 Re0.8Sr0.2NiO2 (Re = La, Pr, Nd) 的 superconducting 性质进行了全从头算（ab initio）研究。结果表明体系是双能带超导体，具有符号反转的 d_{x^2-y^2}(±)-波间隙，且超导由自旋涨落相互作用驱动，其强度比 EPC 和库仑排斥大一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限层镍酸盐（infinite-layer nickelates）作为潜在的新型非常规高临界温度（critical temperature, T_c）超导体家族而受到广泛关注，但其超导配对机制和间隙对称性等基本问题仍处于激烈争论之中，缺乏系统的理论理解。

Method: 采用用于超导体计算的密度泛函理论（density functional theory for superconductors），将电子-声子耦合、屏蔽库仑排斥和自旋涨落相互作用放在同等地位上进行从头算处理，对三种稀土元素（La, Pr, Nd）的最佳掺杂镍酸盐开展系统研究。

Result: 发现 Re0.8Sr0.2NiO2 是双能带超导体，在不同费米面（Fermi surface, FS）口袋上具有符号反转的 d_{x^2-y^2}(±)-波间隙函数。关闭自旋涨落相互作用后 T_c 降至约 0.01 K，证明超导由自旋涨落驱动。自旋涨落相互作用在布里渊区（Brillouin zone, BZ）中心附近的大准二维 FS 口袋上比 EPC 和库仑排斥强一个数量级，尽管 EPC 在 BZ 角的小三维电子 FS 口袋上占主导。节点型 d_{x^2-y^2}(±)-波间隙结构源于 BZ 角处 Lindhard 响应函数的显著峰。

Conclusion: 计算的 FS、超导临界温度、节点间隙结构和超导准粒子态密度与多数现有实验一致；对 La0.8Sr0.2NiO2 和 Pr0.8Sr0.2NiO2 扫描隧道谱（scanning tunneling spectra）等非常规超导性质的预测可立即经受实验验证。

Abstract: Infinite-layer nickelates have recently emerged as a new family of potential unconventional high critical temperature ($T_c$) superconductors. However, fundamental questions such as their superconducting (SC) pairing mechanism and gap symmetry remain under intense debate. Here we present a fully {\it ab initio} theoretical study on the SC properties of optimally doped nickelates $Re$$_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ ($Re=$ La, Pr, Nd), based on the density functional theory for superconductors calculations with electron-phonon coupling (EPC), screened Coulomb repulsion and spin fluctuation (SF) interaction treated on an equal footing. We find that $Re_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ are two-band superconductors with sign reversal $d_{x^2-y^2}(\pm)$-wave gap functions on the different Fermi surface (FS) pockets. Interestingly, when the SF interaction is turned off, $T_c$ becomes negligibly small ($\sim$0.01 K), thus demonstrating that the superconductivity in $Re_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ is driven by SF interaction. Moreover, our {\it ab initio} calculations reveal that the SF interaction is an order of magnitude stronger than both EPC and Coulomb repulsion on the large quasi-two-dimensional FS pocket around the Brillouin zone (BZ) center, thus leading to the SF-mediated pairing mechanism, although the EPC dominates on the small three-dimensional electron FS pockets at the BZ corners. The emergence of nodal $d_{x^2-y^2}(\pm)$-wave gap structure is traced to the pronounced peaks in the Lindhard response function at the BZ corners. Our calculated FS, SC critical temperature, nodal gap structure and SC quasiparticle density of states are consistent with most available experiments. Furthermore, predicted unconventional SC properties such as scanning tunneling spectra of La$_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ and Pr$_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ are ready for immediate experimental verifications.

</details>


### [177] [Evidence for superconductivity at 190 K in a pressure-overdoped cuprate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00942)
*Alexander C. Mark,Huu T. Do,David Rodriguez,D. Gonzalez Arevalo,Adam Denchfield,Eduardo H. T. Poldi,Daniel P. Phelan,N. K. Man,Rssell J. Hemley*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究通过多种测量技术，在准静水压（quasihydrostatic）和不同压缩环境下探究铜氧化物超导体 Pb0.4Bi1.6Sr2Ca2Cu3O10+δ（Bi-2223）在高达 60 GPa 压力下迈斯纳效应（Meissner effect） onset 温度（Tc）的变化规律，发现 Tc 在 60 GPa 时连续升至 190 K，超过目前铜氧化物在常压和高压下所有已报道的临界温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物超导体的临界温度（Tc）可以通过压力调节，压缩可以将空穴掺杂（hole doping）与化学掺杂解耦，从而获得远超常压条件的过掺杂（overdoped）样品。本文旨在系统研究高压环境下 Bi-2223 体系中 Tc 的压力依赖关系，探索是否存在超越已知记录的超导临界温度。

Method: 在高达 60 GPa、不同压缩环境下，采用多种测量技术探测 Tc 处迈斯纳效应的 onset，对 Bi-2223 样品进行系统的压力依赖表征。其中准静水压条件下的压缩提供了与既往研究可比的实验数据。

Result: 准静水压条件下，样品在 25 GPa 以下表现出非单调的 Tc 压力依赖，与先前报道一致；随压力进一步升高，Tc 持续上升到 60 GPa 时的 190 K。该临界温度超过目前铜氧化物在常压及高压下已报道的所有值。

Conclusion: 这些结果支持在高空穴掺杂区存在第二个超导区域（second superconducting regime）的假说，可为理解 Tc 超过既有记录的现象提供理论框架。

Abstract: It is well established that the critical temperature ($T_c$) of cuprate superconductors can be tuned by pressure. For example, compression decouples the hole doping from chemical doping allowing for overdoped samples far beyond what is possible at ambient pressure. In this work, multiple techniques are used to probe the onset of the Meissner effect at $T_c$ as a function of pressure in $\mathrm{Pb_{0.4}Bi_{1.6}Sr_2Ca_2Cu_3O_{10+δ}}$ (Bi-2223) to 60 GPa in different compression environments. Samples compressed under quasihydrostatic conditions exhibit a distinctive non-monotonic pressure dependence of $T_c$ below 25 GPa, in agreement with previous reports. With further increase in pressure $T_c$ climbs continuously to 190 K at 60 GPa. Evidence for critical temperatures exceeding those reported to date for cuprates at ambient and high pressures, the results may be understood in terms of the proposed second superconducting regime at high hole doping.

</details>


### [178] [Anisotropic transport of Josephson vortices in atomic-layer superconductors on vicinal surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01196)
*Wenxuan Qian,Yash Chauhan,Ryohei Nemoto,Keisuke Sagisaka,Shunsuke Yoshizawa,Takashi Uchihashi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy）直接观察了原子台阶上形成的约瑟夫森涡旋（Josephson vortices），并系统研究了Si(111)-(√7×√3)-In原子层超导体在邻晶面上的涡旋输运性质，构建了反映各种方向依赖输运机制的B-T相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子台阶对表面二维超导体有强烈影响，磁场下形成的约瑟夫森涡旋可能主导低温输运现象，但此前缺乏直接的实验验证。本文旨在通过实验手段填补这一空白。

Method: 采用具有邻晶面（vicinal surfaces）的原子层超导体Si(111)-(√7×√3)-In作为研究对象，使用扫描隧道显微镜直接观察约瑟夫森涡旋，并通过变温电阻测量系统探测出平面磁场下的涡旋输运行为，进而构建B-T相图。

Result: 发现随温度降低电阻出现急剧下降，且该行为相对原子台阶方向呈现显著各向异性；在中等磁场下薄层电阻（sheet resistance）的各向异性比可达约10³量级。高温低场区域涡旋表现为热激活蠕变（thermally excited creep），其激活能U_act呈各向异性；增大磁场会各向异性地将U_act抑制至零，在0.10–0.20 T区间出现一维无钉扎涡旋流；最低温度下涡旋运动由量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）主导。

Conclusion: 基于实验测量构建的B-T相图揭示了多个由方向依赖的涡旋输运机制所支配的区域，表明约瑟夫森涡旋在原子台阶处的行为对理解表面二维超导体的低温输运性质具有关键意义。

Abstract: Atomic steps have strong influences on surface two-dimensional superconductors. Josephson vortices formed at the atomic steps under magnetic fields may dominate transport phenomena at low temperatures, but its experimental verification is still lacking. Here, we report the vortex transport properties of atomic-layer superconductor Si(111)-$(\sqrt{7}\times\sqrt{3})$-In with vicinal surfaces, for which Josephson vortices are directly observed by scanning tunneling microscopy.
