A sharp drop in resistance with decreasing temperature $T$, detected under out-of-plane magnetic field $B$, reveals a distinctive anisotropy with respect to the atomic step direction. The anisotropy of sheet resistance, proportional to that of vortex mobility, amounts to the order of $10^3$ at intermediate magnetic fields. In the high-$T$ and low-$B$ region, Josephson vortices exhibit thermally excited creep motions with anisotropic activation energy $U_\mathrm{act}$. A further increase in $B$ suppresses $U_\mathrm{act}$ toward zero anisotropically, resulting in one-dimensional pinning-free vortex flow at $0.10 \lesssim B \lesssim 0.20$ T. At the lowest temperatures, the vortex motion is governed by quantum tunneling. A $B$-$T$ phase diagram constructed based on these measurements reveals multiple regions characterized by directionally dependent vortex-transport mechanisms.

</details>


### [179] [Bulk Ising superconductivity in an intercalated TaSe2 bilayer structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01209)
*Yupeng Li,Zhaopeng Guo,Lihong Hu,Guoan Li,Siqi Wu,Xinyi Zheng,Xiao Deng,Zhiyuan Zhang,Anqi Wang,Xingchen Guo,Ziwei Dou,Peiling Li,Yuke Li,Fanming Qu,Guangtong Liu,Jin-Ke Bao,Guang-Han Cao,Li Lu,Jie Shen,Zhu-An Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文合成了具有非中心对称结构的插层2Hb-TaSe2双层材料，通过电阻率测量、能带计算、厚度依赖的上临界场比率及超导二极管效应等多方面证据，证实了体相伊辛超导电性（bulk Ising superconductivity）的出现，并揭示了其随厚度演化的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体相体系中的伊辛自旋轨道耦合（Ising spin-orbit coupling）因其能够便捷地构建自旋轨道环境并催生奇异量子现象而备受关注，寻找体相伊辛超导体是当前凝聚态物理的重要研究方向。

Method: 合成了非中心对称结构的插层2Hb-TaSe2双层材料，结合电阻率测量、能带结构计算、面内上临界场与Pauli极限之比（Bc2∥/Bp）的厚度依赖测量以及超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect）等多角度手段进行系统分析。

Result: 电阻率测量显示各向异性超导行为，面内上临界场Bc2∥远超过Pauli极限Bp；能带计算显示能带劈裂伴随面外自旋极化。厚度依赖的Bc2∥/Bp比值及超导二极管效应的测量进一步支持该材料的伊辛超导性质，并揭示了体相伊辛超导体随厚度演化的额外特征。

Conclusion: 上述多重证据共同表明该材料中存在体相伊辛超导电性，相关发现为搜索体相伊辛超导体提供了有价值的见解和指导。

Abstract: Ising spin-orbit coupling in bulk systems has drawn considerable interest for its ability to conveniently construct spin-orbit environments and enable exotic quantum phenomena. In this work, we synthesize intercalated 2Hb-TaSe$_2$ bilayers with noncentrosymmetric structure and, through multifaceted analysis, present multiple lines of evidence for the emergence of bulk Ising superconductivity. Resistivity measurements reveal anisotropic superconducting behavior, with a remarkably large in-plane upper critical field $B_{c2}^{\|}$ that exceeds the Pauli limit $B_{p}$. Band structure calculations further show band splitting accompanied by out-of-plane spin polarization. Collectively, these observations point to the presence of Ising superconductivity. Additional measurements of the thickness-dependent ratio $B_{c2}^{\|}$/$B_{p}$ and the superconducting diode effect not only further support the Ising superconducting nature of this material, but also reveal additional features of bulk Ising superconductivity evolving with thickness. Our findings provide valuable insights that may contribute to the search for bulk Ising superconductors.

</details>


### [180] [$d$-spacing distributions as a probe of nematoelastic response in iron-based superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01264)
*Wenting Zhang,Ruixian Liu,Tingjun Zhang,Weiliang Yao,Xüe Fu,Hanqing Xie,Ziye Mo,Ting Guo,Kuo-Feng Tseng,Thomas Keller,Jitae T. Park,Fankang Li,Masaaki Matsuda,Avishek Maity,Long Tian,Pengcheng Dai,Xingye Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用中子Larmor衍射（neutron Larmor diffraction）测量了多种铁基超导体（iron-based superconductors, FeSCs）中面内晶格间距分布的温变展宽，发现该展宽遵循居里-外斯（Curie–Weiss）型行为，并可用含双线性向列弹性耦合与空间变化对称性破缺应力的平均场模型解释，从而提供了一种探测向列相关晶格展宽的体敏感探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁基超导体中的电子向列性（electronic nematicity）与正交晶应变存在双线性耦合，使得向列关联可以在晶格响应中显现，但此前缺乏体敏感（bulk-sensitive）的探测手段来直接测量与向列相关的晶格展宽。

Method: 采用中子Larmor衍射技术，测量电子掺杂Ba(Fe1−xCox)2As2、空穴掺杂Ba0.83K0.17Fe2As2、FeSe及Fe1.07Te中相对d间距的温度依赖分布；在部分样品上施加单轴应力对比实验；并用含双线性向列弹性耦合和空间变化对称性破缺应力的平均场模型（mean-field model）对展宽行为进行理论解释。

Result: 在无外加单轴应力的Ba(Fe1−xCox)2As2晶体中，面内分布宽度εFWHM在四方相降温过程中增大，可由居里-外斯型形式描述；拟合标度T*随Co掺杂降低，其演化与弹性电阻（elastoresistance）推断的向列相图一致。Ba0.83K0.17Fe2As2和FeSe中也观察到类似展宽，而Fe1.07Te在无外加应力时无长程居里-外斯行为，单轴压力下则产生强烈各向异性展宽。

Conclusion: 含双线性向列弹性耦合与空间变化对称性破缺应力的平均场模型可将居里-外斯型展宽归因于重整化的正交晶柔度（renormalized orthorhombic compliance）。中子Larmor衍射由此提供了一种体敏感的向列相关晶格展宽探针，可与电子和弹性测量互补。

Abstract: Electronic nematicity in iron-based superconductors (FeSCs) couples bilinearly to orthorhombic strain, allowing nematic correlations to appear in the lattice response. Here we use neutron Larmor diffraction to measure the temperature-dependent distribution of relative $d$ spacings in electron-doped Ba(Fe$_{1-x}$Co$_x$)$_2$As$_2$, hole-doped Ba$_{0.83}$K$_{0.17}$Fe$_2$As$_2$, FeSe, and Fe$_{1.07}$Te. In Ba(Fe$_{1-x}$Co$_x$)$_2$As$_2$ crystals without intentionally applied uniaxial stress, the in-plane distribution width, $\varepsilon_{\rm FWHM}$, increases on cooling in the tetragonal phase and can be described phenomenologically by a Curie--Weiss-like form. The fitted scale $T^*$ decreases with Co doping and evolves similarly to the nematic phase diagram inferred from elastoresistance, although the two experiments probe different response functions. Related broadening in Ba$_{0.83}$K$_{0.17}$Fe$_2$As$_2$ and FeSe supports extending this interpretation beyond electron-doped BaFe$_2$As$_2$. By contrast, Fe$_{1.07}$Te shows no extended Curie--Weiss-like regime without applied stress, whereas uniaxial pressure produces a strongly anisotropic broadening that can contain contributions from both the field-biased lattice response and inhomogeneous loading. A mean-field model with bilinear nematoelastic coupling and spatially varying symmetry-breaking stress explains the Curie--Weiss-like broadening in terms of the renormalized orthorhombic compliance. Neutron Larmor diffraction therefore provides a bulk-sensitive probe of nematic-related lattice broadening that complements electronic and elastic measurements.

</details>


### [181] [Electron-like high-temperature superconductivity induced by compressive strain in La2PrNi2O7 thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01295)
*Zhiwei Wang,Zhengjie Wang,Huiyu Wang,Mingyi Zhu,Mengzu Shi,Zongyao Huang,Houpu Li,Shoucong Ning,Jing Tao,Tao Wu,Xianhui Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究通过臭氧辅助原子层外延法，在NdAlO3衬底上制备了施加极端压应变（-2.14%）的La2PrNi2O7薄膜，实现了Tc起始60 K的高温超导电性，并与高压块体材料进行系统对比，揭示两者在面外晶格参数和霍尔系数上的本质差异，最终表明压应变与高压通过抑制自旋密度波（SDW）来调控关联景观（correlation landscape），从而触发超导电性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层镍酸盐（bilayer nickelates）中外延压应变被广泛认为可模拟高静水压效应，但这一等价性假设缺乏系统的实验验证。本研究旨在通过覆盖从压应变到张应变的连续应变区间，检验应变压与高压两种调控策略在物理机制上的异同。

Method: 采用臭氧辅助原子层外延法（ozone-assisted atomic-layer epitaxy）在NdAlO3衬底上生长La2PrNi2O7薄膜，实现该材料体系中最极端的压应变（-2.14%）；同时覆盖从-2.14%到+0.91%的连续应变区间，通过磁输运与霍尔测量系统表征了超导性质、晶格响应与载流子类型。

Result: 在-2.14%极端压应变下，薄膜呈现Tc_start为60 K、33 K零电阻及20 K抗磁响应，磁输运确认准二维超导特性。与高压块体对比发现：外延薄膜在面外参数c（或c/ap比）上超导窗口显著发散，而面内参数ap与块体一致；两者均从自旋密度波抑制出发实现超导，但最优超导薄膜呈本征电子型（负霍尔系数），与高压块体及张应变薄膜的孔型（正霍尔系数）截然相反。

Conclusion: 两种调控策略最终都通过调制底层关联景观——超导电性的真正驱动力——而起作用，超越了特定费米面拓扑（Fermi surface topology）的限制。该工作建立了研究镍酸盐多轨道物理的宏观平台，为探索高温超导提供了新维度。

Abstract: The realization of high-temperature superconductivity in bilayer nickelates under epitaxial compressive strain is widely interpreted as mimicking the effects of high hydrostatic pressure. To test the equivalence of these mechanisms, we investigated a comprehensive strain continuum ranging from compressive (-2.14%) to tensile (+0.91%). Crucially, via ozone-assisted atomic-layer epitaxy, we realized high-temperature superconductivity in as-grown La2PrNi2O7 films on NdAlO3 substrates, which induce the most extreme compressive strain in this material system. Under extreme compression (-2.14%), these films exhibit a Tc_onset of 60 K, zero resistance at 33 K, and a diamagnetic response at 20 K, with magnetotransport measurements confirming a quasi-two-dimensional superconducting nature. Comparing our phase diagram with reported data reveals distinct lattice responses: unlike in pressurized crystals, the superconducting window in epitaxial films diverges significantly in the out-of-plane parameter c (or c/ap ratio) but remains consistent with the bulk regarding the in-plane parameter ap. Crucially, while superconductivity in both systems emerges from the suppression of spin-density waves (SDW), Hall measurements reveal a fundamental electronic dichotomy: optimal superconducting films are intrinsically electron-like (exhibiting a negative Hall coefficient), in stark contrast to the hole-like nature (positive Hall coefficient) of high-pressure bulk crystals and non-superconducting tensile films. Ultimately, both tuning strategies effectively modulate the underlying correlation landscape - the true driver of superconductivity - transcending the constraints of specific Fermi surface topologies. This work establishes a macroscopic platform for probing the multi-orbital physics of nickelates, offering a new dimension for investigating high-temperature superconductivity.

</details>


### [182] [Spin Josephson diode effect induced by higher-harmonic spin Josephson currents in a diffusive Josephson junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01991)
*Shin-ichi Hikino*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在准经典格林函数（quasiclassical Green's function）框架下，理论研究了具有铁磁交换场的Rashba金属层扩散约瑟夫森结中的自旋约瑟夫森二极管效应（spin Josephson diode effect, SJDE），发现Rashba自旋轨道耦合与交换场的协同作用可打破空间反演和时间反演对称性，产生有限的φ₀相位移动，从而在不加外磁场条件下实现自旋流的前后向非对称传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需外部磁场的非互易自旋传输（nonreciprocal spin transport）机制，理解自旋约瑟夫森结中对称性破缺如何诱导单向自旋流，为自旋电子学器件设计提供理论基础。

Method: 采用准经典格林函数方法，对含Rashba金属层和铁磁交换场的扩散约瑟夫森结进行解析推导，获得一阶和二阶谐波自旋约瑟夫森电流的表达式，并通过数值计算分析效率与结厚及自旋轨道耦合强度的关系。

Result: 推导出包含额外余弦项和有限φ₀相位移动的自旋电流-相位关系；自旋轨道耦合与交换场协同破坏反演和时间反演对称性，产生固有正反向自旋电流不对称性；数值上发现效率随金属厚度增大而下降，与二阶谐波分量的抑制相关，而对自旋轨道耦合强度依赖反映相位移动增强与谐波抑制之间的竞争效应。

Conclusion: 谐波分量间的相互作用可为非互易自旋传输提供一种机制，且无需抑制自旋单态关联（spin-singlet correlations），为无外场条件下的自旋二极管效应提供了新的物理实现途径。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the spin Josephson diode effect (SJDE) in a diffusive Josephson junction with a Rashba metal layer under a ferromagnetic exchange field. Within the quasiclassical Green's function framework, we derive analytical expressions for the first- and second-harmonic spin Josephson currents. The interplay between Rashba spin-orbit interaction and the exchange field breaks inversion and time-reversal symmetries, generating additional cosine terms in the spin current-phase relations and a finite phi0 phase shift. This phase shift induces an intrinsic asymmetry between forward and backward spin currents, leading to the SJDE without an external magnetic field. Numerical results show that the efficiency decreases with increasing metal thickness due to suppression of the second-harmonic component, while its dependence on spin-orbit interaction strength reflects competing effects between phase shift enhancement and harmonic suppression. These findings demonstrate that the interplay between harmonic components provides a mechanism for nonreciprocal spin transport without requiring suppression of spin-singlet correlations.

</details>


### [183] [Enhanced and robust superconductivity in La0.8Sr0.2NiO2 membranes compressed up to 210 GPa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02042)
*Shu Cai,Yuqing Tian,Shengjun Yan,Jinyu Zhao,Bo Hao,Jianfeng Zhang,Shuaihang Sun,Yang Ding,Qi Wu,Ho-kwang Mao,I. Bozovic,Yuefeng Nie,Liling Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究报道了自支撑La0.8Sr0.2NiO2薄膜在高达210 GPa的超高压下仍保持超导性，其超导转变起始温度呈穹顶状（dome-like）演化，从常压的16 K升至146 GPa时的峰值74.5 K，随后降至210 GPa时的57.4 K，这是高温氧化物超导体中前所未有的超高压鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限层镍酸盐（infinite-layer nickelate）薄膜超导性的发现开启了铜氧化物家族之外探索非常规氧化物超导体的新前沿，但衬底的存在阻碍了极高压力下的研究。此前自支撑Nd0.85Sr0.15NiO2薄膜的研究表明超导转变温度在加压至91 GPa时仍持续上升且无饱和迹象，自然引发了对更高压力下行为的探索需求。

Method: 本研究采用自支撑（freestanding）La0.8Sr0.2NiO2薄膜作为研究对象，通过施加高达210 GPa的超高压（ultrahigh pressure），系统测量超导转变起始温度（superconducting transition onset temperature）随压力的变化关系。

Result: 超导转变起始温度在压力下呈连续的穹顶状演化：从常压下的16 K随压力升高而增加，在146 GPa时达到峰值74.5 K，随后逐渐下降至210 GPa时的57.4 K。

Conclusion: 自支撑La0.8Sr0.2NiO2薄膜的超导性在高达210 GPa的超高压下仍然稳定存在，这种对超高压的鲁棒性在以往的高温氧化物超导体中从未被观测到，为理解镍酸盐超导机制提供了新的实验约束。

Abstract: The discovery of superconductivity in infinite-layer nickelate thin films has opened a new frontier for exploring unconventional oxide superconductors beyond the copper oxide family. However, the presence of substrate hampers investigations under very high pressure. Recently, this problem was circumvented by a study of freestanding Nd0.85Sr0.15NiO2 membranes, which revealed that superconducting transition temperature kept increasing as the pressure was ramped up to 91 GPa, without showing signs of saturation. Naturally, one wonders what would happen at even higher pressures. Here, we report that superconductivity in a freestanding La0.8Sr0.2NiO2 membrane persists under applied ultrahigh pressures up to 210 GPa. The superconducting transition onset temperature exhibits a continuous dome-like evolution, increasing from 16 K at ambient pressure to a peak of 74.5 K at 146 GPa, and then gradually decreasing to 57.4 K at 210 GPa. Such robustness of superconductivity against ultrahigh pressure has not been observed in high-Tc oxide superconductors before.

</details>


### [184] [Pressure-induced concurrent amorphization and superconductivity in topological material NbNiTe5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02342)
*Lingxiao Zhao,Yi Zhao,Bangshuai Zhu,Qi Wang,Cuiying Pei,Juefei Wu,Jin-Ke Bao,Wen-He Jiao,Yanpeng Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究系统考察了拓扑材料NbNiTe5在高压下的结构与电子性质，发现其经历了二阶相变、非晶化并伴随超导出现，超导转变温度（Tc）随压力从9.5 GPa时的0.6 K缓慢升至45.7 GPa时的1.4 K，且超导性受结构无序程度调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索过渡金属硫族化合物超导家族，并深化对无序系统中超导电性机制的理解。

Method: 通过高压下的电阻测量、X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）和拉曼光谱（Raman spectra）等手段，系统研究了NbNiTe5从0.7 GPa至45.7 GPa压力范围内的结构演化与电子性质变化。

Result: 正常态电阻在0.7—5.1 GPa区间呈非单调变化，与X射线衍射和拉曼光谱观察到的层间方向的二阶转变一致；约10 GPa时样品开始非晶化并与超导出现同步；进一步压缩时结构无序增强、超导转变更清晰，超导转变温度（Tc）在45.7 GPa内从0.6 K缓慢升至1.4 K。

Conclusion: NbNiTe5中的超导性受高压下结构无序程度调控，该发现扩展了过渡金属硫族化物超导体的家族，并为理解无序系统中的超导电性提供了新视角。

Abstract: We have systematically studied the structural and electronic properties of a topological material NbNiTe5 under high pressure. The evolution of the normal state resistance shows a non-monotonic trend from 0.7 GPa to 5.1 GPa, in accordance with the second-order transition along the inter-layer direction observed in X-ray diffraction and Raman spectra. At around 10 GPa, the sample starts amorphization, which is concurrent with the emergence of superconductivity. Upon further compression, the structural disorder enhances and the superconducting transition becomes clearer, suggesting that the superconductivity is modulated by the degree of disorder in NbNiTe5 under high pressure. Within 45.7 GPa, the superconducting transition temperature (Tc) slowly rises from 0.6 K at 9.5 GPa to 1.4 K at 45.7 GPa. Our findings extend the family of transition metal chalcogenide superconductors and shed new light on understanding superconductivity in disordered systems.

</details>


### [185] [Cryogenic focused-ion-beam microstructuring enabling quantitative $c$-axis transport measurements in Tl$_2$Ba$_2$CuO$_{6+δ}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02344)
*Ayanesh Maiti,Carsten Putzke,Linus Holeschovsky,Roemer D. H. Hinlopen,Chunyu Guo,Dorothee Herrmann,Seunghyun Khim,Berit H. Goodge,Andre W. Tyler,Michele S. Conroy,Andreas W. Rost,Andrew P. Mackenzie,Philip J. W. Moll*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究利用低温聚焦离子束（cryogenic FIB）微结构化技术制备超净过掺杂铜氧化物Tl2201的输运器件，有效抑制了离子束引起的氧损失，从而实现了精确的绝对输运测量，解决了过掺杂Tl2201中输运与量子振荡测量之间长期存在的分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联量子材料中的绝对输运测量常受无序、不均匀性、几何不确定性和小晶体尺寸限制。聚焦离子束技术（focused ion beam, FIB）可克服上述限制，但在铜氧化物超导体中的应用因离子束诱导损伤而受阻。

Method: 研究对象为清洁的过掺杂铜氧化物Tl2201。对比常规FIB加工与低温FIB微结构化两种工艺，评估其对晶体结构的影响；随后利用低温FIB制备微结构器件，测量面内电阻率、霍尔载流子密度以及c轴输运。

Result: 常规FIB加工导致热驱动的氧损失，而低温FIB微结构化可极大抑制该退化并保持从体相到原子尺度的晶体结构。微结构器件定量复现了既有的面内电阻率和霍尔载流子密度测量结果；c轴输运测量得到的绝对ρc(T)值约为此前报道的三倍，使输运各向异性与已知费米面几何在各项同性弛豫时间近似（isotropic relaxation-time approximation）下定量一致。

Conclusion: 研究解决了过掺杂Tl2201中输运与量子振荡测量之间长期存在的分歧，并将低温FIB微结构化确立为在无序、不均匀性、几何或小晶体尺寸曾限制实验精度的量子材料中进行可靠定量输运测量的有效途径。

Abstract: Absolute transport measurements in correlated quantum materials are often limited by disorder, inhomogeneity, geometric uncertainty, and small crystal size. Focused ion beam (FIB) technology offers a route to overcome many of these limitations by enabling transport devices with precisely defined geometry to be fabricated from lamellae extracted from carefully selected regions of a crystal, but its application to cuprate superconductors has been hindered by ion-beam-induced damage. Here we study the clean overdoped cuprate Tl2201 and show that conventional FIB processing causes thermally driven oxygen loss, while cryogenic FIB microstructuring largely suppresses this degradation and preserves the crystal structure from the bulk to the atomic scale. Microstructured devices quantitatively reproduce established in-plane resistivity and Hall carrier density measurements without rescaling. Applying this approach to $c$-axis transport, we obtain absolute $ρ_c(T)$ values approximately three times larger than previously reported, bringing the transport anisotropy into quantitative agreement with the known Fermi surface geometry within an isotropic relaxation-time approximation. These results resolve a long-standing discrepancy between transport and quantum oscillation measurements in overdoped Tl2201 and establish cryogenic FIB microstructuring as a route to reliable quantitative transport measurements in quantum materials where disorder, inhomogeneity, geometry, or small crystal size have previously limited experimental accuracy.

</details>


### [186] [Superconducting ternary compounds Li-X-B (X=Mo, W) within the mild pressure range: First-principles predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02362)
*Bangshuai Zhu,Juefei Wu,Dexi Shao,Junjie Wang,Yu Han,Cuiying Pei,Qi Wang,Jian Sun,Yanpeng Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与晶体结构预测（crystal structure prediction），沿着五条和四条三元组分线（ternary composition lines, TCLs）分别预测出5个Li-W-B化合物和4个Li-Mo-B化合物，其中LiWB4、Li4MoB2和LiMo2B2在高压下热力学稳定，Li2WB6和Li2WB4具有约11 K的超导转变温度（Tc），Li原子引入可将WB2的稳定压力从约100 GPa降至0 GPa。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压下的超导氢化物研究中，三元化合物有望降低稳定压力并维持超导性，但目前对其探索缺乏系统有效的策略。作者提出三元组分线（TCLs）策略来系统探索三元化合物，并将此策略应用于Li-X-B（X=Mo, W）体系加以验证。

Method: 将第一性原理计算与晶体结构预测相结合，在Li-W-B和Li-Mo-B体系中分别沿五条和四条三元组分线进行系统性探索，并通过全三元搜索（full ternary search, FTS）评估TCLs策略在0 GPa下Li-W-B体系中的有效性。

Result: 预测出5个Li-W-B化合物和4个Li-Mo-B化合物；LiWB4、Li4MoB2和LiMo2B2可在高压下保持热力学稳定，Li2WB6在0 GPa时仅高出凸包（convex hull）约0.02 eV/atom，具有合成潜力。Li2WB6（P6/mmm）和Li2WB4（R-3m）的Tc均约为11 K，与100 GPa下WB2（P6/mmm）的Tc相当；此外发现Li4MoB2（C2/m）在压缩下Tc出现异常升高。

Conclusion: Li原子的引入可将WB2 P6/mmm的实测稳定压力从约100 GPa降低到0 GPa，为降低三元化合物稳定压力提供了候选结构。研究初步验证了TCLs策略在结构预测中的有效性，并展望未来对该策略的进一步改进以推动三元化合物研究。

Abstract: Among the superconducting hydrides under high pressure, a number of studies concentrate on the ternary compounds to explore unique superconductors, which are capable of reducing the stable pressure and maintain superconductivity. In this work, to verify our proposed strategy of ternary composition lines (TCLs) to explore ternary compounds, we combined the first-principles calculations and crystal structure predictions to study the ternary compounds Li-X-B (X=Mo, W) under high pressure. After calculations along five and four TCLs in Li-W-B and Li-Mo-B, respectively, five Li-W-B compounds and four Li-Mo-B compounds were predicted. The compositions of LiWB4, Li4MoB2 and LiMo2B2 could be thermodynamically stable under high pressure, and Li2WB6 is around 0.02 eV/atom above the convex hull at 0 GPa, which has potential for synthesizing. Both of the predicted Li2WB6 P6/mmm and Li2WB4 R-3m are superconducting and their Tc are around 11 K, which are similar to the Tc of WB2 P6/mmm around 100 GPa. An anomalous increase of Tc was found in Li4MoB2 C2/m upon compression. We carried out full ternary search (FTS) to evaluate the validity of the TCLs strategy in Li-W-B system at 0 GPa. Our results are helpful for understanding the phase diagram of Li-X-B (X=Mo, W) under high pressure and the introducing of Li atoms provide candidate structures to reduce the measured stable pressure from ~100 GPa in WB2 P6/mmm to 0 GPa. Meanwhile, we preliminary validate the strategy of TCLs in structure predictions and we expect to improve this strategy in the future, shedding light on the studies of ternary compounds.

</details>


### [187] [Emergence of Double-Dome Superconductivity in the Pressurized Dirac Semimetal BaMg2Bi2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02394)
*Qi Wang,Juefei Wu,Cuiying Pei,Yi Zhao,Yiyan Wang,Yanpeng Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: Dirac半金属BaMg2Bi2在高压下表现出双穹顶超导行为，第一超导穹顶的超导转变温度在4.5 GPa处达到6.67 K，与压力调制的Lifshitz相变相关；第二超导穹顶在10.4 GPa处达到7.22 K，并于环境压力P3m1相到高压Pnma相的结构相变同时出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirac半金属BaMg2Bi2被报道为一种独特的拓扑材料，在常压下表现出与体相带拓扑共存的表面超导电性。该研究旨在通过高压手段系统探究BaMg2Bi2单晶的超导性质，揭示其高压超导行为的物理机制。

Method: 采用高压电阻率测量（high-pressure resistivity measurements）表征超导转变温度随压力的变化；结合高压X射线衍射（high-pressure X-ray diffraction）、霍尔电阻率测量（Hall resistivity measurements）以及理论计算，综合分析超导行为与电子结构及晶体结构演变之间的关联。

Result: 揭示出压力驱动的双穹顶超导行为（double-dome superconducting behavior），第一和第二超导穹顶的超导转变温度分别在4.5 GPa和10.4 GPa处达到最大值6.67 K和7.22 K。第一超导区域与压力调制的Lifshitz相变（Lifshitz transition）密切相关，第二超导相则与环境压力P3m1相到高压Pnma相的结构相变同时出现。

Conclusion: BaMg2Bi2的高压超导行为由电子结构变化（Lifshitz相变）和晶体结构相变共同调控，揭示了该材料中两个截然不同的超导机制区域，为理解拓扑材料中的压力诱导超导提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Dirac semimetal BaMg2Bi2 is reported to be a unique topological material that manifests surface superconductivity that coexistswith bulk band topology at ambient pressure. Here, we present a comprehensive investigation of high-pressure superconductingproperties in BaMg2Bi2 single crystal. Significantly, a pressure-driven double-dome superconducting behavior was revealed, withthe superconducting transition temperature Tc approaching the maximum values of 6.67 K at 4.5 GPa and 7.22 K at 10.4 GPafor the first and second superconducting domes, respectively. The combination of high-pressure X-ray diffraction, Hall resistivitymeasurements, and theoretical calculations demonstrates that, the first superconducting regime is closely related to the pressure-modulated Lifshitz transition, whereas the second superconducting phase emerges concurrently with a structural transition fromthe ambient-pressure P3m1 phase to a high-pressure Pnma phase.

</details>


### [188] [Screening phonon-mediated superconductors from static orbital Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02426)
*Jian-Feng Zhang,Ze-Feng Gao,Xiao-Qi Han,Dingshun Lv,Miao Gao,Kai Liu,Xinguo Ren,Zhong-Yi Lu,Tao Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种基于静态轨道哈密顿量（orbital-based Hamiltonian）的低成本物理框架，无需声子微扰计算即可直接从材料识别强电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling, EPC）体系；经DFPT验证后，在超过36,000种化合物中筛选出34种动态稳定的超导候选材料（T_c > 10 K），并揭示了两种高T_c超导路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理搜索超导体的主要瓶颈在于电子-声子耦合（EPC）计算的高昂计算成本，严重限制了候选材料的筛选规模与效率。

Method: 作者构建了一个低成本的物理透明框架，仅依赖静态轨道哈密顿量即可识别强EPC材料，绕过显式声子微扰计算；框架通过密度泛函微扰理论（density functional perturbation theory, DFPT）在代表性超导体上进行验证，并应用于MattKeyBond数据库中超过36,000种化合物的高通量筛选。

Result: 框架以显著更低的计算成本半定量地捕捉了EPC强度标度；从36,000余种化合物中识别出34种经过DFPT验证的动态稳定超导候选材料，其计算T_c均高于10 K。

Conclusion: 候选材料揭示了两种实现相对高温超导的路线：一种是金属化的共价σ键（metallized covalent σ-bond）路线，更适合实现高T_c超导；另一种是费米能级态密度积累（Fermi-level density-of-states accumulation）路线，虽可提升T_c但增幅通常有限。

Abstract: The first-principles search for superconductors is severely limited by the high cost of electron-phonon coupling (EPC) calculations. Here we develop a low-cost, physically transparent framework that identifies strong-EPC materials directly from static orbital-based Hamiltonians without explicit phonon perturbation calculations. Verification using density functional perturbation theory (DFPT) for representative superconductors shows that the framework captures semi-quantitatively the EPC scale at substantially lower computational cost. Applied to more than 36,000 compounds in the MattKeyBond database, it identifies 34 dynamically stable superconducting candidates with calculated $T_c > 10$ K after DFPT verification. These candidates reveal two distinct routes to relatively high-$T_c$ superconductivity: a metallized covalent $σ$-bond route that is more favorable for achieving high-$T_c$ superconductors, and a Fermi-level density-of-states accumulation route that can enhance $T_c$ but usually to a more limited extent.

</details>


### [189] [Composite quantum geometry of superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02434)
*Florian Simon,Thomas Bernat,Annica M. Black-Schaffer*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究探讨超导态自身的量子几何性质（由Bogoliubov-de Gennes哈密顿量决定），证明在三个一般条件下，BdG量子几何可精确分解为正常态量子几何与配对量子几何之和，展现出简单的复合结构；并进一步给出两轨道自旋单态超导体的一般解析结果，揭示正常态与配对贡献的不可分离纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期大量研究聚焦于超导性与正常态量子几何（quantum geometry）的相互作用，尤其是超流体权重（superfluid weight）方面。然而，超导态本身的量子几何性质尚未被系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者基于Bogoliubov-de Gennes（BdG）哈密顿量分析超导态的量子几何，在超导适配性（superconducting fitness）、配对轨道均匀性（orbital uniformity）以及无正常态自旋翻转项（spin-flip terms）三个一般条件下，推导BdG量子几何的精确分解公式，并给出所有自旋单态与三态（含非酉自旋三态）配对的解析表达式；同时扩展至一般的两轨道自旋单态超导体情形。

Result: 在三个一般条件下，BdG量子几何严格分解为正常态量子几何与配对量子几何（pairing quantum geometry）之和，该分离对所有自旋单态和三态配对均成立；作者给出了各种情形的配对量子几何显式解析公式。对于两轨道非均匀配对超导体，解析结果显示出不可分离的复合BdG量子几何结构。

Conclusion: 该研究为设计具有非平凡拓扑（nontrivial topology）和有限量子度规（finite quantum metric）的超导体及超导混合结构提供了设计规则，并有助于非常规超导性的实验诊断。配对本身即可单独驱动拓扑和有限量子度规，即使在拓扑平凡或平带超导体中亦然。

Abstract: The interplay of superconductivity and the quantum geometry of the normal state has recently been the subject of an array of studies, especially regarding the superfluid weight. In this work, we turn our attention to the quantum geometry of the superconducting state itself, set by the Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) Hamiltonian, which dictates the geometric and topological properties of superconductivity. We show that under three general conditions, namely superconducting fitness, orbital uniformity of the superconducting pairing, and absence of normal-state spin-flip terms, the BdG quantum geometry exactly separates into a sum of the normal-state quantum geometry and an additional pairing quantum geometry, thereby displaying a simple composite structure. We show that this separation holds for all spin-singlet and -triplet pairings, including nonunitary spin-triplet pairing. We further provide explicit analytical formulas for the pairing quantum geometry for all these cases. These results establish how superconducting pairing alone easily drives both topology and a finite quantum metric, thus being present even in topological trivial or flat band superconductors, with no normal state quantum geometry. To complement these results, we also derive the BdG quantum geometry of a general two-orbital spin-singlet superconductor with non-uniform pairing and finite superconducting fitness. Here, our explicit analytical results establish a non-separable composite BdG quantum geometry, with the normal state and pairing contributions generally intertwining, thereby producing even more possibilities for finite quantum geometry. Our results provide design rules for creating superconductors and superconducting hybrid structures with nontrivial topology and finite quantum metric and will additionally help in the experimental diagnosis of unconventional superconductivity.

</details>


### [190] [Superconducting dome due to the Fano-Feshbach shape resonance in artificial high-Tc superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02501)
*Gaetano Campi,Andrea Alimenti,Danielle Villa,G. Alexander Smith,Fedor F. Balakirev,Luis Balicas,Andrea Perali,Sergio Caprara,Gennady Logvenov,Antonio Bianconi1*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy, MBE）生长的SNSN人工高温超导超晶格（Artificial High TC Superlattices, AHTS），在70特斯拉级脉冲磁场下的磁输运测量中，系统验证了Bianconi-Perali-Valletta（BPV）理论预测的超导圆顶（superconducting dome），并通过调节量子几何因子L/d实现对超导圆顶全掺杂范围的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为BPV理论提供令人信服的实验证据——该理论预测通过设计人工超导超晶格的纳米异质结构几何参数，可以实现基于量子材料设计的超导圆顶。

Method: 采用分子束外延生长SNSN超晶格结构（超导掺杂Mott绝缘体层+正常金属间隔层交替排列，周期为d），通过改变量子几何因子L/d即超导层厚度与周期之比来调制有效空穴浓度δ=0.45(1-L/d)。在脉冲磁场（最高72 T）下，对超导圆顶从深度欠掺杂到过掺杂的全掺杂范围进行系统的磁输运测量，追踪电阻转变、上临界磁场（upper critical magnetic field）和Ginzburg-Landau相干长度的演化。

Result: 实验观测到随L/d比例变化的超导圆顶，并通过调变化学势（chemical potential）触发了Fano-Feshbach形状共振（Fano-Feshbach shape resonance），该共振调节了BCS机制中超导能隙与BEC-BCS交叉区域中不同能隙之间的对转移过程。全掺杂范围（δ=0.45(1-L/d)）下的磁输运数据系统性地映射了圆顶各区域的超导特性。

Conclusion: 研究证实了BPV理论的实验有效性：通过量子材料设计（调节L/d量子几何因子）可在人工超晶格中实现并调控超导圆顶，为理解高温超导机制中的Fano-Feshbach共振、BCS-BEC交叉等关键物理过程提供了重要的实验平台和数据支持。

Abstract: In this work we provide compelling experimental validation of the Bianconi Perali Valletta (BPV) theory predicting a superconducting dome based on a quantum material design of Artificial High TC Superlattices (AHTS) made with a selected nanoscale heterostructure geometry. These AHTS are SNSN superlattices of quantum wells of period d, composed of first units, superconducting doped Mott insulator layers with Rashba spin orbit coupling (S) of thickness L, intercalated by second units, normal metal spacers (N). In these superlattices, grown by molecular beam epitaxy (MBE), the experimental superconducting dome is obtained by material quantum design changing the chemical potential via the quantum geometrical factor L/d which tunes the Fano-Feshbach shape resonance in the pair transfer between superconducting gaps in the BCS regime and different gaps in the BEC-BCS crossover. Here we present a systematic magneto-transport study of AHTS artificial superlattices across the full doping range of the superconducting dome, from the deeply underdoped to the overdoped regime, using pulsed magnetic fields up to 72 T. By varying the L/d ratio, we tune the effective hole concentration delta=0.45(1-L/d) and map the evolution of the resistive transitions, the upper critical magnetic field and the Ginzburg-Landau coherence length

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Eigensystems of Dirac operators with singular potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00865)
*Boris Mityagin,Petr Siegl*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界区间上受Dirichlet边界条件约束的一维Dirac算子在具有幂衰减Fourier系数势函数微扰下的谱性质，证明了其本征系生成Riesz基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fourier系数呈幂衰减的势函数族涵盖了大量既非可积函数也非测度的广义函数（distribution），这类广泛势函数对Dirac算子谱结构的影响此前缺乏系统研究，需要加以刻画。

Method: 借助Paley-Zygmund定理（paley-zygmund theorem）确认势函数族的广义性，通过对微扰后Dirac算子谱的定位，分析其本征函数系的结构性质。

Result: 给出了微扰后算子谱的定位结果，并作为主要结论证明了本征函数系生成一个Riesz基（Riesz basis）。

Conclusion: 即使势函数属于高度非正则的广义函数类别，微扰后的Dirac算子本征系仍具备Riesz基这一良好的结构性性质。

Abstract: We perturb one-dimensional Dirac operators on a bounded interval subject to Dirichlet boundary conditions by potentials with Fourier coefficients exhibiting power-decay. As a consequence of Paley-Zygmund theorem, this broad family of potentials comprises distributions that are neither integrable functions nor measures. We localize the spectrum of the perturbed operator and, as the main result, show that its eigensystem generates a Riesz basis.

</details>


<div id='physics.hist-ph'></div>

# physics.hist-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Gauge and Metaphysics of Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02542)
*Álvaro Mozota Frauca*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文反驳了认为广义相对论（general relativity）中的时空因作为规范理论（gauge theory）而应获得与其他理论完全不同的解释的观点，主张广义相对论中的时空可类比于其他模型中的时空，並指出从微分同胚不变性（diffeomorphism invariance）出发的论证是对规范理论概念的误用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分学者认为广义相对论的时空因其规范理论性质而应获得根本不同的诠释，本文旨在审视并反驳这一主张。

Method: 通过审查相关论证，并论证微分同胚不变性论证不适当地将规范理论概念应用于适用性有限的语境中。

Result: 论证表明广义相对论中的时空可以以与其他理论模型中时空类似的方式加以理解，规范理论论证在其适用范围内存在局限。

Conclusion: 广义相对论的时空无需因其规范理论属性而被赋予根本不同的诠释，微分同胚不变性论证误导了对此问题的理解。

Abstract: Some authors have argued that spacetime in general relativity should be given a radically different interpretation from the ones given to spacetime in other theories in virtue of it being a gauge theory. In this article I review this sort of argument and argue against this view. That is, I argue that spacetime in general relativity can be understood analogously to spacetime in other models and that the argument from the diffeomorphism invariance of the theory misapplies the concepts of gauge theory to a context in which they are of limited application.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Rhombohedral Graphene: A Tale of Many Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00167)
*Ahmed Abouelkomsan,Filippo Gaggioli,Daniele Guerci,Liang Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究菱方石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）中的强关联量子相，提出一种基于非相互作用能带的简单指标来识别相图中的强关联区域，并开发了神经网络变分蒙特卡洛（neural-network variational Monte Carlo）方法结合Hartree-Fock理论求解相互作用基态，发现了一系列无经典类比电子晶体，包括维格纳晶体（Wigner crystal）、自掺杂维格纳晶体及"反晶体"（anticrystal）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱方石墨烯实验已发现从手性超导体到电子晶体的丰富关联量子相，但理解其相位图需要有效的理论工具。作者旨在建立一个简单指标来识别强关联区域，并发展可求解相互作用基态的计算方法。

Method: 作者提出一个从非相互作用带色散（noninteracting band dispersion）导出的简单指标，用于在载流子密度和位移场（displacement field）参数空间中标定强关联区域。同时开发了神经网络变分蒙特卡洛方法，并结合Hartree-Fock理论求解相互作用基态。

Result: 计算揭示了多种无经典类比的电子晶体相，按密度递增依次为：维格纳晶体、自掺杂维格纳晶体，以及"反晶体"——即在电子液体中形成空穴晶格的结构。文中还讨论了这些相的实验表现及其与超导电性的可能联系。

Conclusion: 菱方石墨烯中存在丰富的非经典电子晶体相，这些相可能与超导电性存在关联，所提出的指标和计算方法为理解关联量子相提供有效框架。

Abstract: Experiments on rhombohedral graphene have uncovered an extraordinary wealth of correlated quantum phases - from chiral superconductors to electronic crystals - all within a single family of atomically thin materials. Here, we introduce a simple indicator, derived from the noninteracting band dispersion, that identifies strongly correlated regions in the phase diagram of rhombohedral graphene as a function of carrier density and displacement field. We develop a neural-network variational Monte Carlo method, combined with Hartree-Fock theory, to solve the interacting ground states. Our calculation reveals a variety of electron crystals with no classical analog. These include, at increasing density: Wigner crystal, self-doped Wiger crystal, as well as ''anticrystal'', a lattice of holes in an electron liquid. We discuss their experimental manifestations and possible connection to superconductivity.

</details>


### [194] [Shape of Wigner Crystals and Hole Self-Doping in a Mexican-Hat Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00170)
*Minho Luke Kim,Xiao-Gang Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）计算，研究了墨西哥帽色散（Mexican-hat dispersion）下强库仑相互作用诱导的维格纳晶体（Wigner crystal），发现轨道形状需在k=0附近耗尽电子以降低基态能量，并指出在电子-空穴关联作用下，靠近维格纳晶体相变处k=0附近带孔自掺杂（hole self-doping）的维格纳晶体仍可能量上有利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强库仑相互作用下、由环形费米面（ring-like Fermi surface）驱动的维格纳晶体形成机制，以及其与实验观测到的随电子密度降低而出现的两次相变（从盘状费米面自旋-能谷极化费米液体到环状费米面，再到维格纳晶体）的对应关系。

Method: 设计了使维格纳晶体能量最小化的轨道形状，并引入Jastrow因子以及描述电子与掺杂空位之间关联的因子以捕捉库仑诱导的关联效应；通过变分蒙特卡洛计算，标定有效能带参数c₂和c₄，使其重现实验中观测到的两次相变转变。

Result: 发现低基态能量要求轨道形状在k=0附近耗尽电子；即使在优化轨道形状下，只要包含电子-空穴关联，靠近维格纳晶体相变处k=0附近带孔自掺杂的维格纳晶体仍可能量上有利，并估算出掺杂空穴的色散（dispersion）。

Conclusion: k=0附近的电子耗尽和电子-空穴关联是理解墨西哥帽色散下维格纳晶体稳定性及其相变的关键因素，为实验中观测到的密度驱动相变序列提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We study Wigner crystals (WCs) induced by a strong Coulomb interaction from the ring-like Fermi surface of a Mexican-hat dispersion $ε_k= c_2k^2+c_4k^4$. We design orbital shape in order to minimize the energy of the WC, and find that a low ground-state energy requires an orbital shape with a depletion of electrons near $k=0$. To capture the Coulomb-induced correlations, we include a Jastrow factor as well as a factor describing the correlation between electrons and doped vacancies. Using variational Monte Carlo calculations, we calibrate the effective band parameters $c_2$ and $c_4$ to reproduce the two transitions observed experimentally as the electron density is lowered: from a spin-valley-polarized Fermi liquid with a disk-like Fermi surface, to one with a ring-like Fermi surface, and finally to a WC. We find that, even with an optimized orbital shape that depletes electrons near $k=0$, a WC with hole self-doping near $k=0$ can still be energetically favorable near the WC transition, provided that the electron-vacancy correlation is included. We also estimate the dispersion of the doped hole.

</details>


### [195] [Chiral Magnon Mixing by Symmetry-Breaking in Collinear Ferrimagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00210)
*Dhurba R. Jaishi,Tyler J. Slade,S. X. M. Riberolles,Bing Li,Tianxiong Han,D. M. Pajerowski,D. L. Abernathy,Barry Winn,Melissa Graves-Brook,B. G. Ueland,R. J. McQueeney*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究报道了RMn6Sn6（R = Tb, Er）亚铁磁体（ferrimagnetic）中左右旋磁振子（magnon）能带在基平面内有限动量（k）处交叉，形成具有相反动力学手性（dynamical chirality）的模式。该交叉可能以节点线（nodal line）形式保留或打开能隙，取决于亚铁磁序的对称性，后者可通过稀土磁各向异性或外加磁场调控；有能隙的模式包含杂化的手性激发，其手性变为动量依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁体中的磁振子具有由磁矩绕磁化方向右手进动定义的旋角动量；反铁磁体在无外加场时左右旋磁振子简并；而亚铁磁体具有非补偿磁子晶格，理论上应天然存在由内部分子场分裂的左右旋磁振子，但这一预言尚缺乏实验验证。

Method: 以RMn6Sn6（R = Tb, Er）亚铁磁体为研究对象，通过改变稀土磁各向异性或施加外磁场来操纵亚铁磁序的对称性，从而调控磁振子能带交叉的拓扑性质（节点线保持或打开能隙）。

Result: 在RMn6Sn6中观察到左右旋磁振子能带在基平面内有限动量处交叉，定义了具有相反动力学手性的模式。依据亚铁磁序对称性，该交叉可保持为节点线或被打开能隙；打开能隙的模式包含杂化的手性激发，其手性随动量变化。

Conclusion: 亚铁磁体作为天然拥有无外场条件下可自由调控左右旋磁振子分裂的材料平台，为实现手性可调的磁振子激发提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Magnons in ferromagnets possess spin angular momentum defined by right-handed precession of the moment around the magnetization direction. In antiferromagnets with no net magnetization, left-and right-handed magnons are degenerate in the absence of an applied field. Ferrimagnets possess uncompensated magnetic sublattices, which should natively possess right-and left-handed magnons where their energy is split by the internal molecular field. Here, we show that RMn6Sn6 (R = Tb, Er) ferrimagnets possess right and left-handed magnon bands that cross at finite momentum (k) within the basal plane, defining modes with opposite dynamical chirality. Depending on the symmetry of the ferrimagnetic order, which may be manipulated by varying the rare-earth magnetic anisotropy or with applied field, the band crossing may remain a nodal line or may be gapped. The gapped modes contain hybridized chiral excitations whose chirality becomes k-dependent.

</details>


### [196] [Interplay of spin-orbit coupling, crystal field splitting and correlations: a ghost rotationally invariant slave boson treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00254)
*Xue Sun,Walber Hugo Brito,Andreas Gleis,Ran Adler,Tsung-Han Lee,Gabriel Kotliar,Corey Peters*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文利用最近提出的ghost旋转不变奴隶玻色子方法（GRISB）研究Sr₂RuO₄中自旋轨道耦合、晶体场劈裂与电子关联之间的相互作用，重点考察U和J对自旋轨道耦合的影响，以及在不同方法下费米面的形状和Lifshitz相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在理解Sr₂RuO₄这类强关联材料中自旋轨道耦合与电子关联的复杂耦合关系，并检验GRISB方法向动力学平均场理论（DMFT）收敛的特性，以便在低温宽参数范围内进行高效且准确的数值模拟。

Method: 采用基于密度泛函理论和线性化准粒子自洽GW（LQSGW）计算提取参数的Sr₂RuO₄紧束缚模型，在t₂g Hubbard-Kanamori框架下使用GRISB方法，通过增加ghost数量观察各物理量的收敛行为，并在不同Hubbard U和Hund耦合J参数下考察静态与动态观测量。

Result: GRISB对大多数观测量收敛迅速；计算显示Hubbard U增强自旋轨道耦合，而Hund耦合J抑制自旋轨道耦合；此外还研究了不同方法下费米面形状的差异，并考察了该材料中随应变发生的Lifshitz相变。

Conclusion: GRISB方法能够有效地在低温宽参数范围内研究Sr₂RuO₄中的关联效应，揭示了U和J对自旋轨道耦合相反方向的影响，为理解该材料的电子结构与量子相变提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We investigate the interplay of spin-orbit coupling, crystal field splittings, and electronic correlations in the $t_{2g}$ Hubbard-Kanamori model within the recently formulated ghost rotationally invariant slave-boson method (GRISB). In particular, we study a tight binding model of Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ with parameters extracted from density functional theory and linearized quasiparticle self-consistent GW (LQSGW) calculations; we study the behavior of different physical quantities as the number of ghosts increases to examine the convergence of GRISB to dynamical mean field theory (DMFT) and experimental results; and we leverage the ability of GRISB to investigate the model over a wide range of parameters at low temperature. In particular, we examine both static and dynamical observables driven by the spin orbit coupling (SOC) and study how they vary as a function of the Hubbard $U$ and Hund's coupling $J$. GRISB converges quickly for most of these observables, and the calculations reveal the following: $U$ enhances the spin-orbit coupling while $J$ suppresses it. We also study the shape of the Fermi surface within different methodologies, and examine the Lifshitz transition which takes place as a function of strain in this material.

</details>


### [197] [Breakdown of Universal Whirling Order in a Heisenberg Tsai-Type Approximant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00467)
*Farid Labib,Guillaume Beutier,Alessandro Bombardi,Marc de Boissieu,Ryuji Tamura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过X射线共振磁散射实验测量了Heisenberg和non-Heisenberg两类Au-Al-R (R = Gd, Tb) 1/1近似晶体，发现非共面涡旋磁序在Heisenberg极限下并非普适，Gd体系展现出与涡旋模型不相容的不同磁结构，表明涡旋态的普适性不仅依赖Tsai簇几何，还需自旋各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究提出非共面涡旋磁序（noncoplanar whirling magnetic order）是非Heisenberg型Tsai族准晶近似晶体中的普适磁态，但该普适性在Heisenberg极限下是否成立尚未得到验证。

Method: 研究人员对Heisenberg型Au-Al-Gd和non-Heisenberg型Au-Al-Tb两种1/1近似晶体进行了X射线共振磁散射（X-ray resonant magnetic scattering）测量，对比分析两种体系的方位角依赖（azimuthal-angle dependence）及其对应的磁结构。

Result: 测量结果表明，non-Heisenberg的Au-Al-Tb体系与先前确证的涡旋磁结构一致；而Heisenberg的Au-Al-Gd体系表现出定性不同的方位角依赖，与普适涡旋序模型不相容。尽管两种体系的晶体结构几乎相同且磁能尺度相当，它们却稳定了具有相同传播矢量k = (1, 0, 0)但不同的反铁磁基态。此外，Heisenberg极限中出现了近简并竞争磁态的流形（manifold）。

Conclusion: 涡旋磁序的普适性并非仅由Tsai簇几何决定，而是必须依赖自旋各向异性（spin anisotropy）的存在；在Heisenberg极限下，多种近简并磁态相互竞争，揭示了该体系中更丰富的磁性相图。

Abstract: Noncoplanar whirling magnetic order has recently been proposed as a universal magnetic state in non-Heisenberg Tsai-type quasicrystal approximants. Here, X-ray resonant magnetic scattering measurements on Heisenberg and non-Heisenberg Au-Al-R (R = Gd, Tb) 1/1 approximants reveal a clear breakdown of this universality in the Heisenberg limit. While the non-Heisenberg Au-Al-Tb system is well described by the previously identified whirling magnetic structure, the Heisenberg Au-Al-Gd counterpart exhibits qualitatively different azimuthal-angle dependences that are incompatible with the universal whirling-order model. Despite nearly identical crystal structures and comparable magnetic energy scales, the two systems stabilize distinct antiferromagnetic ground states with the same propagation vector k = (1, 0, 0), demonstrating that the apparent universality of whirling order is not a consequence of Tsai-cluster geometry alone but requires spin anisotropy. These results further reveal the emergence of a competing manifold of nearly degenerate magnetic states in the Heisenberg limit.

</details>


### [198] [Negative thermal expansion, lattice dynamics, and complex magnetism in TbFeO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00469)
*Shubham Farswan,Reshma Kumawat,Dipankar Sarkar,Deeksha Singh,Md. Atif Hasan,Devajyoti Mukherjee,Kaushik Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本论文通过X射线衍射、直流磁化率、拉曼散射及X射线光电子能谱对正交铁氧体TbFeO₃进行变温研究，发现其存在负热膨胀现象、自旋-声子耦合（spin-phonon coupling）、双磁振子激发（two-magnon excitation）以及约175 K以下出现的具有序参量式温度演化的宽拉曼模式，表明晶格动力学与自旋关联之间存在显著相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交铁氧体（orthoferrite）体系因具有丰富的磁性和晶格相互作用而备受关注，但TbFeO₃在低温区间的晶格动力学与自旋关联之间的耦合机制尚不清晰，本文旨在通过多维实验手段系统表征其温度依赖行为，揭示潜在的磁-晶格异常（magnetic-lattice anomaly）。

Method: 采用X射线衍射（x-ray diffraction）、直流磁化（DC magnetization）、拉曼散射（Raman scattering）及室温X射线光电子能谱（x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy）四种互补技术，在5-300 K温度范围内对TbFeO₃进行系统研究，并结合线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory）对高能拉曼光谱中的双磁振子谱进行解释。

Result: X射线衍射显示5-300 K区间存在负热膨胀（negative thermal expansion），冷却时晶胞体积呈小而系统的增加且无结构相变。拉曼模式频率偏离Klemens非谐衰减模型，表明存在自旋-声子耦合；A_g和B_{1g}对称性模式线形从低温的高斯主导转变为高温的高斯-洛伦兹混合分布。高能拉曼光谱观测到与Fe亚晶格相关的双磁振子激发，其谱重随温度变化弱。约175 K以下浮现一个宽拉曼模式，其演化具有序参量特征，并与声子异常起始温度吻合，但双磁振子响应无明显对应异常。

Conclusion: 上述结果表明TbFeO₃是一个晶格动力学、自旋关联与局域磁-晶格异常之间存在显著相互作用（interplay）的体系，为理解正交铁氧体中的磁声耦合行为提供了实验基础。

Abstract: We report a temperature-dependent investigation of orthoferrite TbFeO$_3$ using x-ray diffraction, DC magnetization, and Raman scattering, complemented by room-temperature x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy. X-ray diffraction reveals negative thermal expansion over 5-300 K, with a small but systematic increase in unit-cell volume upon cooling in the absence of any structural phase transition. Raman scattering measurements identify the Raman-active phonon modes and show clear deviations from the conventional Klemens anharmonic decay model, particularly in phonon frequencies, indicating the presence of spin-phonon coupling. Two modes of $A_g$ and $B_{1g}$ symmetry exhibit a crossover from Gaussian-dominated line shapes at low temperatures to mixed Gaussian-Lorentzian profiles at higher temperatures, reflecting a transition from inhomogeneous broadening to lifetime-driven dynamics. High-energy Raman spectra reveal two-magnon excitations associated with the Fe sublattice, consistent with linear spin-wave theory, whose spectral weight shows only weak temperature dependence. In addition, a broad Raman mode emerging below $\sim 175$ K exhibits an order-parameter-like temperature evolution and coincides with the onset of phonon anomalies, while no corresponding strong anomaly is observed in the two-magnon response. Taken together, these results establish TbFeO$_3$ as a system with pronounced interplay among lattice dynamics, spin correlations, and emergent local magnetic-lattice anomalies.

</details>


### [199] [Spin-orbit-entangled frustrated magnetism in fcc Ba$_2$(Yb,Nd)NbO$_6$ double perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01022)
*S. M. Hossain,Sk. Soyeb Ali,S. Mohanty,R. Kolay,M. P. Saravanan,A. K. Yogi,Y. Tokiwa,R. Nath,S. K. Panda,M. Majumder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究报道了两种稀土基双钙钛矿（double perovskite）材料Ba₂YbNbO₆和Ba₂NdNbO₆，它们结晶于面心立方（face-centered cubic）结构。磁化率与比热测量证实了由强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）和晶场效应产生的孤立 j_eff=1/2 克莱默双峰（Kramers doublet）基态，密度泛函理论计算进一步支持了这一结论。毫开尔文温区的热力学测量揭示了两种材料中的长程磁有序和中等程度的阻挫（frustration），其磁有序可在具有有限 Kitaev 相互作用的稀土面心立方晶格的有序-by-无序（order-by-disorder）机制中得到理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对候选 Kitaev 材料的搜索主要集中于4d和5d过渡金属化合物，而稀土4f体系的研究仅局限于蜂窝状和三角晶格。本文旨在拓展稀土基 Kitaev 磁性材料的研究范围，探索面心立方晶格中的稀土自旋轨道纠缠磁体。

Method: 采用磁化率（magnetization）和比热（heat-capacity）测量表征热力学性质，并结合密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）计算验证电子结构和有效自旋基态。

Result: 两种材料均表现出强自旋轨道耦合和晶场效应导致的孤立 j_eff=1/2 克莱默双峰基态；毫开尔文温区测量揭示两者存在伴有中等阻挫的长程磁有序。

Conclusion: Ba₂YbNbO₆和Ba₂NdNbO₆被确定为有前景的稀土自旋轨道纠缠磁体（spin-orbit-entangled magnets），其磁有序符合含有限 Kitaev 相互作用的面心立方稀土晶格的 order-by-disorder 理论预测，为进一步确定完整交换张量（exchange tensor）以阐明磁性相互作用的微观起源提供了动力。

Abstract: The search for candidate Kitaev materials has largely focused on 4$d$ and 5$d$ transition-metal compounds with various lattice geometries. In contrast, investigations of rare-earth 4$f$ systems have thus far been restricted mainly to honeycomb and triangular lattices. In this work, we investigate the rare-earth-based double perovskites Ba$_2$YbNbO$_6$ and Ba$_2$NdNbO$_6$, which crystallize in a face-centered cubic structure. Magnetization and heat-capacity measurements establish isolated ${j_{\rm eff}} = 1/2$ Kramers doublet ground states arising from strong spin-orbit coupling (SOC) and crystal electric-field effects, which are further supported by density-functional theory calculations. Millikelvin-temperature thermodynamic measurements reveal long-range magnetic order with moderate frustration in both compounds. The emergence of magnetic order may be understood within an order-by-disorder scenario, as theoretically proposed for rare-earth fcc lattices with finite Kitaev interactions. Our results thus identify Ba$_2$YbNbO$_6$ and Ba$_2$NdNbO$_6$ as promising rare-earth spin-orbit-entangled magnets and motivate further experimental and theoretical investigations aimed at determining the complete exchange tensor to elucidate the microscopic origin of the underlying magnetic interactions.

</details>


### [200] [Magnetic circular dichroism of THz modes and selection rules of Raman-active optical phonons in the polar altermagnet candidate \ce{Mn2Mo3O8}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01062)
*F. Schilberth,K. Vasin,M. Knauft,M. Kondákor,M. Vuckovic,N. Herrmann,K. Penc,M. Minola,B. Keimer,V. A. Martinez,K. Panda,A. A. Sirenko,L. Prodan,V. Tsurkan,A. A. Tsirlin,I. Kézsmárki,J. Deisenhofer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过变温拉曼散射（Raman scattering）和磁光太赫兹时域透射光谱（magneto-optical THz time-domain transmission spectroscopy）研究了共线交替磁体（collinear altermagnet）候选材料 Mn2Mo3O8 中的磁激发与振动激发。结合第一性原理（ab initio）计算识别了全部光学声子（optical phonons），并在磁有序态中发现一个仅电偶极活跃的宽太赫兹吸收带（THz band），该带可能源于双磁振子（two-magnon）激发，且在自旋翻转（spin-flop）转变以上消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体（altermagnet）作为一种新兴的磁性相态，其磁序与晶格振动和磁激发的耦合特性尚待深入理解。此外，该材料此前拉曼研究中一些最低能级的 A1 与 E2 型拉曼模式未被观测到，识别全部光学声子并检验磁有序对声子选择定则（selection rules）的影响，对于理解交替磁体的集体激发具有基础意义。

Method: 采用变温拉曼散射与磁光太赫兹时域透射光谱两种实验手段，并与准确捕获振动本征模式本征频率的第一性原理计算进行对比；同时分析顺磁（paramagnetic）与磁有序（magnetically ordered）两相中光学活性声子的选择定则，并结合膜角动量（pseudo-angular momentum）守恒对拉曼选择定则进行检验。

Result: 识别出全部光学声子，包括此前拉曼研究中未观测到的最低能级 A1 与 E2 型模式。磁有序化后预期的简并顺磁 E2 光学声子分裂为圆偏振模式的现象未被观测到，可能归因于 Mn2+ 典型的弱自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）。相反，在磁有序态中观测到宽的太赫兹激发带具有强磁圆二色性（magnetic circular dichroism），该带推测源于双磁振子激发，仅具电偶极活跃性，并呈现场依赖的双分量精细结构，其在4 T自旋翻转转变以上消失。

Conclusion: Mn2Mo3O8 在磁有序态下磁与晶格自由度间的耦合主要体现在太赫兹磁激发通道中，而非声子分裂中；弱自旋-轨道耦合抑制了预期中的拉曼模式分裂，而双磁振子型的太赫兹吸收带则成为探索其交替磁性序的重要光谱特征。这一发现为交替磁体中磁光响应的研究提供了新的实验证据。

Abstract: We investigated the magnetic and vibrational excitations in the collinear altermagnet candidate \ce{Mn2Mo3O8} by temperature dependent Raman scattering and magneto-optical THz time-domain transmission spectroscopy. By comparison to \textit{ab initio} calculations accurately capturing the eigenfrequencies of the vibrational eigenmodes, we identify all optical phonons, including the lowest-lying Raman modes of $A_1$ and $E_2$ type, which had remained elusive in a previous Raman study. Moreover, we compare the selection rules for optically active phonons in the paramagnetic and the magnetically ordered phases of \ce{Mn2Mo3O8} and analyze the Raman selection rules with respect to pseudo-angular momentum conservation. No evidence of the expected splitting of the degenerate paramagnetic $E_2$ optical phonons into modes with circular polarization upon magnetic ordering could be resolved, likely due to weak spin-orbit coupling typical for Mn$^{2+}$. In contrast, we observe strong magnetic circular dichroism at a broad THz excitation band, emerging in the magnetically ordered state. This band, potentially originating from two-magnon excitations, is only electric-dipole active and features a field-dependent two-component fine structure. Its magnetic circular dichroism vanishes above the spin-flop transition at 4~T.

</details>


### [201] [Pressure-induced self-doping and Fermi surface reconstruction in UAs2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01244)
*Zhenchao Wu,Yingying Cao,Yi-feng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）研究了重费米子化合物UAs2在压力下的电子结构，揭示了压力诱导的轨道选择性电荷重分布（自掺杂效应）如何驱动费米面重构并促成超导的出现，为理解铀基关联5f电子超导体的微观机制提供了电子结构基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAs2在压力下被发现具有重费米子超导电性，其超导转变温度（Tc）是铀基关联5f电子超导体中最高的，但其微观起源尚不清楚，需要通过第一性原理计算来阐明。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）方法，分别在常压和压力条件下计算UAs2的电子结构，并与角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）实验结果进行对比验证。

Result: 常压下计算重现了Kondo晶格特征电子结构，与ARPES一致；压力下发现电子从局域的5f5/2轨道向巡游的5f7/2轨道转移，形成自掺杂效应，驱动5f5/2电子从局域Kondo态向混合价态过渡。超导出现在费米面由两片分离薄片构成且具有增强嵌套的压力区间，当费米面弯曲合并为波纹状三维柱体时超导消失。

Conclusion: 费米面嵌套和电荷涨落可能共同贡献于增强的超导Tc，暗示其超导机制可能与传统重费米子超导体有所不同，为理解UAs2中的超导提供了电子结构层面的解释。

Abstract: Superconductivity has recently been reported in the heavy-fermion compound UAs2 under pressure, with the highest Tc among uranium-based correlated 5f-electron superconductors. To elucidate its microscopic origin, we investigate its electronic structure using density functional theory combined with dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT). At ambient pressure, our calculations reproduce the characteristic Kondo-lattice electronic structure, with flat hybridization bands near the Fermi energy around the Γ and M points, in good agreement with angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES). Under pressure, we find a systematic transfer of electrons from the more localized 5f5/2 orbitals to the more itinerant 5f7/2 orbitals, while the total U-5f occupancy remains nearly unchanged. This orbital-selective charge redistribution constitutes a pressure-induced self-doping effect that drives the 5f5/2 electrons from a localized Kondo regime toward a mixed-valence regime with enhanced charge fluctuations, leading to a dramatic reconstruction of the low-energy electronic structure. Remarkably, superconductivity emerges in the pressure range where the Fermi surface consists of two disconnected sheets with enhanced nesting, but disappears when they bend and merge into a corrugated three-dimensional cylinder. Our results provide an electronic-structure basis for understanding superconductivity in UAs2 and suggest that Fermi-surface nesting and charge fluctuations may contribute to the enhanced superconducting Tc, pointing to a possible distinction from conventional heavy-fermion superconductors.

</details>


### [202] [All-Electric Topological Phase Transitions in Proximity-Coupled Bilayer MnBi2Te4 Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01312)
*Basavaraja G,Mukul Kabir*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无外磁场的可逆机制，通过铁磁绝缘体界面的磁邻近效应控制超薄MnBi2Te4的拓扑相变，利用栅极可调磁各向异性实现层间耦合和能带拓扑的全电学切换，同时显著提升磁性有序温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维极限下的本征磁性拓扑绝缘体（magnetic topological insulator）MnBi2Te4呈现出厚度依赖的轴子态（axion state）和量子反常霍尔态（quantum anomalous Hall, QAH），但两者间的可控转换通常需要超过9特斯拉的极端外磁场，严重限制了实际可调性。作者旨在消除高场需求，探索一种场自由（field-free）、可逆的拓扑相工程途径。

Method: 采用互补的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）和有效哈密顿量建模，研究铁磁绝缘体界面上的磁邻近效应（magnetic proximity effect）；借助栅极调控铁磁绝缘体中的磁各向异性来动态调节邻近诱导的交换偏置（exchange bias），实现层间耦合的切换。此外，利用长程海森堡蒙特卡洛模拟（Heisenberg Monte Carlo simulation）评估封装MnBi2Te4薄膜的磁有序温度。

Result: 计算表明，该栅极可调机制能够在无外磁场条件下实现超薄MnBi2Te4中层间耦合和能带拓扑的全电学切换；同时，封装后的MnBi2Te4薄膜磁有序温度被大幅提升，兼具拓扑相变的电学可调性与更高的热稳定性。

Conclusion: 该栅极可调范式消除了高场依赖并同时改善热稳定性，解决了拓扑物理中的一个关键瓶颈，为可扩展、高温工作的拓扑电子学（topological electronics）提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The intrinsic magnetic topological insulator MnBi2Te4, in the two-dimensional limit, hosts thickness dependent axion and quantum anomalous Hal (QAH) insulating states governed by antiferromagnetic interlayer coupling. However, controlled interconversion between these phases typically requires extreme external magnetic fields exceeding 9 T, limiting practical tunability. Using complementary first-principles calculations and effective Hamiltonian modeling, we demonstrate a field-free, reversible mechanism to engineer topological phase transitions by exploiting magnetic proximity at the interfaces with a ferromagnetic insulator. Gate-tunable magnetic anisotropy within the ferromagnetic insulator dynamically modulates the proximity-induced exchange bias, enabling all-electric switching of interlayer coupling and band topology in ultrathin MnBi2Te4. Crucially, long-range Heisenberg Monte Carlo simulations reveal that the magnetic ordering temperature of the encapculated MnBi2Te4 film is dramatically elevated. By eliminating the high-field requirement and simultaneously improving thermal stability, this gate-tunable paradigm solves a critical bottleneck in topological physics and offers a viable route toward scalable, high-temperature topological electronics.

</details>


### [203] [Quantum geometry and RKKY in flat bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01363)
*Chang-geun Oh,Makoto Shimizu,Youichi Yanase,Shuichi Murakami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究证明在平带（flat band）极限下，RKKY相互作用并未因群速度消失而熄灭，而是由布洛赫态的量子几何（quantum geometry）所介导。研究表明量子度规（quantum metric）的布里渊区平均值控制静态磁化率的远程结构，决定磁关联长度和自旋刚度，并可能在有限尺寸体系中绕过Mermin-Wagner定理的热力学极限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带导带的群速度消失，使得传统上依赖于色散关系（dispersion）的RKKY相互作用图像面临根本性挑战，需要澄清在无色散条件下局域磁矩间是否存在有效的间接交换耦合机制。

Method: 从微观RKKY推导出发，系统分析平带极限下静态磁化率的结构，并关联量子度规的布里渊区平均值与磁关联长度、自旋刚度等物理量之间的关系。

Result: 平带极限下RKKY相互作用由布洛赫态的量子几何介导，Wannier函数的有限空间延展提供了有效的长程耦合通道；增大量子度规会增强磁性刚性（magnetic rigidity）并提升有限尺寸体系中的临界温度。

Conclusion: 确立了量子度规决定有限及低维样品中排序温度的一般性原则，从而可在有限尺寸体系中有效规避Mermin-Wagner定理（Mermin-Wagner theorem）的热力学极限限制，为平带体系中的磁性研究提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Flat conduction bands quench the group velocity and thus challenge conventional, dispersion-driven pictures of the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) interaction, where localized moments are coupled via an effective exchange mediated by conduction electrons. Here we show that RKKY interactions in the flat-band limit are not extinguished by the vanishing group velocity but are instead mediated by the quantum geometry of Bloch states. Starting from a microscopic RKKY derivation, we demonstrate that the Brillouin-zone-averaged quantum metric controls the long-wavelength structure of the static susceptibility, thereby determining the magnetic correlation length and the spin stiffness. As a result, the finite spatial spread of Wannier functions provides an effective long-range coupling channel even when single-particle dispersion is absent. Furthermore, we establish the general principle that the ordering temperature is governed by the quantum metric in finite and low-dimensional samples, effectively circumventing the thermodynamic-limit constraint of the Mermin-Wagner theorem. Specifically, our theoretical investigation reveals that increasing the quantum metric enhances magnetic rigidity and leads to a corresponding rise in the critical temperature within finite-sized systems.

</details>


### [204] [Emergent modular Luttinger liquid from spin-partitioned entanglement in the one-dimensional Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01817)
*Ádám Bácsi,Catalin Pascu Moca,Balázs Dóra*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过玻色化（bosonization）与精确对角化（exact diagonalization）相结合的方法，研究一维排斥性哈伯德模型（Hubbard model）的自旋分块纠缠哈密顿量（spin-partitioned entanglement Hamiltonian），发现其产生一种模数吕廷格液体（modular Luttinger liquid），该液体的性质与物理系统根本不同，其纠缠谱呈现普适的分支层级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相互作用一维费米子系统中的纠缠结构，特别是通过研究自旋分块后的纠缠哈密顿量来揭示其普适的模数性质。

Method: 将玻色化方法与精确对角化计算相结合，对一维排斥性哈伯德模型进行自旋分块（tracing out one spin species）处理，分析纠缠哈密顿量的谱结构、关联函数及模数模（modular eigenstates）性质。

Result: 纠缠谱完全无色散且具有与动量无关的纠缠隙（entanglement gap）；其本征态呈现由单一有效吕廷格参数控制的代数关联，该参数等于电荷与自旋吕廷格参数的几何平均；纠缠谱展现出普适的分支层级结构，与精确对角化结果高度一致；模数基态与无自旋吕廷格液体（spinless Luttinger liquid）的基态几乎相同。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了相互作用一维费米子系统中存在的普适模数结构（universal modular structure），表明自旋分块纠缠哈密顿量具有独立于物理系统的内在普适性。

Abstract: We study the spin-partitioned entanglement Hamiltonian of the one-dimensional repulsive Hubbard model. By combining bosonization with exact diagonalization, we show that tracing out one spin species produces a modular Luttinger liquid, whose properties fundamentally differ from those of the physical system. While the modular spectrum is fully dispersionless and possesses a momentum-independent entanglement gap, its eigenstates exhibit algebraic correlations governed by a single effective Luttinger parameter equal to the geometric mean of the charge and spin Luttinger parameters. The resulting entanglement spectrum displays a universal branching hierarchy in excellent agreement with exact diagonalization. We further demonstrate that the modular ground state is nearly identical to that of the spinless Luttinger liquid. These results uncover a universal modular structure in interacting one-dimensional fermionic systems.

</details>


### [205] [Topological Defects in Triple-$Q$ Magnetic Orders: A Fixed-Lattice Homotopy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01838)
*Jin-Tao Jin,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对N=2和N=3情况下、具有(V₄⋊D₃)×O(N)对称性的M点三重-Q（triple-Q）金兹堡-朗道理论中所有七个稳定相的体缺陷进行了分类，发现正交三重-Q相具有完整的O(3)流形并支持手征性壁和AbelZ₂框架涡旋，而OO(2)相中的分数风数须依附于离散畴壁才能闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重-Q（multiple-Q）磁有序将连续自旋转动与离散晶体扇区（平移和点群变换）结合，产生比传统单-Q磁体更丰富的缺陷结构，需要系统分类。

Method: 将原子晶格视为预定背景，排除晶格位错和向错，保留三个傅里叶场的物理M点标签。利用连续连接到单位元的父群变换构成G₀={e}×SO(N)，通过参考态稳定子H的连通分量G₀/(H∩G₀)而非直接投影H到自旋空间来分类缺陷。

Result: 正交三重-Q相具有完整流形O(3)，呈现手征性壁（chirality walls）和AbelZ₂框架涡旋而非非Abel二元多面体涡旋；OO(2)相的每个连通分量支持整数2π涡旋，而分数风数需附着于离散畴壁且在有非零壁张力时线性禁闭。平移对称性禁止交叉梯度双线性项，将二次弹性扇区简化为各向同性和M点锁定的各向异性两种刚度。

Conclusion: 该分类将三重-Q磁体中的缺陷区分为自由的内部缺陷、晶体畴壁和壁束缚复合体三类，阐明了多重-Q有序系统中对称性对阵形和缺陷拓扑结构的深刻影响。

Abstract: Multiple-$Q$ magnetic orders combine continuous spin rotations with discrete crystalline sectors associated with translations and point-group transformations, producing a richer defect structure than conventional single-$Q$ magnets. We classify the bulk defects of all seven stable phases for $N=2$ and $3$ in the $M$-point triple-$Q$ Ginzburg--Landau theory with $(\Vfour\rtimes\Dthree)\times\OO(N)$ symmetry, where $\Vfour$ is the translation-generated Klein four-group. The atomic lattice is treated as a prescribed background, with lattice dislocations and disclinations excluded and the three Fourier fields retaining their physical $M$-point labels. The parent-group transformations continuously connected to the identity form $G_0=\{e\}\times\SO(N)$. For a reference-state stabilizer $H$, the connected component containing the reference state is $G_0/(H\cap G_0)$, not the quotient obtained by projecting $H$ onto spin space. This distinction gives the orthogonal triple-$Q$ phase the full manifold $\OO(3)$, with chirality walls and Abelian $\ZZ_2$ frame vortices rather than non-Abelian binary-polyhedral vortices. Every connected component of the $\OO(2)$ phases supports an integer $2π$ vortex, whereas fractional windings close only when attached to a discrete-domain wall and are linearly confined at nonzero wall tension. Translation symmetry further forbids cross-gradient bilinears, reducing the quadratic elastic sector to an isotropic and an $M$-point-locked anisotropic stiffness. The classification separates free internal defects, crystalline domain walls, and wall-bound composites in triple-$Q$ magnets.

</details>


### [206] [Diodes and capacitors for the transport of monopoles in fragmented spin ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01883)
*Anoop Raj,Sumiran Pujari,Ludovic D. C. Jaubert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在理论上证明了基于自旋冰（spin ice）中磁性碎片化（magnetic fragmentation）体系所形成畴壁，可设计用于磁单极子（magnetic monopole）输运的二极管和电容器。该概念验证通过超过一百万自旋的模拟加以验证，并讨论了在稀土烧绿石氧化物和人工自旋冰中的实验应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋冰材料中准粒子激发行为类似于磁单极子，其动力学的"磁性电导"（magnetricity）概念已被广泛研究，但鲜有工作聚焦于设计控制磁性电流的器件。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出可用于控制磁单极子输运的二极管与电容器设计方案。

Method: 利用磁性碎片化体系中自旋冰物理与长程反铁磁序共存的性质，该体系中的畴壁（domain wall）在特定制备条件下可作为磁单极子的不对称过滤器：正电荷可沿某一方向通过而负电荷被排斥，反之在相反方向亦然，从而等效于二极管功能。连续排列的畴壁可实现正负电荷分离并形成电荷真空区，构成电容器。所有微观机制均通过超过一百万自旋的模拟进行验证。

Result: 模拟结果验证了片段化自旋冰中畴壁可充当磁单极子二极管和电容器的概念可行性。充电后的电容器原则上可作为磁单极子电池使用。该工作还阐明了畴壁可被理解为由时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）分隔的两个镜像"世界"之间的涌现边界（emergent boundary）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的概念验证为设计控制磁单极子电流的器件提供了理论基础，并将应用前景扩展到稀土烧绿石氧化物与人工自旋冰等实验系统。此外，该思路为手性自旋液体和向列自旋液体中涌现准粒子跨畴壁动力学研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Spin-ice materials are famous for their quasi-particle excitations that behave like magnetic monopoles. Magnetricity is the concept that these monopoles can conduct an AC magnetic current, in analogy with conduction electrons. While monopole dynamics has been intensively studied and is reasonably well understood, very little has been done to design devices in order to control magnetricity. Here we develop a theoretical proof of concept for the design of diodes and capacitors for the transport of monopoles. We use the property of systems with magnetic fragmentation, where spin-ice physics co-exists with long-range antiferromagnetic order. The key point is that magnetic order allows for the existence of domain walls. Under certain conditions of preparation, this domain wall is equivalent to an asymmetric filter for monopoles. In a given direction, positive charges can go through while negative ones are repelled; the opposite applies in the opposite direction. This asymmetry effectively functions like a diode for monopole current. Successive domain walls separate positive from negative charges with a vacuum of charge in between, producing a capacitor for monopoles. Once the capacitor is charged, it can in principle be used as a battery for monopoles. All microscopic mechanisms are explained and our proof of concept is validated by simulations of more than a million spins. Application to experiments are discussed for rare-earth pyrochlore oxides and artificial spin ice. Finally, we discuss in general terms how a domain wall in fragmented spin ice can also be seen as an emergent boundary separating two mirror "worlds" separated by time-reversal symmetry. Beyond spin ice, our work opens a promising direction of investigation for the dynamics of emergent quasi-particles crossing domain walls in chiral and nematic spin liquids, which also possess a broken symmetry.

</details>


### [207] [Microscopic magnetic phase evolution in the Weyl semimetal Mn$_3$Sn revealed by $μ^+$SR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01892)
*U. Miniotaite,O. K. Forslund,H. Luetkens,V. Rai,J. Persson,G. D. Morris,D. Zuhair,D. W. Tam,S. Nandi,Y. Sassa,M. Månsson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过μ子自旋弛豫（muon spin relaxation，μ⁺SR）和体磁化率测量，系统研究了反铁磁（antiferromagnetic，AFM）Weyl半金属Mn₃Sn的磁性相图与动态磁性，发现低温区不存在静态自旋玻璃态，而是呈现增强的铁磁分量与局域化自旋涨落减慢的共存，且在不相称螺旋反铁磁相中磁结构受非谐性（anharmonicity）显著修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn₃Sn是一种备受关注的反铁磁Weyl半金属，其磁性结构包含公度反三角（IT）反铁磁相、不相称（IC）螺旋反铁磁相以及可能存在的低温自旋玻璃态，但各相的性质与共存机制尚不清晰，需要高灵敏度的局域探测手段加以厘清。

Method: 采用μ⁺SR和体磁化率测量，分别对零场（zero-field，ZF）谱进行拟合以提取内部磁场分布，并通过温度依赖的初始不对称性（initial asymmetry）变化追踪不同磁相之间的演变与共存。

Result: 确定了三个特征温度：T_N=418 K、T_t≈275 K和T_f=21 K。在T_f以下未观察到静态自旋玻璃态，而是铁磁分量增强伴随局域化自旋涨落减慢，二者可能解耦；IC螺旋相中磁场分布不对称表明非谐性强烈修饰磁结构；150 K以上出现IC螺旋相与IT反铁磁相共存的局域磁环境；公度IT相中约20%的样品相关不对称性发生未分辨的超快退极化；325 K以上观察到μ子占据位点随温度迁移。

Conclusion: Mn₃Sn展现高度动态的磁性景观，其复杂的磁有序态常常共存并随温度连续演变，而非由截然分离的相界所刻画。

Abstract: We report a comprehensive muon spin relaxation ($μ^+$SR) and bulk magnetization study of the antiferromagnetic (AFM) Weyl semimetal Mn$_3$Sn (composition Mn$_{2.99}$Sn). Mn$_3$Sn is reported to exhibit a commensurate inverse triangular (IT) AFM phase, an incommensurate (IC) helical AFM phase, and a proposed low-temperature spin-glass-like state. In our sample, we establish the respective transition temperatures for these phases to be $T_\mathrm{N} = 418$ K, $T_\mathrm{t} \approx 275$ K, and $T_\mathrm{f} = 21$ K. Investigating the low-temperature regime below $T_\mathrm{f}$, we find no evidence of a static spin-glass state. Instead, the sample exhibits an increasing ferromagnetic (FM) component accompanied by a localized slowing of spin fluctuations, indicating that these phenomena may be decoupled. In the IC helical AFM phase, fitting the zero-field (ZF) spectra reveals an asymmetric internal magnetic-field distribution, indicating that the magnetic structure is heavily modified by anharmonicity. Upon warming above 150 K, a continuous redistribution of muon spectral weight reveals a broad regime of coexisting local magnetic environments associated with the IC helical and IT-AFM phases. In the commensurate IT-AFM phase above $T_\mathrm{t}$, a persistent missing fraction in the initial asymmetry indicates that a subset of implanted muons, corresponding to roughly 20% of the sample-related asymmetry, undergoes unresolved ultrafast depolarization. Finally, we observe temperature-driven shifts in muon site populations above 325 K. Ultimately, our results show a highly dynamic magnetic landscape in Mn$_3$Sn, demonstrating how its complex magnetic orders often coexist and evolve continuously with temperature.

</details>


### [208] [Pressure-Driven Evolution of Electronic and Magnetic Correlations in Bilayer Nickelate La3Ni2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02261)
*Jian Zhou,Rui Song,Haiyan Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过结合密度泛函理论（DFT）、约束随机相位近似（cRPA）和动力学平均场理论（DMFT），研究了加压双层La3Ni2O7中电子与磁关联的演变，发现高压下Kondo屏蔽效应主导超交换相互作用，从而抑制了有效磁交换耦合（Jeff），为穹顶状超导相图提供了微观解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压双层La3Ni2O7中高温超导现象的发现引发了广泛关注，但其随压力变化的超导转变温度（Tc）的微观机制尚不明确，亟需理论解释。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）、约束随机相位近似（constrained random phase approximation, cRPA）与动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）相结合的第一性原理计算方法，系统考察高压下La3Ni2O7的电子与磁关联特性。

Result: 静水压增强了层间跳跃参量与裸超交换能标（4t2/U），但同时通过降低相对关联强度（U/W）将体系推向更巡游（itinerant）的区域。体系呈现轨道选择性演化：Ni dx2-y2态逐渐变得更巡游，而Ni dz2轨道保持更局域（localized）特征，这显著增强了两者间的杂化，使局域dz2磁矩被巡游dx2-y2电子Kondo式屏蔽急剧放大，导致有效磁交换耦合（Jeff）在高压区被抑制。

Conclusion: 高压区Tc的单调下降由Kondo屏蔽对超交换相互作用的优势所驱动，这为La3Ni2O7穹顶状超导相图提供了一致的微观解释。

Abstract: The recent discovery of high-temperature superconductivity in pressurized bilayer La3Ni2O7 has sparked intense research interest, yet the microscopic mechanism governing its pressure-dependent superconducting transition temperature (Tc) remains elusive. In this work, we investigate the electronic and magnetic correlations of La3Ni2O7 under high pressure using a combination of density-functional theory (DFT), constrained random phase approximation (cRPA), and dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). We find that while hydrostatic pressure enhances the interlayer hopping and the bare superexchange energy scale (4t2/U), it simultaneously drives the system toward a more itinerant regime by reducing the relative correlation strength (U/W). Crucially, our results reveal a distinct orbital-selective evolution: the Ni dx2-y2 states become increasingly itinerant, whereas the Ni dz2 orbitals retain a more localized character. This pressure-induced itinerancy significantly enhances the hybridization between the two, leading to a dramatic amplification of the Kondo-like screening of the local dz2 moments by the itinerant dx2-y2 electrons. Consequently, the effective magnetic exchange coupling (Jeff), which serves as the pairing glue, is suppressed in the high-pressure regime. Our findings suggest that the monotonic decrease of Tc at high pressures is driven by the dominance of Kondo screening over superexchange interactions, providing a coherent microscopic explanation for the dome-shaped superconducting phase diagram in La3Ni2O7.

</details>


### [209] [Long and short time linear response of metals: a geometric approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02298)
*Nishchhal Verma,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了金属中时间依赖量子几何张量（time-dependent quantum geometric tensor）的作用。尽管该张量通常被认为在由带内过程主导的金属低能描述中是次级项（subleading），作者指出费米面附近波函数的量子几何在特定场景下具有重要作用，并通过对kagome金属的量化分析，提出Drude与总谱权之比D/S₁可作为晶格尺度上束缚电荷与巡游电荷的探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间依赖量子几何张量捕捉束缚电子的偶极涨落，对理解绝缘体、超导体和平带性质至关重要，但在金属中通常被视为次级贡献。作者希望重新审视这一观点，寻找金属中费米面附近量子几何发挥重要作用的场景，并建立一种晶格尺度上区分束缚与巡游电荷的诊断工具。

Method: 作者计算了金属的时间依赖量子几何张量，解析其发散行为（divergence），并与狄拉克（Dirac）和韦尔（Weyl）半金属中奇异几何张量进行对比。此外，作者定义了Drude谱权与总谱权之比D/S₁这一晶格尺度探针，并将其应用于kagome金属，比较两种范霍夫填充（van Hove fillings）下的响应差异。

Result: 研究发现，尽管量子几何张量在金属中通常为次级项，但在费米面附近的特定场景下具有显著作用。D/S₁比值被识别为区分束缚电荷与巡游电荷的晶格尺度探针，并在kagome金属中量化结果表明，两种范霍夫填充尽管拥有相同的费米面，却表现出不同的响应行为。

Conclusion: 量子几何张量并非在金属描述中一概可忽略，费米面附近波函数的量子几何可能在特定金属体系（如kagome金属）中扮演关键角色。D/S₁比值提供了一种实用的晶格尺度判据，用于探测束缚与巡游电荷的竞争关系，且对范霍夫填充细节敏感，即使费米面相同也可能产生不同响应。

Abstract: The time-dependent quantum geometric tensor, which captures dipole fluctuations of bound electrons, is essential for understanding the electronic properties of insulators, superconductors, and flat bands. It is often considered subleading for low-energy descriptions of metals that are dominated by intra-band processes. Here, we revisit this perspective and highlight scenarios where the quantum geometry of the wavefunctions close to the Fermi surface plays a significant role. We compute the time-dependent quantum geometric tensor for metals, explain its divergence, and contrast it against singular geometric tensors of Dirac and Weyl semi-metals. We identify the ratio of Drude to total spectral weight, $D/\mathcal{S}_1$, as a lattice-scale probe of bound versus itinerant charge, and quantify it in the kagome metal, where the two van Hove fillings respond differently despite identical Fermi surfaces.

</details>


### [210] [Optimized Tensor-Network Renormalization for Quantum Dynamics: Resolving the Spectral Function of $\mathrm{K_2Co(SeO_3)_2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02473)
*Jiahang Hu,Runze Chi,B. Normand,Hai-Jun Liao,T. Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种激发态定制的角传递矩阵重整化群（excitation-tailored corner-transfer-matrix renormalization group，ET-CTMRG）方法，用于解决无限投影纠缠对态（iPEPS）框架下二维量子系统动力学谱计算中的数值不稳定性问题，并在海森堡反铁磁体和三角格XXZ磁体K₂Co(SeO₃)₂的超固态相中取得了与中子散射实验高度一致的定量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络方法为二维量子系统动力学谱函数的研究提供了强大途径，但现有无限投影纠缠对态框架下的方法仅从基态环境构建所需的重整化张量，可能导致严重的数值不稳定，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 作者首先识别了数值不稳定性的根源，然后提出ET-CTMRG方法，通过将激发张量整合进重整化过程，构建更精确的有效哈密顿量矩阵，从而获得可靠且收敛良好的激发谱。

Result: 对于海森堡反铁磁体，该方法将截断误差降低了多个数量级；对于三角格XXZ磁体K₂Co(SeO₃)₂中超固态这一特别复杂的相，该方法获得了与非弹性中子散射测量优异的定量一致性。

Conclusion: ET-CTMRG为研究强关联量子系统的动力学性质提供了一个稳健的框架，克服了此前方法在数值稳定性和精度方面的局限。

Abstract: Tensor-network methods have opened a powerful route for the study of dynamical spectral functions in two-dimensional quantum systems. However, existing approaches within the framework of infinite projected entangled-pair states construct the required renormalization tensors solely from the ground-state environment and can suffer from severe numerical instability. We identify the origin of this instability and introduce an excitation-tailored corner-transfer-matrix renormalization-group (ET-CTMRG) method to resolve it. By incorporating excitation tensors into the renormalization procedure, the method constructs a substantially more accurate effective Hamiltonian matrix and thereby yields reliable and well-converged excitation spectra. For Heisenberg antiferromagnets, it reduces truncation errors by orders of magnitude and for the particularly complex case of the supersolid phase in the triangular-lattice XXZ magnet $\mathrm{K_2Co(SeO_3)_2}$, it achieves excellent quantitative agreement with inelastic neutron-scattering measurements. ET-CTMRG therefore provides a robust framework for investigating the dynamical properties of strongly correlated quantum systems.

</details>

### [211] [Effects of light-cluster degrees of freedom on collective flows in heavy-ion collisions at FOPI energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02383)
*Xin Li,Si-Pei Wang,Rui Wang,Zhen Zhang,Jie Pu,Chun-Wang Ma,Lie-Wen Chen*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文在晶格玻尔兹曼-尤林-乌伦贝克输运模型（lattice Boltzmann–Uehling–Uhlenbeck transport model）中耦合轻核团簇（light-cluster）形成的动力学方法，研究了显式轻核自由度对FOPI能量下Au+Au碰撞集体流（collective flows）的影响，发现动力学团簇效应在E_beam=120–150 A MeV时显著改变质子v1–v4流，在250–400 A MeV仍可见，在600 A MeV以上逐渐减弱，且能定性复现实验观测的v2/A核子数标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解轻核团簇形成对重离子碰撞中集体流观测量（collective flow observables）的影响，需要超越传统仅包含核子自由度的输运框架，引入显式的轻核动力学自由度以更准确地解释实验数据。

Method: 采用耦合了轻核团簇形成动力学的晶格玻尔兹曼-尤林-乌伦贝克输运模型（lattice Boltzmann–Uehling–Uhlenbeck transport model），并使用依赖密度、动量和同位旋的N5LO Skyrme赝势（Skyrme pseudopotential）。首先通过中心Au+Au碰撞中的轻核产额与FOPI数据对比进行基准检验，随后分析中间碰撞参数下质子与轻核（氘核、氚核、³He、⁴He）的集体流，并将含与不含动力学轻核自由度的质子流计算结果进行比较。

Result: 动力学轻核团簇效应在E_beam=120–150 A MeV时显著修正质子v1–v4流，在250–400 A MeV时仍可见，在≥600 A MeV时逐渐减弱；轻核流的束能量依赖性与FOPI数据整体一致，且在≥400 A MeV时符合更好；该动力学方法能定性复现v2/A核子数标度行为。

Conclusion: 在约600 A MeV以下的重离子碰撞中，动力学处理轻核团簇形成对解释集体流至关重要；而在更高碰撞能量下，团簇效应对质子流的影响较小。

Abstract: Within a lattice Boltzmann-Uehling-Uhlenbeck transport model coupled to a kinetic approach for light-cluster formation, we investigate the impact of explicit light-cluster degrees of freedom on collective flows in Au+Au collisions at FOPI energies with beam energies $E_{\rm beam}$= $120$--$1500 A$ MeV by using a density-, momentum-, and isospin-dependent N$5$LO Skyrme pseudopotential. We first benchmark the kinetic approach by comparing the calculated light-cluster yields with FOPI data in central Au+Au collisions. We then analyze the collective flows of protons and light nuclei (deuterons, tritons, $^{3}\mathrm{He}$, and $^{4}\mathrm{He}$) in mid-central collisions. For protons, calculations with and without dynamical light-cluster degrees of freedom are compared to quantify the influence of dynamical cluster formation on proton directed ($v_1$), elliptic ($v_2$), triangular ($v_3$), and quadrangular ($v_4$) flows. We find that the dynamical light-cluster effect appreciably modifies proton $v_1$--$v_4$ flows at $E_{\rm beam}=120$--$150 A$ MeV, remains visible at $E_{\rm beam}=250$--$400 A$ MeV, and gradually weakens at $E_{\rm beam}\gtrsim 600 A$ MeV. For light nuclei, the kinetic approach captures the overall beam-energy dependence of the FOPI flow data, with better agreement for $E_{\rm beam}\geq 400 A$ MeV. We further examine the nucleon-number scaling of $v_2/A$ in both model calculations and experimental data, finding that the kinetic light-cluster formation approach qualitatively reproduces the observed scaling behavior. These results highlight the importance of a dynamical treatment of light-cluster formation for interpreting collective flows in heavy-ion collisions below about $600 A$ MeV, although the clustering effects on proton flows are minor at higher collision energies.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>
