### [212] [An asymptotically solvable model of many-body critical phases: mobility edges, scars, and inverted scars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00157)
*Yi-Ting Tu,Zi-Jian Li,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个可渐近求解的一维相互作用自旋链模型，其空间结构诱导了层次化的镜像多体共振，导致热力学极限下出现两种相，分别满足和违反弱本征态热化假说（ETH）。两相之间存在有限温度相变（对应热力学多体迁移率边），且存在多体疤痕和倒转疤痕等稀有本征态。结果还暗示准周期MBL中可能存在离域稀有区域，挑战了无雪崩不稳定性的传统观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机多体局域化（MBL）系统的预热（prethermal）区由偶然的多体共振主导，而大尺寸确定性系统中还存在源于势垒结构的另一类共振，可导致单粒子临界相，但其在相互作用系统中的后果尚不清楚。本文旨在澄清这类结构性共振在相互作用系统中的物理后果。

Method: 构建了一个一维最近邻相互作用自旋链的可渐近求解模型，其空间结构诱导出层次化的镜像多体共振。在热力学极限下推导两种相，以弱本征态热化假说的满足与违反来表征，并分析稀有本征态的异常行为以及两相之间的转变性质。

Result: 两种相类似于通常的MBL相和ETH相，但存在表现为相反相行为的稀有本征态，即多体疤痕（many-body scars）和倒转疤痕（inverted scars）。两相可被有限温度相变所分隔，对应于热力学多体迁移率边（many-body mobility edge），这与传统认知相悖。此外，在局域化的相互作用Aubry-André模型中发现了离域稀有区域的存在。

Conclusion: 结构性多体共振可在确定性系统中诱导出不同于随机系统的MBL相变行为，包括有限温度相变和稀有异常本征态。这些发现挑战了准周期MBL中不存在雪崩（avalanche）不稳定性的普遍信念。

Abstract: While the prethermal regime of random many-body localized (MBL) systems is dominated by accidental many-body resonances, another class of resonances, originating from the underlying potential structure, is expected in large-size deterministic systems. It is known that this class of resonances can lead to single-particle critical phases that are neither localized nor extended, but the consequences in interacting systems remain unclear. In this work, we construct an asymptotically solvable model of a one-dimensional nearest-neighbor interacting spin chain, whose spatial structure induces a hierarchy of mirror-like many-body resonances. We derive two phases in the thermodynamic limit, characterized by the satisfaction and violation of a version of the weak eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH). While these two phases are similar to the usual MBL and ETH phases, there exist rare eigenstates that behave like the opposite phase, interpreted as many-body scars and inverted scars. Surprisingly, the two phases can be separated by a finite-temperature phase transition, corresponding to a thermodynamic many-body mobility edge, which was often believed to be impossible. Our results also suggest the existence of delocalized rare regions in an otherwise-localized interacting Aubry-André model, even if there are no low-disorder regions like those in random systems. This challenges the common belief that there is no avalanche instability in quasiperiodic MBL.

</details>


### [213] [Neural Quantum States for Nuclear Magnetic Resonance Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00178)
*Bharadwaj Chowdary Mummaneni,Bo Xing*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文利用神经量子态（Neural Quantum States, NQS）作为变分量子态的替代表示，结合时间相关变分原理（Time-Dependent Variational Principle, TDVP）和投影时间相关变分蒙特卡洛（p-tVMC）两种传播方法，计算了4个实验参数化分子（2至5个自旋）的^1H核磁共振（NMR）谱。TDVP在所有谱线位置和强度上均与实验吻合（平均谱均方误差<10^-3）；通过将传播转换到化学位移哈密顿量的相互作用框架，可将积分步数减少约8倍，并成功实现了14自旋分子（蔗糖）的精确传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统从第一性原理预测NMR谱需要对2^N维量子态进行传播，当耦合自旋数目N增大时计算变得不可行。本文旨在探索神经量子态（Neural Quantum States, NQS）这类以人工神经网络表达的变分量子态，作为该问题的高效替代表示方案。

Method: 采用两种量子态传播方法——时间相关变分原理（TDVP）与投影时间相关变分蒙特卡洛（p-tVMC）——对四个实验参数化的分子（2至5个自旋）计算其^1H NMR谱。为克服积分步数随谱带宽陡增的问题，将传播变换到化学位移哈密顿量的相互作用参考系（interaction frame）中执行。

Result: TDVP方法以平均谱均方误差<10^-3准确再现了所有谱线位置和强度；p-tVMC方法也复现了相同的谱特征，其精度取决于每步优化参数。相互作用参考系传播在保持同等精度的同时，使3自旋系统积分步数减少约8倍，4和5自旋系统减少至少一个数量级，并成功实现了14自旋分子（sucrose）的精确蒙特卡洛传播。

Conclusion: 神经量子态结合相互作用参考系传播方案可有效克服维度灾难，将NMR谱预测的可行范围从5自旋以内扩展到14自旋系统。该结果为基于神经网络的量子多体模拟方法在大规模NMR谱预测中的应用提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Predicting a nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectrum from first principles requires propagating a quantum state of dimension $2^N$ for $N$ coupled spins, which becomes intractable beyond larger $N$. We benchmark Neural Quantum States (NQS), a class of variational quantum states expressed as an artificial neural network, as an alternative representation for this problem. Using two propagation methods, the Time-Dependent Variational Principle (TDVP) and projected time-dependent Variational Monte Carlo (p-tVMC), we compute the $^1$H spectra of four ($2 \to 5$ spins) experimentally parameterized molecules. TDVP reproduces all line positions and intensities with average spectral mean squared errors of $<10^{-3}$; p-tVMC reproduces the same features, with accuracy determined by its per-step optimization parameters. One dominant obstacle to larger systems is the steep growth of the number of integration steps with spectral bandwidth, which can be removed by propagating in the interaction frame of the chemical-shifted Hamiltonian. Retaining the same accuracy, this reduces the number of integration steps roughly eightfold for the 3-spin system and by at least an order of magnitude for the 4- and 5-spin systems, and it enables a 14-spin molecule (sucrose) to be accurately propagated via Monte Carlo sampling.

</details>


### [214] [Spin Quantum Hall Effect: the Critical Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00849)
*Hrant Topchyan,Win Nuding,Andreas Klümper,Ara Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文利用近期发展的S矩阵（S-matrix）方法对自旋量子霍尔效应（spin quantum Hall effect, SQHE）网络模型进行了高精度数值计算，精确测定了局域化长度临界指数ν和边界临界指数μ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋量子霍尔效应提供了少数几个已知精确临界指数的安德森局域化（Anderson localization）转变实例，是检验无序拓扑系统理论和共形场论（conformal field theory）的重要测试平台。

Method: 采用近期发展的S矩阵方法（S-matrix approach）对随机网络（random network）进行高精度数值计算，该网络模型通过将Chalker-Coddington模型中的随机U(1)相位替换为随机SU(2)矩阵构建，属于Altland-Zirnbauer分类中的C对称性类别（symmetry class C）。

Result: 计算得到了局域化长度临界指数（localization-length exponent）ν和边界临界指数（boundary critical exponent）μ的高精度数值估计。

Conclusion: 该结果有望为无序拓扑系统理论、共形场论以及二维无序超导体中的准粒子输运研究提供更精确的数值基准。

Abstract: The spin quantum Hall effect (SQHE) provides one of the few examples of an Anderson localization transition for which exact critical exponents are known, making it an important testing ground for theories of disordered topological systems and conformal field theory. The corresponding network model, obtained by replacing the random $U(1)$ phases of the Chalker--Coddington model with random $SU(2)$ matrices, belongs to symmetry class C of the Altland--Zirnbauer classification and is believed to describe quasiparticle transport in two-dimensional disordered superconductors with broken time-reversal symmetry. In this work, we perform high-precision numerical calculations of the localization-length exponent ($ν$) and the boundary critical exponent ($μ$) for the SQHE network model using the recently developed $S$-matrix approach to random networks.

</details>


### [215] [Predictive Formulas for Scattering Mean Free Path for General Disordered Dielectric Media Beyond the Long-Wavelength Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01016)
*Jaeuk Kim,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文推导了统计均匀的两相介电介质（two-phase dielectric media）在维度 d=1,2,3 下的散射平均自由程（scattering mean free path）ℓs 的预测公式。公式基于有效动态介电常数的精确强对比展开（strong-contrast expansion），适用于任意形状、多分散颗粒介质及非颗粒介质，并通过有限差分时域（finite-difference time-domain）模拟对五种非超均匀与超均匀（hyperuniform）模型进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Mie散射估计仅适用于相同半径的圆形或球形散射体，无法处理任意形状、多分散颗粒以及非颗粒介质。作者旨在建立一种更具普适性、可基于微结构信息预测波输运性质的解析框架。

Method: 该方法利用有效动态介电常数的精确强对比展开推导散射平均自由程的闭合公式，微结构信息通过谱密度（spectral density）进入公式。作者将公式应用于五种超均匀与非超均匀模型，并采用有限差分时域模拟对预测结果进行验证。

Result: 当 k1/s ≲ 1（k1 为入射波数，s 为比表面）时，预测与模拟结果吻合良好，且在适用范围内与Mie理论一致；对二维横磁极化（transverse-magnetic polarization）情形精度显著提高。当 k1/s ≳ 1 时，Mie估计更为准确。对超均匀介质，若在低 k 下 χ̃V(k) ∼ k^α，理论预测 ℓs ∼ k1^{-(d+1+α)}；隐身超均匀（stealthy hyperuniform）介质在有限波数区间内呈透明特性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种基于微结构的解析途径，可预测并设计一般无序介电材料中的波输运行为，超越了传统Mie理论的适用范围。

Abstract: We derive predictive formulas for the scattering mean free path $\ell_s$ of statistically homogeneous two-phase dielectric media in dimensions $d=1,2,3$. Unlike Mie-based estimates limited to identical circular or spherical scatterers, the formulas apply to arbitrarily shaped and polydisperse particulate media as well as nonparticulate media, with microstructure entering through the spectral density. The formulas are based on the exact strong-contrast expansion for the effective dynamic dielectric constant. We apply them to five nonhyperuniform and hyperuniform models and validate selected cases using finite-difference time-domain simulations. For $k_1/s \lesssim 1$, where $k_1$ is the incident wavenumber and $s$ is the specific surface, the predictions agree well with simulations and are consistent with Mie theory where applicable, while improving accuracy for two-dimensional transverse-magnetic polarization. Mie estimates become more accurate for $k_1/s \gtrsim 1$. For hyperuniform media with $\widetildeχ_V(k)\sim k^α$ at small $k$, the theory predicts $\ell_s\sim k_1^{-(d+1+α)}$; stealthy hyperuniform media are transparent over a finite wavenumber interval. These results provide a microstructure-based route to predict and design wave transport in general disordered dielectric materials.

</details>


### [216] [Griffiths phase in clique percolation in random geometric graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01487)
*Vasilii Tiselko,Olga Valba,Alexander Gorsky*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本研究讨论了随机几何图（random geometric graphs）系综中不同核函数（kernels）下的团渗流（clique percolation），发现尖锐截断下存在具有幂律行为的宽 Griffiths 相，幂律核下识别出团尺寸依赖的边界，并简要分析了指数核下的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同几何约束核函数如何影响随机几何图中的团渗流行为，特别是 Griffiths 相的存在及其边界性质。

Method: 在随机几何图系综中，针对三种不同核函数（尖锐截断、幂律核、指数核）分析团渗流行为，考察各参数空间中的渗流临界点和边界结构。

Result: 尖锐截断核下发现宽 Griffiths 相（Griffiths phase）并具有幂律行为；其一侧边界对应于 ER 系综（ER ensemble）渗流临界点的推广（大而有限簇内出现渗流），另一侧边界对应于整个聚类系统可发生渗流的参数点。幂律核下识别出团尺寸依赖的有效 ER 与几何区域之间的边界，并同样存在 Griffiths 相。

Conclusion: 不同核函数产生不同的团渗流模式，Griffiths 相在多种核下均存在，所发现的结果可能具有潜在的实际应用价值。

Abstract: In this study, we discuss the clique percolation in the ensembles of random geometric graphs with different kernels that quantify the geometrical constraints. For the sharp cut-off we find the wide Griffiths phase of extended criticality with the power-law behavior. One boundary of the Griffiths phase is the generalization of a percolation critical point for the ER ensemble when the percolation within the large but finite cluster emerges. The second boundary corresponds to the point in the parameter space when the percolation in the entire clustered system becomes available. For the power-law kernel, richer behavior with a clique-size-dependent boundary between the effective ER and geometric regimes has been identified. The Griffiths phase in this case exists as well. Finally, the pattern with the exponential kernel has been analyzed. We briefly discuss the possible applications of our findings.

</details>


### [217] [Tunneling the Loss Landscape: Bypassing Memorization with Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01833)
*Lai Shun Chan,Xiaotian Zhang,Yue Shang,Ge Zhang,Entao Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文从统计物理学（statistical physics）的视角将神经网络训练中的"顿悟"（grokking）现象刻画为一种计算玻璃弛豫过程，并提出三组件框架（参数迁移率、副本关联、分形维数）直接表征训练动力学，进而引入受交换蒙特卡洛（swap Monte Carlo）算法启发的优化插件SAM-Swap来加速泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于grokking的解释多依赖经典机器学习机制（如权重范数），而来自统计物理学的计算玻璃弛豫理论虽然在宏观层面提供了统一框架，却缺乏对训练动力学的直接实证验证。

Method: 引入三组件框架，通过参数迁移率（parameter mobility, PM）以及玻璃动力学中的两个代表性测量指标——副本关联（replica correlation, RC）和分形维数（fractal dimension, FD）来直接表征训练动力学；并基于此提出状态感知蒙特卡洛参数交换（SAM-Swap）优化插件。

Result: 标准优化过程展现出清晰的玻璃动力学特征，使grokking网络陷入动力学受阻的记忆化状态（kinetic arrested memorization state），表现为迁移率坍塌、强历史依赖性和通道状运动。对比SAM-Swap、权重衰减和高斯梯度噪声后发现，加速泛化始终与参数空间中的随机探索（类似物理中的扩散）相关。

Conclusion: 训练动力学与玻璃动力学之间存在定量吻合，SAM-Swap作为一种优化插件能够有效加速泛化，且加速泛化的核心机制是参数空间中的随机探索行为。

Abstract: Grokking is a striking phenomenon in neural network training, where a model can undergo a prolonged period of pure memorization before abrupt generalization. While previous works have attempted to interpret it through classical machine learning mechanisms like weight norm, recent research draws an analogy from statistical physics, framing grokking as a form of computational glass relaxation. This theory defines the initial memorization as a result of `fast cooling' where the training loss is reduced so quickly that a glass state is formed, followed by a `slow relaxation' towards final generalization. Although providing a unifying framework for representative grokking theories, this perspective has remained largely at the theoretical on macroscopic level without direct empirical validation on training dynamics. Here we introduce a three-component framework to directly characterize the training dynamics via parameter mobility (PM), and two representative measurements from glassy dynamics: replica correlation (RC) and fractal dimension (FD). We demonstrate that standard optimization presents clear signatures of glass dynamics and inherently traps the grokking network in a kinetic arrested memorization state with a collapsed mobility, strong history dependence, and channel-like motions. This quantitative agreement motivates us to introduce State-Aware Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping (SAM-Swap), an optimization plug-in that can accelerate generalization, inspired by swap Monte Carlo algorithm widely used in glass dynamics. Comparing SAM-Swap, weight decay, and Gaussian gradient noise, we find that accelerated generalization is consistently associated with random exploration in the parameter space, similar to diffusion in physics.

</details>


### [218] [Bayesian Inference with Structured Signal: Static Replica Symmetry Breaking on the Nishimori Line in the Planted Spin Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02373)
*Andrea Vincenzo Dell'Abate,Louise Budzynski*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯推断中信号先验存在相关性（非独立同分布）的影响，通过在随机正则图上构建植入选定伊辛模型（planted spin glass）作为最小模型，发现信号先验的不同相态会分别阻碍或促进推断：顺磁相中相关性降低重构阈值，铁磁相中先验本身即可实现部分恢复，而先验处于复制对称破缺（RSB）相时，后验在Nishimori条件下出现静态RSB转变，从而为非可分离相关先验导致推断问题出现静态RSB提供了实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯推断的理论模型普遍假设信号分量独立同分布（i.i.d.），但真实信号通常具有相关性；为研究信号先验相关性对推断的影响，需要一个最小可控的模型来系统性地评估不同先验相态下的推断行为。

Method: 采用随机正则图上的植入选定自旋玻璃（planted spin glass）作为最小模型，信号从具有耦合参数κ的伊辛模型（Ising model）中采样；依据先验所处的相态（顺磁相、铁磁相、复制对称破缺相）分别分析重构阈值、部分恢复条件以及后验中的静态复制对称破缺转变，并使用信念传播（Belief Propagation）讨论算法性能。

Result: 在顺磁（paramagnetic）相中，信号相关性降低重构阈值，更弱的信号强度即可实现恢复；在铁磁（ferromagnetic）相中，先验本身已支持部分恢复，并确定了观测提供额外信息的阈值；当先验处于复制对称破缺（RSB）相时，在后验中检测到Nishimori条件下的静态RSB转变。

Conclusion: 非可分离的相关先验可使贝叶斯最优推断问题出现静态复制对称破缺，这是独立同分布先验下无法出现的现象；该玻璃态（glassy phase）对信念传播等算法的性能具有重要影响。

Abstract: A common assumption in theoretical models of Bayesian inference is that the signal has i.i.d. components. To study the effect of correlations in the signal prior, we consider a minimal model: the planted spin glass on random regular graphs, where the signal is sampled from an Ising model with coupling $κ$. Depending on the phase of the prior, we find that adding structure in the signal can either help or hinder inference. In the paramagnetic regime, correlations in the signal lower the reconstruction threshold, so that weaker signal strength is sufficient for recovery. In the ferromagnetic regime, the prior alone already enables partial recovery, and we identify the threshold above which the observations provide additional information. When the prior itself is in a replica symmetry breaking (RSB) phase, we detect a static RSB transition in the posterior under Nishimori conditions. This provides an example where a non-separable, correlated prior leads to static RSB in a Bayes-optimal inference problem. We discuss the consequences of this glassy phase for algorithmic performance, in particular for Belief Propagation.

</details>

### [219] [Ermakov-Painleve' II Symmetry Reduction of a Class of Moving Boundary Problems for a Reciprocal Extended mKdV Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00681)
*Colin Rogers,Sandra Carillo*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文通过互反变换（reciprocal transformation）将经典孤子型修正KdV（mKdV）方程的可积推广与一个含源项的新非线性演化方程联系起来，并利用Ermakov–Painlevé II（第二类Painlevé）对称约化求解了一类Stefan型移动边界问题的精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立经典可积孤子方程（mKdV）与其含源项的推广形式之间的联系，并探索能否通过对称约化途径求解具有实际物理意义的移动边界问题（moving boundary problem）。

Method: 采用互反变换（reciprocal transformation）将可积的mKdV扩展方程映射为含源项的新非线性演化方程；随后运用Ermakov–Painlevé II对称约化（symmetry reduction）方法获得Stefan型移动边界问题一类情形的精确解。

Result: 成功建立了两个方程之间的互反变换关系，并通过Ermakov–Painlevé II对称约化获得了Stefan型移动边界问题的精确解。

Conclusion: 互反变换与Ermakov–Painlevé II对称约化的结合，为求解含源项非线性演化方程及对应的移动边界问题提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: A reciprocal transformation is applied to link an integrable extension of the classical solitonic mKdV to a novel nonlinear evolution equation incorporating a source term. Application of Ermakov-Painleve' II symmetry reduction is made to determine the exact solution to a class of associated moving boundary problems of Stefan-type.

</details>


### [220] [Deformation algorithm: Deforming (2+1)-dimensional integrable systems to higher dimensional ones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01293)
*Wang Fa-Ren,Jia Man,Lou S Y*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一的守恒律变形（conservation-law deformation）框架，将（1+1）维可积系统的变形算法推广至（2+1）维模型，针对各向异性Kadomtsev-Petviashvili（KP）方程和各向同性Nizhnik-Novikov-Veselov（NNV）方程，构造了无限族的（m+3）维可积KP与NNV层级及其闭式Lax对，并通过Lax算子的交换子消逝条件严格验证了可积性。截断辅助空间变量得到有限维主系统，进一步对称约化不仅恢复了原始（2+1）维方程，还生成了两个不同的Harry-Dym（HD）型互反可积系统，其中NNV约化首次产生了空间各向同性的二维HD系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于守恒律的变形算法此前仅适用于（1+1）维可积系统的升维构造，向（2+1）维模型的推广长期是一个未解难题，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 引入一组相互对易的场依赖变形算子，系统构造（m+3）维可积KP与NNV层级并推导其闭式Lax对；通过截断辅助空间变量获得有限维主系统，再经对称约化得到HD型互反系统，并通过Lax算子交换子消逝条件验证可积性。

Result: 成功构建了无限族的（m+3）维KP与NNV可积层级，获得（3+1）维KP系统和（4+1）维广义NNV系统；对称约化恢复原始（2+1）维方程，并产生两个HD型互反系统——各向异性（2+1）维HD模型以及首个空间各向同性二维HD系统。平行比较揭示原始方程的对称性直接决定高维变形与HD对偶子系统的对称特性。

Conclusion: 该工作将守恒律变形猜想从（1+1）维推广至更高维度，同时适用于强Lax与弱Lax结构，并为多维可积系统构造互反联系（reciprocal links）提供了普适途径。

Abstract: The deformation algorithm based on conservation laws can lift (1+1)-dimensional integrable systems to higher-dimensional counterparts while preserving Lax integrability, yet its generalization to (2+1)-dimensional models has remained an open problem. This paper establishes a unified deformation framework for two (2+1)-dimensional integrable equations: the anisotropic Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (KP) equation and the isotropic Nizhnik-Novikov-Veselov (NNV) equation. By introducing a set of mutually commuting field-dependent deformation operators, we systematically construct infinite families of ($m+3$)-dimensional integrable KP and NNV hierarchies, derive their closed-form Lax pairs, and rigorously verify integrability via the vanishing commutator condition of Lax operators. For each high-dimensional hierarchy, concrete finite-dimensional master systems are obtained by truncating auxiliary spatial variables: a (3+1)-dimensional KP system and a (4+1)-dimensional generalized NNV system. Further symmetry reductions recover the original (2+1)-dimensional KP and NNV equations, and more importantly produce two distinct Harry-Dym (HD)-type reciprocal integrable systems. The KP reduction yields an anisotropic (2+1)-dimensional HD model, while the NNV reduction generates the first spatially isotropic two-space-dimensional HD system reported so far, filling a notable gap in existing literature. Parallel comparison of the KP and NNV branches reveals that the spatial symmetry of the original two-dimensional parent equation directly governs the symmetry properties of its high-dimensional deformations and HD dual subsystems. Our work not only extends the conservation-law deformation conjecture beyond (1+1)-dimensions to accommodate both strong Lax and weak Lax structures, but also provides a universal route to construct reciprocal links for multi-dimensional integrable systems.

</details>

### [221] [Hybrid BaTiO3/TiO2 Metasurface for Efficient Gigahertz-Speed Free-Space Electro-Optic Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00286)
*Zhongpeng Sun,Kerolos M. A. Yousef,Michael Domm,Agham Posadas,Xudong Li,Marcus Ossiander,Maryna L. Meretska,Yiwei Ju,Isabel Barth,Jason Tischler,Theodore P. Letsou,Moaz Waqar,Amirhassan Shams-Ansari,Xiaoqing Pan,Alexander A. Demkov,Federico Capasso*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种基于BaTiO3(BTO)/TiO2混合超表面（metasurface）的自由空间电光调制器，通过可扩展的BTO薄膜生长与成熟的TiO2纳米加工结合，实现了大孔径、高效、吉赫兹（GHz）速度的电光调制，透射率调制效率约0.020每伏，-3 dB电光带宽约0.8 GHz，有效泡克尔斯系数（Pockels coefficient）约151 pm/V。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间电光调制器（free-space electro-optic modulator）是新兴光子系统的关键器件，但其性能受限于调制效率、带宽和器件孔径之间的权衡（trade-off）。现有方案难以同时实现大孔径、高效和高速调制，亟需一种可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 采用射频磁控溅射（radio-frequency magnetron sputtering）可扩展生长BTO薄膜，并结合成熟的TiO2纳米加工技术，在未刻蚀的BTO层上图案化TiO2超表面。器件支持导模共振（guided-mode resonance），品质因子（quality factor）超过1300，光学限制因子（confinement factor）约0.8；连续BTO层最大限度利用外加电压，使光学场与驱动场在BTO内实现最大重叠。

Result: 0.3 mm × 0.3 mm超表面器件实现了约0.020每伏的透射率调制效率和约0.8 GHz的-3 dB电光带宽，BTO的有效泡克尔斯系数约151 pm/V。

Conclusion: 该工作为高性能自由空间电光调制器提供了一条可扩展的制造路线，适用于激光雷达（LiDAR）、自由空间光通信（free-space optical communication）和可重构光学计算（reconfigurable optical computing）等应用。

Abstract: Free-space electro-optic modulators are key to emerging photonic systems, yet their performance remains limited by trade-offs between modulation efficiency, bandwidth, and device aperture. Here we report a hybrid BaTiO3 (BTO)/TiO2 metasurface for large-aperture, efficient, gigahertz-speed free-space electro-optic modulation. Combining scalable BTO film growth by radio-frequency magnetron sputtering with mature TiO2 nanofabrication, we pattern the metasurface in TiO2 on an unetched BTO layer. The resulting devices support guided-mode resonances with quality factors exceeding 1300 and an optical confinement factor of ~0.8, while the continuous BTO layer makes efficient use of the applied voltage, together maximizing the overlap between the optical and driving fields within the BTO. A device with a 0.3 mm x 0.3 mm metasurface achieves a transmittance modulation efficiency of ~0.020 per volt and a -3 dB electro-optic bandwidth of ~0.8 GHz, with an effective Pockels coefficient of ~151 pm/V for the BTO. This establishes a scalable route to high-performance free-space electro-optic modulators for LiDAR, free-space optical communication, and reconfigurable optical computing.

</details>


### [222] [Solitons in optical couplers: introduction and perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00696)
*Boris A. Malomed*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文是一篇迷你综述，简要总结并讨论了光学耦合器中亮孤子（bright soliton）的理论与实验研究进展，重点聚焦于双芯波导中线性芯间耦合、群速度色散和自聚焦Kerr非线性共同作用下的孤子行为，并回顾了双芯非线性光纤中孤子切换及对称结构下稳定非对称双芯孤子自发涌现的最新实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该领域具备进一步拓展的理论与实验研究空间，作者希望通过系统梳理已有成果，为后续在光学耦合器中的亮孤子研究指明方向。

Method: 采用迷你综述（minireview）的形式，先简要介绍领域背景，再重点回顾双芯非线性光纤中孤子切换（soliton switching）的实验结果，以及对称双芯结构耦合器中稳定非对称双芯孤子自发涌现的实验发现。

Result: 文章汇总了相对较新的实验成果，涵盖双芯非线性光学光纤中的孤子切换现象，以及对称双芯耦合器中稳定非对称孤子的自发生成。

Conclusion: 该综述为光学耦合器中亮孤子的理论深化与实验推进提供了方向性讨论，强调对称双芯结构中非对称孤子的自发涌现是值得持续关注的研究课题。

Abstract: This minireview provides a brief summary and a discussion of directions for further development of theoretical and, chiefly, experimental studies of bright solitons in optical couplers, i.e., dual-core waveguides which combine the linear inter-core coupling (tunneling of light between the parallel cores with the intra-core group-velocity dispersion and self-focusing Kerr (cubic) nonlinearity. Following a short introduction to the field, the article focuses on a brief review of relatively recent experimental results for the switching of solitons in dual-core nonlinear optical fibers and the spontaneous emergence of stable asymmetric two-core solitons in the couplers with the symmetric dual-core structure.

</details>


### [223] [Tip-Enhanced Vibrational Ladder Climbing in Surface Molecular System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01698)
*Tatsuto Mochizuki,Shota Takahashi,Atsunori Sakurai,Toshiki Sugimoto*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本研究在扫描隧道显微镜（STM）形成的等离激元针尖-衬底纳米间隙中，利用针尖增强和频产生（TE-SFG）光谱实现了吸附在Pt(111)表面CO分子的振动阶梯爬升（vibrational ladder climbing），观测到直至3-4跃迁的热带峰（hot-band peaks），并通过光学布洛赫方程（optical Bloch equations）数值模拟再现了实验结果，展示了TE-SFG在表面分子体系振动激发控制方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获得高能振动态（high-lying vibrational states）对于主动控制分子反应至关重要，但目前缺乏在表面分子体系中有效探测和操控高振动能级的实验手段。

Method: 在扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscope）的等离激元（plasmonic）针尖-衬底纳米间隙中激发CO分子，采用针尖增强和频产生（tip-enhanced sum-frequency generation, TE-SFG）光谱探测振动跃迁信号；同时利用光学布洛赫方程（optical Bloch equations）对红外脉冲能量依赖的振动激发过程进行数值分析。

Result: 随着红外脉冲能量（infrared pulse energy）的增加，CO分子的热带峰（hot-band peaks）依次出现，最高可观测到3-4振动跃迁，表明高振动能级被逐步布居（stepwise population）；光学布洛赫方程的数值模拟成功捕捉了这些特征随能量的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 研究证明TE-SFG具备探测表面分子体系振动阶梯爬升的能力，为在纳米尺度获取高能振动态并控制振动激发提供了一条有前景的途径。

Abstract: Achieving high-lying vibrational states is essential for actively controlling molecular reactions. We demonstrate vibrational ladder climbing of CO adsorbed on Pt(111) within the plasmonic tip-substrate nanogap formed in a scanning tunneling microscope, detected via tip-enhanced sum-frequency generation (TE-SFG). As the infrared pulse energy increases, hot-band peaks appear sequentially up to the 3-4 transition, indicating the stepwise population of higher vibrational states. Numerical analysis using the optical Bloch equations captures the observed energy dependence of these features. These results demonstrate the capability of TE-SFG to probe vibrational ladder climbing in surface molecular systems and suggest a promising route toward accessing high-lying vibrational states and controlling vibrational excitation at the nanoscale.

</details>


### [224] [Direct Wafer Bonding of Crystal-Ion-Sliced GaP Thin Films for Photonic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02251)
*Hossein Esfandiar,Emanuel Glück,Fabian Ganss,Ulrich Kentsch,Dennis Arslan,Jana Paeschke,Sebastian Ritter,Andreas Ihring,Muyi Yang,Isabelle Staude,Stefan Facsko,Falk Eilenberger,Carolin Rothhardt,Sebastian W. Schmitt*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种基于晶体离子切片（crystal ion slicing）与直接晶圆键合（direct wafer bonding）技术制备磷化镓（GaP）绝缘体上薄膜的方法，实现了近体单晶质量的高质量GaP薄膜，并在1550 nm处测得约0.9 dB/cm的平面波吸收损耗，为多功能异质集成和CMOS后道工艺兼容的高质量GaP薄膜光子学提供了可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磷化镓（GaP）因其高折射率、宽光学透明窗口和强二阶非线性响应，被认为是集成光子学中极具前景的材料平台。然而，现有GaP薄膜平台依赖异质外延生长或牺牲层释放（sacrificial-layer release），在灵活集成方面受到限制，缺乏独立于供体和靶衬底的柔性集成方案。

Method: 采用晶体离子切片（crystal ion slicing）与直接晶圆键合（direct wafer bonding）技术，分别以熔融石英和SiO₂/Si热氧化硅衬底作为代表性平台，制备磷化镓绝缘体上（GaP-on-insulator）薄膜，并在转移后进行退火（post-transfer annealing）处理以恢复晶体质量。

Result: 退火后的GaP薄膜展现出接近体单晶的晶体质量，残余应变低，表面光滑适合纳米光子制造，键合界面均匀。退火还恢复了接近外延GaP的线性光学色散（n和k），在1550 nm（电信C波段）测得估计平面波吸收损耗为0.9 dB/cm。

Conclusion: 该制备方法建立了通往高质量GaP薄膜光子学的可扩展路径，与多种异质集成（heterogeneous integration）方案及CMOS后道（back-end-of-line）工艺兼容，且不受供体和靶衬底限制，具备灵活集成优势。

Abstract: Gallium phosphide (GaP) is a promising material platform for integrated photonics because of its high refractive index, broad optical transparency, and strong second-order nonlinear response. Here, we demonstrate GaP-on-insulator thin films fabricated by crystal ion slicing and direct wafer bonding, using fused silica and SiO$_2$/Si/Si thermally oxidized silicon substrates as representative platforms. Unlike GaP thin-film platforms that rely on heteroepitaxial growth or sacrificial-layer release, the presented approach enables the flexible integration of crystalline GaP thin films, independent of both donor and target substrates. Following post-transfer annealing, the films exhibit near-bulk crystalline quality with low residual strain, smooth surfaces suitable for nanophotonic fabrication, and homogeneous bonding interfaces. Furthermore, annealing restores the linear optical dispersion (n and k) approaching that of epitaxially grown GaP with estimated plane wave absorption loss of 0.9 dB/cm at 1550 nm in the telecom C-band. The demonstrated approach establishes a scalable pathway toward high-quality GaP thin-film photonics compatible with versatile heterogeneous integration and back-end-of-line CMOS processing.

</details>


### [225] [Gallium phosphide on insulator for nanophotonics and quantum technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02328)
*Tobias Bucher,Otto Arnold,Muyi Yang,Zifei Zhang,Katsuya Tanaka,Annkathrin Köhler,Berit Marx-Glowna,Duk-Yong Choi,Isabelle Staude,Carsten Ronning*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文通过离子切片（ion slicing）技术制备了磷化镓绝缘体上（GaP-on-insulator）衬底，利用He⁺离子注入能量和剂量调控分裂深度与剥离行为，并通过阳极键合和等离子体增强直接晶圆键合将薄膜转移到非晶衬底上。经过500 °C退火和抛光处理后，薄膜保持单晶结构且光学性能接近体材料，同时保留了近纯净的二阶非线性响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磷化镓（gallium phosphide, GaP）因其高折射率、低光吸收和强二阶非线性，是可见光与近红外光子学及量子技术的有前景材料平台。然而，如何将高质量单晶GaP薄膜集成到异质衬底上仍是一个挑战，因此需要开发灵活的制备方法。

Method: 通过控制He⁺离子注入能量和剂量来调控体相GaP的分裂深度与剥离行为，实现薄膜剥离；随后采用阳极键合（anodic bonding）和等离子体增强直接晶圆键合（plasma-enhanced direct wafer bonding）将薄膜转移到非晶衬底上。利用沟道卢瑟福背散射谱（channeling Rutherford backscattering spectrometry）和X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）表征薄膜结晶性，并通过500 °C退火和后续抛光来降低注入引起的无序和光学吸收。

Result: 转移后的GaP薄膜保持单晶结构，退火和抛光后实现近体材料的线性光学性能。此外，(110)取向的GaP薄膜表现出与闪锌矿二阶非线性磁化率张量（zinc-blende second-order nonlinear susceptibility tensor）预期一致的偏振依赖性，证明其近纯净的二阶非线性响应。

Conclusion: 该灵活的制备方法使得不同取向的高质量单晶GaP薄膜能够与异质平台集成，适用于自由空间和集成纳米光子学（nanophotonics）以及非线性和量子光学器件。

Abstract: Gallium phosphide is a promising material platform for visible and near-infrared photonics and quantum technologies owing to its high refractive index, low optical absorption, and strong second-order nonlinearity. Here, we demonstrate the fabrication of GaP-on-insulator substrates by ion slicing. The splitting depth and exfoliation behavior of bulk GaP are tailored by controlling the He$^{+}$ ion implantation energy and fluence, enabling thin-film transfer onto amorphous substrates by anodic bonding and plasma-enhanced direct wafer bonding. Channeling Rutherford backscattering spectrometry and X-ray diffraction confirm that the transferred layers retain their single-crystalline structure, while implantation-induced disorder and optical absorption are substantially reduced by annealing at 500 °C and subsequent polishing. The annealed films exhibit linear optical properties approaching those of bulk GaP. In addition, a (110)-oriented GaP thin film shows the characteristic polarization dependence expected from the zinc-blende second-order nonlinear susceptibility tensor, demonstrating a near-pristine second-order nonlinear response. This flexible fabrication approach enables the integration of high-quality single-crystalline GaP with variable orientation for free-space and integrated nanophotonics as well as nonlinear and quantum optical devices.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [Memory with Onsager-Casimir symmetry: Rotating particle in a viscoelastic fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00344)
*Debankur Das,Niloyendu Roy,Niklas Windbacher,Clemens Bechinger,Matthias Krüger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过实验和理论研究了非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）流体中旋转布朗粒子的随机动力学，发现旋转增强长期扩散率并在垂直于旋转轴的平面内产生时间反对称的交叉关联。作者引入最小线性模型，通过消除慢浴变量获得具有非互易记忆核的广义朗之万方程（generalized Langevin equation），该记忆核随时间旋转形成对数螺旋，并满足Onsager-Casimir对称性。研究建立了双时交叉关联与粒子横向响应之间的几何构造联系，确立了旋转胶体在粘弹性流体中作为时间非局域随机动力学中Onsager-Casimir对称性的最小实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对非马尔可夫介质中旋转布朗粒子动力学的时间非局域效应理解有限，尤其是旋转如何影响扩散和交叉关联尚缺乏系统研究。作者希望通过实验与理论相结合，揭示旋转在non-Markovian流体动力学中扮演的角色，并检验时间反演对称性等基本原理在非局域随机动力学中的适用性。

Method: 实验上测量旋转布朗粒子在非马尔可夫流体中的随机动力学；理论上引入一个最小线性模型，将示踪粒子与慢浴自由度耦合，旋转通过平流耦合（advective coupling）进入。消去浴变量后推导出具有非互易记忆核（non-reciprocal memory kernel）的广义朗之万方程，并利用Onsager-Casimir对称性和涨落-耗散关系（fluctuation-response relation）建立交叉关联与横向响应的几何联系。

Result: 实验发现旋转增强粒子的长期扩散率，并在垂直旋转轴平面内产生正交位移分量间的时间反对称交叉关联。理论模型得到的记忆核随时间旋转形成对数螺旋（logarithmic spiral），在旋转矢量反转时满足Onsager-Casimir对称性。由此导出的几何构造将双时交叉关联与粒子在体相中的横向响应联系起来，该关系涉及响应的反对称扇区，有别于普通爱因斯坦关系（Einstein relation）。实验与理论定性一致。

Conclusion: 旋转胶体在粘弹性（viscoelastic）流体中是时间非局域随机动力学中Onsager-Casimir对称性的最小实验实现。该工作为理解非马尔可夫介质中的时间反演对称性和平移响应提供了一个新的理论框架和实验验证。

Abstract: We study the stochastic dynamics of a rotating Brownian particle in a non-Markovian fluid. Experimentally, we find that rotation enhances the long-time diffusivity of the particle and generates time-antisymmetric cross-correlations between orthogonal displacement components in the plane perpendicular to the rotation axis. To rationalize these observations, we introduce a minimal linear model in which a tracer is coupled to a slow bath degree of freedom and rotation enters through an advective coupling. Eliminating the bath variable yields a generalized Langevin equation with a non-reciprocal memory kernel. This kernel rotates in time, forming a logarithmic spiral, and it obeys Onsager-Casimir symmetry under reversal of the rotation vector, and the corresponding fluctuation-response relation. From the latter we obtain a geometric construction that links two-time cross-correlations to the transverse response of the particle in bulk. Unlike the ordinary Einstein relation, this relation involves the antisymmetric sector of the response. Our experiments and theory are in qualitative agreement, establishing rotating colloids in viscoelastic fluids as a minimal realization of Onsager-Casimir symmetry in time-nonlocal stochastic dynamics

</details>


### [227] [LieStoNet: Learning Lie Symmetries from Spatiotemporal Data for Stochastic Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01582)
*Shida Liu,Abhishek Gupta,Sumit Sinha,L. Mahadevan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出LieStoNet，一个端到端、无模板（template-free）的框架，直接从时空轨迹中发现随机微分方程（SDE）的Lie点对称（Lie-point symmetries），无需预指定对称群、模板或正则坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性对现代机器学习与物理至关重要，但随机动力系统中相关连续对称性很少已知，且SDE的对称性发现此前基本未被探索。

Method: 基于Gaeta和Quintero（1999）的SDE Lie-对称理论，LieStoNet从增量中学习漂移与扩散的神经替代模型，通过强制执行SDE判定方程学习可投影生成元（projectable generators），并分别对Lie括号封闭性、Lie代数公理（双线性、反对称性、Jacobi恒等式）以及非冗余独立基进行正则化。替代模型还定义了相应的Fokker-Planck方程，可并行发现其Lie点对称。

Result: 在多个具有已知解析对称性的典型SDE上，LieStoNet恢复的生成元与真实对称代数一致，为含噪动力学提供了可解释的对称性发现。

Conclusion: LieStoNet实现了对SDE Lie点对称性的可解释自动发现，弥补了随机动力系统对称性发现领域的空白。

Abstract: Symmetry is central to modern machine learning and physics: invariances and equivariances improve sample efficiency, robustness, and out-of-distribution generalization, while symmetry principles guide scientific modeling. Yet for stochastic dynamical systems the relevant continuous symmetries are rarely known, and symmetry discovery for SDEs has remained essentially unexplored. We introduce \textit{LieStoNet}, an end-to-end, \emph{template-free} framework for discovering Lie-point symmetries of SDEs directly from spatiotemporal trajectories, without prespecifying symmetry groups, templates, or canonical coordinates. Building on the seminal SDE Lie-symmetry theory of Gaeta and Quintero (1999), which formalizes Lie-point SDE symmetries and their relation to Fokker-Planck symmetries, LieStoNet learns neural surrogates for drift and diffusion from increments, then learns projectable generators by enforcing the SDE determining equations, separately regularizing for closure under Lie brackets, adherence to the Lie algebra axioms (bilinearity, antisymmetry, Jacobi), and a non-redundant independent basis. The surrogate also defines an associated Fokker-Planck equation, enabling optional discovery of its Lie-point symmetries in parallel. Across multiple canonical SDEs with known analytic symmetries, LieStoNet recovers generators consistent with the ground-truth symmetry algebra, providing interpretable symmetry discovery for noisy dynamics. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/sumit-sinha-seas/LieStoNet_Final.git}{this link}.

</details>


### [228] [Inhomogeneous Ising Model on 2D kagomé Lattice: Fermionic field approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02156)
*Shahane A. Khachatryan,Zhidong Zhang,Ara G. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了kagomé晶格上的二维非均匀伊辛模型（2DIM），通过将其映射到非对称八顶点模型并构造相应的R矩阵，利用费米子表示计算了配分函数和热力学量，得到了精确的临界曲面方程，并验证了在特殊极限下还原为方格和一维伊辛模型的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究kagomé晶格上的二维非均匀伊辛模型，旨在通过精确求解扩展对伊辛模型可解性的理解，并验证与已知伊辛模型结果的一致性。

Method: 将二维非均匀伊辛模型映射到非对称八顶点模型（eight-vertex model）并构造对应的R矩阵；采用费米子表示（fermionic representation）计算配分函数，在热力学极限下推导精确临界曲面方程及热力学量表达式。

Result: 在热力学极限下获得了决定模型相变的临界曲面（critical surface）的精确方程，并计算了铁磁情形下的自由能、比热和自发磁化。当一或两个耦合常数消失时，模型分别退化为方格伊辛模型和一维伊辛模型，重现了对应的精确临界耦合和自由能。

Conclusion: 通过映射到八顶点模型的R矩阵方法，成功精确求解了kagomé晶格上的非均匀伊辛模型，并验证了其在退化极限下与已知伊辛模型结果的正确一致性。

Abstract: We investigate the two-dimensional inhomogeneous Ising model (2DIM) on the kagom'e lattice by mapping it onto a particular non-symmetric eight-vertex model and constructing the corresponding $R$-matrix. Using a fermionic representation, we evaluate the partition function and derive explicit expressions for the main thermodynamic quantities. In the thermodynamic limit, we obtain an exact equation for the critical surface determining the phase transition of the model. We also calculate the free energy, specific heat, and spontaneous magnetization in the ferromagnetic case. Furthermore, we show that when one or two coupling constants vanish, the model reduces, respectively, to the square-lattice and one-dimensional Ising models. In both limits, our results reproduce the corresponding exact critical couplings and free energies.

</details>


### [229] [Noise-enhanced temporal boundary states in non-Hermitian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01179)
*Jielong Zhang,Bozheng Xue,Xianfeng Man,Baizhan Xia*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在二维周期驱动的非厄米系统中引入周期性重复和完全随机的两类时间噪声（temporal noise），在系综平均下意外地实现了时间界面处响应强度的指数增强，并实验上在机器人超材料网络中验证了这一噪声增强的时间边界态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为噪声普遍是拓扑保护的威胁，会削弱甚至破坏拓扑态。本文旨在探究时间噪声是否可能逆转这一认知，在周期驱动非厄米系统中反而成为增强拓扑态的建设性因素。

Method: 将周期性重复的时间噪声和完全随机的时间噪声引入二维周期驱动非厄米系统；采用平均超算符（averaged superoperator）分析验证无噪声能带结构在两类噪声下均被保留，并解析噪声对增长模和衰减模增长率的影响；通过机器人超材料网络进行实验验证。

Result: 在系综平均下，两类时间噪声均驱动时间界面处响应强度的指数增强；噪声在保持无噪声能带结构不变的同时，提高增长模的增长率并抑制衰减模的衰减率；实验上成功实现了噪声增强的时间边界态（temporal boundary state）。

Conclusion: 时间噪声可作为一种建设性成分，使拓扑态明确涌现并赋予拓扑器件卓越的鲁棒性，推翻了噪声必然削弱拓扑态的普遍预期。

Abstract: Time-periodic modulation introduces a synthetic degree of freedom to manipulate topological phases. This synthetic dimension can trigger a phase transition that localizes a boundary state at the temporal interface. Noise is widely deemed a fundamental threat to topological protection, universally anticipated to weaken or even destroy topological states. Here, we introduce periodically repeated temporal noise and fully random temporal noise into a 2D periodically driven non-Hermitian system. Paradoxically, under ensemble averaging, such temporal noise drives an exponential enhancement of the response intensity at the temporal interface. An averaged superoperator analysis shows that the noiseless band structure is preserved under both types of noise. Yet the noise increases the growth rate of growing modes while suppressing the decay rate of decaying ones. Finally, we experimentally realize the noise-enhanced temporal boundary state in a robotic metamaterial network. This finding establishes temporal noise as a constructive ingredient, enabling the unambiguous emergence of topological states and conferring exceptional robustness upon topological devices.

</details>


### [230] [Algorithmic overlaps in the Baxter-Wu model: cluster dynamics under Novotny-Evertz updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01280)
*Ian Pilé,Lev Shchur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov chain Monte Carlo）模拟Baxter-Wu模型，利用Novotny-Evertz亚格子冻结单簇更新方法追踪连续自旋构型的空间重叠。研究发现该重叠量作为算法可观测量遵循相变热力学：其均值在临界温度Tc处从有序相的平台降至零，行为类似序参量；重叠量在临界点不发散，其有限尺寸值以指数ψ=0.378(4)的幂律衰减，该指数小于伊辛（Ising）和Potts模型在标准Fortuin-Kasteleyn簇动力学下得到的约0.42，表明其反映的是动力学性质而非模型的静态属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究在伊辛和Potts模型中利用算法重叠（algorithmic overlap）作为观测量研究相变临界行为，但尚未有工作对三自旋相互作用的Baxter-Wu模型进行类似分析。作者旨在检验该重叠量在Baxter-Wu模型中是否同样遵循热力学行为，并考察其临界指数特征。

Method: 采用Novotny-Evertz亚格子冻结单簇更新（sublattice-freezing single-cluster update）对Baxter-Wu模型进行马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛模拟，记录连续自旋构型之间的空间重叠，并追踪其均值（mean）和方差（variance）在临界区域的行为。通过跨越十一个系统尺寸的有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析，提取临界点处重叠量的幂律衰减指数。

Result: 单簇重叠均值在临界温度Tc处表现出类似序参量的行为，从有序相的平台有限值降至零；重叠量在临界点不发散，保持有限并遵循干净幂律衰减U2(Tc)~L^{-ψ}，指数ψ=0.378(4)，小于伊辛和Potts模型在标准Fortuin-Kasteleyn簇动力学下获得的约0.42。

Conclusion: 即使在三自旋相互作用的Baxter-Wu模型中，算法重叠量仍然作为遵循相变热力学的可观测量起作用。但临界指数的大小反映了所采用的Novotny-Evertz亚格子冻结动力学特征，而非该模型的任何静态性质，表明重叠量的临界行为对更新动力学方案具有敏感性。

Abstract: We study the spatial overlap of successive spin configurations generated by Markov chain Monte Carlo simulations of the Baxter-Wu model. Using the Novotny-Evertz sublattice-freezing single-cluster update, we track the mean and variance of the algorithmic overlap across the critical region. We show that, even in this three-spin model, the overlap acts as an algorithmic observable that follows the thermodynamics of the transition: the single-cluster overlap mean behaves like an order parameter, dropping from a finite ordered-phase plateau toward zero across $T_c$. The overlap does not diverge at criticality, instead it remains finite and its finite-size value decays as a clean power law, $U_2(T_c)\sim L^{-ψ}$, over eleven sizes with an exponent $ψ{=}0.378(4)$ smaller than the value $\approx0.42$ found for the Ising and Potts models under standard Fortuin-Kasteleyn cluster dynamics, indicating that it reflects the Novotny-Evertz sublattice-freezing dynamics rather than any static property of the model.

</details>


### [231] [Universal Scaling of the Magnetocaloric Effect in 2D Ising Monolayers and Bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01811)
*Basit Iqbal,Kingshuk Sarkar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟研究了二维铁磁 Ising 模型在方格子、蜂窝格子和三角格子上的单层与双层结构中的磁热效应（Magnetocaloric Effect, MCE），发现磁熵变化与配位数负相关而临界温度正相关，并证明了不同配位数和层数下存在普适的 MCE 标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究二维磁晶格系统中磁热效应的标度普适性（universal scaling），并厘清配位数与层数对磁热响应的影响规律，为设计高效磁制冷材料提供理论指导。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟，通过 Binder 累积量（Binder cumulant）分析确定各结构的临界温度（T_c）；分别计算磁熵变化（-ΔS_M）、绝热温度变化（ΔT_ad）、磁格律乃森参数（magnetic Grüneisen parameter, Γ_M）、相对制冷功率（relative cooling power）与制冷容量（cooling capacity），并进行峰值归一化和温度标度以检验普适标度曲线；同时进行磁滞（hysteresis）分析。

Result: T_c 随配位数 r 从蜂窝（r=3）到三角（r=6）格子单调增加；而 -ΔS_M 则随配位数增加而减小，在蜂窝格子达到最大。经峰值归一化和温度标度后，-ΔS_M 和场指数 n 在不同磁场和各晶格结构上塌缩为统一的普适主曲线。ΔT_ad 随配位数增大而增大，Γ_M 则与 -ΔS_M 趋势一致。尽管 -ΔS_M 峰值随配位数下降，相对制冷功率和制冷容量基本不变。低配位数晶格在升温过程中磁滞回线宽度衰减更快。

Conclusion: 二维单层和双层磁格子中磁热效应具有不依赖配位数和层数的普适标度行为，该普适性为设计磁制冷材料提供了定量准则。

Abstract: We report a Monte Carlo study of the magnetocaloric effect (MCE) in two-dimensional ferromagnetic Ising models on square, honeycomb, and triangular lattices with monolayer and bilayer configurations. Using Binder cumulant analysis, we determine the critical temperature ($T_c$) of each structure and find that $T_c$ increases with coordination number, from the honeycomb ($r=3$) to the triangular ($r=6$) lattice. In contrast, the magnetic entropy change ($-ΔS_M$) decreases with coordination number, reaching its maximum for the honeycomb lattice. After normalization by their peak values and appropriate temperature scaling, both $-ΔS_M$ and the field exponent $n$ collapse onto universal master curves for different magnetic fields and across all six lattice structures at a fixed low field. This demonstrates universal MCE scaling independent of coordination number and layer count. Critical scaling analysis further supports the observed universality and power-law behavior. Unlike $-ΔS_M$, the adiabatic temperature change ($ΔT_{ad}$) increases with coordination number, whereas the magnetic Grüneisen parameter ($Γ_M$) follows the same trend as $-ΔS_M$. Although the peak value of $-ΔS_M$ decreases with coordination number, the relative cooling power and cooling capacity remain nearly unchanged due to compensating broadening of the $-ΔS_M$ curves. The field dependence of $-ΔS_M$, relative cooling power, and cooling capacity follows power laws up to $\sim2.6$ T (assuming $J\approx1$ meV). Hysteresis analysis shows that lattices with lower coordination numbers exhibit a faster reduction in loop width with increasing temperature. These results establish the universal scaling behavior of the magnetocaloric effect in two-dimensional monolayer and bilayer magnetic lattices and provide guidelines for designing magnetic refrigerants.

</details>


### [232] [Gap distributions between successive personal bests in cricket: Data and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01882)
*Priyanka D. Bhoyar,Prashant M. Gade*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用板球运动员的职业生涯数据（来自ESPN Cricinfo的Test、ODI和T20顶尖球员），研究个人最佳成绩之间的间隔记录统计。发现间隔的经验分布可用截断幂律P(g) ∝ g^−α e^−λg描述，指数范围在0.799≤α≤0.843，远偏离经典记录理论预测的均匀分布P(g)~1/g。当破坏时间顺序后偏差显著减小，表明职业生涯的时间演化对记录发生起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典记录理论（classical record theory）假设独立同分布（i.i.d.）序列，预测记录间隔服从通用分布P(g)~1/g，但运动员职业生涯受学习、老化、能力变化和外部因素影响，违反这些假设。作者旨在检验非平稳、路径依赖系统中记录统计是否发生改变，并以板球为实证对象。

Method: 收集ESPN Cricinfo上顶尖Test、ODI和T20板球球员的职业生涯记录，定义个人最佳成绩之间的局数间隔（inter-record gaps）作为研究对象。采用截断幂律（truncated power law）拟合经验分布，并通过bootstrap shuffle方法打乱局数的时间顺序，保留个人得分分布和职业生涯长度，比较打乱前后指数变化以检验时间演化对记录统计的影响。

Result: 经验间隔分布由截断幂律P(g) ∝ g^−α e^−λg描述，指数α在0.799至0.843之间，明显偏离经典理论的均匀分布预测。bootstrap打乱时间顺序后，指数显著增大至α≈0.939–0.979，且大部分偏差消失，说明时间顺序对记录分布有实质影响。

Conclusion: 个人最佳成绩的演进保留了球员职业生涯时间组织的信息，无法用简单的随机记录过程完全解释。该研究推广了记录统计在非平稳和路径依赖系统中的应用，表明经典的i.i.d.记录理论需扩展以适应更复杂的现实系统。

Abstract: Successive personal best performances provide a natural measure of progression in an athlete's career. Classical record theory predicts a universal gap distribution, $P(g)\sim 1/g$, for independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) sequences. However, sporting careers are shaped by learning, aging, changes in ability, and external influences that violate these assumptions. We investigate the statistics of inter-record gaps, defined as the number of innings between successive personal best scores, in cricket. Using career records of leading Test, ODI, and T20 players obtained from ESPN Cricinfo. We find that the empirical distributions are well described by truncated power law $P(g) \propto g^{-α} e^{-λg}$ with exponents in the range (0.799 $\leq α\leq$ 0.843). Much of this deviation disappears when the temporal ordering of innings is destroyed, indicating that career evolution plays a key role in shaping record occurrence. Bootstrap-shuffled careers, which preserve individual score distributions and career lengths while removing temporal ordering, yield significantly larger exponents ($α\approx 0.939\text{--}0.979$). These findings show that the progression of personal best performances retains information about the temporal organization of a player's career and cannot be fully explained by simple stochastic record processes. More generally, they illustrate how record statistics are altered in nonstationary and path-dependent systems.

</details>


### [233] [Front Selection Is Not Determined by Renormalization-Group Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02211)
*Ko Okumura*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用统一的重整化群（renormalization group, RG）框架研究Fisher–Kolmogorov–Petrovsky–Piskunov（FKPP）方程，发现其行波解构成连续族RG不动点，所有速度v≥2的前沿均RG稳定，但渐近选择态并非由RG稳定性决定，从而证明RG不动点稳定性与渐近态选择是两个不同的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FKPP方程是前沿传播（front propagation）与渐近态选择（asymptotic-state selection）的典型范例，但传统RG理论无法清晰区分RG稳定性与渐近态选择这两个概念，需要阐明两者之间的关系。

Method: 采用统一的重整化群框架对FKPP方程的行波解进行不动点分析，考察解的RG稳定性条件，并对比渐近选择态的判定标准。

Result: 研究发现FKPP方程的行波解构成一个连续族（continuous family）的RG不动点，其中所有满足速度v≥2的前沿均为RG稳定；然而渐近被选择的前沿并非由RG稳定性唯一确定，两者并不一致。

Conclusion: FKPP方程提供了一个明确的反例，说明RG不动点稳定性与渐近态选择是两个需要区分开来的独立概念，为理解更广泛的前沿传播问题提供了方法论启示。

Abstract: The Fisher--Kolmogorov--Petrovsky--Piskunov equation provides a paradigmatic example of front propagation and asymptotic-state selection. Using a unified renormalization-group (RG) framework, we show that its traveling-wave solutions form a continuous family of RG fixed points and that all fronts with $v \ge 2$ are RG-stable. Nevertheless, the asymptotically selected front is not determined by RG stability. The FKPP equation therefore provides an explicit example in which RG fixed-point stability and asymptotic-state selection are distinct concepts.

</details>


### [234] [Quantum resetting with memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02297)
*Gabriele de Mauro,Manas Kulkarni,Satya N. Majumdar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具有均匀记忆的量子随机重置（quantum stochastic resetting）协议，每次重置使系统回到整个历史上均匀随机选择的某一时刻所访问的状态。该过程是非幺正的、非马尔可夫的，并且是经典偏好重定位模型的直接量子推广。作者在能量本征基下精确推导了任意时间无关哈密顿量下密度矩阵每个元素的演化，并发现哈密顿量仅通过对应的玻尔频率（Bohr frequencies）进入动力学。由此区分了有能隙系统（gapped systems）和无能隙系统（gapless systems）两类：前者非对角元以代数方式衰减且振幅随log t周期振荡，最终达到与重置率无关的稳态；后者因任意小玻尔频率的存在而无法达到稳态，位置分布在普适的超慢时间尺度上扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机重置（stochastic resetting）已在文献中被广泛研究，但量子版本的随机重置大多局限于马尔可夫重置协议（如固定时间间隔或指数分布重置），缺乏对具有记忆效应的量子重置动力学的一般性理解。本文旨在引入一种均匀记忆型的量子随机重置协议，并系统分析其在不同量子系统（有能隙与无能隙）中的动力学行为，填补量子随机重置理论中的空白。

Method: 作者提出一种均匀记忆的量子随机重置协议，每次重置事件将系统送回其全部历史中均匀随机挑选的一个时刻所对应的状态。在能量本征基下，他们对该非幺正、非马尔可夫动力学进行精确求解，推导出密度矩阵任意元素的演化方程，并证明哈密顿量仅通过玻尔频率进入动力学。基于玻尔频率的离散或连续性质，作者将量子系统划分为有能隙与无能隙两大类，分别分析稳态行为或扩散行为，并通过两能级系统、谐振子和自由量子粒子三个实例加以验证。

Result: 在gapped systems（离散能谱）中，密度矩阵的对角元保持不变，而非对角元以连续可变的指数代数衰减，且振幅随log t周期性振荡；系统最终趋近于一个与重置率无关、且保留初始状态强记忆的稳态。在gapless systems（连续能谱）中，任意小玻尔频率的存在阻止了稳态的建立，位置分布转而以普适的log(rt)/r超慢（ultra-slow）尺度扩散，且不依赖于初始态和哈密顿量的具体细节。

Conclusion: 该均匀记忆型量子随机重置揭示了量子系统中一种全新的非马尔可夫弛豫行为：有能隙系统表现出代数衰减和log周期振荡，而无能隙系统展示出普适的超慢扩散。这一结果与经典偏好重定位模型存在显著差异，为理解量子重置动力学中的记忆效应提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We introduce a quantum stochastic resetting protocol with uniform memory, in which each resetting event returns the system to a state visited at a time chosen uniformly from its entire history. The resulting dynamics is nonunitary, non-Markovian and a direct quantum generalization of the classical preferential relocation model. Working in the energy eigenbasis, we derive the exact evolution of every density-matrix element for an arbitrary time-independent Hamiltonian and show that the Hamiltonian enters the dynamics only through the corresponding Bohr frequencies. This leads to a natural distinction between two classes of quantum systems: gapped and gapless. In \emph{gapped systems} (systems with a discrete energy spectrum), while the diagonal elements remain unchanged, the off-diagonal elements of the density matrix in the energy eigenbasis decay algebraically with a continuously varying exponent and with an amplitude that oscillates periodically in $\log t$. The system therefore approaches a stationary state that is independent of the resetting rate and retains a strong memory of the initial state. In \emph{gapless systems} (systems with a continuous energy spectrum), arbitrarily small Bohr frequencies prevent stationarity. Instead, the position distribution spreads on the universal (ultra-slow) scale $\log(rt)/r$, independently of the initial state and of the details of the Hamiltonian. We illustrate these results with a two-level system, a harmonic oscillator, and a free quantum particle, and contrast them with their classical counterparts.

</details>

Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: LHCb合作组在pp、pO和OO碰撞中测量了ϒ(2S)和ϒ(3S)介子相对于ϒ(1S)态的产生率比值，发现OO碰撞中ϒ(2S)被显著压制约2倍，而pO碰撞中的压制效应很弱，表明小体系OO碰撞中出现了额外的压制机制，与夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）形成相关的模型能成功描述数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在通过测量不同碰撞体系中ϒ激发态（excited states）相对于基态的产率，探究夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma，QGP）形成对底夸克偶素（bottomonium）产生的压制效应，并区分冷核物质效应与色屏蔽（color screening）机制的贡献。

Method: 利用LHCb探测器在pp、pO和OO碰撞中分别测量ϒ(1S)、ϒ(2S)和ϒ(3S)介子的产率，计算ϒ(2S)/ϒ(1S)和ϒ(3S)/ϒ(1S)的比值，并将pp碰撞结果与先前不同质心能量下的LHCb测量进行比较，同时将pO与OO碰撞的结果进行对比分析。

Result: pp数据中的产率比值与之前LHCb在不同质心能量下的测量结果一致；pO碰撞中ϒ(2S)和ϒ(3S)仅出现轻微的相对压制；而OO碰撞中ϒ(2S)被压制约2倍，且ϒ(3S)也有被压制的证据。OO数据中的显著压制与pO数据中的微弱效应形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: OO碰撞体系中观察到的显著压制表明，即使在相对较小的OO碰撞系统中也会出现额外的压制机制；包含OO碰撞中夸克-胶子等离子体形成的模型能成功描述数据，该结果对理解冷核物质效应与解禁闭夸克-胶子等离子体中色屏蔽之间的相互作用具有重要意义。

Abstract: The production rates of $\mathitΥ(2S)$ and $\mathitΥ(3S)$ mesons relative to that of the $\mathitΥ(1S)$ state are measured in $pp$, $p$O, and OO collisions by the LHCb collaboration. The ratios measured in $pp$ data are consistent with previous LHCb measurements at different center-of-mass energies. Only slight relative suppression of the $\mathitΥ(2S)$ and $\mathitΥ(3S)$ states is found in $p$O collisions, while in OO collisions the $\mathitΥ(2S)$ is suppressed by a factor of $\sim2$, with evidence for suppression of the $\mathitΥ(3S)$. The significant suppression in OO data, compared to the small effect in $p$O data, shows the emergence of additional suppression mechanisms in the relatively small OO collision system. Models incorporating quark-gluon plasma formation in OO collisions successfully describe the data. Implications for the interplay between cold nuclear matter effects and color screening in a deconfined quark-gluon plasma are discussed.

</details>


### [236] [Hadron Spectroscopy: Experimental Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00191)
*Volker Crede*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨了强子谱学（hadron spectroscopy）作为理解量子色动力学（QCD）和强子内部结构的关键工具。传统上认为质子由三个夸克组成这一说法具有误导性，实际强子结构远比此复杂。近年来，许多不符合传统夸克模型分类的奇特强子（exotic hadrons）的发现，使强子谱学研究进入一个令人兴奋的新时代，并继续在探索物质深层结构和宇宙基本结构中发挥重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强子（hadron）的形成是核物理和粒子物理中最引人入胜的现象之一，但传统"质子由三个夸克组成"的理解过于简化且具有误导性。要深入理解强子结构和强相互作用理论，需要系统绘制强子态谱并揭示其复杂内部结构，这正是强子谱学研究的核心动机。

Method: 采用强子谱学（hadron spectroscopy）的方法，类比原子光谱学通过离散能级揭示原子结构，通过映射强子态的谱，利用实验技术和计算方法的进步来研究强子结构和强相互作用理论——量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics, QCD）。

Result: 过去几十年中，强子谱学进入了一个激动人心的新时代，发现了许多不适合传统夸克模型（quark model）中普通重子（baryon）和介子（meson）分类的奇特强子（exotic hadrons），其中许多发现来自重味（heavy-flavor）领域。

Conclusion: 随着实验技术和计算方法的持续进步，强子谱学将继续作为探索物质最深层次和理解宇宙基本结构的重要工具。

Abstract: One of the most fascinating phenomena in nuclear and particle physics is the formation of light hadrons -- strongly interacting particles -- out of massless gluons and almost massless quarks. These particles exhibit rich excitation spectra due to their complex quark-gluon structures. Short-lived pairs of virtual quarks and antiquarks are continually formed and annihilated inside hadrons. Every physics student probably knows that the proton consists of three quarks. This statement is misleading, though. For example, the proton only shows a constant excess of three quarks versus antiquarks from the outside, and these three quarks are not even well defined. Understanding hadrons goes well beyond explaining their properties in terms of three quarks for baryons and a quark-antiquark pair for mesons. Much like atomic spectroscopy revealed the structure of atoms through discrete energy levels, hadron spectroscopy seeks the mapping of the spectrum of hadronic states and to use that information to better understand the underlying theory of the strong force -- Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD). Over the past few decades, hadron spectroscopy has entered an exciting new era with the discovery of many exotic hadrons that do not fit neatly into the traditional quark model classification of ordinary baryons and mesons. Many of the discoveries come from the heavy-flavor sector. As experimental techniques and computational methods continue to advance, hadron spectroscopy will remain a vital tool for probing the deepest layers of matter and understanding the fundamental structure of the universe.

</details>


### [237] [Charm-quark collectivity from small to large systems with ALICE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02161)
*Marcello Di Costanzo*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用ALICE探测器在LHC Run 3期间收集的铅-铅（Pb-Pb）和氧-氧（OO）碰撞数据，在质心能量 $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}} = 5.36$ TeV下测量了瞬时粲强子（prompt charm hadrons）的椭圆流（elliptic flow, $v_2$），涵盖了D介子和 $\Lambda_c^+$ 重子，并首次观测到粲夸克扇区中间横动量区重子与介子椭圆流的分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在通过探测粲夸克在不同尺寸夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）介质中的椭圆流，评估粲夸克在传播过程中的热平衡化（thermal equilibration）程度，并检验重子-介子椭圆流差异在粲夸克扇区是否同样存在。

Method: 在中心快度区间（$|y| < 0.8$）利用强子衰变道重建信号候选粒子，并采用标量积（Scalar Product, SP）技术测量椭圆流系数 $v_2$。分析涵盖半中心Pb-Pb碰撞中的D^0、D^+、D_s^+介子及 $\Lambda_c^+$ 重子，以及边缘Pb-Pb碰撞和中心OO碰撞中的瞬时D介子$v_2$。

Result: D介子的测量结果达到了前所未有的精度并拓展了低横动量（low-$p_{\text{T}}$）范围；在粲夸克扇区的中间横动量区间，首次观测到重子与介子椭圆流的分裂现象。

Conclusion: 边缘Pb-Pb碰撞和中心OO碰撞中的D介子$v_2$测量结果为研究粲夸克在逐渐减小的夸克-胶子等离子体介质中传播时所达到的热平衡化程度提供了新的探针与约束。

Abstract: In these proceedings, the elliptic flow ($v_2$) measurement of prompt charm hadrons in lead-lead (Pb-Pb) and oxygen-oxygen (OO) collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}} = 5.36$ TeV, using the data collected during LHC Run 3 by the ALICE detector, is presented. The analysis is performed at midrapidity ($|y| < 0.8$) and hadronic decay channels are used to reconstruct the signal candidates. The $v_2$ coefficient is measured via the Scalar Product (SP) technique. The $v_2$ of prompt D$^0$, D$^+$, D$_\text{s}^+$ mesons, and $Λ_{\text{c}}^+$ baryons in semicentral Pb-Pb collisions is reported. These results achieve unprecedented precision and low-$p_{\text{T}}$ reach for the D mesons and highlight the first observation of the splitting of baryon and meson elliptic flow at intermediate $p_{\text{T}}$ in the charm sector. The prompt D-meson $v_2$ is also measured in peripheral Pb-Pb collisions and central OO collisions, probing the degree of thermal equilibration reached by charm quarks propagating in progressively smaller quark-gluon plasma media.

</details>

### [238] [Dense Hamiltonians at the Parseval Limit: The Noncommutative BH Constant is Exponential and the Quantum FEI Conjecture is False](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01424)
*Joseph Slote*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文对每个维度 d≥1 构造了一个范数为1的厄米算子，其 Pauli 展开含有 N(d)=exp(Ω(d²)) 个系数，每个系数度为 d 且幅值为 1/√N(d)——这是 Parseval 恒等式所允许的最大幅值。该构造证明了非交换 Bohnenblust–Hille 常数满足 BH_M2(d)≥exp(Ω(d))，确立了其指数增长，并与经典超立方体情形渐近分离；同时该算子是酉的，从而推翻 Bu 等人（2024）提出的量子 Fourier 熵-影响猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在探究非交换 Bohnenblust–Hille（BH）常数 BH_M2(d) 的渐近增长率，该问题在量子信息与算子理论中具有重要意义。此外，作者希望检验 Bu 等人提出的量子 Fourier 熵-影响（Fourier Entropy-Influence）猜想是否成立——这是 Friedgut 和 Kalai 经典猜想的自然推广。

Method: 对于每个 d≥1，构造了一个范数为1的厄米算子，其 Pauli 展开包含 N(d)=exp(Ω(d²)) 个度为 d 的系数项，每项幅值为 1/√N(d)，达到 Parseval 恒等式允许的最大幅值。该构造同时满足酉性条件，使得所得算子成为 Montanaro 和 Osborne（2010）意义下的量子布尔函数（quantum Boolean function）。

Result: 该构造推导出非交换 BH 常数下界 BH_M2(d)≥exp(Ω(d))，结合先前工作中证明的上界，确立了 BH_M2(d) 的渐近增长是指数形式。下界还渐近地将 BH_M2(d) 与经典超立方体 BH 常数 BH_{±1}(d) 分离，后者已知为亚指数增长（≤C^{√(d log d)}）。此外，该构造作为量子布尔函数，反例否证了 Bu 等人（2024）提出的量子 Fourier 熵-影响猜想。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式构造，彻底解决了非交换 Bohnenblust–Hille 常数的渐近增长率问题（为指数增长），并证明其与经典情形存在本质渐近差异。同时，该构造提供了量子 Fourier 熵-影响猜想的反例，表明该猜想的经典对应版本不能直接推广到量子布尔函数领域。

Abstract: For each $d\geq 1$ we construct a norm-1 Hermitian operator whose Pauli expansion contains $N(d)=\exp(Ω(d^2))$ terms, each of degree $d$ and magnitude $1/\sqrt{N(d)}$ - the largest magnitude permitted by Parseval's identity. For comparison, if a bounded diagonal operator (or equivalently, a bounded degree-$d$ function on the Boolean cube) has $N(d)$ Pauli coefficients, all of magnitude $Ω(1/\sqrt{N(d)})$, then $N(d)\leq \exp(\widetilde{O}(d^{1.5}))$.
  This construction implies the noncommutative Bohnenblust--Hille (BH) constant satisfies $\mathrm{BH}_{M_2}(d)\geq\exp(Ω(d))$. Together with the upper bounds proved in prior work, this settles the asymptotic growth of $\mathrm{BH}_{M_2}(d)$ as exponential. Our lower bound also asymptotically separates $\mathrm{BH}_{M_2}(d)$ from the (classical) hypercube BH constant $\mathrm{BH}_{\{\pm 1\}}(d)$, which in turn is known to be subexponential: $\mathrm{BH}_{\{\pm 1\}}(d)\leq C^{\sqrt{d \log d}}$.
  Our Hamiltonians are also unitary and thus quantum Boolean functions in the sense of Montanaro and Osborne (2010). As such they refute the quantum Fourier Entropy-Influence conjecture of Bu et al. (2024), a natural generalization of the classical Fourier Entropy-Influence conjecture due to Friedgut and Kalai (1996).

</details>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Search for a top-philic Z' boson decaying into a $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ pair in a final state with jets and an electron or muon in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13.6 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01251)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了CMS实验对顶夸克亲和Z'玻色子（top-philic Z' boson）的搜寻，基于质子-质子对撞数据（$\sqrt{s}$=13 TeV，积分亮度138 fb$^{-1}$），排除了在0.5–3 TeV质量范围内、多种内禀宽度下的Z'玻色子产生截面，设置了95%置信水平的上限，是目前对这类玻色子最严格的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论上顶夸克亲和Z'玻色子可作为超出标准模型（beyond Standard Model）新物理的信号载体，但其存在尚未被证实，需要在高能对撞机数据中检验。该研究旨在利用LHC的完整Run 2数据，对这类粒子进行迄今最灵敏的搜寻。

Method: 分析采用CMS实验在2016–2018年收集的质心能量13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb$^{-1}$），搜寻与$t\bar{t}$对联合产生并衰变为另一$t\bar{t}$对的顶夸克亲和Z'玻色子。利用机器学习的喷注鉴别算法识别由洛伦兹增强（Lorentz-boosted）顶夸克强子衰变产物合并形成的大半径喷注，并以两个最高横动量顶夸克候选者的不变质量分布作为判别变量，覆盖0.5–3 TeV质量范围及4%、10%、20%、50%四种内禀宽度。

Result: 观测结果与标准模型背景预期一致，未发现显著超出。在95%置信水平下，对每种衰变宽度分别给出了作为质量函数的Z'玻色子产生截面上限，结果为迄今对顶夸克亲和Z'玻色子最严格的约束。

Conclusion: LHC Run 2全数据集的分析未发现顶夸克亲和Z'玻色子存在的证据，所设置的上限为该类新物理模型提供了目前最强的实验限制，可用于约束相关理论模型的参数空间。

Abstract: A search for a top-philic Z' boson in a final state with jets and an electron or muon is presented. The search is based on a sample of proton-proton collision data collected at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC during 2016$-$2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The top-philic Z' boson is produced in association with a top-antitop quark pair ($\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$) and decays into a $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ pair, as it couples exclusively to top quarks. The analysis aims to identify a heavy Z' boson that produces Lorentz-boosted top quarks, whose hadronic decay products are merged into large-radius jets. A machine-learning algorithm is employed to identify such jets. The distribution of the invariant mass of the two top quark candidates with the highest transverse momentum is used as the discriminant variable in a Z' boson mass range of 0.5$-$3 TeV, with intrinsic widths of 4, 10, 20, and 50% relative to its mass. The results obtained are found to be in agreement with the standard model background prediction. Upper limits at 95% confidence level are set on the production cross section of the Z' boson, for each of the decay widths as a function of its mass. These results represent the most stringent constraints to date on the existence of a top-philic Z' boson.

</details>


### [240] [Combination of vector boson scattering measurements in leptonic final states in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01289)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究基于CMS探测器在质心能量13 TeV下采集的138 fb⁻¹数据，对质子-质子对撞中的矢量玻色子散射（vector boson scattering）进行了统计组合测量，同时约束同号WW、异号WW、WZ和ZZ四个通道的信号率，并与标准模型（Standard Model）预言进行对比。组合分析通过相干框架显著提升了灵敏度，所有结果与标准模型一致；此外还进行了电荷分离测量以探测W玻色子的产生电荷不对称性（charge asymmetry）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量玻色子散射是探测电弱对称性破缺（electroweak symmetry breaking）机制的关键过程，但单一通道的统计灵敏度有限。通过对多个通道进行统计组合，可以更全面且精度更高地检验标准模型预言，并为超出标准模型的新物理提供约束窗口。

Method: 研究使用了CMS探测器在2016—2018年采集、积分亮度为138 fb⁻¹的数据集。事件选择要求包含两个矢量玻色子（其中至少一个发生轻子衰变），并且至少有两个附加喷注（jets），这些喷注重质量大、赝快度（pseudorapidity）间隔大。对各通道信号率进行提取，并在相干框架中同时约束WW（同号与异号）、WZ和ZZ通道，此外还对W玻色子按电荷区分进行电荷分离测量。

Result: 组合分析在相干框架下同时约束全部通道，相比单个输入提升了整体灵敏度；所有信号率提取结果均与标准模型预言一致。电荷分离测量提供了对W玻色子产生电荷不对称性的敏感度。

Conclusion: 通过对矢量玻色子散射多通道的统计组合，该研究实现了一致的全局视角，验证了标准模型的预言，并为进一步探测电荷不对称性等精细效应提供了新的实验基础。

Abstract: A statistical combination of vector boson scattering measurements in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV is presented. The analysis is based on a dataset corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, collected with the CMS detector in 2016$-$2018. Events are selected to contain two vector bosons, with at least one decaying leptonically, and at least two additional jets with a high invariant mass and a large pseudorapidity separation. Signal rates are extracted for same-sign WW, opposite-sign WW, as well as for WZ and ZZ channels, and are compared with the standard model predictions. The combination provides a global view of vector boson scattering by simultaneously constraining all channels in a coherent framework, improving sensitivity beyond individual inputs, with all results in agreement with the standard model predictions. In addition, a charge-separated measurement is performed in which W bosons are distinguished according to their electric charge, allowing sensitivity to the production charge asymmetry.

</details>


### [241] [Long-Delayed Afterpulse Measurement of JUNO 20-inch Photomultiplier Tubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01943)
*Xiaojie Luo,Cailian Jiang,Haojie Dong,Yuduo Guan,Gaosong Li,Zhonghua Qin,Zhenning Qu,Junyu Shao,Liangjian Wen,Zeyuan Yu,Boyi Zheng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文针对大型液闪探测器（如江门中微子实验JUNO）中宇宙线缪子等高强度事件诱发的PMT长延迟后脉冲进行了系统性测量，刻画了打拿极（dynode）型与微通道板（MCP）型两种20英寸PMT的后脉冲时间分布，并定量给出了微秒尺度后脉冲的产额及其随主光强的线性增长关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高精度中微子实验中，高强度事件会诱发光电倍增管（PMT）后脉冲（afterpulse），对延迟物理信号的测量构成仪器本底干扰。为系统性评估该本底，需对大型液闪探测器（如江门中微子实验JUNO）中PMT的长延迟后脉冲进行专门测量与定量表征。

Method: 在JUNO的两种20英寸PMT（打拿极型和微通道板型）上进行延时后脉冲测量：先在1.8毫秒的直读波形窗口内刻画后脉冲时间分布，再利用滑动窗口读出策略将测量范围扩展到20毫秒。

Result: 观测到不同的长延迟后脉冲成分，且强烈依赖PMT倍增结构：打拿极PMT在约260微秒处出现宽带后脉冲峰；MCP-PMT则在约90微秒处有显著峰、约550微秒处有额外成分，以及一个更小的毫秒尺度宽带成分。微秒尺度后脉冲产额在选定的延迟窗口内处于每主光电子的10^-3量级，并随主光强近似线性增加。

Conclusion: 高强度事件后累积的延迟活动不可忽略，这些定量结果为PMT响应表征及高精度中微子实验中延迟关联本底的精确建模提供了关键输入。

Abstract: In large-scale liquid scintillator detectors such as the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO), high-intensity events like cosmic muons induce photomultiplier tube (PMT) afterpulses that can interfere with the analysis of delayed physics signals. To systematically evaluate this instrumental background, we present a dedicated measurement of long-delayed afterpulses in two types of JUNO 20-inch PMTs: a dynode-based PMT and a microchannel-plate (MCP) PMT. The afterpulse time profiles were first characterized within a direct 1.8~ms waveform window and were further extended to 20~ms using a sliding-window readout strategy. Distinct long-delayed components are observed, revealing a strong dependence on the PMT multiplication structure. The dynode PMT exhibits a broad afterpulse component peaking at approximately 260~$μ$s, whereas the MCP-PMT shows a pronounced peak around 90~$μ$s, an additional component around 550~$μ$s, and a much smaller, broadly distributed millisecond-scale component. For the microsecond-scale components, the afterpulse yield per primary photoelectron is at the $10^{-3}$ level in the selected delayed windows and increases approximately linearly with the primary light intensity. The accumulated delayed activity can therefore become non-negligible following high-intensity events. These quantitative findings provide critical inputs for PMT response characterization and for the accurate modeling of delayed correlated backgrounds in high-precision neutrino experiments.

</details>


### [242] [Construction, commissioning, and performance of the ENUBET demonstrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02164)
*F. Acerbi,I. Angelis,L. Bomben,M. Bonesini,F. Bramati,A. Branca,C. Brizzolari,G. Brunetti,G. T. Burton,S. Capelli,M. Capitani,S. Carturan,M. G. Catanesi,S. Cecchini,N. Charitonidis,F. Cindolo,J. Cogan,G. Cogo,G. Collazuol,D. D'Ago,F. Dal Corso,C. Delogu,G. De Rosa,S. Dolan,A. Falcone,M. Feltre,A. Gola,D. Guffanti,L. Halić,F. Iacob,C. Jollet,A. Kallitsopoulou,B. Kliček,Y. Kudenko,Ch. Lampoudis,M. Laveder,P. Legou,S. Levorato,A. Longhin,L. Ludovici,E. Lutsenko,L. Magaletti,G. Mandrioli,S. Marangoni,A. Margotti,V. Mascagna,M. Mattiazzi,N. Mauri,J. McElwee,L. Meazza,A. Meregaglia,M. Mezzetto,L. Munteanu,M. Pari,T. Papaevangelou,E. G. Parozzi,L. Pasqualini,G. Paternoster,L. Patrizii,M. Perrin-Terrin,M. Pozzato,M. Prest,F. Pupilli,E. Radicioni,A. C. Ruggeri,G. Saibene,D. Sampsonidis,C. Scian,L. Silvestrin,G. Sirri,M. Stipčević,M. Tenti,F. Terranova,M. Torti,S. E. Tzamarias,E. Vallazza,C. Vallée*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了ENUBET仪器化衰变隧道最终全尺寸原型（称为Demonstrator）的构建与测试结果，该原型基于铁吸收体和闪烁体瓦片（scintillator tiles）配合波长位移光纤（wavelength-shifting fibers）和硅光电倍增管（SiPMs）读取，是一种纵向采样量能器（longitudinal sampling calorimeter）技术。在CERN East Area设施的2022、2023和2024年束流测试实验中，该原型展示了完全可扩展性，并在高达5 GeV的带电粒子束下评估了能量分辨率、线性度和粒子鉴别能力，结果与GEANT4模拟预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ENUBET项目需要通过轻子鉴别（lepton identification）在衰变隧道内实现中微子监测，因此需要开发一种可扩展的专用纵向采样量能器技术。此前尚未有全尺寸原型在真实束流条件下验证其完整性能和可扩展性。

Method: 构建了名为Demonstrator的ENUBET仪器化衰变隧道的最终全尺寸原型，其基于铁吸收体和闪烁体瓦片，通过波长位移光纤和SiPM进行读出。在CERN East Area设施进行了2022、2023和2024年三轮束流测试，使用能量高达5 GeV的带电粒子束，逐通道评估了响应，并测量了电子能量分辨率、线性度和粒子鉴别能力。同时将实验结果与GEANT4模拟预测进行了对比。

Result: 实验结果显示该纵向采样量能器技术具有完全可扩展性（full scalability），在能量分辨率、线性度和粒子鉴别能力方面的表现满足ENUBET通过轻子鉴别进行中微子监测的要求。实验数据与GEANT4模拟预测一致，验证了ENUBET的全模拟框架。尽管识别出了一些局限性，但这些限制可在未来开发中直接解决。

Conclusion: Demonstrator原型的性能满足ENUBET衰变隧道内基于轻子鉴别实现中微子监测的需求，并验证了ENUBET全模拟的可靠性。已识别的局限性可在后续开发中加以解决，为该技术未来的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: A final full-size prototype of the instrumented decay tunnel of ENUBET, referred to as the Demonstrator, was constructed and its performance was tested during the 2022, 2023 and 2024 beam test campaigns at the CERN East Area facility. We demonstrate the full scalability of this specialized longitudinal sampling calorimeter technology, based on iron absorbers and scintillator tiles readout by wavelength-shifting fibers and SiPMs. In addition, we evaluate the channel-by- channel response and the performance in terms of electron energy resolution, linearity, and particle identification capabilities, using charged particle beams with energies up to 5 GeV. A comparison with the predictions of a GEANT4 simulation of the prototype is also presented. The results meet the requirements for neutrino monitoring through lepton identification in the decay tunnel and validate the ENUBET full simulation. Although some limitations have been identified, they can be readily addressed in future developments.

</details>
