Our analysis yields a quantitative assessment of the range of validity of the weak coupling assumption in a circuit. Due to the correspondence of the two results, we feel confident in applying the weak coupling Lindblad model also for analyzing heat transport in quantum circuits consisting, e.g. of qubits and/or non-linear resonators.

</details>


### [279] [Strain engineering of transient exciton diffusion in WSe2 monolayers at cryogenic temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02513)
*Roberto Rosati,Mohammed Adel Aly Nouh,Robert Schmidt,Rudolf Bratschitsch,Ermin Malic*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了低温下hBN封装的单层WSe2中，应变对非平衡激子（exciton）瞬态光致发光与扩散的影响，发现拉伸应变可加速热激子瞬态扩散（源于亮—暗激子间声子介导散射被抑制），压缩应变则通过M声子发射引起瞬态扩散的周期性调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钨基过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenide）中暗激子（dark exciton）为能量最低态，主导低温光致发光与激子热化/传播。应变可改变亮暗激子相对能量差，但其对非平衡激子瞬态光致发光与扩散的影响此前基本未被探索。

Method: 在低温下对hBN封装的单层WSe2施加拉伸与压缩应变，结合瞬态光致发光（transient photoluminescence）测量与激子时空扩散分析，系统考察非平衡激子的动力学行为。

Result: 拉伸应变使热激子过剩能量（excess energy）突然增加，从而加速瞬态扩散，机理归结为亮到暗激子的声子介导散射被抑制；压缩应变下，应变驱动的M声子发射导致瞬态激子扩散出现周期性调制。

Conclusion: 应变可用于工程化调控二维半导体的瞬态光致发光与激子扩散，为二维材料中激子输运与光学性质的可控设计提供微观物理依据。

Abstract: Tungsten-based transition metal dichalcogenides exhibit dark excitons as the energetically lowest states. These are crucial for exciton thermalization and propagation and they dominate low-temperature photoluminescence via the emergence of pronounced phonon sidebands. After a resonant excitation, highly mobile hot dark excitons are formed, which quickly thermalize into an equilibrium distribution. The application of strain modifies the exciton energy landscape and, in particular, the relative energy separation between bright and dark exciton states. The impact of strain on the transient photoluminescence and diffusion of non-equilibrium excitons has remained largely unexplored so far. In this work, we investigate the spatiotemporal exciton dynamics in strained hBN-encapsulated WSe2 monolayers at cryogenic temperatures. We demonstrate that tensile strain abruptly increases the excess energy of hot excitons, thereby accelerating their transient diffusion. We trace this back to suppressed phonon-mediated scattering from bright to dark excitons. Furthermore, we predict a periodic modulation of the transient exciton diffusion in the presence of a compressive strain resulting from strain-driven emission of M phonons. The gained microscopic insights illustrate how strain can be used to engineer transient photoluminescence and exciton diffusion in technologically promising 2D semiconductors.

</details>


### [280] [Thermal Stability of Two-Dimensional Crystals with Extended OH Hydrogen-Bonded Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02544)
*Alexander V. Savin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过对沉积在六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, h-BN）基底上的分子单层结构进行数值模拟，发现含有苯环和羟基的分子能够形成具有线性氢键链（OH⋯OH⋯OH⋯）的稳定二维晶体，这些结构具有高熔点（47–377 °C），其中若干分子与h-BN构成的多层结构有望用于开发可高温运行的质子交换膜（proton-exchange membrane）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究含苯环与羟基基团的有机分子能否在平整的h-BN基底上形成稳定的二维有序结构，并评估其热稳定性以及在质子交换膜等应用中的潜力。

Method: 对七种分子（苯酚、氢醌、4-苯基苯酚、4-(4-苯基苯基)苯酚、对乙酰氨基酚、4-羟基苯甲酰苯胺及4,4-二羟基苯甲酰苯胺）在h-BN基底上的单层结构进行了动力学数值模拟（numerical simulation of dynamics），分析了分子间氢键链的形成情况以及二维晶体的熔化起始温度。

Result: 上述七种分子均能在h-BN表面形成稳定的二维晶体，其熔化起始温度分别为47、187、127、247、167、307和377 °C。苯环保证分子与基底之间的强相互作用，同时不阻碍延伸氢键链的形成。

Conclusion: 由h-BN片层与氢醌、对乙酰氨基酚和4-羟基苯甲酰苯胺分子构成的多层结构可用于开发能够在高温下运行的新型质子交换膜。

Abstract: Numerical simulations of the dynamics of monolayer structures of molecules deposited on a sheet of hexagonal boron nitride (h-BN) have been performed. It is shown that molecules containing benzene rings and hydroxyl groups in their structure can form stable two-dimensional crystals with linear chains of hydrogen bonds OH$\cdots$OH$\cdots$OH$\cdots$ Such structures are formed by the following molecules: phenol (C$_6$H$_5$OH), hydroquinone (C$_6$H$_4$(OH)$_2$), 4-phenylphenol (C$_6$H$_5$--C$_6$H$_4$OH), 4-(4-phenylphenyl)phenol (C$_6$H$_5$--C$_6$H$_4$--C$_6$H$_4$OH), paracetamol (CH$_3$C(O)NHC$_6$H$_4$OH), 4-hydroxybenzanilide (C$_6$H$_5$C(O)NHC$_6$H$_4$OH) and 4,4-dihydroxybenzanilide (C$_6$H$_4$OHC(O)NHC$_6$H$_4$OH). On the one hand, the benzene rings in these molecules ensure their strong interaction with the flat substrate; on the other hand, they do not hinder the formation of extended hydrogen-bonded chains. The monolayer structures of these molecules exhibit high thermal stability: the onset melting temperatures of their 2D crystals are 47, 187, 127, 247, 167, 307, and 377 $^\circ$C, respectively. The simulations allow us to conclude that multilayer structures composed of h-BN sheets and molecules of hydroquinone, paracetamol, and 4-hydroxybenzanilide can be used for the development of novel proton-exchange membranes capable of operating at elevated temperatures.

</details>


### [281] [Magnetic Skyrmion Interacting with Optical Skyrmion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02552)
*Lan Bo,Jian Chen,Xichao Zhang,Yan Zhou,Chengwei Qiu,Masahito Mochizuki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了单个磁性斯格明子（magnetic skyrmion, MSk）与光学斯格明子（optical skyrmion, OSk）光束之间的相互作用，识别出三种不同的非线性动力学模式：旋转、跳跃和摆线运动，并通过将光驱动力分解为梯度、轨道角动量和自旋角动量贡献来阐明各自的物理作用，最终在统一框架下桥接了拓扑粒子与拓扑场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性斯格明子和光学斯格明子分别体现了物质和光中的拓扑性，但二者之间的相互作用尚未被系统研究。本文旨在探索单个MSk与OSk光束相互作用时涌现的动力学行为，并理解其背后的物理机制。

Method: 将光学驱动力分解为梯度力、轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum）贡献和自旋角动量（spin angular momentum）贡献三个部分，分别对应径向约束、方位角漂移和进动调制的作用。在三维情况下，耦合获得由差分Gouy相位支配的传播依赖相位，从而产生z不对称的跳跃轨迹。

Result: 识别出三种不同的非线性动力学模式：旋转、跳跃和摆线运动。跳跃运动源于OSk光束的方位角不对称性，并通过MSk与OSk之间的磁化-极化耦合表现出空间选择性。在三维情况下，差分Gouy相位导致z不对称的跳跃轨迹。

Conclusion: 这些结果在统一框架中桥接了拓扑粒子与拓扑场，为光磁子学（optomagnonic）控制提供了具有螺旋性选择性和相位可编程性的新路径。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions (MSks) and optical skyrmions (OSks) embody topology in matter and in light, respectively. Here we investigate the interaction between a single MSk and an OSk beam. Three distinct nonlinear dynamical modes are identified: rotation, skipping, and trochoidal motion. By decomposing the optical driving force into gradient, orbital-angular-momentum, and spin-angular-momentum contributions, we clarify their respective roles of radial confinement, azimuthal drift, and precessional modulation. The skipping motion arises from the azimuthal asymmetry of the OSk beam and exhibits spatial selectivity originating from the magnetization-polarization coupling between the MSk and OSk. In three dimensions, the coupling acquires a propagation-dependent phase dominated by the differential Gouy phase, which yields $z$-asymmetric skipping trajectories. These results bridge topological particles and topological fields within a unified framework, offering helicity-selective and phase-programmable routes to optomagnonic control.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [282] [Constructing Self-Regulating Field Theoriesfrom Primal Wave Fields without Background Manifolds:The Emergence of the Coordinate Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01650)
*Terence R. Smith*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个严格的数学框架，从被称为"原始波动场"（primal wave fields）的非局域周期原语中推导出连续时空坐标连续体和自调控场动力学，通过构建三层代数架构（$W_0$、$W_A$、$W_I$）摆脱了对实数背景坐标容器的依赖，从而在波数分数空间中天然正则化相互作用积分，消除点状几何无穷大，无需外部数学截断。该方法还以热扩散方程为例，论证紫外线灾难（ultraviolet catastrophe）并非热扩散过程时间反演的固有属性，而是经典扩散方程点表示法的产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续介质力学（continuum mechanics）将物理现象建模在预先存在的实数背景之上，这在计算点状场相互作用时不可避免地引入非物理的局部数学发散（奇异性）。作者旨在通过构造独立于任何背景坐标容器的代数架构，从根本上消除这些基础性缺陷。

Method: 作者构建三层代数架构——基础集合 $W_0$、交换环（commutative ring）$W_A$ 和分式域 $W_I$。利用在通用同步时钟格点上的最小原始追踪算子集合，证明光滑时空坐标连续体作为稠密、相交的有理共振网络 $Q$ 的拓扑完备化（topological completion）自然涌现。在该波数分数空间中直接表述物理场运算，可原生正则化相互作用积分。

Result: 该框架消除了逐点几何无穷大，使场传播内在有界，无需依赖外部数学截断。将该方法应用于热扩散方程后表明，紫外线灾难并非热扩散过程逆时运行的固有属性，而是源自经典扩散方程基于点（point-based）的表示方式。

Conclusion: 研究结论指向一个根本性认识：物理理论中的奇异性问题可以通过放弃点状实数背景表示、转向非局域周期波原语来系统性解决。这一框架不仅提供了内在正则化（regularization）机制，还对经典物理问题（如紫外线灾难）的根源提供了新解释。

Abstract: This paper introduces a rigorous mathematical framework in which the continuous space-time coordinate continuum and self-regulating field dynamics are derived from non-local, periodic primitives termed primal wave fields. Traditional continuum mechanics models physical phenomena over a pre-existing background of real numbers ($R$), an approach that inherently introduces unphysical, localized mathematical divergences (singularities) when computing point-like field interactions. We resolve these foundational vulnerabilities by constructing a three-tiered algebraic architecture - the base set $W_0$, the commutative ring $W_A$, and the field of fractions $W_I$ - independent of any background coordinate container. Utilizing a minimal set of primitive tracking operators over a universal synchronous clock lattice, we demonstrate that the smooth space-time coordinate continuum emerges naturally as the topological completion of a dense, intersecting rational resonance network $Q$. Finally, we show that formulating physical field operations directly within this wave-number fraction space natively regularizes interaction integrals, eliminating point-wise geometric infinities and rendering field propagation intrinsically bounded without relying on external mathematical cutoffs. An application of this approach demonstrates that the ultraviolet catastrophe is not an intrinsic property of the thermal diffusion process run backwards in time, but rather of the point-based representation of the classical diffusion equation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [283] [PolymerGPT: Multi-property Optimization with a Decoder-Based GPT Model for Generative Polymer Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01431)
*Charlie Pyle,Adarsh Gadari,C. Adrian Figg,Zhenquan Jia,Yaohang Li,Chunjiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PolymerGPT，一个基于解码器架构GPT模型的聚合物生成框架，通过学习的条件前缀（conditioning prefixes）将多达37种常用聚合物物理性质直接纳入生成过程，支持多性质同时优化以及支架（scaffold）条件约束，实验表明其在无条件与条件生成中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物性质预测已获较多关注，但面向期望性质的逆向生成设计（inverse generative design）研究有限；现有方法仅支持单一性质优化，而宏观材料行为的准确预测需要同时控制多个物理性质。

Method: 提出PolymerGPT，一种基于解码器（decoder）架构的GPT模型，通过可学习的条件前缀将最多37种常用聚合物性质嵌入生成过程，同时支持指定支架的支架条件（scaffold condition），实现多性质协同优化的直接生成。

Result: 实验显示PolymerGPT在无条件与条件生成中均取得优异性能，同时保持高的有效性（validity）、独特性（uniqueness）和新颖性（novelty）；以五种关键性质为条件时，生成结构的预测值与所有目标性质同时高度吻合。

Conclusion: PolymerGPT提供了一种变革性的框架，支持对大规模聚合物性质集合的直接优化，填补了多性质约束下聚合物逆向生成设计的空白。

Abstract: Polymer property prediction and inverse generative design targeting desired properties are two crucial tasks in machine learning-assisted polymer design. While the former has received considerable attention, there have been limited methods developed for the latter. Existing methods focus on single-property optimization in the generative process, whereas accurate prediction of macroscopic material behavior requires simultaneous control of multiple physical properties. In this paper, we provide a transformative framework for direct optimization of a large collection of polymer properties. We propose PolymerGPT, a decoder-based GPT model that incorporates up to 37 commonly used polymer properties into the generative process via learned conditioning prefixes. It also supports a scaffold condition that specifies a desired scaffold for predicted structures. Our experimental results demonstrate that PolymerGPT achieves exceptional performance for unconditional and conditional generation while maintaining high validity, uniqueness, and novelty. Conditioning on five key properties yields generated structures whose predicted values closely match all target properties simultaneously.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [284] [Renormalizations in holomorphic field theories on Kähler manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00546)
*Minghao Wang,Junrong Yan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明闭实解析Kähler流形上的全纯场论（holomorphic field theories）所产生的Feynman图积分（Feynman graph integrals）在三种重整化方案——热核重整化（heat-kernel renormalization）、柯西主值重整化（Cauchy principal value renormalization）和zeta函数重整化（zeta-function renormalization）——下均为收敛的，且三者给出相同的结果。证明基于代数几何中的奇妙紧化（wonderful compactifications）理论，并由此推导出这些图积分的规范反常公式（gauge anomaly formula）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Feynman图积分的发散性是微扰量子场论（perturbative quantum field theories）研究的核心问题之一，严格的表述通常需要引入重整化（renormalization）机制。作者旨在为闭实解析Kähler流形上的全纯场论提供一种几何化的严格收敛性证明，并建立多种重整化方案之间的一致关系。

Method: 证明方法基于代数几何中的奇妙紧化理论，该理论为Feynman图积分提供了几何理解。作者分别针对热核重整化、柯西主值重整化和zeta函数重整化三种方案，证明了全纯场论中Feynman图积分的收敛性，并验证三种重整化过程产生相同的值。

Result: 主要结果为：来自闭实解析Kähler流形上全纯场论的Feynman图积分在三种重整化方案下均收敛，且三者得到相同的数值结果。由此进一步推导出这些图积分的规范反常公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为全纯场论中的Feynman图积分提供了统一的收敛性证明，建立了三种常用重整化方案之间的一致性，并通过几何方法（奇妙紧化）加深了对这些积分的理解。规范的推导出规范反常公式是该收敛性结果的直接推论。

Abstract: The divergence of Feynman graph integrals is one of the central issues in the study of perturbative quantum field theories. A rigorous formulation of these integrals usually requires renormalization. In this paper, we prove that the Feynman graph integrals arising from holomorphic field theories on closed real-analytic Kähler manifolds are convergent with respect to heat-kernel renormalization, Cauchy principal value renormalization, and zeta-function renormalization. Moreover, these three renormalization procedures produce the same value. Our proof is based on the theory of wonderful compactifications in algebraic geometry, which provides a geometric understanding of these integrals. As a consequence, we establish a gauge anomaly formula for these graph integrals.

</details>


### [285] [Remarks on Regular Approximations to the Robin Aharonov-Bohm Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00637)
*Cesar R. de Oliveira*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究$\mathbb R^3$空间中具有可穿透环形螺线管和可变电导率的薛定谔算子族，证明了它们可以在强预解意义下逼近带Robin边界条件的磁阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆算子。同时证明了通过光滑势和内部势垒的近似可得到Dirichlet边界条件，该结论适用于更一般的光滑闭合紧致曲面及连续有界磁势情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆（Aharonov-Bohm）效应是量子力学中的重要现象，其严格的数学描述通常涉及带奇异边界条件的算子。本文旨在为磁阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆算子在Robin边界条件下的情形建立可穿透势（penetrable potentials）的近似框架，拓展此前已有结果的应用范围。

Method: 采用Γ-收敛（Γ-convergence）技术建立强预解（strong resolvent）意义下的算子逼近。具体构造两类近似：一类为带Robin参数$L>0$的可穿透环形螺线管（toroidal solenoid）势族，另一类在螺线管内部施加光滑势和势垒以获得Dirichlet边界条件。结论推广到一般光滑闭紧曲面及连续有界磁势。

Result: 证明了带可变电导率的可穿透环形螺线管薛定谔算子族在强预解意义下收敛于带Robin边界条件的磁阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆算子；同时展示了通过光滑势加内部势垒的近似途径可导出Dirichlet边界条件。近似结果对更一般的光滑闭合紧致曲面和连续有界磁势同样成立。

Conclusion: 该文给出了磁阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆算子带Robin边界条件的严格可穿透势近似方案，并通过Γ-收敛方法统一了Robin与Dirichlet两种边界条件情形的构造路径，为相关边界条件下的量子力学问题提供了数学基础。

Abstract: In the space $\mathbb R^3$, for Robin parameter $L>0$, it is shown that there is a family of Schrödinger operators with penetrable toroidal solenoid and variable conductivity that approximates the magnetic Aharonov-Bohm operator with a Robin boundary condition at the solenoid (border). It is also shown that approximations via smooth potentials and then a barrier in the solenoid interior give Dirichlet boundary conditions. The approximations are in the strong resolvent sense and obtained through the $Γ$-convergence technique, and they hold for the more general setting of smooth, closed and compact surfaces and continuous and bounded magnetic potentials.

</details>


### [286] [Generalized Marginals of tie -Wigner Distribution nand Lagrangian Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00751)
*Maurice de Gosson*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于辛群在拉格朗日格拉斯曼流形上的传递作用，发展了一种拉格朗日Radon变换的辛几何表述，将经典维格纳变换边缘（视为初等断层图）自然推广到任意拉格朗日子空间，从而建立了广义断层成像的统一理论框架，并将其应用于泡利重构问题，建立了从广义拉格朗日断层图重构密度算子的公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典断层成像（tomography）仅考虑位置和动量测量，且在辛旋转下缺乏统一处理。作者希望利用辛群和拉格朗日格拉斯曼流形的几何结构，为拉格朗日Radon变换提供一种自然的辛表述，从而将断层成像推广到任意拉格朗日子空间。

Method: 论文采用几何方法，基于辛群（symplectic group）在拉格朗日格拉斯曼流形（Lagrangian Grassmannian）上的传递作用，构造拉格朗日Radon变换（Lagrangian Radon transform）的辛形式表述。该框架将维格纳变换（Wigner transform）的边缘视为初等断层图（elementary tomograms），并将其推广至任意拉格朗日子空间。

Result: 作者建立了广义断层成像（generalized tomography），统一处理了由辛旋转（symplectic rotations）联系的位置与动量测量；并作为应用，重新审视了泡利重构问题（Pauli reconstruction problem），确立了从广义拉格朗日断层图重构密度算子（density operators）的重构公式。

Conclusion: 辛几何框架为断层成像提供了统一且自然的数学基础，能够统一位置与动量测量并推广到任意拉格朗日子空间，同时为解决泡利重构问题提供了新的工具和重构公式。

Abstract: We develop a symplectic formulation of the Lagrangian Radon transform based on the transitive action of the symplectic group on the Lagrangian Grassmannian. This geometric framework naturally extends the classical notion of the marginals of the Wigner transform, viewed as elementary tomograms, to arbitrary Lagrangian subspaces. The resulting generalized tomography provides a unified treatment of position and momentum measurements related by symplectic rotations. As applications, we revisit the Pauli reconstruction problem and establish reconstruction formulas for density operators from generalized Lagrangian tomograms.

</details>


### [287] [Analysis of Conjectural Improvements to Minkowski's Lower Bound on the Sphere Packing Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00920)
*Carlo Vanoni,Alan Guo,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Torquato和Stillinger关于高维球堆积密度下界猜想的框架下，证明了一族超均匀（hyperuniform）对关联函数能够在足够高的维度中对Minkowski经典下界产生多项式改进（形式为 φ_max ≳ d^β 2^{-d}，对任意固定 β>1），并进一步利用Cohn–Elkies对偶线性规划上界公式独立导出了相同的指数渐近速率，为Torquato–Stillinger下界猜想提供了新的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Minkowski经典下界在高维欧氏空间球堆积问题中相对较弱，Torquato和Stillinger基于对关联函数（pair-correlation function）优化框架猜测指数级改进的可能性。本文旨在条件性地检验这一猜想，并探索高维无序致密堆积（disordered dense packings）存在的理论依据。

Method: 在Torquato–Stillinger可实现性（realizability）猜想的条件下，构造一族简单的超均匀（hyperuniform）对关联函数，证明其可在足够高维度中导致密度下界多项式改进为 φ_max ≳ d^β 2^{-d}（任意固定 β>1）。同时，从Cohn–Elkies对偶线性规划（dual linear programming）上界公式出发，独立推导相同的指数渐近速率，分析其径向目标检验函数无法渐近排除具有Torquato–Stillinger密度标度的堆积。

Result: 证明了对满足可实现性猜想的超均匀对关联函数族，密度下界可达到 φ_max ≳ d^β 2^{-d} 的多项式改进形式；随着多项式的指数（polynomial exponent）随维度增大，该族连续逼近先前猜想的指数改进。Cohn–Elkies对偶公式的分析也独立给出了相同的指数渐近速率，两种方法的吻合为Torquato–Stillinger下界猜想提供了有力证据。

Conclusion: 两种独立方法（对关联函数优化框架与Cohn–Elkies对偶线性规划上界公式）在指数渐近速率上的一致性，支持了高维空间中可能确实存在异常致密的无序球堆积，并加强了Torquato–Stillinger下界猜想的可信度。

Abstract: Torquato and Stillinger conjectured an exponential improvement of Minkowski's classical lower bound on the maximal density of sphere packings in high-dimensional Euclidean space $\mathbb{R}^d$ using a pair-correlation-function optimization framework. Conditional on their realizability conjecture, we show that a simple family of hyperuniform pair correlation functions yields polynomial improvements over Minkowski's lower bound of the form $φ_{\mathrm{max}} \gtrsim d^β2^{-d}$ for every fixed $β>1$ in sufficiently high dimensions. As the polynomial exponent is allowed to increase with dimension, this family continuously approaches the previously conjectured exponential improvement. We further derive the same exponential asymptotic rate independently from the Cohn--Elkies dual linear programming upper bound formulation, demonstrating that its radial objective test functions cannot asymptotically exclude packings with the Torquato--Stillinger density scalings. The agreement between these alternative approaches provides new evidence that exceptionally dense disordered sphere packings may exist in high dimensions and strengthens the case for the Torquato--Stillinger conjectural lower bound.

</details>


### [288] [Connes spectral distance on twisted fuzzy torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01110)
*Zhi-Kang You,Bing-Sheng Lin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了模糊环面（fuzzy torus）上态之间的Connes谱距离（Connes spectral distance）。作者通过构造对易子与反对易子形式的Dirac算子，建立了模糊环面的谱三元组（spectral triple），系统分析了谱距离的性质，发现了谱距离之间的互反毕达哥拉斯定理（reciprocal Pythagorean theorem），并发现对角态的最优元素仍为对角形式。文中还显式计算了几类简单态的谱距离，发现了循环对称性，并进一步探讨了带共形扭曲（conformal twist）的模糊环面与谱距离的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱距离是非交换几何（noncommutative geometry）中度量空间的重要概念，但在模糊环面这类非交换流形上，其结构性质尚未被系统刻画。本文旨在通过谱三元组的构造，深入理解模糊环面上谱距离的代数与几何性质，并探索共形变形对谱距离的影响。

Method: 作者在模糊环面上通过对易子和反对易子构造Dirac算子，进而构建谱三元组；利用谱距离的定义研究态之间的距离性质；引入条件期望函数（conditional expectation function）构造最优元素（optimal element），实现Lipschitz半范数（Lipschitz seminorm）的压缩；并对简单态（包括基本态和简单混合态）进行显式谱距离计算；最后通过引入共形扭曲构造新的模糊环面以研究参数与谱距离的关联。

Result: 获得了以下主要结果：谱距离之间成立互反毕达哥拉斯定理；条件期望函数可诱导Lipschitz半范数的压缩；任意对角态（diagonal states）对应的最优元素仍为对角形式；对基本态和简单混合态实现了谱距离的显式计算；对角态的最优元素与谱距离均表现出特定种类的循环对称性（cyclic symmetry）；此外还揭示了共形参数与谱距离之间的定量关系。

Conclusion: 研究系统地揭示了模糊环面上谱距离的丰富代数结构，特别是对角态情形下的对称性与毕达哥拉斯型恒等式，并为非交换几何中度量信息的计算与理解提供了新的工具与视角。共形扭曲的引入进一步说明谱距离对几何变形敏感，可能为基于谱距离的物理模型提供参考。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the Connes spectral distance between states on the fuzzy torus. We construct a Dirac operator by commutators and anticommutators. Based on this Dirac operator, we construct a spectral triple of the fuzzy torus. We study some properties of the spectral distance on the fuzzy torus. We find that there is a reciprocal Pythagorean theorem between the spectral distances. We construct a conditional expectation function of the optimal element which can lead to a contraction of the corresponding Lipschitz seminorm. We find that for any diagonal states, the corresponding optimal elements of spectral distances are also diagonal. We explicitly calculate the spectral distances of some simple states, including basic states and some simple mixed states. We find that there are some kinds of cyclic symmetry in both the optimal elements and the spectral distances between the diagonal states. Furthermore, we also construct a fuzzy torus with some type of conformal twist, and study the relation between conformal parameters and spectral distances.

</details>


### [289] [Dual Variational Principles for Curl Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01219)
*Arash Yavari,Amit Acharya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对卷曲力（curl forces）作用下粒子动力学的对偶变分表述（dual variational formulation），通过引入位置与速度的对偶变量及辅助函数，构造了一个对偶作用量（dual action），其欧拉-拉格朗日方程能够同时恢复原始运动方程及其给定初始条件。该表述通过二维和三维非线性卷曲力场以及经典齐格勒柱（Ziegler column）加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷曲力是依赖于位置的、非保守且非耗散力，通常无法由普通势能导出，因此其运动方程一般无法由标准变分原理（variational principle）得到。本文旨在弥补这一空缺，为非保守卷曲力动力学提供一种可行的变分描述。

Method: 作者引入与位置和速度对偶的变量以及一个辅助函数，构造了"前对偶作用量"（pre-dual action），将运动方程作为约束嵌入其中。通过对原始变量求驻值，定义了对偶到原始的映射，代入前对偶作用量后得到完全以对偶变量表达的对偶作用量；其对偶哈密顿量（dual Hamiltonian）沿驻定对偶轨迹守恒。

Result: 对偶作用量的欧拉-拉格朗日方程同时恢复了原始运动方程及其指定的初始条件。辅助对偶哈密顿量沿驻定对偶轨迹守恒，但不代表物理能量。使用二维与三维非线性卷曲力场及经典齐格勒柱三个示例验证了该表述的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，即使在没有普通势能或传统拉格朗日量（Lagrangian）的情况下，非保守卷曲力动力学仍可具有变分描述。

Abstract: Curl forces are position-dependent, non-conservative, and non-dissipative forces that, in general, cannot be derived from an ordinary potential energy. Consequently, their equations of motion do not, in general, follow from a standard variational principle. In this paper, we present a dual variational formulation for particle dynamics under curl forces. By introducing variables dual to position and velocity and an auxiliary function, we construct a pre-dual action in which the equations of motion act as constraints. Stationarity with respect to the primal variables defines a dual-to-primal mapping, whose substitution into the pre-dual action gives an action expressed entirely in terms of the dual variables. The Euler--Lagrange equations of the dual action recover both the original equations of motion and their prescribed initial conditions. We also introduce an auxiliary dual Hamiltonian that is conserved along stationary dual trajectories, although it does not represent the physical energy. The formulation is illustrated using two nonlinear curl force fields in two and three dimensions and the classical Ziegler column. These examples demonstrate that non-conservative curl-force dynamics can admit variational descriptions even in the absence of an ordinary potential energy or a conventional Lagrangian.

</details>


### [290] [Beyond $K$-Theory: Geometry and Holomorphy in Hyperbolic Band Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01596)
*Steven Rayan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双曲带理论（hyperbolic band theory）中拓扑K-理论（topological $K$-theory）对全带问题的分类局限。作者区分运动学全纯性（kinematical holomorphy）与动力学全纯性（dynamical holomorphy），证明在紧致双曲曲面上存在三类K-理论意义上的不可分解性（nonfactorization），并揭示Kotani–Sunada底部带Hessian与Hodge内积之间的关系，进而通过Torelli定理和一致化原理恢复底层复结构与双曲结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在欧氏晶体中，拓扑K-理论为带隙自由费米子相提供了明确的稳定分类原则，但该方法在双曲带理论中对于全带问题不够决定性：广义动量扇区在模空间中变化，而映射到普通K-类会丢失其几何与全纯变化信息。作者旨在精确刻画这一信息损失，并探索拓扑在双曲背景下仍能保留的物理量。

Method: 本文将信息损失形式化为可观测因子分解问题（observable-factorization problem）。在紧致双曲曲面$X$上，作者在三个层面——纤维化$K^0(X)$、占据态$K^0(B)$以及算子代数K-理论——证明非因子分解性。同时，利用Kotani–Sunada底部带Hessian与$H^1(X;\mathbb{R})$上的Hodge内积之间的显式关系（精确到面积因子），结合积分相交形式恢复同调标记的主极化雅可比（principally polarized Jacobian）。作者还区分有限秩扇区与热力学体相，并研究算术同余塔中解析与$C^s$可观测量的收敛速率。

Result: 非因子分解性影响到的量包括谱与Higgs谱曲线、Berry全纯性与量子度规、部分填充的Hall响应、费米面与节点几何。拓扑保留的量为量子化配对与局域电荷。由Hessian与Hodge内积的对应关系，通过Torelli与一致化定理可以恢复底层复曲面与双曲曲面（但不含完整Teichmüller标记）。在算术同余塔中，解析可观测量以$O(|G_n|^{-\eta})$（$\eta>0$）收敛，$C^s$可观测量以$O((\log|G_n|)^{-s})$收敛，而既有的相干大秩极限可恢复体密度态矩。

Conclusion: 拓扑K-理论在双曲带理论中对稳定带间隙分类充分，但对全带问题不足够决定性，其信息损失本质上源于扇区空间的几何与全纯结构在K-类映射中被压缩。该损失可通过可观测因子分解问题精确刻画，且拓扑仍能保留量子化配对、局域电荷等量。底部带Hessian与Hodge内积的对应建立了带理论数据与曲面复几何之间的桥梁，并由此通过Torelli和一致化原理确定底层曲面。

Abstract: Topological $K$-theory supplies the decisive stable classification principle for gapped free-fermion phases in Euclidean crystals once symmetry and stabilization are fixed. In hyperbolic band theory, by contrast, it is less decisive for the full band problem: generalized momentum sectors vary through moduli spaces, but passing to ordinary $K$-classes collapses their geometric and holomorphic variation. We distinguish kinematical holomorphy, in which complex geometry organizes the sector spaces, from dynamical holomorphy, in which that geometry informs a Hamiltonian or projector, and formulate the resulting loss as an observable-factorization problem.
  For a compact hyperbolic surface $X$, we prove nonfactorization in three settings: fibrewise $K^0(X)$, occupied-state $K^0(B)$, and operator-algebraic $K$-theory. The affected quantities include spectra and Higgs spectral curves, the Berry holonomy and the quantum metric, the partially filled Hall response, and the Fermi surface and nodal geometries. We also identify the quantized pairings and local charges retained by topology.
  Up to an explicit area factor, the Kotani--Sunada bottom-band Hessian is the Hodge inner product on $H^1(X;\mathbb R)$. Together with the integral intersection form, it recovers the homology-marked principally polarized Jacobian and hence, by Torelli and uniformization, the underlying complex and hyperbolic surface, though not a full Teichmüller marking. We also separate finite-rank sectors from the thermodynamic bulk. Locally faithful covers reproduce polynomial traces exactly and control continuous spectral observables. In arithmetic congruence towers, analytic observables converge as $O(|G_n|^{-η})$ for some $η>0$, and $C^s$ observables as $O((\log |G_n|)^{-s})$, while established coherent large-rank limits recover bulk density-of-states moments.

</details>


### [291] [Collective superintegrable systems from the Guillemin--Sternberg torus action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01878)
*L. Feher*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种研究集体哈密顿量（collective Hamiltonians）超可积性（superintegrability）的新方法。通过对连通半单紧致李群 G 在辛流形 M 上的哈密顿作用，借助 Guillemin 和 Sternberg（1983）提出的哈密顿环面作用，证明了哈密顿函数的泛函维度（functional dimensions）满足维度等式，从而确保阿贝尔泊松代数构成一个超可积系统，并补充了 Bolsinov 和 Jovanović 的先前工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超可积系统在可积系统理论中具有重要意义，但此前对于在连通半单紧致李群（connected semisimple compact Lie group）哈密顿作用下保持不变的集体哈密顿量的超可积性研究尚不充分。作者旨在发展一种新的系统性方法，从辛流形上的哈密顿群作用出发，建立集体哈密顿量超可积性的统一判定条件。

Method: 论文利用 Guillemin 和 Sternberg（GS, 1983）提出的哈密顿环面作用（Hamiltonian torus action）这一工具。设 J: M → g* 为 G 作用的动量映射（momentum map），定义 H := J*(C∞(g*)G) 为其在辛流形上的函数空间，并考虑其在 C∞(M) 中的中心化子（centralizer）F。作者证明功能性维度满足等式 ddim(H) + ddim(F) = dim(M)，结合非平凡性条件，确保阿贝尔泊松代数（Abelian Poisson algebra）H ⊂ C∞(M) 构成超可积系统。

Result: 证明了对于半单紧致李群哈密顿作用下的集体哈密顿量，其函数维度与中心化子函数维度之和等于辛流形维度。在非平凡性条件下，这一结果保证了 H 是超可积系统，并进一步得出 GS 环面作用的动量映射自然给出该系统的作用变量（action variables）。

Conclusion: 该工作为集体超可积性（collective superintegrability）提供了新的理论视角，是对 Bolsinov 和 Jovanović 早期成果的重要补充，并为利用群论与辛几何工具构造和分析超可积系统提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to the superintegrability of collective Hamiltonians invariant under a Hamiltonian action of a connected semisimple compact Lie group, $G$, on a symplectic manifold, $M$. By exploiting a Hamiltonian torus action that goes back to Guillemin and Sternberg [GS,1983], we demonstrate that the functional dimensions of $\mathfrak{H} := \mathcal{J}^*(C^\infty(\mathfrak{g}^*)^G)$, where $\mathcal{J}: M \to \mathfrak{g}^*$ is the momentum map of the $G$ action, and its centralizer $\mathfrak{F}$ in $C^\infty(M)$ satisfy the equality $\mathrm{ddim}(\mathfrak{H}) + \mathrm{ddim}(\mathfrak{F}) = \mathrm{dim}(M)$. Together with a non-triviality condition, this ensures that the Abelian Poisson algebra $\mathfrak{H}\subset C^\infty(M)$ represents a superintegrable system, and it also follows that the momentum map of the GS torus action yields action variables for the system. Our work provides a new insight into collective superintegrability complementing earlier results of Bolsinov and Jovanović.

</details>


### [292] [Geometric theory of generalised continua using moving frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01996)
*Boris Kolev,Cl{é}ment Ecker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过规范理论（gauge theory）框架对有限应变下的广义连续介质（generalised continua）进行统一分类，将广义构型定义为经典构型上的活动标架（moving frames），并证明参考广义构型的不变性是规范不变性的充要条件，由此恢复微观形态理论（micromorphic theory）作为任意物质方向（directors of matter）变形的一般理论，并通过结构群约化（structural group reduction）系统分类一阶广义介质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义连续介质理论虽能超越经典Cauchy弹性描述内部长度尺度效应和高阶力学加载，但现有高阶/高梯度模型（如应变梯度、微观形态、微极模型）缺乏系统化的模型选择判据，且在运动学描述中局部标架（''物质的directors''）的物理地位存在模糊性——它们究竟是描述微结构状态变化的物理量，还是仅作为变形的任意运动学描述符。

Method: 采用有限应变下广义连续介质的规范理论（gauge-theoretic）表述。定义广义构型为经典构型上的活动标架（moving frames），通过参考广义构型的不变性建立规范不变性的充要条件；利用结构群约化（structural group reduction）对一阶广义介质进行分类，通过对流标架（convected frames）恢复应变梯度连续介质，并处理约束介质（如力偶应力介质）。

Result: 证明了参考广义构型不变性是规范不变性的充要条件，由此将微观形态理论（micromorphic theory）恢复为任意物质方向（directors of matter）变形的一般性理论；通过结构群约化给出了第一阶广义介质的系统分类，并通过对流标架恢复应变梯度连续介质，最终处理了约束介质（如力偶应力介质）。

Conclusion: 该工作为广义连续介质模型提供了统一的分类框架，解决了模型选择缺乏系统判据的问题，并澄清了局部标架的物理地位——将其理解为物质的物理方向而非任意运动学描述符。

Abstract: Generalised continuum theories couple the macroscopic deformation and the micro-/meso-scopic deformation of an underlying micro-structure. They can account for internal length-scale effects and higher-order mechanical loadings absent from classical Cauchy elasticity and has shown its efficiency in modelling metamaterials. This has led to a proliferation of higher-grade and higher-order models (e.g. strain-gradient, micromorphic, micro-polar) whose underlying kinematic reduction strategies, in particular to reduce the number of material parameters, are rarely identifiable from the free energy alone. It leaves two open issues: the lack of a systematic criterion for selecting an appropriate model, and a persistent ambiguity regarding the physical status of the local frames (''directors of matter'') used in their kinematic description. Are they physical quantities describing the change of state of the given micro-structure or arbitrary kinematic descriptors of its deformation\,? This work addresses both questions through a gauge-theoretic formulation of generalised continua in finite strains. While relying on tools and modelling choices consistent with the existing geometric literature on continuum mechanics, the present approach departs from it in its objectives, aiming at a unifying classification of available mechanical models rather than the description of a specific microstructural phenomenon such as defects. In this work, generalised configurations are defined as moving frames over classical configurations, and invariance with respect to the reference generalised configuration is shown to be a necessary and sufficient condition for a gauge invariance, recovering the micromorphic theory as the general-purpose theory of the deformation of arbitrary directors of matter. A systematic classification of first-order generalised media follows from structural group reduction, while strain-gradient continua are recovered through convected frames, and finally constrained media (e.g. couple-stress) are addressed.

</details>


### [293] [A Hamiltonian approach to the CH-KP equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02028)
*R. Camassa,G. Falqui,E. Sforza*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从欧拉方程出发，通过长波渐近分析（long wave asymptotics）与哈密顿约化（Hamiltonian reduction）技术，推导出一个描述浅水层表面准单向波传播的模型方程——CH-KP方程，并借助狄拉克约化（Dirac reduction）得到其哈密顿结构，同时提供了弱peakon型解的碰撞行为示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型大多仅考虑纯单向传播或单一平面坐标的影响，缺乏同时涵盖非线性、色散以及弱第二平面坐标依赖性的统一方程。本文旨在填补这一空白，从基础流体力学方程出发系统推导出更一般的准单向波传播模型。

Method: 从欧拉方程（Euler equations）出发，采用长波渐近方法与哈密顿约化技术相结合；通过分层流体的全非线性长波系统进行狄拉克约化来获取模型的哈密顿结构；选择特定标度（scaling）使得系统在极限情形下约化为一维CH方程。

Result: 推导出CH-KP方程，它是著名的Kadomtsev–Petviashvili（KP）方程的温和非线性版本，其平衡了非线性、色散与弱的第二平面坐标依赖性；并给出了弱peakon型解（weak peakon-type solutions）的碰撞行为示例，用以展示极限方程支持的碰撞特性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，从欧拉方程出发可以通过约化流程得到具有明确哈密顿结构的准单向波传播模型，该模型在特定标度下可约化为一维CH方程，且其弱peakon型解的碰撞行为为理解极限方程的非线性动力学提供了参考。

Abstract: A model governing quasi-unidirectional wave propagation at the surface of a shallow layer of water is derived from Euler equations by long wave asymptotics together with Hamiltonian reduction techniques. The balance between nonlinearity, dispersion and weak dependence on the second planar coordinate is examined and results in the so-called CH-KP equation, a mildly nonlinear version of the well known Kadomtsev-Petviashvili equation. The Hamiltonian structure of the model is obtained by Dirac reduction from the intermediate step of a fully-nonlinear, long-wave system for stratified fluids. A particular scaling is considered that reduces such system to the one-dimensional CH equation in a limiting case. Examples of weak peakon-type solutions are provided, chosen to illustrate the collision behavior supported by the limiting equation.

</details>


### [294] [Quantum Vortices in a Boundary Layer: New Results and Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02057)
*Sergei V. Talalov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了薄圆形量子涡丝（quantum vortex filament）在无限平面表面附近的运动，发现该量子系统非常适合边界层理论（boundary layer theory）。通过作者先前提出的经典闭合涡丝量子化方法，计算了涡旋能量谱 E=E(p)，并证明了该函数对流体速度 v 存在复杂非平凡依赖，且涡旋的有效质量（effective mass）具有张量性质，在特定量子态下可同时出现正、负有效质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子涡旋系统在平面边界附近的动力学特性，并考察其与经典边界层理论的适配性，以深化对量子涡旋能量谱和有效质量等性质的理解。

Method: 采用作者先前开发的经典闭合涡丝量子化新方法（a novel quantization method for classical closed vortex filaments），对薄圆形量子涡丝在无限平面表面（表面外侧流体以平行于表面的非零速度 v 运动）附近的运动进行建模与量化分析。

Result: 推导出涡旋能量谱 E=E(p)（p 为涡旋环的总动量），证明该函数对流体速度 v 具有复杂非平凡依赖；发现涡旋的逆有效质量（inverse effective mass）为张量（tensorial）性质，且在特定量子态下系统可同时表现出正和负的有效质量。

Conclusion: 该量子涡丝系统展现出与边界层理论高度契合的独特性质，同时揭示了涡旋有效质量结构的新奇特征（正负共存及张量性质），拓展了量子涡旋系统理论的研究范畴。

Abstract: Here, we investigate the motion of a thin circular quantum vortex filament near the infinite planar surface. The fluid surrounding this surface moves with a non-zero velocity $\bf{v}$, which is parallel to the surface. We study the specific features of this quantum system and show that they are quite suitable for the boundary layer theory. The developed model allows us to calculate the vortex energy spectrum, $E = E({\bf p})$, where ${\bf p}$ is the total momentum of a vortex ring. 
We have demonstrated that this function has complex non-trivial dependence on the velocity $\bf{v}$. It is stated that the inverse effective mass of a vortex under consideration is of a tensorial nature. In certain quantum states, the system shows the possibility of both negative and positive effective mass existing. This study employs a novel quantization method for classical closed vortex filaments, developed by the author earlier.

</details>


### [295] [$q$-Deformed Topological Recursion: Quantum Curves and Non-perturbative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02179)
*Fridolin Melong,Raimar Wulkenhaar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了$(r,s)$-艾里结构（Airy structures）的$q$-形变及其通过$q$-差算子（$q$-difference operators）的实现，构建了与$q$-量子曲线（$q$-quantized curve）相关的矩阵系统的全阶$q$-WKB解，并证明了非微扰连通$q$-振幅满足平移$q$-环方程（shifted $q$-loop equations），这些方程可解释为$q$-形变$\mathcal{W}(\mathfrak{gl}_r)$代数的Ward恒等式。主要结果是对满足$q$-拓扑型性质的容许$(r,s,q)$参数对与$q$-Casimir构型给出严格分类，确保半经典展开由$q$-拓扑递归（$q$-topological recursion）唯一控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在连接量子谱曲线（quantum spectral curves）与可积系统（integrable systems），探索$q$-形变背景下Airy结构及其拓扑递归的推广。

Method: 通过$q$-差算子构造$(r,s)$-Airy结构的$q$-形变实现，构建全阶$q$-WKB解的方法，并利用$q$-环方程建立与$q$-形变$\mathcal{W}(\mathfrak{gl}_r)$代数的联系，进而对满足$q$-拓扑型性质的参数配置进行分类。

Result: 得到$q$-形变非微扰连通振幅满足平移$q$-环方程，并被诠释为Ward恒等式；同时完成了容许$(r,s,q)$参数对及$q$-Casimir构型的严格分类。

Conclusion: $q$-拓扑型性质保证了半经典展开由$q$-拓扑递归唯一决定，为镜曲线（mirror curves）的$q$-量子化及其底层代数结构提供了新的见解。

Abstract: This work investigates the $q$-deformation of $(r,s)$-Airy structures and their realization via $q$-difference operators, providing a bridge between quantum spectral curves and integrable systems. We construct an all-order $q$-WKB solution for the matrix systems associated with the $q$-quantized curve $E_q(x,y)=0$. We demonstrate that the resulting non-perturbative connected $q$-amplitudes satisfy a set of shifted $q$-loop equations, which can be interpreted as the Ward identities of a $q$-deformed $\mathcal{W}(\mathfrak{gl}_r)$ algebra. Our main result provides a rigorous classification of admissible $(r,s,q)$ pairs and $q$-Casimir configurations that satisfy the $q$-topological type property. This ensures that the semi-classical expansion is uniquely governed by the $q$-topological recursion, offering new insights into the $q$-quantization of mirror curves and their underlying algebraic structures.

</details>


### [296] [On the local conformal structure of Imaginary Liouville theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02543)
*Baptiste Cerclé,Romain Usciati*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在虚数Liouville理论（Imaginary Liouville theory）框架下建立了Ward恒等式（Ward identities）和Belavin-Polyakov-Zamolodchikov（BPZ）微分方程，为中央荷（central charge）小于1的非有理共形场论（non-rational CFT）实现共形自举程序（conformal bootstrap program）提供了第一步严格的数学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非有理共形场论中中央荷小于1的情形至今缺乏严格的路径积分构造与自举验证。此前虚数Liouville理论刚被提出作为此类理论的路径积分构建方案，但其内在性质尚未被系统研究。

Method: 作者在已建立的虚数Liouville理论路径积分框架内，推导并建立了Ward恒等式和BPZ微分方程，推导过程依赖一个关于虚数高斯乘法混沌（Imaginary Gaussian multiplicative chaos）的Laplace变换衰减的精确猜想。

Result: 作者成功建立了Ward恒等式和BPZ微分方程，这是首个超越精确计算、系统研究虚数Liouville理论内在性质的工作。

Conclusion: 所建立的恒等式与方程标志着朝向中央荷小于1的共形场论共形自举程序严格实现的第一步，为后续深入的非有理共形场论研究奠定了数学基础。

Abstract: Imaginary Liouville theory was recently proposed as a path integral construction of a non-rational conformal field theory (CFT) with central charge less than one (Usciati et al. 2026). In the present work, we establish Ward identities and Belavin-Polyakov-Zamolodchikov differential equations in this framework, assuming a precise conjecture on the decay of the Laplace transform of Imaginary Gaussian multiplicative chaos. These results provide a first step towards a rigorous implementation of the conformal bootstrap program for conformal field theories with central charge lower than one. This work is the first to investigate the intrinsic properties of Imaginary Liouville theory beyond exact computations.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.IM'></div>

# astro-ph.IM [[Back]](#toc)

### [297] [Directional Anisotropic Sensitivity Curves for Pulsar Timing Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00250)
*Daniel J. Oliver,Jeffrey S. Hazboun,Martine Maggi,Jeremy G. Baier,Kyle E. Gourlie*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该论文将方向性Fisher信息（directional Fisher information）重新表述为天空上的有效灵敏度曲线（effective sensitivity curves），区分了辐射计（radiometer）和完整Fisher估计量，并引入一种天空加权曲线，在各项同性极限下恢复标准各向同性曲线。作者在类IPTA脉冲星计时阵列上验证了该框架，并将其作为灵敏度软件包hasasia的扩展发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing array）经过二十年观测，已报告随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational wave background）的强有力证据，其最可能来源是超大质量黑洞双星（supermassive black hole binaries）的旋进群体。由于该群体有限，背景的各向异性不可避免，会在天空上产生追踪最响亮双星的亮点，因此需要开发描述各向异性背景的有效灵敏度框架。

Method: 论文基于各向异性背景的Fisher形式体系，将方向性Fisher信息重铸为天空上的有效灵敏度曲线，区分了辐射计与完整Fisher两种估计量，并提出一种天空加权曲线将各向异性天空简化为单一谱。作者实现了五种天空分解基：像素（pixel）、球谐（spherical harmonic）、平方根球谐（square-root spherical harmonic）、辐射计和主图（principal-map）。

Result: 该框架在类IPTA（International Pulsar Timing Array）脉冲星计时阵列上进行了验证：表征了角响应、分析了Fisher结构、恢复了注入的各向异性热点，并预报了未来阵列的灵敏度。该框架已作为灵敏度软件包hasasia的扩展公开发布。

Conclusion: 该工作为脉冲星计时阵列各向异性引力波背景的探测与表征提供了一个统一且可扩展的灵敏度分析工具，在各项同性极限下与标准结果一致，同时支持多种天空分解基以适应不同分析需求。

Abstract: After two decades of observations, pulsar timing array collaborations have reported strong evidence for a stochastic gravitational wave background, most likely sourced by an inspiraling population of supermassive black hole binaries. Because that population is finite, anisotropy in the background is inevitable, producing hotspots on the sky that track the loudest binaries. Building on the Fisher formalism for anisotropic backgrounds, we recast the directional Fisher information as effective sensitivity curves on the sky, distinguishing the radiometer and full-Fisher estimators, along with a sky-weighted curve that reduces the anisotropic sky to a single spectrum and recovers the standard isotropic curve in the isotropic limit. We implement five sky-decomposition bases: pixel, spherical harmonic, square-root spherical harmonic, radiometer, and principal-map. We demonstrate the framework on an IPTA-like pulsar timing array: characterizing the angular response, analyzing the Fisher structure, recovering injected anisotropic hotspots, and forecasting future-array sensitivity. The framework is released as an extension to the sensitivity software package hasasia.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [298] [Temperature-driven inversion and nonlinear dynamics in ChatGPT-like AIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00939)
*Neil F. Johnson,Frank Yingjie Huo,Bella Xinrui Li*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过对11个类ChatGPT人工智能模型生成的12000段文本续写进行统计分析，发现这类自回归（autoregressive）模型在提高解码器温度（decoder temperature）时，其长期输出群体的熵并不像普通多态系统那样单调增加，而是先经过一个熵极大值后进入粒子数反转（population inversion）状态。研究者揭示了隐藏坐标作为有效非线性映射（nonlinear map）状态变量的证据，其轨迹平均值能强有力地预测输出重复现象，表明类ChatGPT人工智能应被视为一类可控的非线性物理系统，而非"随机鹦鹉"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通多态系统升高温度会增加可访问状态范围从而增加熵，但研究者在类ChatGPT人工智能中发现相反的熵行为。作者希望通过揭示其内在动力学机制，验证这些模型是否可以被视为一类可测量的非线性物理系统，而不仅仅是随机语言生成器。

Method: 研究者收集了来自11个不同人工智能模型的12000段文本续写数据，通过改变解码器温度参数，分析输出群体随时间的演化轨迹。他们利用统计物理方法追踪熵的变化，并识别出冻结态、循环、间歇性和噪声诱导有序等现象，进一步通过寻找隐藏坐标构建有效非线性映射来描述系统的动力学状态。

Result: 研究发现，类ChatGPT模型的输出群体在长时间演化中会经历熵极大值后进入粒子数反转，而非随温度升高单调增加熵。该过渡过程伴随着冻结态、循环、间歇性和噪声诱导有序等复杂动力学现象。研究者找到了一个隐藏坐标作为系统状态变量，该坐标的轨迹平均值在独立的测试轨迹中能够强有力地预测输出重复程度。

Conclusion: 类ChatGPT人工智能不应被视为"随机鹦鹉"（stochastic parrots），而应被理解为一类新的可控非线性物理系统，其内部动力学是可测量的，并且可以通过外部扰动进行调控。

Abstract: Increasing the temperature of an ordinary many-state system increases access to a wider range of states and hence increases its entropy. We find the opposite in ChatGPT-like AIs, even though raising the decoder temperature likewise increases access to a wider range of states (next-token choices). Across 12,000 continuations from 11 AIs, autoregressive feedback drives the long-time output population through an entropy maximum and into population inversion. The transition features frozen states, cycles, intermittency and noise-induced ordering. We present evidence of a hidden coordinate that acts as the state variable of an effective nonlinear map. Its trajectory average strongly predicts output repetition in separate test trajectories. ChatGPT-like AIs therefore behave not as `stochastic parrots', but as a new class of controllable nonlinear physical systems whose internal dynamics can be measured and perturbed.

</details>


### [299] [Local network growth: How simple rules drive network complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01853)
*Alexei Vazquez*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本书提出，真实网络（如互联网、社交网络、蛋白质相互作用网络等）的全局规律并非由上而下强加，而是由纯局部规则逐步涌现而来——每个新节点仅基于已接触的节点及其近邻做出决策，无需全局视野。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去二十年中，复杂网络的全局模式被归因于"优先连接"（preferential attachment）机制，该规则在字面意义上要求每个新节点在建立连接前对整个网络进行全局扫描。作者认为这一前提与真实网络的形成过程不符，因此希望提出一种更简洁、更符合实际的解释框架。

Method: 作者逐一论证多种局部规则如何产生全局规律，包括：跟随链接的冲浪者、朋友介绍朋友的社交过程、基因连同其连接被复制等示例。书中将数学推导置于可略过的方框中，以平实语言向普通读者与专业读者同时讲述。

Result: 研究表明，从一组有限的局部规则出发，可以涌现出真实复杂系统完整的典型特征，包括少量巨型枢纽（hubs）、节点间的短路径、密集局部聚类（clustering）、社群结构以及多条冗余路径。

Conclusion: 引用图（citation graphs）、万维网、社会联系、蛋白质相互作用和项目调度等看似迥异的系统，均可通过同一组局部规则逐步生长而成，一次一个局部决策地自我建构。

Abstract: The Internet, a living cell, a circle of friends, a billion-dollar construction project: these systems share almost nothing -- yet, drawn as networks, they look astonishingly alike. Each has a few giant hubs among a multitude of sparsely connected nodes, short paths between any two parts, dense local clustering, communities, and many redundant routes. For two decades such patterns have been credited to "preferential attachment," the rich getting richer -- a rule that, taken literally, asks every newcomer to survey the whole network before it links. This book makes a simpler case, and defends it one mechanism at a time: the global regularities of real networks are not imposed from above but emerge from purely local rules, in which each new node acts only on a node it has reached and that node's immediate neighbours. A surfer following links, a friend introducing a friend, a gene copied with its connections -- none consults the network as a whole, yet each builds, in the aggregate, the full and unmistakable signature of a real complex system. Written for the curious reader as much as the specialist, with the ideas told in plain language and the mathematics set aside in boxes that can be skipped, it shows how citation graphs, the web, social ties, protein interactions, and project schedules all grow themselves from the same handful of local rules -- one local decision at a time.

</details>


### [300] [Microscopic dynamics of consensus formation in multi-agent LLM Naming Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02178)
*Cristiano De Nobili,Vijayasri Iyer,Alessandro Codello,Raffaella Burioni*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过最小化大语言模型（Large Language Model, LLM）命名游戏模型，发现解码温度（decoding temperature）T是控制去中心化LLM种群达成共识的关键架构依赖参数，并建立了从随机命名游戏推广到(π,φ)临界线的平均场理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明去中心化LLM智能体种群能自发达成共享约定的共识，但其内部随机性如何塑造宏观有序化的微观机制尚未被探索。

Method: 构建最小LLM命名游戏（LLM Naming Game），其中听者的决策是解码温度T下的单token LLM调用。将每次互动分解为库存内和库存外两个通道，条件速率分别为π(T)和φ(T)。基于两个速率的平均场理论（mean-field theory）推导解析有序化条件。

Result: 在三种开源架构中共识总能达成，但通过三种不同的听者机制实现：许可型（repaint噪声主导）、近确定性型和保守型（missed-collapse主导）。有效有限尺寸指数β(T)随温度变化，温度敏感性α在约0.67到约0之间随架构而变。

Conclusion: 解码温度作为LLM去中心化种群的架构依赖控制参数涌现，可用统计物理学工具箱定量刻画。

Abstract: Decentralized populations of Large Language Model (LLM) agents can spontaneously reach consensus on shared conventions, yet the microscopic mechanisms by which their internal stochasticity shapes macroscopic ordering remain unexplored. We study a minimal LLM Naming Game in which the listener's decision is a single-token LLM call at decoding temperature $T$, replacing the inventory check of the deterministic Naming Game. Each interaction decomposes into an in-inventory and an out-inventory channel with conditional rates $π(T)\!\equiv\!P(\text{YES}\mid w\in P_j)$ and $φ(T)\!\equiv\!P(\text{YES}\mid w\notin P_j)$, whose balance controls an ordering-disordering drift. A mean-field theory of the two-rate dynamics yields an analytical ordering condition that generalizes the consensus threshold of the stochastic Naming Game to a critical line in the $(π,φ)$ plane. Across three open-weight architectures, consensus is always reached, but through three distinct listener regimes: permissive (repaint-noise dominated), near-deterministic, and conservative (missed-collapse dominated). The effective finite-size exponent $β(T)$ in $t_{\rm conv}\!\sim\!N^β$ shifts with temperature, and the temperature-sensitivity $α$ in $t_c\!\sim\!e^{αT}$ ranges from ${\approx}\,0.67$ to ${\approx}\,0$ across architectures. Decoding temperature thus emerges as an architecture-dependent control parameter for decentralized LLM populations, quantitatively characterized by the statistical-physics toolkit.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

### [301] [Physics-informed neural networks for two-dimensional wall-reactive solute dispersion in canonical shear flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00856)
*Nanda Poddar,Subham Dhar*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural network, PINN）的无网格框架，用于模拟剪切流中被吸收壁约束的二维壁反应性溶质扩散过程。通过将对流-扩散方程与Robin边界条件嵌入统一损失函数，PINN成功重建了时空浓度场，并与交替方向隐式（alternating-direction implicit, ADI）有限差分基准结果在多种反应性条件下良好吻合，为边界耦合反应性输运问题提供了可解释的无网格分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在剪切流中，反应性溶质的扩散受平流拉伸、横向扩散与边界交换动力学的共同调控。虽然经典解析方法和基于网格的数值求解器已广泛刻画这些输运机制，但在不对称反应性环境中精确解析溶质羽流的时空演化仍计算成本高昂，亟需更高效且准确的求解工具。

Method: 本文构建了一个物理信息神经网络（PINN）框架，将控制方程——对流-扩散方程（convection-diffusion equation）与Robin边界条件——嵌入统一的损失函数中进行联合优化。该无网格方法用于模拟三种典型剪切流（Couette、Poiseuille及Couette-Poiseuille流）中被吸收壁包围的二维壁反应性溶质扩散，计算条件为Pe=10，涵盖不可渗透壁、对称吸收（β1,β2)=(1,1)以及十倍不对称壁反应性（β1,β2)=(0.2,2)和(2,0.2)等不同情景。

Result: PINN预测与交替方向隐式（ADI）有限差分基准在非反应性、对称性和不对称反应性三种体系中均表现出密切一致。利用训练后网络的可微性质，提取了壁分辨输运诊断量，包括表观轴向扩散系数、累积壁移除动力学和局部吸收通量。结果表明，剪切廓线主导了反应性吸收的流向组织，而壁反应性不对称则诱发横向不对称性，从而改变宏观扩散速率。

Conclusion: 该框架确立了PINN作为可解释的无网格工具在剪切流中边界耦合反应性输运分析中的可行性，为处理复杂反应性边界条件下的输运问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: The dispersion of reactive solutes in shear flows is governed by the interplay between advective stretching, transverse diffusion, and boundary exchange kinetics. While classical analytical methods and grid-based numerical solvers have extensively characterised these transport mechanisms, accurately resolving the spatiotemporal evolution of solute plumes in asymmetric reactive environments remains computationally demanding. In this study, we introduce a physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework to simulate two-dimensional wall-reactive solute dispersion in canonical shear flows (Couette, Poiseuille, and Couette-Poiseuille) bounded by absorbing walls. By embedding the governing convection-diffusion equation and Robin boundary conditions into a unified loss function, the mesh-free PINN reconstructs the spatiotemporal concentration field. The network predictions are validated against an alternating-direction implicit (ADI) finite-difference benchmark, showing close agreement across non-reactive, symmetric, and asymmetric reactive regimes. The computations are carried out at $\mathrm{Pe}=10$ for impermeable walls, symmetric absorption $(β_1,β_2)=(1,1)$, and tenfold asymmetric wall-reactivity contrasts $(β_1,β_2)=(0.2,2)$ and (2,0.2). Leveraging the differentiable nature of the trained PINN, we extract wall-resolved transport diagnostics, including the apparent axial dispersion coefficient, cumulative wall-removal dynamics, and localised uptake fluxes. The results show that the imposed shear profile governs the streamwise organisation of reactive uptake, while unequal wall reactivities induce transverse asymmetry that modifies the macroscopic spreading rate. Overall, this framework establishes PINNs as an interpretable mesh-free tool for analysing boundary-coupled reactive transport in shear flows.

</details>


### [302] [Stable and Efficient One-Way Modelling of Convective Disturbances in Laminar Boundary Layers: OWNS-Summation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01155)
*Elliot J. Badcock,Shahid Mughal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对单向纳维–斯托克斯（one-way Navier–Stokes，OWNS）谱投影子有理近似中递归求值导致舍入误差乘法放大的问题，提出了一种加法式部分分式求和算法（OWNS-Summation，OWNS-S）。该算法在每个解析子求解中独立作用于同一输入状态并加权求和，避免误差累积，使近似阶数N成为纯收敛参数且支持并行计算；同时引入配对贪心参数选择过程，并在不可压缩、高超声速和跨声速边界层的数值实验中验证了其有效性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的递归形式OWNS-R将近似写成N个解析因子（resolvent factor）的乘积，导致舍入误差被乘性放大，对近似阶数施加了一个无法事先预知的、依赖流的（flow-dependent）上限。这一缺陷限制了单向推进方法在更复杂流动中的可靠应用，因此本文旨在消除递归求值带来的数值不稳定性。

Method: 将同一谱投影子有理近似重写为加法式部分分式求和（additive partial-fraction sum）形式，即OWNS-S。每个解析子求解作用于相同的输入状态并独立贡献到加权和中，从而避免乘性误差放大。同时提出一种配对贪心（paired greedy）参数选择过程，从上下游谱区的解析估计中获取候选极点，避免在数值推进过程中进行特征分解（eigen-decomposition）。

Result: 在精确算术下OWNS-S与OWNS-R等同，但浮点运算下二者不再等价；使用与OWNS-R相同的辅助极点时，OWNS-S在所有测试配置中保持精度，表明递归求值而非极点选择是数值不稳定性的主要来源。OWNS-S在不可压缩、高超声速和跨声速边界层上均通过验证；在跨声速情形中，扰动谱从亚声速拓扑重组为超声速拓扑，单向计算以抛物线稳定性方程（parabolised stability equations）无法达到的流向分辨率连续穿过该转变。

Conclusion: OWNS-S通过加法式求和替代乘性递归，将近似阶数从受误差累积限制的未知参数转变为纯粹的收敛参数，同时天然支持并行解析子求解。该方法在谱拓扑转换等挑战性流动条件下仍能保持精度，为单向空间推进方法提供了更稳健、更高效的数值实现。

Abstract: One-way spatial marching methods separate upstream- from downstream-propagating disturbances using a rational approximation of a spectral projector. Among existing one-way Navier--Stokes (OWNS) formulations, the recursive variant, OWNS-R, is the most economical, evaluating the approximation as a product of $N$ resolvent factors. This product amplifies rounding errors multiplicatively, imposing a flow-dependent upper limit on the approximation order that cannot be known in advance.
  We reformulate the same approximation as an additive partial-fraction sum (OWNS-Summation, OWNS-S). In exact arithmetic, the recursive and summation evaluations are equivalent when supplied with identical weights and poles; in floating-point arithmetic, they are not. Each of the $N$ resolvent solves acts on the same input state and contributes independently to a weighted sum, preventing multiplicative error amplification. The approximation order $N$ therefore becomes a pure convergence parameter, and the solves can run in parallel.
  Using the same auxiliary poles as OWNS-R, OWNS-S remains accurate in every configuration tested, showing that the recursive evaluation, rather than the poles, is the dominant source of instability. A paired greedy parameter-selection procedure is also introduced, with candidates drawn from analytic estimates of the upstream and downstream spectral regions, avoiding eigen-decomposition during the numerical march.
  OWNS-S is validated on incompressible, hypersonic and transonic boundary layers. In the transonic case, the disturbance spectrum reorganises from a subsonic to a supersonic topology during the march. The one-way computation proceeds continuously through this transition at a streamwise resolution unattainable by the parabolised stability equations.

</details>


### [303] [Elasto-hydrodynamics of droplet-pool-interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00274)
*Md Sultan,Purbarun Dhar*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验、理论与模拟相结合的方法，系统探讨了聚合物弹性流体中液滴撞击液池时弹性储能、流体弛豫以及惯性-弹性-毛细力竞争如何共同控制空腔（cavity）、冠部（crown）和Worthington射流（Worthington jet）的时空演化，揭示了与牛顿流体相比全新且截然不同的形态学区域，并推导了空腔半径和射流半径演化的理论模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿流体中液滴撞击液池的流体动力学已被广泛研究，但弹性流体（elastic fluids）或Boger流体对应的流体动力学事件仍属于未探索的领域，缺乏系统的理解与表征。

Method: 采用实验、理论和数值模拟三管齐下的方法：在势流（potential-flow）框架内基于能量守恒推导空腔半径演化理论；利用FENE-P模型推导Worthington射流半径随时间演化的理论；通过合理变换液滴-液池的牛顿流体与弹性流体组合，在较宽的撞击韦伯数（Weber number）和德博拉数（Deborah number）范围内系统探索相关事件。

Result: 发现弹性流体中液滴撞击液池展现出与牛顿流体完全不同且截然不同的形态学区域，且这些新发现由流体弹性（elasticity）驱动而非黏度或界面张力；液滴撞击动能的30-40%可在空腔膨胀过程中以弹性能形式被拉伸的聚合物链储存；在弹性-毛细（elasto-capillary）区域，弹性应力与毛细应力的竞争导致射流半径呈指数衰减。

Conclusion: 本研究显著推进了液滴-液池相互作用弹性流体动力学（elastohydrodynamics）中惯性、毛细力与弹性三者相互作用的未探索范式，为该领域提供了系统的理论基础与实验依据。

Abstract: In Newtonian fluids, impact of a droplet on a liquid pool births a cavity, crown, capillary waves, and Worthington jet. The corresponding hydrodynamic events for elastic or Boger fluids, however, remain an uncharted domain of comprehension and exploration. We thoroughly investigate, via experiments, theory, and simulations, how elastic energy storage, fluid relaxation, and competitive inertio elasto capillarity govern the spatio temporal evolution of the cavity, the crown, and the ensuing Worthington jet in polymeric elastic fluids. The events are systematically explored over a wide range of impact Weber and Deborah numbers, considering varied Newtonian and elastic fluid droplet pool combinations, and revealing new, and distinct morphological regimes compared to Newtonian counterparts. We illustrate that these new findings are purely driven by fluid elasticity, and not by viscosity or interfacial tension. We derive a theory for cavity radius evolution, using energy conservation within potential-flow framework. We show that 30-40 % of the droplets kinetic impact energy may be stored as elastic energy by the stretching polymer chains during cavity expansion. Appealing to the FENE P model, we derive a theory for the temporal evolution of the radius of the elongated Worthington jet. We show that in elasto capillary regime, competitive elastic and capillary stresses lead to exponential decay of the jet radius. The role of elastic stresses and the local velocity field in governing cavity evolution, morphology, and jet formation are further elucidated through computer simulations. Our findings significantly advance the uncharted paradigm of interplay between inertia, capillarity, and elasticity in droplet-pool interaction elastohydrodynamics.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [304] [Noise-induced stability of insect swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00314)
*Justin Faber,Annabelle Boots,Dolores Bozovic*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于声学通讯（acoustic communication）的理论模型来描述飞行昆虫蚊群的聚群行为，其中每个个体被吸引向其邻居的加权平均场（weighted mean field）。该简单框架能够描述多种聚群动力学，包括成对昆虫以螺旋轨道脱离群体的现象；数值模型显示随机噪声（stochastic noise）反而增强蚊群稳定性；并发现特定疟蚊物种的蚊群处于失稳转变的边缘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行昆虫蚊群表现出凝聚力，但缺乏鸟类群体或鱼群中观察到的局部速度对齐（local velocity alignment），昆虫之间导致集体行为的交互规则和通信渠道尚未被确定。

Method: 提出基于个体间声学通讯的理论模型，其中每个个体被吸引至其邻居的加权平均场，并通过数值模拟（numerical simulation）研究该模型的动力学行为。

Result: 该框架能描述广泛的聚群动力学，包括成对昆虫以螺旋轨道（helical orbit）相互环绕并脱离群体的现象；数值模型表明随机噪声通过干扰对形成而增强蚊群稳定性；特定疟蚊物种产生的蚊群处于失稳转变（transition to instability）的边缘。

Conclusion: 相当简单的局部交互规则足以描述群居生物系统的集体行为，为理解昆虫聚群的声学通信机制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Flying insect swarms exhibit cohesion without the local velocity alignment observed in flocks of birds or schools of fish. The interaction rules and channels of communication between insects that lead to collective behavior have not yet been determined. We propose a theoretical model based on acoustic communication between swarm members, where each individual is attracted to a weighted mean field of its neighbors. We demonstrate that this simple framework can describe a wide range of swarming dynamics, including a phenomenon where pairs of insects break free from the swarm in a helical orbit around each other. Counterintuitively, our numerical model suggests that stochastic noise enhances the stability of an insect swarm, as it interferes with pair formation. Finally, we demonstrate that a specific species of malarial mosquito produces swarms that reside on the edge of a transition to instability. Our study shows that fairly simple local interaction rules can be sufficient to describe the collective behavior of swarming biological systems.

</details>


### [305] [Water and the Many-Body Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01097)
*Albert Libchaber,Tsvi Tlusty*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文借鉴费米液体理论（Fermi liquid theory）中准粒子（quasiparticle）的概念，将电子穿越费米海（Fermi sea）重新规范化后成为准粒子的思想迁移到水体系：电子类比为偶极子（dipole），费米海类比为氢键极性液体，并追问水的集体模式（collective modes）是否具有足够长的寿命和传播距离，以使分子机器（molecular machines）能够相互作用并同步化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出一个跨领域类比：将凝聚态物理中Nozières关于费米液体重正化的核心教训推广到水的复杂液体系统，探究水的集体激发能否支撑分子机器的长程相互作用与同步行为。

Method: 采用类比推理（analogical reasoning）的方法论，将电子在费米海中被屏蔽、重整化的物理图像映射为偶极子在氢键极性液体中的行为，并以此框架重新审视水的集体模式。

Result: 本文的核心科学问题是：水的集体模式是否足够长寿命且传播足够远，以支持分子机器间的相互作用与同步化。摘要本身未给出明确的数值或实验结论。

Conclusion: 以Nozières的准粒子概念为启发，提出将水视为一个可对偶极子进行重整化的极性液体系统，并将其集体模式视为分子机器间同步通信的可能媒介，为水的动力学研究提供了一个新的理论视角。

Abstract: An electron crosses the cold Fermi sea. The sea recoils, screens, remembers. It returns the electron dressed: a quasiparticle with a mass, a lifetime, and a Green function. This was Nozières' lesson. We carry it to water. Electrons become dipoles; the Fermi sea becomes a hydrogen-bonded polar liquid. We ask the same question: can the collective modes of water live long enough and reach far enough for molecular machines to interact and synchronize?

</details>


### [306] [Funnel-like protein energy landscapes emerge from functional evolution under thermal fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01143)
*Norifumi Maruyama,Macoto Kikuchi*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过二维格点蛋白模型探究折叠性蛋白是否可以在没有直接折叠性选择压力下，仅凭功能选择自然涌现。研究发现只有适当环境温度范围内，高适应度序列才能自发获得漏斗状能量景观（funnel-like energy landscape），表明可折叠蛋白可作为维持功能于热涨落条件的热力学结果而涌现，而非进化的直接目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然蛋白通过进化塑造的漏斗状能量景观稳定其特定天然结构，但折叠性本身是否是进化的直接选择目标尚不清楚。作者旨在检验一种更根本的可能性：仅凭功能选择（functional selection）是否足以使可折叠蛋白涌现。

Method: 采用含四种氨基酸类型的二维格点蛋白模型，将蛋白功能定义为在环境温度T下预定局部结构形成活性位点的平衡概率，并以此概率作为进化适应度。通过多重正则蒙特卡罗（multicanonical Monte Carlo）方法跨越整个适应度范围采样氨基酸序列，并从平衡构象系综构建其能量与自由能景观。

Result: 仅在适当的环境温度范围内，高适应度序列才自发获得漏斗状能量景观，尽管折叠性从未包含在适应度定义中；高适应度序列仅由有限数量的天然构象支持。相反，在低温下，高适应度序列表现出崎岖、玻璃态（glass-like）的能量景观和更大的结构多样性。

Conclusion: 可折叠蛋白不必是进化的直接目标，而可以作为在热涨落下维持功能的热力学后果而涌现：局部功能约束组织全局折叠结构，从而产生漏斗状能量景观。

Abstract: Proteins perform biological functions by folding into specific native structures that are stabilized by funnel-like energy landscapes shaped through evolution. We investigate whether such foldable proteins can emerge solely from selection for function, without any direct selection for foldability. Using a two-dimensional lattice protein model with four amino-acid types, we define protein function as the equilibrium probability that a prescribed local structure forming an active site is realized at environmental temperature $T$, and use this probability as the evolutionary fitness. Amino-acid sequences spanning the entire fitness range are sampled by multicanonical Monte Carlo method and their energy and free-energy landscapes are constructed from the equilibrium conformational ensemble. We find that only within an appropriate range of environmental temperatures do high-fitness sequences spontaneously acquire funnel-like energy landscapes, despite foldability never being included in the fitness definition. High-fitness sequences are furthermore supported by only a limited number of native conformations. At low temperatures, in contrast, high-fitness sequences exhibit rugged, glass-like energy landscapes and much greater structural diversity. These results suggest that foldable proteins need not be direct targets of evolution, but can emerge as a thermodynamic consequence of maintaining function under thermal fluctuations, whereby local functional constraints organize the global fold and give rise to funnel-like energy landscapes.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [307] [Laskar's frequency map analysis revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02182)
*Zhicheng Tong,Yong Li*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文建立了Laskar频率映射分析（frequency map analysis）的指数收敛性，严格优于经典多项式界；通过选取适当的加权函数，在满足Diophantine或Brjuno非共振条件的解析准周期（analytic quasi-periodic）框架下实现指数收敛速率，并将理论推广至解析几乎周期（analytic almost periodic）情形，为频率映射分析提供了首个统一的指数收敛理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Laskar频率映射分析仅具有多项式收敛速率（polynomial bounds），无法满足天文力学等应用中对高精度频率提取的需求，因此需要建立更优的收敛理论。

Method: 通过选择适当的加权函数（weighting functions）来实现指数收敛；在非共振条件（nonresonance conditions）方面分别处理Diophantine与Brjuno两类约束；推广Bourgain的框架以容纳更广泛类型的各向异性空间结构（anisotropic spatial structures），从而将理论从准周期情形扩展到几乎周期情形。

Result: 建立了频率映射分析的指数收敛速率，严格改进了经典的多项式界；首次在统一框架下揭示了解析性、空间结构、非共振条件、加权函数选择与收敛速率之间的相互作用；新方法还为加权Birkhoff平均（weighted Birkhoff averages）的研究提供了新见解。

Conclusion: 该理论框架为频率映射分析提供了首个统一的指数收敛理论，对天体力学（celestial mechanics）等应用领域具有重要影响，并为加权Birkhoff平均的研究开辟了新的分析途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the exponential convergence of Laskar's pioneering frequency map analysis, strictly improving upon classical polynomial bounds. By utilizing appropriately chosen weighting functions, we achieve such exponential rates in the analytic quasi-periodic regime for frequency vectors satisfying Diophantine or Brjuno nonresonance conditions. Furthermore, we extend the theoretical framework beyond the analytic quasi-periodic regime into the analytic almost periodic setting. By employing and generalizing Bourgain's framework, we accommodate a much broader class of anisotropic spatial structures. These results yield the first unified theory of exponential convergence for frequency map analysis, revealing the interplay among analyticity, spatial structures, nonresonance conditions, the choice of weighting functions, and convergence rates. This framework has important implications for applications in fields including celestial mechanics. Moreover, the novel techniques developed herein yield new insights into the study of weighted Birkhoff averages.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [308] [Residue construction of quantized Coulomb branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01379)
*Daniil Klyuev*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了任意 G,N 下量子化 BFN 库仑分支（quantized BFN Coulomb branch）在局域化（abelianization）映射下的像的完整描述，并以此为基础，为 Gaiotto 和 Okazaki 引入的球面迹（sphere trace）提供了数学构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子化库仑分支在数学物理中具有重要地位，但通过局域化映射描述其像长期以来缺乏一般性的统一框架。作者旨在针对任意 G,N 建立该像的完整描述，并将其应用于 Gaiotto-Okazaki 球面迹的数学构造。

Method: 本文采用局域化（abelianization）映射方法，将量子化 BFN 库仑分支 $\mathcal{A}_{G,N}^{\hbar=1}$ 的像用根与留数条件（roots and residue condition）刻画，该条件类似于 Ginzburg-Kapranov-Vasserot 在 DAHA 构造中提出的方案。对于一般的味参数（flavor parameters），多数情形（如无环的箭图规范理论）下描述对所有味均成立，个别情形需取一般或形式味参数。

Result: 作为推论，对任意量子化锥形库仑分支（在零味或小味情形下），作者给出了 Gaiotto-Okazaki 球面迹的数学构造。

Conclusion: 该工作将局域化描述统一到一般性的框架下，并为球面迹提供了严格的数学基础，打通了物理构造与数学理论之间的桥梁。

Abstract: We describe the image of a quantized BFN Coulomb branch $\mathcal{A}_{G,N}^{\hbar=1}$ under localization (abelianization) map for any $G,N$. In most cases, such as quiver gauge theories without loops, this description works for any flavors, sometimes we need to take generic or formal flavor parameters. The answer is given by a roots and residue condition similar to Ginzburg---Kapranov---Vasserot construction of DAHA arXiv:alg-geom/9512017. As a corollary, for any quantized conical Coulomb branch (and zero or small flavors) we provide a mathematical construction of the sphere trace introduced by Gaiotto and Okazaki arXiv:1911.11126.

</details>


### [309] [Hecke algebra representations from the Katz-Long-Moody construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01727)
*Haru Negami*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Katz-Long-Moody（KLM）构造，并精确分类了由此产生的辫群（braid group）表示何时可穿过Hecke代数分解。除了一个例外情形，该性质仅依赖于自由群部分的特征值，与构造参数无关；同时还给出了穿过Temperley-Lieb代数分解的精确判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KLM构造是生成辫群表示的重要方法，但此前关于其表示何时能通过Hecke代数或因式化（factor through）Hecke代数的问题尚未得到精确分类。明确这一条件有助于理解KLM构造与量子群表示论之间的联系。

Method: 对KLM构造产生的辫群表示进行系统的代数分析，分类其穿过Hecke代数分解的条件；通过考察自由群部分的特征值（eigenvalues）与构造参数的关系，给出精确的分类准则。

Result: 证明了除了一个例外的双股辫（two-strand）情形外，KLM表示穿过Hecke代数分解的性质仅取决于自由群部分的特征值，与构造参数无关；并给出了表示穿过Temperley-Lieb代数分解的精确判据。

Conclusion: 完成了KLM构造产生的辫群表示穿过Hecke代数和Temperley-Lieb代数分解条件的精确刻画，加深了对KLM构造及其在辫群表示理论中作用的理解。

Abstract: We study the Katz-Long-Moody (KLM) construction and classify exactly when the resulting braid group representations factor through Hecke algebras. We show that, except for one exceptional two-strand family, this property depends only on the eigenvalues of the free-group part and is independent of the construction parameter. We also give an exact criterion for when the resulting representations factor through Temperley-Lieb algebras.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [310] [Parton distribution functions from lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00251)
*Martha Constantinou,Krzysztof Cichy*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文综述了用格点QCD（lattice QCD）计算部分子分布函数（parton distribution functions, PDFs）的理论基础与当前进展，重点介绍基于空间非局域矩阵元的现代方法（如大动量有效理论、短距离因子化和短距离算符乘积展开），并总结了质子、介子和胶子PDF等最新格点计算结果及其系统性不确定度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDFs直接描述QCD中强子的部分子结构，但传统定义依赖于光锥关联（light-cone correlations），而格点QCD在欧几里得时空（Euclidean spacetime）中表述，两者之间存在根本性的不匹配，需要新的理论框架来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 首先回顾夸克和胶子PDF的光锥定义、Mellin矩（Mellin moments）及与QCD因子化（QCD factorization）的联系；继而阐述大动量有效理论（large-momentum effective theory）、短距离因子化（short-distance factorization）和短距离算符乘积展开（short-distance operator product expansion）如何将欧几里得格点可观测量关联到光锥部分子结构。

Result: 概述了近五年在Wilson线算符重整化（renormalization of Wilson-line operators）、微扰匹配（perturbative matching）、有限动量和有限距离效应、x依赖性的重建以及系统性不确定度等方面的改进；并总结了质子夸克PDF、π介子和K介子PDF、胶子PDF以及twist-3分布的选择性格点计算结果。

Conclusion: 格点QCD正从原理验证性（proof-of-principle）计算向系统性可改进的确定发展，能够为实验数据和全局QCD分析提供补充，共同刻画强子的部分子结构。

Abstract: Parton distribution functions (PDFs) provide one of the most direct ways to describe the partonic structure of hadrons in QCD. They encode nonperturbative information about quarks, antiquarks, and gluons as functions of the partonic momentum fraction $x$, and they connect this microscopic structure to experimentally measurable high-energy scattering processes through QCD factorization. This makes PDFs interesting to pursue with lattice QCD, which provides a first-principles formulation of the strong interaction. The challenge is that PDFs are defined through light-cone correlations, while lattice QCD is formulated in Euclidean spacetime.
  This chapter introduces the theoretical foundations and current status of lattice-QCD calculations of PDFs, with emphasis on modern approaches based on spatially nonlocal matrix elements. We first review the light-cone definitions of quark and gluon PDFs, their Mellin moments, and the connection to QCD factorization. We then explain how large-momentum effective theory, short-distance factorization, and the short-distance operator product expansion make it possible to relate Euclidean lattice observables to light-cone partonic structure. Particular attention is given to the elements that have improved over the last five years: renormalization of Wilson-line operators, perturbative matching, finite-momentum and finite-distance effects, reconstruction of the $x$ dependence, and systematic uncertainties.
  We summarize selected lattice results for proton quark PDFs, pion and kaon PDFs, gluon PDFs, and twist-3 distributions, highlighting both recent progress and remaining challenges. As will be demonstrated, lattice QCD is moving from proof-of-principle calculations toward systematically improvable determinations that can complement experimental data and global QCD analyses in mapping the partonic structure of hadrons.

</details>


### [311] [Arbitrary-Distance Quantum Error Correction with Gauss's Law for $\mathbb Z_2$ Lattice Gauge Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00165)
*Neel S. Modi,Lento Nagano,Masazumi Honda,Nobuyuki Yoshioka,Christian W. Bauer*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文在Rajput、Roggero和Wiebe先前工作的基础上，将基于$\mathbb Z_2$高斯定律约束（Gauss's law constraints）构造的量子纠错码（quantum error-correcting codes, QECCs）推广为可抵抗任意$t$比特量子错误（$t$为任意正整数）的通用方案，并推导了该族代码中给定码距（code distance）下最优代码的物理量子比特数，最后通过多项指标与其他高效量子纠错码进行了系统对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前已有研究表明$\mathbb Z_2$高斯定律约束可用于构造对任意单量子比特错误鲁棒的量子纠错码，但缺乏对任意$t$比特错误的推广。本文旨在将该构造推广至任意$t$比特错误，并评估其相对于通用量子纠错码的性能优势。

Method: 推广高斯定律码（Gauss's law code）的构造，使其能抵抗任意$t$比特量子错误；通过最小化给定码距所需的物理量子比特数，推导该码族中最优码的构造参数。在码容量（code capacity）设定下，从物理量子比特数、编码哈密顿量的局域性（locality）以及逻辑错误率（logical error rate）等指标出发，将所得码与通用的高效量子纠错码进行比较。

Result: 与对所有晶格自由度采用与领域无关（domain-agnostic）的通用码相比，高斯定律码的主要优势在于显著降低了编码哈密顿量的局域性。此外，当$t \le 3$（即码距$d \le 7$）时，物理量子比特的开销也有所降低。

Conclusion: 高斯定律码在编码哈密顿量局域性方面具有明显优势，并在较小码距范围内同时减少了物理量子比特开销，表明利用物理对称性构造的量子纠错码在实用层面具有竞争力。

Abstract: It has previously been shown by Rajput, Roggero, and Wiebe that $\mathbb Z_2$ Gauss's law constraints can be used to build efficient quantum error-correcting codes (QECCs) that are robust against arbitrary single-qubit errors. In this work, we generalize the construction to be robust against arbitrary $t$-qubit errors, where $t$ is any positive integer. This includes a derivation of the optimal Gauss's law code within the considered family by minimizing the number of physical qubits required for a given code distance. Finally, we compare our codes against other efficient QECCs on metrics such as the number of physical qubits, the locality of the encoded Hamiltonian, and the logical error rate in the code capacity setting. Compared to using a domain-agnostic code for every lattice degree of freedom, we find that the Gauss's law code primarily excels at reducing the locality of the encoded Hamiltonian. Moreover, the physical qubit overhead is also reduced for $t \le 3$ (distance $d \le 7$).

</details>
